<style>
:root{--green:#164b3b;--green2:#1d674f;--gold:#d6b36a;--cream:#fbf8ef;--ink:#1e332c;--muted:#718078;--line:#dce5dc;--panel:rgba(255,255,255,.94);--shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(17,49,38,.10);--radius:24px;}
*{box-sizing:border-box;}html,body{margin:0;min-height:100%;font-family:"Cairo",Tahoma,sans-serif;color:var(--ink);}body{background:radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%,rgba(217,194,137,.17),transparent 28%),linear-gradient(135deg,#f7f4e9,#edf5ef);overflow-x:hidden;transition:background .25s ease;}
button,input,select,textarea{font:inherit;}button{cursor:pointer;border:0;}button:focus-visible,input:focus-visible,select:focus-visible,textarea:focus-visible{outline:3px solid 104px;gap:18px;position:relative;isolation:isolate;}.app:before{content:"۞";position:fixed;z-index:-1;right:-38px;top:34%;font-size:240px;color:rgba(22,75,59,.035);pointer-events:none;}.topbar{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto 1fr;align-items:center;gap:14px;width:100%;max-width:1450px;margin:0 auto;}.capsule{background:var(--panel);border:1px solid rgba(211,224,212,.92);box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(22,75,59,.18);}.brand-logo,.teacher-photo{flex-shrink:0;display:grid;place-items:center;border:0;overflow:hidden;background:#edf4eb;color:var(--green);transition:transform .18s,box-shadow .18s;}.brand-logo{width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:18px;border:1px solid #e2dfc9;}.brand-logo:hover,.teacher-photo:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 7px 16px #0e36231c;}.brand-logo img,.teacher-photo img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;}.brand-copy,.teacher-copy{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;min-width:0;}.click-title{padding:0;background:transparent;text-align:right;color:var(--green);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(16px,1.45vw,21px);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:240px;}.tiny-caption{font-size:11px;color:#839088;}.teacher-photo{width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:50%;border:2px .18s;}.verse-bubble:hover{background:#dcebdc;transform:translateY(-1px);}.verse-bubble.active{background:var(--green);color:#fff;border-color:var(--green);box-shadow:0 4px 10px input{width:72px;border:1px solid 0;}.verse-card{width:min(100%,1020px);position:relative;border-radius:28px;border:1px solid #e4ddc4;box-shadow:var(--shadow);padding:clamp(24px,4vw,52px) clamp(17px,4vw,55px) 30px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.97),rgba(255,253,245,.97));overflow:hidden;text-align:center;}.verse-card:before,.verse-card:after{content:"۞";position:absolute;color:rgba(183,150,75,.17);font-size:40px;top:13px;}.verse-card:before{right:17px;}.verse-card:after{left:17px;}.card-topline{display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;gap:11px;font-size:11px;color:#8a927e;margin-bottom:15px;}.ornament-line{height:1px;width:clamp(24px,8vw,95px);background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#c7ad69,transparent);}.bismillah{font-family:"Amiri",serif;font-size:clamp(24px,3vw,34px);color:#54745e;margin-bottom:20px;display:none;}.bismillah.show{display:block;}.verse-text{font-family:"Amiri","Traditional Arabic",serif;font-size:clamp(34px,4.5vw,55px);font-weight:700;line-height:2.05;color:#203c2d;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:.1em .25em;direction:rtl;max-width:920px;margin:0 auto;}.word{display:inline-block;padding:0 2px;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;transition:background .15s,color .15s,box-shadow .15s;}.word:hover{background:#eef3df;color:#164b3b;}.word.speaking{background:#f5df9e;color:#634c17;box-shadow:0 0 0 4px #f5df9e66;animation:pulseWord .8s infinite alternate;}
.tajweed{font-weight:700;}
/* ألوان أحكام التجويد المطلوبة */
.tajweed.slnt,.tajweed.silent,.tajweed.sukoon,.tajweed.ham_wasl,.tajweed.laam_shamsiyah,.tajweed.idgham_wo_ghunnah,.tajweed.idgham_mutajanisayn,.tajweed.idgham_mutaqaribayn{color:#808080!important;} /* الساكن وهمزة الوصل واللام الشمسية والإدغام بغير غنة */
.tajweed.madda_normal,.tajweed.madda_badal{color:#c99700!important;} /* المد الطبيعي ٢ حركة */
.tajweed.madda_permissible,.tajweed.madda_obligatory_monfasel{color:#e67e22!important;} /* المد الجائز المنفصل والعارض للسكون */
.tajweed.madda_obligatory,.tajweed.madda_muttasil,.tajweed.madda_obligatory_mottasel{color:#e53935!important;} /* المد الواجب المتصل */
.tajweed.madda_necessary{color:#8f1018!important;} /* المد اللازم ٦ حركات */
.tajweed.ghunnah,.tajweed.idgham_ghunnah,.tajweed.idgham_shafawi,.tajweed.ikhafa,.tajweed.ikhafa_shafawi,.tajweed.iqlab{color:#27ae60!important;} /* الغنة والإخفاء والإقلاب والإدغام بغنة */
.tajweed.qalaqah{color:#00bcd4!important;} /* القلقلة */
.tajweed.tafkhim,.tajweed.ra{color:#0d47a1!important;} /* التفخيم */

.dark-mode .tajweed.slnt,.dark-mode .tajweed.silent,.dark-mode .tajweed.sukoon,.dark-mode .tajweed.ham_wasl,.dark-mode .tajweed.laam_shamsiyah,.dark-mode .tajweed.idgham_wo_ghunnah,.dark-mode .tajweed.idgham_mutajanisayn,.dark-mode .tajweed.idgham_mutaqaribayn{color:#9e9e9e!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_normal,.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_badal{color:#dfb128!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_permissible,.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_obligatory_monfasel{color:#f39c12!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_obligatory,.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_muttasil,.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_obligatory_mottasel{color:#ff5252!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.madda_necessary{color:#ff1744!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.ghunnah,.dark-mode .tajweed.idgham_ghunnah,.dark-mode .tajweed.idgham_shafawi,.dark-mode .tajweed.ikhafa,.dark-mode .tajweed.ikhafa_shafawi,.dark-mode .tajweed.iqlab{color:#2ecc71!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.qalaqah{color:#26c6da!important;}
.dark-mode .tajweed.tafkhim,.dark-mode .tajweed.ra{color:#64b5f6!important;}

rgba(16,52,35,.19);backdrop-filter:blur(16px);display:none;gap:12px;flex-direction:column;direction:rtl;}.pen-toolbar.show{display:flex;animation:rise .18s ease-out;}@keyframes rise{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.pen-toolbar-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;}.pen-toolbar-head input{width:100%;accent-color:#28664c;}.record-indicator{font-size:11px;color:#a45147;display:none;}.record-indicator.show{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;}.record-indicator solid #e5deca;border-radius:24px;width:min(520px,100%);padding:24px;box-shadow:0 24px 70px rgba(7,30,18,.25);animation:rise .18s ease-out;}.modal h2{margin:0 0 6px;color:#174b3b;font-size:20px;}.modal p{margin:0 0 16px;color:#7b887e;font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;}.modal label{display:block;font-size:12px;color:#536d5c;margin-bottom:6px;}.modal input,.modal select,.modal textarea{width:100%;border:1px solid #dce6da;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 12px;background:white;color:#20382b;outline:none;}.modal textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:150px;line-height:1.9;}.modal-actions{display:flex;justify-content:flex-start;gap:8px;margin-top:16px;}.modal-actions .control-btn{min-width:95px;}.chapter-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:11px;margin:12px 0;}.background-image{background-size:cover;background-position:center;background-repeat:no-repeat;}.background-image:after{content:"";position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(255,255,255,.24);pointer-events:none;z-index:-1;}.app.dark-mode{--ink:#edf4eb;--muted:#a5b6aa;--panel:rgba(26,49,40,.92);--line:#375445;background:radial-gradient(circle @media(max-width:900px){.app{padding:12px 12px 110px;gap:13px}.topbar{grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:9px}.surah-pill{grid-column:1/-1;grid-row:2;justify-self:center;min-height:55px;width:auto}.brand{grid-column:2;grid-row:1;justify-self:stretch}.teacher{grid-column:1;grid-row:1;justify-self:stretch}.capsule{min-height:65px;padding:8px 11px;gap:8px}.brand-logo,.teacher-photo{width:44px;height:44px}.click-title{font-size:14px;max-width:170px}.tiny-caption{font-size:9px}.surah-name{font-size:23px}.top-status{top:12px;left:12px;display:none}.verse-card{padding:31px 17px 24px}.verse-text{line-height:1.95}.center-wrap{gap:10px}.control-card{padding:11px}.bottom-zone{bottom:13px;max-width:calc(100vw - 95px);left:calc(50% + 22px);gap:5px;padding:7px}.bg-label{display:none}.bg-btn{width:25px;height:25px}.bg-upload{font-size:10px;padding:7px}.pen-fab{width:54px;height:54px;left:12px;bottom:13px}.pen-toolbar{left:10px;bottom:77px;width:calc(100vw - 20px);padding:12px}.pen-settings{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.pen-settings .slider-field:last-child{grid-column:1/-1}.audio-row{gap:6px}.control-btn{padding:9px 11px;font-size:12px}.verse-bubble{width:31px;min-width:31px;height:31px}.more-btn{height:31px;min-width:38px}.verse-stage{min-height:240px}}
@media(max-width:480px){.app{padding-inline:8px}.capsule{border-radius:19px;padding:7px}.brand-logo,.teacher-photo{width:39px;height:39px}.click-title{font-size:12px;max-width:125px}.teacher-name{font-size:11px;max-width:125px}.tiny-caption{font-size:8px}.surah-pill{padding:8px 21px;min-height:50px}.surah-name{font-size:21px}.control-card{border-radius:18px;padding:9px 7px}.audio-row .control-btn span.optional{display:none}.select-wrap{max-width:calc(100vw - 30px)}.select-wrap select{max-width:130px}.verse-text{font-size:clamp(31px,8vw,40px)}.verse-card{border-radius:22px;padding:34px 12px 22px}.verse-card:before,.verse-card:after{font-size:27px}.ayah-end{min-width:36px;height:36px;font-size:18px}.card-topline{margin-bottom:11px}.bismillah{font-size:25px;margin-bottom:13px}.chapter-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.toolset{gap:4px}.tool-btn{width:34px;height:33px}.pen-toolbar{max-height:72vh;overflow:auto}.pen-settings{gap:8px}.bg-upload{padding:6px 7px}.api-note{font-size:9px}}

/* تحسينات هذه النسخة: مساحة متوازنة عند التكبير الافتراضي 100% */
html,body{width:100%;min-height:100%;}html{font-size:16px;scroll-behavior:smooth;}.top-status{display:none!important;}.app{min-height:100svh;padding:12px 20px 88px;gap:10px;}.topbar{max-width:1500px;gap:10px;}.capsule{min-height:66px;padding:8px 13px;}.surah-pill{min-height:58px;padding:9px 22px;}.surah-kicker{display:none;}.center-wrap{gap:9px;max-width:1220px;}.control-card{padding:9px 12px;border-radius:20px;}.verse-nav{min-height:32px;gap:6px;}.verse-bubble{min-width:31px;height:31px;font-size:12px;}.more-btn{height:31px;}.audio-row{margin-top:8px;gap:7px;}.control-btn{padding:8px 12px;}.nav-arrow,.icon-only{width:37px;height:37px;min-width:37px;padding:0;border-radius:12px;}.nav-arrow svg,.icon-only svg{display:block;}.verse-stage{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:0;padding:2px 0;}.verse-card{display:table;width:fit-content;max-width:min(100%,1120px);padding:24px clamp(20px,3vw,42px) 18px;border-radius:23px;}.verse-card:before,.verse-card:after{font-size:28px;top:8px;}.card-topline{margin-bottom:8px;}.bismillah{font-family:"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;font-size:clamp(21px,2.5vw,30px);margin-bottom:8px;}.verse-text{font-family:"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;font-size:clamp(31px,3.4vw,49px);line-height:1.8;max-width:min(84vw,980px);gap:.06em .20em;letter-spacing:0;font-feature-settings:"liga" 1,"calt" 1;}.word{padding:0 1px;}.ayah-end{min-width:36px;height:36px;font-size:18px;margin:0 3px;}.verse-footer{margin-top:10px;font-size:10px;}.api-note{max-width:900px;margin-top:0;}.bottom-zone{gap:6px;padding:7px 9px;bottom:12px;max-width:calc(100vw - 105px);overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:thin;}.bg-label{font-size:10px;}.bg-btn{width:27px;height:27px;flex:0 0 27px;}.bg-cycle{flex:0 0 25px;width:25px;height:27px;border-radius:9px;background:#edf4e9;color:#245842;font-size:22px;line-height:1;display:grid;place-items:center;}.custom-background-buttons{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;}.custom-thumb{background-size:cover;background-position:center;background-repeat:no-repeat;}.bg-upload{font-size:10px;padding:6px 9px;}.pen-fab{width:51px;height:51px;bottom:12px;left:12px;border-radius:17px;}.pen-toolbar{bottom:72px;left:12px;width:min(900px,calc(100vw - 24px));max-height:min(66vh,500px);overflow:auto;padding:11px 13px;gap:9px;}.sticker-actions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.sticker-actions small{font-size:10px;color:#7d897f;}.sticker-layer{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:55;pointer-events:none;}.sticker-item{position:fixed;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;max-width:min(240px,60vw);pointer-events:auto;touch-action:none;user-select:none;}.sticker-item img{display:block;max-width:min(220px,55vw);max-height:30vh;object-fit:contain;cursor:move;filter:drop-shadow(0 5px 9px #20382b25);touch-action:none;}.sticker-cancel{padding:5px 11px;border-radius:10px;background:#fff4ef;border:1px solid #e5b7aa;color:#923d2c;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;box-shadow:0 3px 10px #40201418;}.dark-mode .control-card,.dark-mode .bottom-zone,.dark-mode .pen-toolbar{background:rgba(28,43,36,.96);border-color:#3e5948;color:#e3eee6;}.dark-mode .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(27,42,35,.98),rgba(32,47,39,.98));border-color:#526c53;color:#eef3dc;}.dark-mode .verse-text{color:#f3f2df;}.dark-mode .bismillah{color:#bdd5ba;}.dark-mode .control-btn{background:#2a4637;color:#e3eee6;border-color:#3e5948;}.dark-mode .control-btn.primary{background:#256548;color:white;}.dark-mode .bg-cycle{background:#2a4637;color:#e3eee6;}.dark-mode .bg-upload{background:#2a4637;color:#e3eee6;}.dark-mode .verse-footer{color:#c1d1c6;}
@media(max-width:900px){.app{padding:10px 12px 96px;gap:9px}.topbar{gap:8px}.capsule{min-height:59px;padding:7px 9px}.surah-pill{min-height:49px;grid-row:2}.center-wrap{gap:8px}.verse-card{padding:22px 14px 15px}.verse-text{font-size:clamp(30px,5vw,43px);line-height:1.85;max-width:90vw}.bottom-zone{left:calc(50% + 20px);bottom:10px;max-width:calc(100vw - 94px)}.bg-label{display:none}.bg-btn{width:24px;height:24px;flex-basis:24px}.bg-cycle{width:23px;flex-basis:23px}.bg-upload{font-size:9px;padding:6px}.pen-fab{width:48px;height:48px;left:10px;bottom:10px}.pen-toolbar{left:10px;bottom:66px;width:calc(100vw - 20px)}.top-status{display:none}}
@media(max-width:480px){.app{padding:7px 7px 90px;gap:7px}.capsule{border-radius:17px;padding:6px;min-height:52px}.brand-logo,.teacher-photo{width:36px;height:36px}.click-title{max-width:118px;font-size:11px}.teacher-name{font-size:10px;max-width:118px}.surah-pill{padding:7px 17px;min-height:44px}.surah-name{font-size:20px}.control-card{padding:7px 6px}.audio-row{gap:5px;margin-top:6px}.control-btn{padding:7px 8px;font-size:11px}.nav-arrow,.icon-only{width:33px;height:33px;min-width:33px}.select-wrap{padding:3px 6px}.select-wrap select{max-width:110px;font-size:11px}.verse-bubble{width:27px;min-width:27px;height:27px;padding:0;font-size:11px}.more-btn{height:27px;min-width:31px}.verse-card{display:block;width:fit-content;max-width:100%;border-radius:18px;padding:24px 9px 13px}.verse-text{font-size:clamp(27px,7.5vw,35px);line-height:1.95;max-width:calc(100vw - 55px);gap:.05em .15em}.bismillah{font-size:22px}.ayah-end{min-width:30px;height:30px;font-size:16px}.bottom-zone{left:50%;transform:translateX(-42%);max-width:calc(100vw - 75px);padding:5px 6px;gap:4px}.bg-upload{font-size:9px;padding:5px 6px;white-space:nowrap}.pen-toolbar{max-height:65vh;padding:9px}.pen-settings{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.sticker-actions small{font-size:9px}.api-note{font-size:9px}.sticker-cancel{font-size:10px;padding:4px 8px}}
@media(max-height:760px) and (min-width:901px){.app{padding-top:8px;gap:6px}.capsule{min-height:56px}.surah-pill{min-height:48px}.control-card{padding:6px 10px}.audio-row{margin-top:5px}.verse-card{padding-top:18px;padding-bottom:12px}.verse-text{font-size:clamp(28px,3.2vw,43px);line-height:1.65}.bismillah{font-size:25px;margin-bottom:5px}.verse-footer{margin-top:7px}.pen-toolbar{max-height:60vh}}


:root{--title-font:"Cairo",sans-serif;--surah-font:"Amiri",serif;--teacher-font:"Cairo",sans-serif;--mushaf-font:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;}
#programTitleButton{font-family:var(--title-font);}#surahTitle{font-family:var(--surah-font);}#teacherNameButton{font-family:var(--teacher-font);}.verse-text,.bismillah,.ayah-end{font-family:var(--mushaf-font);}
.surah-pill{cursor:pointer;user-select:none;transition:transform .16s,box-shadow .16s;}.surah-pill:hover{transform:translateY(-1px);box-shadow:0 12px 28px rgba(22,75,59,.23);}
.audio-row{direction:rtl;gap:9px;}.nav-arrow{width:38px;height:38px;min-width:38px;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:0 4px 12px #183e2c12;}.nav-arrow svg{width:18px;height:18px;}
.board-nav{position:fixed;right:18px;bottom:16px;z-index:63;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:0;background:transparent;border:0;border-radius:0;box-shadow:none;backdrop-filter:none;}.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{height:42px;min-width:42px;border:1px solid #deeadf;background:#f5f9f3;color:#22553e;border-radius:13px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:7px;transition:all .16s;}.board-nav-btn:hover,.age-mode-btn:hover{background:#e5f0e4;transform:translateY(-1px);}.age-mode-btn{padding:0 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;white-space:nowrap;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#174b3a,#287658);color:#fff;border-color:#397b5e;}.age-mode-btn:hover{background:#205e49;}.settings-fab{position:fixed;left:15px;bottom:73px;z-index:72;width:45px;height:45px;border-radius:15px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fefcf5,#efe4c6);color:#245640;border:1px solid #ddc98f;box-shadow:0 7px 19px #143d2926;transition:transform .16s;}.settings-fab:hover{transform:translateY(-2px) rotate(12deg);}.pen-fab{z-index:72;}.pen-toolbar{left:72px;bottom:13px;width:min(650px,calc(100vw - 150px));max-height:32vh;overflow:auto;padding:8px 10px;gap:8px;flex-direction:row;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;border-radius:17px;z-index:71;}.pen-toolbar.show{display:flex;}.pen-toolbar-head{width:auto;gap:5px;}.pen-toolbar-head strong{font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap;}.pen-toolbar-head small{display:none;}.pen-toolbar-head .control-btn{display:none;}.toolset{gap:4px;flex-wrap:nowrap;}.tool-btn{width:33px;height:32px;min-width:33px;border-radius:9px;}.tool-btn svg{width:18px;height:18px;}.colors{gap:5px;flex-wrap:nowrap;}.colors>span{display:none!important;}.color-dot{width:20px;height:20px;min-width:20px;}.color-picker{width:25px;height:25px;}.pen-settings{display:flex;gap:7px;grid-template-columns:none;align-items:center;}.slider-field{min-width:78px;width:86px;}.slider-title{font-size:9px;margin-bottom:2px;}.slider-field input{height:12px;}.sticker-actions{gap:5px;}.sticker-actions small{display:none;}.sticker-actions .control-btn{padding:7px 9px;font-size:10px;white-space:nowrap;}
.settings-backdrop{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:120;background:rgba(9,28,20,.43);backdrop-filter:blur(7px);display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;}.settings-backdrop.show{display:flex;}.settings-panel{width:min(900px,100%);max-height:min(92vh,950px);overflow:auto;background:#fcfdf9;border:1px solid #d9e5d9;border-radius:24px;box-shadow:0 25px 90px #0926193b;padding:22px;direction:rtl;color:#223e30;}.settings-header{display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:20px;margin-bottom:18px;}.settings-header h2{margin:3px 0 3px;font-size:25px;color:#164b3b;}.settings-header p,.settings-help{font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;color:#718078;margin:0;}.settings-kicker{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;color:#9d7f3d;font-weight:800;}.settings-close{height:38px;width:38px;border-radius:13px;background:#f2f5ef;color:#335d46;font-size:25px;line-height:1;}.settings-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:13px;}.settings-section{border:1px solid #e0e9df;border-radius:18px;padding:15px;background:#fff;}.settings-section h3{margin:0 0 7px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:15px;}.settings-section h3>span{display:grid;place-items:center;width:27px;height:27px;border-radius:9px;background:#edf4e8;color:#2d674d;font-size:13px;}.settings-help{margin-bottom:12px;}.font-setting{margin-top:10px;}.font-setting label,.settings-section>label{display:block;font-size:11px;color:#5b6c60;margin-bottom:5px;font-weight:800;}.font-row{display:flex;gap:6px;}.font-row input,.settings-select{width:100%;min-width:0;border:1px solid #dce7dc;border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;background:#fafcf8;color:#214733;font-size:12px;}.mini-action{border-radius:10px;background:#e9f2e7;color:#245840;border:1px solid #d7e8d7;padding:8px 11px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;white-space:nowrap;}.mini-action:hover{background:#dcebd9;}.mini-action.subtle{background:#f7f4eb;color:#786333;border-color:#ece2c9;}.settings-background-actions{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:8px 0 10px;}.settings-bg-gallery{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:7px;}.settings-bg-tile{height:65px;border-radius:11px;border:2px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #dce5da;position:relative;background-size:cover;background-position:center;overflow:hidden;}.settings-bg-tile.active{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px #176247;}.settings-bg-tile span{position:absolute;right:4px;bottom:4px;left:4px;font-size:9px;padding:2px 4px;background:#ffffffd9;border-radius:5px;color:#284b38;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}.settings-identity{grid-column:1/-1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;}.settings-footer{margin-top:15px;padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid #e5ece2;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;font-size:10px;color:#718078;}.settings-footer>div{display:flex;gap:7px;}.mushaf-options{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:13px;}.mushaf-card{border:1px solid #dbe6da;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fff,#f5f8f1);border-radius:17px;padding:15px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;min-width:0;}.mushaf-card h3{margin:0;font-size:16px;color:#1e553d;}.mushaf-card p{font-size:11px;line-height:1.8;color:#738076;margin:0;}.mushaf-card input[type=url]{width:100%;direction:ltr;text-align:left;border:1px solid #dce6dc;border-radius:10px;background:#fff;padding:10px;font-size:12px;}.mushaf-status{font-size:10px;color:#66806d;line-height:1.7;}.blank-display .center-wrap{display:none!important;}
.blank-display .board-nav{display:flex!important;}.blank-display .pen-fab,.blank-display .settings-fab{display:grid!important;place-items:center;}.blank-display .app{min-height:100svh;padding-bottom:20px;}.blank-display .brand .tiny-caption,.blank-display .teacher .tiny-caption{display:none;}.blank-display .surah-pill{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff,#f7f5eb);color:#22543d;border-color:#d7c88f;box-shadow:0 7px 22px #173a2815;}.blank-display .surah-name{font-size:clamp(20px,2vw,28px);}.blank-display .topbar{padding-top:10px;}
@media(max-width:760px){.settings-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.settings-panel{padding:14px;border-radius:18px}.settings-header h2{font-size:21px}.settings-footer{align-items:flex-start;flex-direction:column}.settings-identity{grid-column:auto}.settings-bg-gallery{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))}.mushaf-options{grid-template-columns:1fr}.board-nav{right:8px;bottom:8px;padding:5px;gap:4px}.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{height:37px;min-width:36px;border-radius:11px}.age-mode-btn{padding:0 9px;font-size:10px}.pen-toolbar{left:67px;bottom:8px;width:calc(100vw - 130px);max-height:45vh;gap:5px;padding:6px;align-items:center}.pen-settings{display:none}.colors{gap:3px}.tool-btn{width:29px;min-width:29px;height:29px}.pen-fab{bottom:8px;left:8px;width:46px;height:46px}.settings-fab{left:9px;bottom:61px;width:39px;height:39px}.audio-row{gap:6px}.audio-row .control-btn{padding:7px 9px}.audio-row .nav-arrow{padding:0;min-width:34px;width:34px;height:34px}}
@media(max-width:420px){.board-nav-btn{min-width:32px;width:32px}.age-mode-btn{font-size:9px;padding:0 6px}.pen-toolbar-head{display:none}.toolset{gap:3px}.colors{gap:3px}.settings-bg-gallery{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}


.range-extra-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:8px;}.range-action-row{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0 2px;}.danger-soft{background:#fff0eb!important;color:#934634!important;border-color:#e8c1b6!important;}.danger-soft:hover{background:#ffe5dc!important;}.blank-display .app:before{display:none;}
@media(max-width:480px){.range-extra-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.range-action-row .control-btn{flex:1;justify-content:center}}


/* توافق واجهة الإعدادات وشريط القلم القصير */
#drawingCanvas{pointer-events:none!important;z-index:50!important;}.settings-fab{z-index:73;}.pen-fab{z-index:74;}.pen-toolbar{z-index:72;}.board-nav{z-index:73;}.audio-row #recordButton,.audio-row #listenRecording{border-radius:12px;}.audio-row #openRange{background:#f6f2e7;color:#806b34;border-color:#eadfbc;}.settings-section input[type=text]{box-sizing:border-box;}.settings-section .font-row .mini-action{flex:0 0 auto;}.mushaf-card button{justify-content:center;}


/* تحسينات الإصدار: إعدادات الهوية والأحجام والخلفيات والمعنى والملصقات */
#programTitleButton,#teacherNameButton{pointer-events:none;cursor:default;}
#teacherPhotoButton{pointer-events:none;cursor:default;}
#teacherPhotoButton:hover{transform:none;box-shadow:none;}
#programTitleButton{font-size:var(--title-size,21px)!important;max-width:clamp(120px,20vw,300px);}
#surahTitle{font-size:var(--surah-size,28px)!important;}
#teacherNameButton{font-size:var(--teacher-size,18px)!important;}
#verseText{font-size:var(--mushaf-size,52px)!important;}
#bismillah{font-size:calc(var(--mushaf-size,52px)*.62)!important;}
.ayah-end{min-width:34px!important;height:34px!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;position:relative;padding:0 4px;margin:0 6px!important;color:#8b6b2c!important;isolation:isolate;}
.ayah-end:before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:5px 3px;border:1.5px solid #c8a95e;transform:rotate(45deg);border-radius:5px;z-index:-1;background:rgba(251,242,217,.25);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px rgba(212,188,123,.12);}
.ayah-end:after{content:"۞";position:absolute;inset:-1px;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:35px;color:rgba(184,145,54,.37);z-index:-2;pointer-events:none;}
.verse-stage{flex-direction:column;gap:12px;align-items:center;}
.verse-card{width:fit-content;max-width:100%;min-width:min(100%,310px);padding:clamp(20px,3.5vw,42px) clamp(14px,3.5vw,40px) 20px;}
.control-card{width:max-content;max-width:100%;padding:8px 10px;}
.audio-row{gap:6px;margin-top:6px;}
.audio-row .control-btn{padding:8px 11px;}
.audio-row #playVerse{min-width:0;}
.audio-row #openRange{display:none!important;}
.api-note{display:none!important;}
.verse-footer{margin-top:12px;}
.tafsir-link{font-size:13px;font-weight:800;text-decoration:none;background:#f6f2e7;border:1px solid #e4d7b4;border-radius:11px;padding:8px 14px;}
.tafsir-panel{display:none;width:min(880px,100%);background:rgba(255,255,255,.96);border:1px solid #dfd2aa;border-radius:16px;padding:14px 18px;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(17,49,38,.08);color:#244331;text-align:right;}
.tafsir-panel.show{display:block;animation:rise .16s ease-out;}
.tafsir-panel[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.tafsir-panel-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;color:#866b30;font-size:14px;}
.tafsir-panel p{font-size:14px;line-height:2;margin:8px 0 0;white-space:pre-wrap;}
.tafsir-edit-icon{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:10px;background:#edf4e8;color:#245840;font-size:18px;}
.range-modal{width:min(780px,100%);}
.range-modal .chapter-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;}
.range-modal .modal-actions{display:flex;}
.mushaf-modal{width:min(720px,100%);}
.mushaf-launch{border:1px solid #dbe6da;cursor:pointer;text-align:center;color:#244d38;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:175px;}
.mushaf-launch:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 8px 22px #1c4e3615;}
.mushaf-launch-icon{display:grid;place-items:center;width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:14px;background:#eaf2e7;color:#285d43;font-size:25px;font-weight:800;}
.mushaf-launch h3{margin:0;}
.mushaf-launch p{text-align:center;}
.size-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:90px 1fr 48px;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;padding:7px 8px;border-radius:10px;background:#f7faf5;}
.size-row label{margin:0!important;font-size:10px!important;white-space:nowrap;}
.size-row input{width:100%;min-width:0;accent-color:#28664c;}
.size-row output,.strength-row output{font-size:10px;color:#245840;font-weight:800;min-width:36px;text-align:center;}
.background-strength{margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px;border-radius:12px;background:#f7faf5;}
.background-strength>label{margin-bottom:6px;}
.strength-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:10px;color:#718078;}
.strength-row input{flex:1;min-width:60px;accent-color:#28664c;}
.background-strength .settings-help{margin:7px 0 0;}
.identity-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;width:100%;}
.identity-input{display:block;width:100%;min-width:0;border:1px solid #dce7dc;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 10px;background:#fafcf8;color:#214733;font-size:12px;}
.identity-actions{grid-column:1/-1;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:4px;}
.identity-actions .settings-help{margin:0;}
.settings-identity{align-items:stretch!important;}
.settings-identity .font-setting{width:100%;}
.board-nav .age-mode-btn{width:42px;min-width:42px;padding:0;border-radius:13px;}
.pen-toolbar{width:min(850px,calc(100vw - 110px));left:72px;bottom:14px;max-height:40vh;flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:nowrap;gap:8px;overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:hidden;align-items:center;white-space:nowrap;}
.pen-toolbar-head{flex:0 0 auto;}
.pen-toolbar-head .control-btn{display:none;}
.toolset,.colors,.pen-settings{flex:0 0 auto;flex-wrap:nowrap;}
.pen-settings{display:flex!important;grid-template-columns:none;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
.slider-field{width:85px;min-width:75px;}
.slider-title{font-size:9px;white-space:nowrap;}
.pen-settings input[type=range]{width:100%;height:12px;}
.sticker-item{position:absolute;width:150px;height:150px;min-width:60px;min-height:60px;resize:both;overflow:visible;touch-action:none;border:1px dashed rgba(32,99,71,.36);border-radius:8px;padding:2px;box-sizing:border-box;background:rgba(255,255,255,.07);}
.sticker-item img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;cursor:move;touch-action:none;user-select:none;}
.sticker-actions-mini{position:absolute;top:-13px;left:-13px;display:flex;gap:3px;z-index:4;}
.sticker-actions-mini button{width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#f8f6ee;border:1px solid #d9c890;color:#245640;box-shadow:0 2px 7px #0002;font-size:17px;line-height:1;font-weight:800;padding:0;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{position:static;width:24px;height:24px!important;padding:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fff0ed!important;color:#a64236!important;border:1px solid #e6b9b0!important;font-size:20px!important;line-height:1!important;}
@media(max-width:760px){
 .identity-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.identity-actions{grid-column:auto}
 .range-modal{width:min(680px,100%)}
 .pen-toolbar{left:62px;width:calc(100vw - 118px);bottom:8px;padding:6px;gap:6px;flex-wrap:nowrap;overflow-x:auto}
 .pen-toolbar-head{display:none}
 .pen-settings{display:flex!important;gap:5px}
 .slider-field{width:65px;min-width:62px}
 .slider-title{font-size:8px;gap:3px}
 .tool-btn{width:28px;min-width:28px;height:28px}
 .color-dot{width:18px;height:18px;min-width:18px}
 .color-picker{width:22px;min-width:22px}
 .board-nav .age-mode-btn{width:36px;min-width:36px;height:37px}
 .verse-card{width:fit-content;min-width:min(100%,280px)}
 .tafsir-panel{padding:11px 13px}
 #verseText{font-size:var(--mushaf-size,52px)!important;line-height:1.8}
}


/* تحسينات هذه النسخة: حفظ دائم، مظهر جذاب حسب الفئة، ونوافذ مريحة */
.brand-logo{border-radius:50%!important;width:54px;height:54px;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#f4e9bf,#fffdf3);}
.brand-logo img,.teacher-photo img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block;border-radius:50%;}
.teacher-photo{border-radius:50%!important;overflow:hidden;}
.range-modal{max-height:calc(100vh - 28px);overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-width:thin;}
.range-modal form{min-width:0;}
.range-modal .range-action-row{gap:7px;}
.range-modal .range-action-row .control-btn{flex:1 1 auto;justify-content:center;min-height:40px;}
.range-modal .field-group input[type=number]{min-width:0;}
.ayah-end:after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
.verse-footer{display:none!important;}
.verse-stage{gap:18px!important;}
.tafsir-panel{margin-top:5px!important;}
.pen-toolbar{width:min(1000px,calc(100vw - 112px));max-height:46vh;}
.sticker-actions-mini{position:absolute;inset:0;display:block;pointer-events:none;z-index:4;}
.sticker-actions-mini button{position:absolute;pointer-events:auto;width:28px;height:28px;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-smaller{left:-14px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-larger{right:-14px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{position:absolute!important;top:-14px!important;right:-14px!important;left:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
body.child-theme .brand.capsule{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,246,252,.98),rgba(242,234,255,.96));border-color:#e6c8ec;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(174,113,183,.12);}
body.child-theme .teacher.capsule{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(245,250,255,.98),rgba(230,245,255,.96));border-color:#c9dff5;}
body.child-theme .surah-pill{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a64b91,#7253bc 55%,#428cc5)!important;border-color:#c895e2!important;box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(119,80,165,.2)!important;}
body.child-theme .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,249,253,.98),rgba(249,246,255,.98),rgba(243,252,255,.98));border-color:#e4c6e7;box-shadow:0 14px 34px rgba(145,92,161,.13);}
body.child-theme .control-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,251,254,.96),rgba(245,250,255,.96));border-color:#e2d0ef;}
body.child-theme .program-logo{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ffe6f2,#e6e5ff);}
body.adult-theme .brand.capsule{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.98),rgba(239,245,236,.96));border-color:#cbd9ca;}
body.adult-theme .teacher.capsule{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.98),rgba(246,241,225,.96));border-color:#e4d6b5;}
body.adult-theme .surah-pill{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#133c31,#245a43 60%,#94773e)!important;border-color:#c1a664!important;}
body.adult-theme .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.98),rgba(255,252,240,.98),rgba(241,247,239,.98));border-color:#d9c99d;}
body.adult-theme .control-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.96),rgba(245,248,239,.96));border-color:#d5e0d0;}
@media(max-width:760px){.range-modal{max-height:calc(100vh - 16px);padding:16px;}.pen-toolbar{width:calc(100vw - 112px);max-width:1000px;}.sticker-actions-mini button{width:26px;height:26px}}



/* شريط قلم ممتد ومتوازن مع أزرار التنقل والوضع */
.pen-toolbar{left:72px!important;right:176px!important;width:auto!important;max-width:none!important;min-width:320px;bottom:14px;max-height:min(46vh,360px);overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:hidden;box-sizing:border-box;}
.pen-toolbar .toolset,.pen-toolbar .colors,.pen-toolbar .pen-settings{flex-shrink:0;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-smaller,.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-larger{display:grid;place-items:center;width:30px!important;height:30px!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fffef9,#e8f0e6)!important;border:1px solid #b9cdbb!important;box-shadow:0 5px 14px #153d292b!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-smaller{left:-17px!important;top:50%!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-larger{right:-17px!important;top:50%!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{position:absolute!important;top:-14px!important;right:-14px!important;left:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
@media(max-width:760px){.pen-toolbar{left:62px!important;right:118px!important;width:auto!important;min-width:160px;bottom:8px;max-height:42vh}.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-smaller{left:-12px!important}.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-larger{right:-12px!important}}
/* تدرجات الهوية تتفاعل بوضوح مع وضع الكبار والصغار */
body.child-theme .brand.capsule,body.child-theme .teacher.capsule{background-image:linear-gradient(130deg,rgba(255,255,255,.98),rgba(247,225,255,.96),rgba(226,246,255,.98))!important;}
body.adult-theme .brand.capsule,body.adult-theme .teacher.capsule{background-image:linear-gradient(130deg,rgba(255,255,255,.99),rgba(238,245,232,.98),rgba(246,237,213,.97))!important;}
.range-modal{overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-gutter:stable;}


/* تخصيص الإطارات حسب اختيار المعلم */
.frame-settings-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:10px;}
.frame-settings-grid .settings-select{width:100%;}
[data-frame-style="none"]{border-color:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;outline:none!important;}
.brand[data-frame-style="classic"],.teacher[data-frame-style="classic"]{border:2px solid #c5a34b!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(197,163,75,.14),0 8px 20px rgba(24,65,48,.08)!important;}
#surahPill[data-frame-style="classic"]{border:2px solid #f0d37b!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(240,211,123,.2)!important;}
.control-card[data-frame-style="classic"],.verse-card[data-frame-style="classic"]{border:2px solid #c5a34b!important;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(197,163,75,.13),0 10px 28px rgba(24,65,48,.1)!important;}
.brand[data-frame-style="ornate"],.teacher[data-frame-style="ornate"],#surahPill[data-frame-style="ornate"],.control-card[data-frame-style="ornate"],.verse-card[data-frame-style="ornate"]{border:3px double #bd9a4a!important;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(199,165,83,.11),0 8px 24px rgba(31,76,53,.11)!important;}
.brand[data-frame-style="glow"],.teacher[data-frame-style="glow"],#surahPill[data-frame-style="glow"],.control-card[data-frame-style="glow"],.verse-card[data-frame-style="glow"]{border:1px solid #b9d2b7!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(83,157,113,.14),0 0 22px rgba(83,157,113,.17)!important;}
.brand[data-frame-style="dashed"],.teacher[data-frame-style="dashed"],#surahPill[data-frame-style="dashed"],.control-card[data-frame-style="dashed"],.verse-card[data-frame-style="dashed"]{border:2px dashed #8ba88e!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
#app[data-frame-style="classic"]{outline:2px solid #c5a34b;outline-offset:-7px;}
#app[data-frame-style="ornate"]{outline:4px double #bd9a4a;outline-offset:-9px;}
#app[data-frame-style="glow"]{outline:2px solid rgba(83,157,113,.58);outline-offset:-8px;box-shadow:inset 0 0 28px rgba(83,157,113,.13);}
#app[data-frame-style="dashed"]{outline:2px dashed #8ba88e;outline-offset:-8px;}
#app[data-frame-style="none"]{outline:none;box-shadow:none;}
/* رموز اختيار الخلفية والوضع من دون إطارات */
.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;outline:none;background:transparent!important;border-radius:50%!important;}
.board-nav-btn:hover,.age-mode-btn:hover{background:rgba(95,132,101,.12)!important;transform:translateY(-1px);}
.pen-fab,.settings-fab{border-radius:50%!important;}
/* علامة + بدل النقاط */
.more-btn{font-size:25px!important;font-weight:800!important;letter-spacing:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;line-height:1!important;}
/* نافذة السورة: X أعلى اليسار، ولا تغلق بالنقر خارجها */
.modal{position:relative;}
.modal-close-x{display:none;position:absolute;left:13px;top:12px;width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:50%;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f6f1df,#e8f0e6);color:#23533c;border:1px solid #d6dfca;font-size:25px;line-height:1;z-index:3;box-shadow:0 3px 10px #173d2618;}
.range-modal .modal-close-x{display:flex;}
.range-modal{max-height:calc(100vh - 22px);overflow:auto;}
.range-modal #modalTitle{padding-left:42px;}
.range-modal .quran-search-results{max-height:210px;overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;border-radius:12px;}
.quran-search-results:empty{display:none;}
.quran-search-results{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;margin-top:7px;}
.quran-search-result{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:3px;width:100%;text-align:right;background:#f7faf5;border:1px solid #e0e9dd;border-radius:11px;padding:8px 10px;color:#214733;}
.quran-search-result strong{font-size:11px;color:#245c40;}
.quran-search-result span{font-family:"Amiri","Amiri Quran",serif;font-size:16px;line-height:1.6;}
.quran-search-result:hover{background:#eaf4e7;border-color:#b8d4b8;}
.quran-search-empty{padding:9px;color:#7d897f;font-size:11px;}
.range-extra-grid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);}
.range-action-row.compact-playback-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:7px;}
.range-action-row.compact-playback-row .control-btn{padding:8px 7px;min-height:38px;font-size:11px;white-space:normal;}
.range-action-row.prompt-row{margin-top:2px;}
.range-action-row.prompt-row .control-btn{min-height:35px;padding:7px 12px;font-size:11px;}
/* ملصق بحدود وأربعة أركان تحكم: ثلاثة أسهم قابلة للسحب والرابع × */
.sticker-item{max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;min-width:60px;min-height:60px;}
.sticker-item img{width:100%!important;height:100%!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;object-fit:contain!important;touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini{inset:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-resize-handle{display:grid!important;place-items:center;position:absolute!important;width:29px!important;height:29px!important;padding:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fff,#e9f4e8)!important;border:1px solid #91b298!important;color:#174b3b!important;font-size:19px!important;font-weight:800!important;line-height:1!important;box-shadow:0 4px 13px #153d2930!important;pointer-events:auto!important;touch-action:none!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;user-select:none!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .resize-nw{left:-14px!important;top:-14px!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .resize-sw{left:-14px!important;bottom:-14px!important;top:auto!important;right:auto!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .resize-se{right:-14px!important;bottom:-14px!important;top:auto!important;left:auto!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{display:grid!important;place-items:center;pointer-events:auto!important;position:absolute!important;top:-14px!important;right:-14px!important;left:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;width:29px!important;height:29px!important;border-radius:50%!important;padding:0!important;font-size:22px!important;line-height:1!important;background:#fff0ed!important;color:#a64236!important;border:1px solid #e6b9b0!important;box-shadow:0 4px 13px #40201420!important;z-index:6;}
@media(max-width:560px){.frame-settings-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.range-action-row.compact-playback-row{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.range-action-row.compact-playback-row #playRangeButton{grid-column:1/-1}.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-resize-handle,.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{width:26px!important;height:26px!important}.range-modal{padding:18px 15px}}


.frame-control-options{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:6px;margin-top:8px;padding-top:7px;border-top:1px solid #e8eee5;}.frame-control-options label{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:5px;min-width:0;font-size:10px;color:#617267;font-weight:700;}.frame-control-options input[type=range]{width:58px;min-width:38px;accent-color:#28664c;}.frame-control-options input[type=color]{width:28px;height:25px;padding:2px;border:1px solid #d9e4d7;border-radius:7px;background:#fff;}.frame-control-options select{width:83px;min-width:0;border:1px solid #dce7dc;border-radius:7px;padding:4px;background:#fafcf8;color:#214733;font-size:10px;}@media(max-width:560px){.frame-control-options{grid-template-columns:1fr}}


.surah-search-results{display:none;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;max-height:190px;overflow:auto;margin-top:6px;padding:3px;border:1px solid #e2e9de;border-radius:12px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 8px 22px rgba(22,75,59,.08);}.surah-search-results.show{display:flex;}.surah-search-result{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;width:100%;padding:8px 10px;border:0;border-radius:8px;background:#f7faf5;color:#214733;text-align:right;font-size:12px;}.surah-search-result:hover,.surah-search-result:focus-visible{background:#e6f1e3;}.surah-search-result small{color:#7b887d;font-size:10px;}
.sticker-item.selected-sticker{outline:2px solid rgba(43,118,79,.62);outline-offset:2px;}
.board-nav{background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;padding:0!important;backdrop-filter:none!important;border-radius:0!important;}.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;background:transparent!important;border-radius:50%!important;}

.frame-settings-grid .field-group{min-width:0;padding:10px;border:1px solid #e0e9df;border-radius:12px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff,#f7faf5);}.frame-settings-grid .field-group>label{display:block;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:800;font-size:11px;color:#4f6657;}.frame-control-options{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)!important;}.frame-control-options label{min-height:28px;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;font-size:11px;}.frame-control-options input[type=range]{width:92px;min-width:70px;flex:0 1 92px;}.frame-control-options input[type=color]{flex:0 0 30px;}.frame-control-options select{width:96px;flex:0 0 96px;}

/* تحسينات الشاشة المطلوبة: خلفية كاملة الدقة، أزرار دائرية بيضاء، ووضع سبورة */
body.whiteboard-mode{background:#fff!important;background-image:none!important;background-color:#fff!important;}body.whiteboard-mode #app:before{display:none!important;}
.board-nav{background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;padding:0!important;gap:9px!important;}
.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{width:44px!important;height:44px!important;min-width:44px!important;padding:0!important;display:grid!important;place-items:center!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fff!important;color:#151515!important;border:1px solid #e8e8e8!important;box-shadow:0 4px 13px rgba(0,0,0,.14)!important;transition:transform .15s,box-shadow .15s!important;}
.board-nav-btn svg,.age-mode-btn svg{stroke:#151515!important;}
.board-nav-btn:hover,.age-mode-btn:hover{background:#fff!important;color:#000!important;box-shadow:0 5px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.20)!important;transform:translateY(-2px)!important;}
.age-mode-btn.active{border:2px solid #245a43!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(36,90,67,.16),0 4px 13px rgba(0,0,0,.14)!important;}
body.child-theme .age-mode-btn.active{border-color:#9460bd!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(148,96,189,.18),0 4px 13px rgba(0,0,0,.14)!important;}
.settings-tabs{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:12;display:flex;gap:8px;overflow-x:auto;white-space:nowrap;padding:8px 2px 12px;margin:0 0 8px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.97);scrollbar-width:thin;}
.settings-category-tab{flex:1 0 auto;min-height:43px;padding:9px 14px;border:1px solid #dbe7dc;border-radius:14px;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fff,#f1f6ee);color:#315743;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(26,65,42,.06);transition:all .16s;}
.settings-category-tab.active{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#174b3b,#287256);border-color:#174b3b;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 5px 15px rgba(22,75,59,.2);}
.settings-section[hidden]{display:none!important;}.settings-section[data-settings-category]{grid-column:1/-1;}
.settings-section-active{animation:settingsPanelIn .16s ease-out;}
@keyframes settingsPanelIn{from{opacity:.75;transform:translateY(3px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}
.range-modal .field-group>label[for="surahSearch"]{display:block;padding:0;border-radius:0;background:transparent!important;color:#536d5c!important;font-weight:700;}
#drawingCanvas{position:fixed!important;left:0!important;top:0!important;right:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;width:100vw!important;height:100vh!important;pointer-events:none!important;touch-action:none!important;z-index:50!important;}
body:not(.child-theme) .settings-tabs{background:rgba(255,255,255,.97);}
body.child-theme .settings-tabs{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,249,253,.98),rgba(243,249,255,.98));}
@media(max-width:760px){.board-nav{gap:5px!important;right:8px!important;bottom:8px!important}.board-nav-btn,.age-mode-btn{width:38px!important;height:38px!important;min-width:38px!important}.settings-tabs{gap:6px;padding-top:5px}.settings-category-tab{min-height:39px;padding:7px 11px;font-size:11px}.pen-toolbar{z-index:72!important}}


/* تحسينات عرض الخلفية والهوية وأوضاع الشاشة */
.brand-logo,.teacher-photo{border-radius:50%!important;overflow:hidden!important;aspect-ratio:1/1;flex:0 0 auto;isolation:isolate;}
.brand-logo{width:52px;height:52px;padding:0;border:2px solid #e2d4a7!important;}
.teacher-photo{width:52px;height:52px;padding:0;border:2px solid #e1c981!important;}
.brand-logo img:not([hidden]),.teacher-photo img:not([hidden]){display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;object-fit:cover!important;object-position:center center!important;border-radius:50%!important;}
#logoFallback,#teacherFallback{display:grid;place-items:center;width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;}
#logoFallback[hidden],#teacherFallback[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.app.background-image:after{display:none!important;content:none!important;}
.background-image{background-image:none!important;}
.board-nav{gap:8px!important;}
.board-nav .board-nav-btn,.board-nav .mode-menu-trigger{width:43px!important;height:43px!important;min-width:43px!important;padding:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fff!important;color:#1e332c!important;border:1px solid #e4e9e2!important;box-shadow:0 3px 11px rgba(16,45,30,.15)!important;display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;}
.board-nav .board-nav-btn:hover,.board-nav .mode-menu-trigger:hover{background:#f3f7f0!important;transform:translateY(-2px)!important;}
.mode-picker-wrap{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;}
.mode-menu-trigger{gap:3px!important;}
.mode-current-icon{display:grid;place-items:center;line-height:0;}
.mode-menu-chevron{flex:0 0 auto;opacity:.7;}
.mode-menu{position:absolute;bottom:calc(100% + 10px);right:0;z-index:100;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;min-width:164px;padding:7px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.98);border:1px solid #e0e8dd;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(19,50,32,.2);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);}
.mode-menu[hidden],.verse-view-menu[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.mode-menu button{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;width:100%;padding:10px 11px;border-radius:10px;background:transparent;color:#243c2e;text-align:right;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;white-space:nowrap;}
.mode-menu button:hover,.mode-menu button.active{background:#eaf3e7;color:#164b3b;}
.legacy-age-buttons[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.verse-view-wrap{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;}
.verse-view-menu{position:absolute;z-index:100;bottom:calc(100% + 8px);right:0;min-width:154px;padding:6px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;border:1px solid #dce6da;border-radius:13px;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 10px 25px rgba(20,50,31,.18);}
.verse-view-menu button{padding:9px 10px;background:transparent;border-radius:9px;color:#244b37;text-align:right;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;}
.verse-view-menu button:hover,.verse-view-menu button.active{background:#edf5e9;}
.center-wrap.all-view-wrap{max-width:1500px;width:100%;}
.all-verses-card[hidden],.verse-card[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.all-verses-card{width:100%;max-width:1500px;height:clamp(250px,calc(100svh - 250px),780px);overflow:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain;margin:0 auto;padding:clamp(16px,2.2vw,30px);background:rgba(255,255,255,.95);border:1px solid #e0e6d8;border-radius:24px;box-shadow:0 9px 28px rgba(19,50,32,.09);direction:rtl;scrollbar-gutter:stable;}
.all-verses-heading{text-align:center;font-family:"Amiri","Cairo",serif;color:#6d815f;font-size:clamp(15px,1.8vw,22px);font-weight:700;padding:0 0 10px;margin:0 0 8px;border-bottom:1px solid #dce6d7;}
.all-bismillah{font-family:"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;font-size:clamp(23px,2.5vw,31px);text-align:center;color:#557660;margin:4px 0 13px;}
.all-verse-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:baseline;justify-content:flex-start;gap:.08em .2em;direction:rtl;text-align:right;padding:10px 5px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(210,221,206,.56);font-family:"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;font-size:clamp(24px,2.35vw,36px);line-height:1.95;color:#203c2d;scroll-margin:10px;}
.all-verse-row:last-child{border-bottom:0;}
.all-verse-row.current-row{background:rgba(221,237,213,.38);border-radius:10px;}
.all-verse-row .word{margin:0 .025em;}
.all-verse-row .ayah-end{margin-inline:4px;flex:0 0 auto;transform:scale(.84);transform-origin:center;}
.all-verse-tafsir{display:block;flex:0 0 100%;padding:10px 13px;margin:7px 0 2px;border-radius:12px;background:#f5f7ef;border-right:3px solid #d6b36a;color:#37503e;font-family:"Cairo",sans-serif;font-size:13px;line-height:1.9;}
.all-verse-tafsir button{margin-right:8px;background:transparent;color:#31694a;text-decoration:underline;font-size:11px;}
.dark-mode .all-verses-card{background:rgba(29,49,39,.97);border-color:#3f5c49;}
.dark-mode .all-verse-row{color:#f0f3df;border-color:#3d5b48;}
.dark-mode .all-verse-row.current-row{background:rgba(60,103,70,.32);}
.dark-mode .all-verse-tafsir{background:#213a2c;color:#e7efdf;}
/* شريط القلم مقتصد العرض ويظهر على مقاس محتواه مع إمكانية السحب أفقيًا */
.pen-toolbar{width:max-content!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 145px)!important;min-width:0!important;flex-wrap:nowrap!important;overflow-x:auto!important;overflow-y:hidden!important;scrollbar-width:thin!important;padding:7px 8px!important;gap:7px!important;}
.pen-toolbar-head,.toolset,.sticker-actions,.colors,.pen-settings{flex:0 0 auto!important;}
.pen-toolbar-head{gap:4px!important;}

.tool-btn{width:29px!important;min-width:29px!important;height:29px!important;border-radius:8px!important;}

/* تعديلات عرض الآيات وأزرار الاستماع وشريط القلم */
.background-image:after{display:none!important;background:transparent!important;}

.pen-toolbar{position:fixed!important;left:84px!important;right:auto!important;top:auto!important;bottom:18px!important;z-index:72!important;width:max-content!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 250px)!important;min-width:0!important;max-height:none!important;display:none;flex-direction:row!important;flex-wrap:nowrap!important;align-items:center!important;gap:7px!important;padding:7px 8px!important;overflow-x:auto!important;overflow-y:hidden!important;white-space:nowrap!important;transform:none!important;}

/* جميع الآيات في تدفق متصل مثل المصحف، باستعمال الخط المختار من الإعدادات */
.center-wrap.all-view-wrap{max-width:1200px!important;width:calc(100% - 72px)!important;}
.all-verses-card{width:100%!important;max-width:1120px!important;height:clamp(250px,calc(100svh - 245px),780px)!important;padding:clamp(15px,2vw,25px)!important;text-align:justify;text-align-last:auto;}
.all-verse-row{display:inline!important;flex-wrap:initial!important;align-items:initial!important;justify-content:initial!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;border:0!important;background:transparent!important;border-radius:0!important;font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;font-size:var(--mushaf-size,52px)!important;line-height:2.05!important;white-space:normal!important;color:#203c2d;}

#verseText .tajweed,#allVersesCard .tajweed,.all-verse-row .tajweed{font-weight:inherit!important;font-synthesis:none!important;text-shadow:none!important;font-kerning:normal;font-variant-ligatures:contextual common-ligatures;}

/* === إضافات تخصيص السبورة ونافذة PDF وفهرس العناصر === */
#settingsCategoryTabs [data-settings-tab="mushaf"],
.settings-section[data-settings-category="mushaf"]{display:none!important;}
#screenItemTray{position:fixed;left:8px;top:26%;z-index:10095;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:7px;max-height:50vh;max-width:64px;overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;padding:5px;border-radius:18px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.78);border:1px solid rgba(205,218,204,.75);box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(13,43,25,.13);backdrop-filter:blur(10px);scrollbar-width:thin;}
.screen-tray-item{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:44px;height:44px;min-width:44px;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid #d9e4d6;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fff,#f1f6ed);color:#20523a;cursor:pointer;padding:3px;overflow:visible;box-shadow:0 2px 6px #1c422014;}
.screen-tray-item:hover,.screen-tray-item.active{border-color:#b89b53;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px #d9c27c55,0 4px 12px #173b251c;transform:translateY(-1px);}
.screen-tray-item img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;border-radius:7px;}
.screen-tray-item .tray-glyph{font-size:22px;font-weight:800;line-height:1;}
.screen-tray-item .tray-label{position:absolute;left:49px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);white-space:nowrap;background:#153f30;color:#fff;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:7px;font-size:10px;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .15s;}
.screen-tray-item:hover .tray-label{opacity:1;}
.screen-tray-delete{position:absolute;right:-6px;top:-6px;display:grid;place-items:center;width:18px;height:18px;padding:0;border-radius:50%;background:#fff0ed;color:#a43e31;border:1px solid #e2afa7;font-size:14px;font-weight:900;line-height:1;z-index:3;box-shadow:0 2px 6px #0002;}
#pdfSidePanel{position:fixed;right:16px;top:105px;width:min(48vw,760px);height:min(76vh,850px);min-width:300px;min-height:250px;max-width:calc(100vw - 90px);max-height:calc(100vh - 125px);resize:both;overflow:hidden;display:none;flex-direction:column;z-index:10020;background:#fffdf7;border:1px solid #d2ddcf;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 18px 54px #102b1f40;direction:rtl;}
#pdfSidePanel.show{display:flex;}
#pdfSideHeader{height:42px;min-height:42px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px;padding:5px 8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#174b3b,#287658);color:#fff;cursor:move;user-select:none;touch-action:none;}
#pdfSideHeader strong{font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}
.pdf-head-actions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;flex:0 0 auto;}
.pdf-head-actions button{width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;background:#ffffff20;color:white;font-weight:900;font-size:15px;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:0;}
.pdf-head-actions button:hover{background:#ffffff36;}
#pdfSideFrame{width:100%;height:100%;flex:1;border:0;background:#f6f1e7;transform-origin:top center;}
.pdf-panel-note{font-size:10px;padding:4px 8px;background:#f6f2e7;color:#6c7b6d;border-top:1px solid #e5e7dc;}
#boardColorOptions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:7px 0 12px;}
.board-color-chip{width:29px;height:29px;min-width:29px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid white;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #cad9c9;cursor:pointer;}
.board-color-chip.active{box-shadow:0 0 0 2px #1c6849,0 0 0 4px #fff;}
#boardBackgroundColorPicker{width:35px;height:32px;padding:1px;border:1px solid #d5e1d4;border-radius:9px;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;}
.settings-bg-delete{position:absolute;left:4px;top:4px;z-index:3;width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:0;background:#fff0ed;color:#9f352b;border:1px solid #e1b1a8;font-weight:900;font-size:16px;line-height:1;box-shadow:0 2px 7px #29150e22;}
.settings-bg-tile{position:relative;}
.frame-shape-field{margin-top:8px;padding-top:7px;border-top:1px solid #e6ece3;}
.frame-shape-field label{display:block;margin-bottom:5px;font-weight:800;font-size:10px;color:#536d5c;}
.frame-shape-field select{width:100%;border:1px solid #dce7dc;border-radius:9px;padding:7px 8px;background:#fafcf8;color:#214733;font-size:11px;}
.range-modal .chapter-grid{display:grid!important;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))!important;gap:8px!important;align-items:start;}
.range-modal .chapter-grid .field-group{min-width:0;}

.range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row #playRangeButton{grid-column:1/-1;}
.range-modal .range-read-row{margin-top:0;}
.range-modal .range-reciter-last{margin-top:1px;}
body.whiteboard-mode{background-color:var(--board-background-color,#f0e2cc)!important;background-image:var(--board-background-image,none)!important;background-size:cover!important;background-position:center center!important;background-repeat:no-repeat!important;background-attachment:fixed!important;}

 #screenItemTray{left:3px;top:27%;max-width:48px;max-height:56vh;gap:5px;padding:3px;border-radius:13px}

 .screen-tray-item .tray-glyph{font-size:18px}

 #pdfSideHeader{height:38px;min-height:38px}

}
