<style id="quran-display-final-fixes">
/* ترتيب الشريط العلوي: كبسولة السورة ولوحة التحكم متجاورتان */
.topbar{grid-template-columns:minmax(175px,1fr) auto minmax(175px,1fr)!important;gap:12px!important;max-width:1550px!important;align-items:center!important;}
.surah-control-group{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:9px;min-width:0;direction:rtl;position:relative;z-index:30;}
.surah-control-group>.surah-pill{flex:0 0 auto!important;justify-self:auto!important;min-width:132px!important;max-width:190px!important;min-height:64px!important;padding:8px 13px!important;border-radius:20px!important;}
.surah-control-group>.control-card{width:max-content!important;max-width:min(100%,580px)!important;flex:0 1 auto!important;padding:6px 8px!important;border-radius:17px!important;box-shadow:0 4px 15px rgba(18,57,41,.05)!important;backdrop-filter:none!important;overflow:visible!important;}
.surah-control-group .verse-nav-wrap{position:relative;display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;min-width:0;}
.surah-control-group .verse-nav{gap:5px!important;min-height:30px!important;flex-wrap:nowrap!important;}
.surah-control-group .verse-bubble{min-width:29px!important;width:29px!important;height:29px!important;padding:0!important;font-size:11px!important;}
.surah-control-group .more-btn{min-width:29px!important;width:29px!important;height:29px!important;font-size:20px!important;}
.surah-control-group .audio-row{margin-top:5px!important;gap:5px!important;flex-wrap:nowrap!important;}
.surah-control-group .control-btn{padding:7px 9px!important;min-height:32px!important;border-radius:10px!important;font-size:11px!important;gap:5px!important;}
.surah-control-group .nav-arrow,.surah-control-group .icon-only{width:32px!important;min-width:32px!important;height:32px!important;padding:0!important;}
.surah-control-group #playVerse{padding:7px 10px!important;}
.surah-control-group .control-card .verse-nav-wrap{z-index:100;}
.top-status{position:static!important;align-self:center!important;margin:-7px auto 0!important;font-size:10px!important;}
.center-wrap{max-width:1200px!important;width:100%!important;min-height:0!important;margin-top:0!important;}
.verse-stage{width:100%;min-height:240px;padding-top:4px!important;}

/* نافذة شبكية لاختيار أرقام الآيات، بدل تمديد الأرقام على الشاشة الرئيسية */
.verse-number-popup{position:absolute;z-index:250;top:calc(100% + 9px);left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(390px,calc(100vw - 28px));max-height:min(58vh,440px);overflow:auto;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(6,minmax(30px,1fr));gap:7px;padding:12px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.99);border:1px solid #d9e3d5;border-radius:17px;box-shadow:0 16px 36px rgba(20,52,34,.23);scrollbar-width:thin;}
.verse-number-popup[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.verse-picker-number{min-width:0;min-height:34px;padding:2px 4px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid #e0e9dd;background:#f5f8f1;color:#264d3a;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}
.verse-picker-number:hover{background:#e3efdf;}
.verse-picker-number.active{background:var(--green);color:#fff;border-color:var(--green);}

/* خط مصحف واحد متّسق؛ لا نفصل علامة المد عن الحرف الذي تتبعه */
#verseText,.verse-text,.verse-text .word,#verseText .tajweed,#allVersesCard,.all-verse-row,.all-verse-row .word,.all-verse-row .tajweed{
 font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;
 font-synthesis:none!important;font-kerning:normal!important;font-variant-ligatures:normal!important;font-feature-settings:"liga" 1,"calt" 1!important;letter-spacing:normal!important;word-spacing:normal!important;}
#verseText{font-size:var(--mushaf-size,52px)!important;font-weight:400!important;line-height:2.25!important;max-width:1000px!important;}
#verseText .word{font-weight:400!important;line-height:inherit!important;vertical-align:baseline!important;white-space:nowrap!important;padding:0 1px!important;margin:0!important;}
#verseText .tajweed,.all-verse-row .tajweed{display:inline!important;font-family:inherit!important;font-size:inherit!important;font-weight:inherit!important;line-height:inherit!important;vertical-align:baseline!important;letter-spacing:normal!important;white-space:inherit!important;text-shadow:none!important;font-synthesis:none!important;}
/* الرمز المرقّم وحده: إزالة الرمز الزخرفي الفارغ الذي كان يُرسم بطبقة pseudo-element */
.ayah-end:after,.ayah-end::after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
.ayah-end{isolation:isolate;vertical-align:baseline!important;}
.ayah-end:before,.ayah-end::before{z-index:-1!important;}
.all-verses-card{width:100%!important;max-width:1120px!important;height:clamp(300px,calc(100svh - 205px),790px)!important;overflow:auto!important;overscroll-behavior:contain!important;scrollbar-gutter:stable;outline-offset:3px;}
.all-verses-card:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #d8b76c;}
.center-wrap.all-view-wrap{max-width:1160px!important;width:100%!important;}
.all-verse-row{display:inline!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;border:0!important;line-height:2.35!important;white-space:normal!important;word-spacing:normal!important;letter-spacing:normal!important;}
.all-verse-row .word{display:inline!important;white-space:nowrap!important;line-height:inherit!important;font-weight:400!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;vertical-align:baseline!important;}
.all-verse-row .ayah-end{display:inline-grid!important;place-items:center!important;position:relative!important;vertical-align:baseline!important;min-width:1.38em!important;width:1.38em!important;height:1.38em!important;margin:0 .025em 0 .055em!important;padding:0!important;border:1px solid #c9aa64!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fbf2d9!important;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px #fff8e7!important;color:#805f25!important;font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;font-size:.7em!important;line-height:1!important;white-space:nowrap!important;transform:none!important;flex:none!important;}
.all-verse-row .ayah-end:before,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
.all-verse-row .ayah-end:before{content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;inset:4px 3px!important;border:1px solid #c8a95e!important;border-radius:3px!important;transform:rotate(45deg)!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;z-index:-1!important;}
.all-verse-row .ayah-end:after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
.all-verse-row .all-verse-tafsir{display:block!important;flex:0 0 100%!important;width:100%!important;white-space:normal!important;}

@media(max-width:1250px){
 .topbar{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr)!important;align-items:center!important}
 .topbar>.brand{grid-column:1;grid-row:1;justify-self:start;}
 .topbar>.teacher{grid-column:2;grid-row:1;justify-self:end;}
 .surah-control-group{grid-column:1/-1;grid-row:2;justify-self:center;width:100%;flex-wrap:nowrap}
 .surah-control-group>.control-card{max-width:calc(100% - 160px)!important}
}
@media(max-width:720px){
 .app{padding:8px 8px 92px!important;gap:9px!important}
 .topbar{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr)!important;gap:7px!important}
 .surah-control-group{flex-direction:column;gap:6px;width:100%;align-items:stretch}
 .surah-control-group>.surah-pill{width:100%!important;max-width:none!important;min-height:46px!important}
 .surah-control-group>.control-card{width:100%!important;max-width:100%!important;padding:7px 5px!important}
 .surah-control-group .verse-nav{flex-wrap:wrap!important}
 .surah-control-group .audio-row{flex-wrap:wrap!important;gap:4px!important}
 .surah-control-group .control-btn{padding:6px 8px!important}
 .verse-number-popup{grid-template-columns:repeat(5,minmax(30px,1fr));max-height:52vh}
 .all-verses-card{height:calc(100svh - 270px)!important;min-height:235px!important;padding:12px!important}
 #verseText{font-size:var(--mushaf-size,42px)!important;line-height:2.25!important}
 .all-verse-row{font-size:var(--mushaf-size,42px)!important;line-height:2.2!important}
}
</style>
<style id="ayah-symbol-menu-scroll-polish">
/* رمز آية واحد فقط: الرقم داخل دائرة وإطار مربع خارجي، بلا زخرفة فارغة إضافية */
:root{
  --ayah-circle-size:1.32em;
  --ayah-square-size:1.52em;
  --ayah-symbol-color:#c5a45b;
}
.ayah-end,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end{
  display:inline-grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  position:relative!important;
  isolation:isolate!important;
  width:var(--ayah-circle-size)!important;
  min-width:var(--ayah-circle-size)!important;
  height:var(--ayah-circle-size)!important;
  padding:0!important;
  margin:0 .035em 0 .07em!important;
  border:1.25px solid var(--ayah-symbol-color)!important;
  border-radius:50%!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,248,226,.72)!important;
  color:#805f25!important;
  font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;
  font-size:.72em!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  text-indent:0!important;
  vertical-align:middle!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  flex:none!important;
  transform:none!important;
  overflow:visible!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
}
.ayah-end::before,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end::before{
  content:""!important;
  display:block!important;
  position:absolute!important;
  top:50%!important;
  left:50%!important;
  width:var(--ayah-square-size)!important;
  height:var(--ayah-square-size)!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:1px solid var(--ayah-symbol-color)!important;
  border-radius:2px!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  transform:translate(-50%,-50%)!important;
  transform-origin:center!important;
  z-index:-1!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
.ayah-end::after,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end::after{
  content:none!important;
  display:none!important;
}
.ayah-end:hover{background:rgba(214,179,106,.12)!important;}
/* تقليل الفراغات بين الكلمات مع إبقاء اتجاه وتشكيل المصحف */
#verseText.verse-text{
  column-gap:.075em!important;
  row-gap:.08em!important;
  word-spacing:normal!important;
}
#verseText .word{
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
}
.all-verse-row{
  word-spacing:-.035em!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
}
.all-verse-row .word{
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
}
/* قائمة عرض آية واحدة/جميع الآيات تنفتح أسفل زر الشرطات */
#verseViewWrap{position:relative!important;overflow:visible!important;}
#verseViewMenu{
  top:calc(100% + 8px)!important;
  bottom:auto!important;
  left:50%!important;
  right:auto!important;
  transform:translateX(-50%)!important;
  z-index:500!important;
}
/* عرض جميع الآيات يمتد حتى أسفل الشاشة؛ شريط التمرير مخفي لكنه فعال */
.center-wrap.all-view-wrap{
  width:100%!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  align-self:stretch!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode{
  width:100%!important;
  align-items:stretch!important;
}
.all-verses-card{
  display:block!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  width:100%!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  height:calc(100svh - 205px)!important;
  min-height:300px!important;
  max-height:none!important;
  margin:0!important;
  border-radius:24px!important;
  overflow:auto!important;
  overflow-x:hidden!important;
  overflow-y:auto!important;
  overscroll-behavior:contain!important;
  scrollbar-width:none!important;
  -ms-overflow-style:none!important;
  scroll-behavior:smooth!important;
}
.all-verses-card::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
@media(max-width:1250px){
  .center-wrap.all-view-wrap{width:100%!important;max-width:none!important}
  .all-verses-card{height:calc(100svh - 270px)!important;min-height:235px!important;border-radius:21px!important}
}
@media(max-width:720px){
  .all-verses-card{height:calc(100svh - 270px)!important;min-height:220px!important;padding:12px!important;border-radius:18px!important}
}
@media(max-width:480px){
  .all-verses-card{height:calc(100svh - 280px)!important;min-height:200px!important}
}
</style>
<style id="final-user-layout-fixes">
/* رمز الآية: رقم واحد فقط، بلا دائرة أو مربع أو زخرفة إضافية */
.ayah-end,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end,
#verseText .ayah-end{
  display:inline!important;
  position:static!important;
  isolation:auto!important;
  box-sizing:content-box!important;
  width:auto!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  height:auto!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  margin:0 .045em!important;
  border:0!important;
  border-radius:0!important;
  outline:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  transform:none!important;
  color:#8b6b2c!important;
  font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;
  font-size:.82em!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
  text-align:inherit!important;
  vertical-align:baseline!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
}
.ayah-end::before,.ayah-end::after,
.ayah-end:before,.ayah-end:after,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end::before,.all-verse-row .ayah-end::after,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end:before,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:after{
  content:none!important;
  display:none!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:none!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  width:0!important;height:0!important;
}
.ayah-end:hover,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:hover{background:transparent!important;color:#6f4e19!important;text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:2px;}

/* عرض جميع الآيات: نفس العرض الوسطي القديم تقريبًا، لكنه يمتد رأسيًا حتى أسفل الشاشة.
   أدوات أسفل الشاشة تبقى فوقه، والتمرير مخفي بصريًا لكنه مستمر. */
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode{
  position:fixed!important;
  top:var(--all-verses-top,150px)!important;
  right:0!important;
  bottom:0!important;
  left:0!important;
  width:100vw!important;
  height:auto!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  max-height:none!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  align-items:stretch!important;
  gap:0!important;
  z-index:8!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  overflow:visible!important;
  background:transparent!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode>.all-verses-card{
  display:block!important;
  pointer-events:auto!important;
  position:relative!important;
  flex:0 1 1080px!important;
  width:min(1080px,calc(100vw - 32px))!important;
  max-width:1080px!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  height:auto!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  max-height:none!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
  padding:18px 24px 125px!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(213,224,209,.92)!important;
  border-radius:24px 24px 0 0!important;
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.94)!important;
  box-shadow:0 8px 26px rgba(22,58,39,.08)!important;
  overflow-x:hidden!important;
  overflow-y:auto!important;
  overscroll-behavior:contain!important;
  scrollbar-width:none!important;
  -ms-overflow-style:none!important;
  scroll-behavior:smooth!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode>.all-verses-card::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode>.all-verses-card .all-verses-heading{display:none!important;}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode .all-verse-row{line-height:2.2!important;word-spacing:normal!important;letter-spacing:0!important;}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode .all-verse-row .word{margin:0!important;padding:0!important;}
/* زرّ الخلفيات والقلم والإعدادات تبقى ظاهرة أمام صندوق الآيات */
#boardNav,.bottom-zone,.pen-fab,.pen-toolbar,.settings-fab{z-index:63!important;}
.pen-toolbar{z-index:72!important;}
#drawingCanvas{z-index:50!important;}
@media(max-width:720px){
  .verse-stage.all-verses-mode>.all-verses-card{width:calc(100vw - 18px)!important;flex-basis:calc(100vw - 18px)!important;padding:13px 12px 135px!important;border-radius:19px 19px 0 0!important}
}
</style>
<style id="range-modal-top-actions-and-audio-follow">
.range-modal-top-actions{position:absolute;left:53px;top:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;z-index:4;direction:rtl;}
.range-modal-top-actions .control-btn{height:34px;min-height:34px;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap;box-shadow:0 3px 9px rgba(23,61,38,.1);}
.range-modal #modalTitle{padding-left:250px;min-height:36px;}
.range-modal-top-actions .range-top-blank{background:#fff4ed;border-color:#efd3c6;color:#8d4233;}
.all-verses-card .all-verse-row.current-row{background:rgba(241,229,190,.22);border-radius:12px;transition:background .18s;}
.word.speaking{scroll-margin:30px;}
@media(max-width:560px){.range-modal-top-actions{left:50px;top:11px;gap:3px}.range-modal-top-actions .control-btn{padding:4px 6px;font-size:10px}.range-modal #modalTitle{padding-left:222px;font-size:17px}}
</style>
<style id="user-board-sticker-frame-fixes">

/* تخصيصات نهائية حسب الملاحظات: لون السبورة، الملصقات، وشفافية الإطارات */
body.whiteboard-mode,body.whiteboard-mode #app{background:#f0e2cc!important;background-color:#f0e2cc!important;background-image:none!important;}
body.whiteboard-mode #app{background:transparent!important;}
body.whiteboard-mode #app:before{display:none!important;}
.blank-display .surah-pill{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#245943,#174533)!important;color:#fff!important;border-color:#b7c9b1!important;}
.blank-display #surahTitle{color:#fff!important;}
.sticker-item{position:absolute!important;display:block!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;min-width:24px!important;min-height:24px!important;resize:none!important;overflow:visible!important;touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;padding:0!important;background:transparent!important;outline:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
.sticker-item.selected-sticker{outline:none!important;outline-offset:0!important;}
.sticker-item img{display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;object-fit:contain!important;border:0!important;outline:none!important;filter:none!important;cursor:move!important;touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini{position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;display:block!important;pointer-events:none!important;z-index:8!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-resize-handle{display:none!important;}
.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{position:absolute!important;top:-12px!important;right:-12px!important;left:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;display:grid!important;place-items:center!important;width:28px!important;height:28px!important;min-width:28px!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fff!important;color:#c62828!important;border:1px solid #efb7b7!important;box-shadow:0 3px 12px #0002!important;font-size:21px!important;font-weight:800!important;line-height:1!important;opacity:0!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important;transition:opacity .12s ease,visibility .12s ease!important;z-index:9!important;}
.sticker-item:hover .sticker-cancel,.sticker-item:focus-visible .sticker-cancel{opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;pointer-events:auto!important;}
.frame-control-options label{gap:7px!important;}
.frame-control-options input[type=range]{width:92px;min-width:65px;flex:0 1 92px;}
@media(max-width:560px){.sticker-actions-mini .sticker-cancel{width:27px!important;height:27px!important;min-width:27px!important}}

</style>
<style id="board-text-overlay-and-image-position-fixes">
/* Texto flutuante transparente: a própria tela continua sendo seu fundo. */
#stickerLayer{position:fixed!important;inset:0!important;z-index:10010!important;pointer-events:none!important;overflow:visible!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item{position:absolute!important;z-index:var(--sticker-z,10011)!important;pointer-events:auto!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;min-width:24px!important;min-height:24px!important;resize:none!important;overflow:visible!important;touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;background:transparent!important;outline:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item img{display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;object-fit:contain!important;background:transparent!important;border:0!important;outline:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;filter:none!important;cursor:move!important;touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-resize-handle{position:absolute!important;right:-12px!important;bottom:-12px!important;display:none;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;width:27px!important;height:27px!important;min-width:27px!important;padding:0!important;border:1px solid #b7cdb8!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:rgba(246,250,243,.98)!important;color:#245740!important;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(20,55,32,.2)!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;touch-action:none!important;z-index:5!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker .sticker-resize-handle{display:flex!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-actions-mini,#stickerLayer .sticker-cancel{display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
#boardTextLayer{position:fixed!important;inset:0!important;z-index:9995!important;pointer-events:none!important;overflow:visible!important;}
.board-text-item{position:absolute!important;display:block!important;z-index:9996!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 16px)!important;min-width:0!important;min-height:0!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;outline:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;background:transparent!important;overflow:visible!important;pointer-events:auto!important;touch-action:manipulation!important;user-select:text!important;}
.board-text-item.selected{outline:none!important;}
.board-text-content{display:block!important;min-width:110px!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 20px)!important;min-height:1.25em!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;outline:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;background:transparent!important;background-color:transparent!important;resize:none!important;overflow-wrap:anywhere!important;white-space:pre-wrap!important;word-break:normal!important;direction:rtl!important;text-align:right!important;line-height:1.55!important;cursor:text!important;caret-color:currentColor!important;user-select:text!important;-webkit-user-select:text!important;}
.board-text-content:empty::before{content:attr(data-placeholder);opacity:.42;pointer-events:none;}
/* مقبض صليب صغير لتحريك النص، بلا زر أو كلمة مستقلة. */
.board-text-drag{position:absolute!important;top:-13px!important;right:-13px!important;display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:27px!important;height:27px!important;min-width:27px!important;padding:0!important;border:1px solid #b7cdb8!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:rgba(246,250,243,.98)!important;color:#245740!important;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(20,55,32,.18)!important;cursor:move!important;touch-action:none!important;z-index:4!important;}
.board-text-item.selected .board-text-drag,.board-text-item:hover .board-text-drag{display:flex!important;}
.board-text-resize{position:absolute!important;left:-12px!important;bottom:-12px!important;display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:23px!important;height:23px!important;min-width:23px!important;padding:0!important;border:1px solid #b7cdb8!important;border-radius:6px!important;background:rgba(246,250,243,.98)!important;color:#245740!important;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(20,55,32,.18)!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;touch-action:none!important;z-index:4!important;}
.board-text-item.selected .board-text-resize,.board-text-item:hover .board-text-resize{display:flex!important;}
#penToolbar{z-index:10020!important;}
#penToggle,#settingsToggle,#boardNav{z-index:10030!important;}
.board-text-font-panel{position:fixed!important;left:84px;bottom:76px;z-index:10040!important;display:grid;gap:11px;width:min(310px,calc(100vw - 20px));padding:14px!important;border:1px solid #cbdcc9!important;border-radius:16px!important;background:rgba(255,255,250,.98)!important;color:#213e2e!important;box-shadow:0 14px 36px rgba(16,52,35,.24)!important;direction:rtl!important;white-space:normal!important;backdrop-filter:blur(12px)!important;}
.board-text-font-panel[hidden]{display:none!important;}
.board-text-font-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;font-size:14px;color:#174b3b;}
.board-text-font-close{padding:5px 9px!important;border-radius:8px!important;border:1px solid #d6e3d3!important;background:#edf4e9!important;color:#315b42!important;font-size:11px!important;font-weight:700!important;}
.board-text-font-field{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:#617567;}
.board-text-font-field select{width:100%;min-width:0;padding:8px 10px!important;border:1px solid #d4e2d3!important;border-radius:9px!important;background:#fff!important;color:#244634!important;font-size:12px!important;}
.board-text-size-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
.board-text-size-row input{flex:1;min-width:0;accent-color:#28664c;}
.board-text-size-row output{min-width:45px;color:#245840;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;text-align:center;}
.board-text-bold-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px;color:#405b49;}
.board-text-bold-row input{accent-color:#28664c;}
.board-text-font-panel p{margin:0!important;font-size:10px!important;line-height:1.7!important;color:#78877a!important;}
#boardTextFontToggle.active{background:#d7ead7!important;color:#174b3b!important;border-color:#8db394!important;}
.settings-backdrop,.modal-backdrop{z-index:10050!important;}
.toast{z-index:10060!important;}
@media(max-width:760px){
 .board-text-font-panel{left:8px;bottom:65px;width:min(300px,calc(100vw - 16px));padding:11px!important}
 .board-text-content{min-width:90px!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 18px)!important}
 .board-text-drag{top:-27px!important;padding:4px 7px!important}
}
</style>
<style id="qurancom-uthmanic-font-and-layering">
@font-face{
  font-family:"UthmanicHafs";
  src:url("https://verses.quran.foundation/fonts/quran/hafs/uthmanic_hafs/UthmanicHafs1Ver18.woff2") format("woff2"),
      url("https://verses.quran.foundation/fonts/quran/hafs/uthmanic_hafs/UthmanicHafs1Ver18.ttf") format("truetype");
  font-style:normal;font-weight:400 800;font-display:swap;
}
:root{--mushaf-font:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif;}
#verseText,.verse-text,.verse-text .word,#verseText .tajweed,#allVersesCard,.all-verse-row,.all-verse-row .word,.all-verse-row .tajweed,.bismillah,.all-bismillah,.ayah-end,.all-verse-row .ayah-end{
  font-family:var(--mushaf-font,"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran","Amiri",serif)!important;
}
/* الطبقة النشطة للنص أو الصورة تتقدم فوق محتوى الصفحة، دون تغطية أدوات التحكم. */
#stickerLayer{z-index:10010!important;}
#boardTextLayer{z-index:10012!important;}
#boardTextLayer .board-text-item{z-index:var(--board-text-z,10013)!important;}
#penToolbar{z-index:10020!important;}
#penToggle,#settingsToggle,#boardNav{z-index:10030!important;}
</style>
<style id="qurancom-reference-and-backup-enhancements">
:root{--quran-paper:#fff7ea;--quran-ink:#11100e;}
.verse-stage{background:var(--quran-paper)!important;}
.verse-card,#allVersesCard,.all-verses-card{background:var(--quran-paper)!important;background-color:var(--quran-paper)!important;background-image:none!important;color:var(--quran-ink)!important;border-color:#eadac3!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
.verse-card{border-radius:4px!important;}
#verseText,.verse-text,.verse-text .word,#allVersesCard,.all-verse-row,.all-verse-row .word,.all-verse-row .tajweed,.bismillah,.all-bismillah{font-family:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran","Traditional Arabic",serif!important;font-synthesis:none!important;font-kerning:normal!important;font-variant-ligatures:contextual common-ligatures!important;color:var(--quran-ink);}
.verse-text,.all-verse-row{line-height:2.12!important;letter-spacing:0!important;word-spacing:0!important;}
.ayah-end,.all-verse-row .ayah-end{display:inline-grid!important;position:relative!important;place-items:center!important;vertical-align:middle!important;width:1.7em!important;min-width:1.7em!important;height:2.18em!important;max-width:1.7em!important;padding:0!important;margin:0 .11em!important;overflow:visible!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background-image:none!important;background-repeat:no-repeat!important;background-position:center center!important;background-size:0 0!important;background-color:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;color:#961b1b!important;font-family:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran",serif!important;font-size:.79em!important;line-height:1!important;white-space:nowrap!important;isolation:isolate!important;transform:none!important;cursor:pointer;}
.ayah-end:before,.ayah-end:after,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:before,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:after{content:none!important;display:none!important;}
.ayah-end-number{position:relative!important;z-index:1!important;color:#961b1b!important;font-size:.82em!important;line-height:1!important;font-weight:400!important;letter-spacing:0!important;text-shadow:none!important;}
.quran-section-marker{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;vertical-align:middle;margin-inline:.12em .24em;color:#1c8261;font-family:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran",serif;font-size:.72em;line-height:1;white-space:nowrap;user-select:none;}
.quran-section-marker .rub-symbol{display:inline-grid;place-items:center;width:1.45em;height:1.45em;color:#137c5b;border:1px solid #bda15d;outline:1px solid #8e3029;outline-offset:-3px;border-radius:38% 38% 42% 42%;background:#fff7ea;font-size:1em;}
.quran-sajdah-marker{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;vertical-align:middle;margin-inline:.14em;color:#8d2222!important;font-family:"UthmanicHafs","Amiri Quran",serif!important;font-size:.7em!important;line-height:1!important;border:1px solid #bc9f5d;border-radius:45%;width:1.55em;height:1.55em;background:#fff7ea!important;}
.surah-pill{position:relative!important;padding-left:49px!important;}
.surah-close-control{position:absolute!important;left:7px!important;top:50%!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;width:28px!important;height:28px!important;min-width:28px!important;border-radius:50%!important;display:grid!important;place-items:center!important;padding:0!important;font-size:21px!important;font-weight:500!important;line-height:1!important;background:#fff5ee!important;color:#9e342a!important;border:1px solid #e9c1b5!important;box-shadow:0 2px 6px #00000010!important;z-index:2;}
.surah-close-control:hover{background:#ffe5dc!important;transform:translateY(-50%) scale(1.05)!important;}
#drawingCanvas{z-index:10030!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
#stickerLayer{z-index:10010!important;}
#boardTextLayer{z-index:10012!important;}
#pdfSidePanel{z-index:10020;}
.pdf-stretch-controls{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:5px 9px;background:#fbf8ef;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e5d9;flex:0 0 auto;}
.pdf-stretch-controls label{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;min-width:150px;flex:1;font-size:10px;color:#355341;white-space:nowrap;}
.pdf-stretch-controls input{min-width:55px;flex:1;width:75px;accent-color:#28664c;height:13px;}
.pdf-stretch-controls output{font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#245840;min-width:36px;text-align:center;}
#pdfSideViewport{position:relative;flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto;background:#fff;overscroll-behavior:contain;}
#pdfSideFrame{display:block;max-width:none;max-height:none;flex:none;border:0;transform-origin:top left;background:#fff;}
.backup-actions{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:12px;}
.backup-actions .control-btn{min-width:145px;justify-content:center;}
.backup-section{grid-column:auto;}
@media(max-width:760px){.ayah-end,.all-verse-row .ayah-end{width:1.14em!important;min-width:1.14em!important;height:1.68em!important;font-size:.77em!important}.surah-close-control{width:25px!important;height:25px!important;left:5px!important}.surah-pill{padding-left:42px!important}.pdf-stretch-controls{gap:5px;padding:4px 6px}.pdf-stretch-controls label{min-width:110px;font-size:9px}.backup-actions .control-btn{flex:1}}
.settings-bg-tile{display:block;cursor:pointer;}
.settings-bg-tile:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #d8b76c;outline-offset:2px;}
.settings-bg-tile .settings-bg-delete{cursor:pointer;}
</style>

<style id="targeted-ux-audio-marker-fixes">
.surah-close-control,#surahPill .surah-close-control,#surahPill #surahCloseBtn,#surahPill .surah-title-close,#surahPill .surah-clear-x{display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
.surah-pill{padding-left:22px!important;}
@media(max-width:760px){.surah-pill{padding-left:17px!important;}}
.surah-input-wrap{position:relative!important;display:block!important;width:100%!important;min-width:0!important;}
.surah-input-wrap #surahSearch{width:100%!important;padding-left:39px!important;padding-right:10px!important;}
#clearSurahSearch{position:absolute!important;left:6px!important;top:50%!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;display:grid!important;place-items:center!important;width:25px!important;height:25px!important;min-width:25px!important;padding:0!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#fff0ed!important;color:#9b3429!important;border:1px solid #e5b8af!important;font-size:18px!important;line-height:1!important;z-index:3!important;}
#clearSurahSearch:hover{background:#ffe3dc!important;}
#verseText.reciting-verse .word,#verseText.reciting-verse .word *,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row.reciting-verse .word,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row.reciting-verse .word *{color:#555650!important;text-shadow:none!important;}
.all-verse-row.reciting-verse{background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
#pdfSidePanel{resize:none!important;overflow:hidden!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 24px)!important;max-height:calc(100vh - 96px)!important;min-width:220px!important;min-height:200px!important;}
#pdfResizeHandle{position:absolute!important;right:0!important;bottom:0!important;z-index:10!important;width:25px!important;height:25px!important;display:grid!important;place-items:center!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:8px 0 14px 0!important;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fffdf6,#dfeadd)!important;color:#1d5a40!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;touch-action:none!important;box-shadow:-1px -1px 4px #102b1f18!important;}
#pdfResizeHandle:hover{background:#dcebd8!important;color:#103e2b!important;}
#pdfSideViewport{position:relative!important;flex:1 1 auto!important;min-height:0!important;overflow:auto!important;}
#pdfSideFrame{display:block!important;min-height:100%!important;}
@media(max-width:760px){#pdfResizeHandle{width:29px!important;height:29px!important;}}
</style>

<style id="arabic-ayah-marker-and-board-polish">
:root{--verse-card-opacity:.96;--ayah-ornament-red:#8e2528;--ayah-ornament-green:#28785a;--ayah-ornament-gold:#c8a45a;}
/* رقم الآية: رقم عربي واضح داخل علامة طولية مستوحاة من مصحف المرجع، بلا رمز إضافي داخلها. */
.ayah-end,
#verseText .ayah-end,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end{
  display:inline-grid!important;place-items:center!important;position:relative!important;isolation:isolate!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;width:1.44em!important;min-width:1.44em!important;height:1.56em!important;min-height:1.56em!important;
  padding:0!important;margin:0 .06em!important;border:1.45px solid var(--ayah-ornament-red)!important;
  border-radius:46% 46% 43% 43%!important;outline:1px solid var(--ayah-ornament-green)!important;outline-offset:-3px!important;
  background:rgba(255,249,235,.78)!important;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.9),inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(200,164,90,.45)!important;
  color:var(--ayah-ornament-red)!important;font-family:'Amiri','Noto Naskh Arabic','Amiri Quran',serif!important;
  font-size:.78em!important;font-weight:700!important;line-height:1!important;vertical-align:middle!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;cursor:pointer!important;opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;filter:none!important;text-decoration:none!important;
  transform:none!important;overflow:visible!important;flex:none!important;
}
.ayah-end .ayah-end-number,
#verseText .ayah-end .ayah-end-number,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end .ayah-end-number{
  display:block!important;position:relative!important;z-index:2!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;
  font-family:'Amiri','Noto Naskh Arabic','Amiri Quran',serif!important;font-size:1em!important;font-weight:700!important;
  line-height:1!important;color:var(--ayah-ornament-red)!important;text-indent:0!important;opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;
}
.ayah-end::before,.all-verse-row .ayah-end::before{
  content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;pointer-events:none!important;z-index:0!important;
  inset:-.13em .07em!important;border:1px solid rgba(40,120,90,.72)!important;border-radius:47% 47% 45% 45%!important;
  background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;transform:none!important;
}
.ayah-end::after,.all-verse-row .ayah-end::after{
  content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;pointer-events:none!important;z-index:0!important;
  top:-.09em!important;bottom:-.09em!important;left:50%!important;width:.26em!important;margin-left:-.13em!important;
  background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 4%,#b83b31 0 24%,#2d8060 25% 42%,transparent 44%),radial-gradient(circle at 50% 96%,#b83b31 0 24%,#2d8060 25% 42%,transparent 44%)!important;
}
.ayah-end:hover,.ayah-end:focus,.ayah-end:active,
.all-verse-row .ayah-end:hover,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:focus,.all-verse-row .ayah-end:active{
  opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;filter:none!important;color:var(--ayah-ornament-red)!important;
  background:rgba(255,249,235,.78)!important;text-decoration:none!important;outline-color:var(--ayah-ornament-green)!important;
}
/* علامات الربع والسجدة: الرمز وحده بلا إطار أو لون بني. */
.quran-section-marker{display:inline!important;border:0!important;outline:0!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;border-radius:0!important;padding:0!important;}
.quran-section-marker .rub-symbol{display:inline!important;width:auto!important;height:auto!important;min-width:0!important;min-height:0!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;border:0!important;outline:0!important;outline-offset:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;color:#111!important;font-family:'Amiri Quran','Amiri',serif!important;font-size:.88em!important;line-height:inherit!important;}
.quran-sajdah-marker{display:inline!important;width:auto!important;height:auto!important;min-width:0!important;min-height:0!important;padding:0!important;margin:0 .12em!important;border:0!important;outline:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;color:#111!important;font-family:'Amiri Quran','Amiri',serif!important;font-size:.88em!important;line-height:inherit!important;vertical-align:baseline!important;}
/* آية واحدة: الإطار يتبع عرض النص بدل أن يتمدد على مساحة كبيرة. */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{
  display:table!important;width:fit-content!important;min-width:0!important;max-width:calc(100vw - 20px)!important;
  padding:clamp(10px,1.45vw,18px) clamp(12px,1.8vw,22px)!important;margin:0 auto!important;
  border-radius:19px!important;overflow:visible!important;
}
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #verseText{max-width:calc(100vw - 54px)!important;width:max-content;}
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card:before,.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card:after{font-size:19px!important;top:5px!important;opacity:.55!important;}
/* الشفافية تطبق على خلفية بطاقة الآية فقط ولا تخفّض وضوح النص. */
.verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(255,253,245,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)))!important;}
.all-verses-card{background:rgba(255,255,255,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96))!important;}
.app.dark-mode .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(27,42,35,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(32,47,39,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)))!important;}
.app.dark-mode .all-verses-card{background:rgba(29,49,39,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96))!important;}
body.child-theme .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,249,253,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(249,246,255,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(243,252,255,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)))!important;}
body.adult-theme .verse-card{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(255,252,240,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)),rgba(241,247,239,var(--verse-card-opacity,.96)))!important;}
/* عناصر الفهرس الجانبي بلا ×؛ الإزالة تتم من العنصر المحدد عبر Delete أو من زر إغلاقه الأصلي عند وجوده. */
.screen-tray-delete{display:none!important;}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item{opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item.active{border-color:#b89b53!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px #d9c27c66,0 4px 12px #173b251c!important;}
#verseCardOpacitySetting .strength-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;}
#verseCardOpacitySetting input{flex:1;min-width:60px;accent-color:#28664c;}
#verseCardOpacitySetting output{min-width:36px;text-align:center;font-size:10px;color:#245840;font-weight:800;}
@media(max-width:480px){.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{padding:8px 9px!important;border-radius:15px!important;}.ayah-end,#verseText .ayah-end,.all-verse-row .ayah-end{width:1.4em!important;min-width:1.4em!important;height:1.5em!important;min-height:1.5em!important;font-size:.72em!important;}}
</style>
<style id="font-preset-select-style">
.font-row .font-presets-select{flex:0 0 112px;width:112px;min-width:0;padding:7px 6px;font-size:10px;border-radius:9px;background:#fafcf8;color:#214733;border:1px solid #dce7dc;} .font-row input{flex:1 1 0;width:auto!important;min-width:55px;}
@media(max-width:540px){.font-row .font-presets-select{flex-basis:88px;width:88px;font-size:9px;padding:6px 3px}.font-row input{flex:1 1 0;min-width:40px}.font-row [data-font-upload]{padding:6px 6px;font-size:10px}}
</style>

<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link rel="preload" href="https://verses.quran.foundation/fonts/quran/hafs/uthmanic_hafs/UthmanicHafs1Ver18.woff2" as="font" type="font/woff2" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Almarai:wght@400;700&family=Amiri:wght@400;700&family=Cairo:wght@400;600;700;800&family=Changa:wght@400;700&family=Noto+Naskh+Arabic:wght@400;700&family=Reem+Kufi:wght@400;700&family=Tajawal:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" media="print" onload="this.media='all'">

<style id="quran-font-marker-final-tweaks">
@font-face{font-family:'UthmanicHafs';src:url('https://verses.quran.foundation/fonts/quran/hafs/uthmanic_hafs/UthmanicHafs1Ver18.woff2') format('woff2'),url('https://verses.quran.foundation/fonts/quran/hafs/uthmanic_hafs/UthmanicHafs1Ver18.ttf') format('truetype');font-style:normal;font-weight:100 900;font-display:swap;}
:root{--ayah-marker-frame-opacity:.78;}
/* Apply the chosen settings fonts to the actual screen elements, including nested Quran words. */
#programTitleButton,.brand .click-title{font-family:var(--title-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;}
#surahTitle{font-family:var(--surah-font,'Amiri',serif)!important;}
#teacherNameButton{font-family:var(--teacher-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;}
#verseText,#verseText .word,#verseText .word .tajweed,#bismillah,#allVersesCard,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row .word,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row .word .tajweed,.all-verse-row,.all-verse-row .word,.all-verse-row .word .tajweed{font-family:var(--mushaf-font,'UthmanicHafs',serif)!important;}
/* Ayah numbers use the authentic Uthmanic Hafs glyphs; the decorative end-mark is a separate opacity-controlled layer. */
#verseText button.ayah-end,#verseText button.ayah-end:hover,#verseText button.ayah-end:focus,#verseText button.ayah-end:active,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end:hover,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end:focus,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end:active,
button.ayah-end,button.ayah-end:hover,button.ayah-end:focus,button.ayah-end:active{
 display:inline-grid!important;place-items:center!important;position:relative!important;isolation:isolate!important;vertical-align:middle!important;
 width:1.36em!important;min-width:1.36em!important;max-width:1.36em!important;height:1.70em!important;min-height:1.70em!important;max-height:1.70em!important;
 box-sizing:border-box!important;flex:0 0 1.36em!important;overflow:visible!important;padding:0!important;margin:0 .045em!important;
 border:0!important;outline:0!important;border-radius:0!important;background:transparent!important;background-image:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;
 color:#8e2528!important;font-family:'UthmanicHafs',serif!important;font-size:.78em!important;font-weight:400!important;line-height:1!important;letter-spacing:0!important;
 opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;filter:none!important;text-decoration:none!important;text-indent:0!important;transform:none!important;cursor:pointer!important;
}
#verseText button.ayah-end .ayah-end-number,#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end .ayah-end-number,button.ayah-end .ayah-end-number{
 display:block!important;position:relative!important;z-index:2!important;opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;
 font-family:'UthmanicHafs',serif!important;font-size:.98em!important;font-weight:400!important;line-height:1!important;letter-spacing:0!important;
 color:#8e2528!important;text-shadow:none!important;background:transparent!important;padding:0!important;margin:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;white-space:nowrap!important;
}

 content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;z-index:1!important;pointer-events:none!important;
