   [9,[{v:41,t:'عَنِ ٱلْمُجْرِمِينَ',end:true},{v:42,t:'مَا سَلَكَكُمْ فِى سَقَرَ',end:true},{v:43,t:'قَالُوا۟ لَمْ نَكُ مِنَ ٱلْمُصَلِّينَ',end:true},{v:44,t:'وَلَمْ نَكُ',end:false}]],
   [10,[{v:44,t:'نُطْعِمُ ٱلْمِسْكِينَ',end:true},{v:45,t:'وَكُنَّا نَخُوضُ مَعَ ٱلْخَآئِضِينَ',end:true},{v:46,t:'وَكُنَّا نُكَذِّبُ بِيَوْمِ ٱلدِّينِ',end:true}]],
   [11,[{v:47,t:'حَتَّىٰٓ أَتَىٰنَا ٱلْيَقِينُ',end:true},{v:48,t:'فَمَا تَنفَعُهُمْ شَفَـٰعَةُ ٱلشَّـٰفِعِينَ',end:true},{v:49,t:'فَمَا لَهُمْ عَنِ',end:false}]],
   [12,[{v:49,t:'ٱلتَّذْكِرَةِ مُعْرِضِينَ',end:true},{v:50,t:'كَأَنَّهُمْ حُمُرٌ مُّسْتَنفِرَةٌ',end:true},{v:51,t:'فَرَّتْ مِن قَسْوَرَةٍۭ',end:true}]],
   [13,[{v:52,t:'بَلْ يُرِيدُ كُلُّ ٱمْرِئٍ مِّنْهُمْ أَن يُؤْتَىٰ صُحُفًا مُّنَشَّرَةً',end:true},{v:53,t:'كَلَّا ۖ بَل لَّا يَخَافُونَ ٱلْـَٔاخِرَةَ',end:true}]],
   [14,[{v:54,t:'كَلَّآ إِنَّهُۥ تَذْكِرَةٌ',end:true},{v:55,t:'فَمَن شَآءَ ذَكَرَهُۥ',end:true},{v:56,t:'وَمَا يَذْكُرُونَ إِلَّآ أَن يَشَآءَ ٱللَّهُ ۚ',end:false}]],
   [15,[{v:56,t:'هُوَ أَهْلُ ٱلتَّقْوَىٰ وَأَهْلُ ٱلْمَغْفِرَةِ',end:true}]]
  ]}
 ]);
}
function getFallbackChapterVerses(ch){
 if(ch===108)return fallbackKawthar();
 if(ch===112)return fallbackIkhlas();
 if(ch===73)return fallbackMuzzammil();
 if(ch===74)return fallbackMuddaththir();
 return null;
}
function fallbackIkhlas(){
 return buildFallbackVersesFromLines(112,[
  {page:604,juz:30,lines:[
   [3,[{v:1,t:'قُلْ هُوَ ٱللَّهُ أَحَدٌ',end:true},{v:2,t:'ٱللَّهُ ٱلصَّمَدُ',end:true},{v:3,t:'لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ',end:true}]],
   [4,[{v:4,t:'وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُۥ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌۢ',end:true}]]
  ]}
 ]);
}
function fallbackKawthar(){return [
 {id:1,verse_key:'108:1',verse_number:1,page_number:602,v2_page:602,juz_number:30,hizb_number:60,text_qpc_hafs:'إِنَّآ أَعۡطَيۡنَٰكَ ٱلۡكَوۡثَرَ',text_uthmani:'إِنَّآ أَعْطَيْنَـٰكَ ٱلْكَوْثَرَ',text_uthmani_tajweed:'إِ<tajweed class=ghunnah>نَّ</tajweed><tajweed class=madda_obligatory_monfasel>آ</tajweed> أَعْ<tajweed class=tafkhim>طَ</tajweed>يْنَ<tajweed class=madda_normal>ـٰ</tajweed>كَ <tajweed class=ham_wasl>ٱ</tajweed>لْكَوْثَ<tajweed class=tafkhim>رَ</tajweed>',end_word:{char_type_name:'end',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14},words:[{position:1,audio_url:'wbw/108_001_001.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'إِنَّآ',text_uthmani:'إِنَّآ'},{position:2,audio_url:'wbw/108_001_002.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'أَعۡطَيۡنَٰكَ',text_uthmani:'أَعْطَيْنَـٰكَ'},{position:3,audio_url:'wbw/108_001_003.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'ٱلۡكَوۡثَرَ',text_uthmani:'ٱلْكَوْثَرَ'}]},
 {id:2,verse_key:'108:2',verse_number:2,page_number:602,v2_page:602,juz_number:30,hizb_number:60,text_qpc_hafs:'فَصَلِّ لِرَبِّكَ وَٱنۡحَرۡ',text_uthmani:'فَصَلِّ لِرَبِّكَ وَٱنْحَرْ',text_uthmani_tajweed:'فَ<tajweed class=tafkhim>صَ</tajweed>لِّ لِ<tajweed class=tafkhim>رَ</tajweed>بِّكَ وَ<tajweed class=ham_wasl>ٱ</tajweed>نْحَ<tajweed class=tafkhim>رْ</tajweed>',end_word:{char_type_name:'end',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14},words:[{position:1,audio_url:'wbw/108_002_001.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'فَصَلِّ',text_uthmani:'فَصَلِّ'},{position:2,audio_url:'wbw/108_002_002.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'لِرَبِّكَ',text_uthmani:'لِرَبِّكَ'},{position:3,audio_url:'wbw/108_002_003.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:14,text_qpc_hafs:'وَٱنۡحَرۡ',text_uthmani:'وَٱنْحَرْ'}]},
 {id:3,verse_key:'108:3',verse_number:3,page_number:602,v2_page:602,juz_number:30,hizb_number:60,text_qpc_hafs:'إِنَّ شَانِئَكَ هُوَ ٱلۡأَبۡتَرُ',text_uthmani:'إِنَّ شَانِئَكَ هُوَ ٱلْأَبْتَرُ',text_uthmani_tajweed:'إِ<tajweed class=ghunnah>نَّ</tajweed> شَانِئَكَ هُوَ <tajweed class=ham_wasl>ٱ</tajweed>لْأَ<tajweed class=qalaqah>بْ</tajweed>تَ<tajweed class=tafkhim>رُ</tajweed>',end_word:{char_type_name:'end',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:15},words:[{position:1,audio_url:'wbw/108_003_001.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:15,text_qpc_hafs:'إِنَّ',text_uthmani:'إِنَّ'},{position:2,audio_url:'wbw/108_003_002.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:15,text_qpc_hafs:'شَانِئَكَ',text_uthmani:'شَانِئَكَ'},{position:3,audio_url:'wbw/108_003_003.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:15,text_qpc_hafs:'هُوَ',text_uthmani:'هُوَ'},{position:4,audio_url:'wbw/108_003_004.mp3',page_number:602,v2_page:602,line_number:15,text_qpc_hafs:'ٱلۡأَبۡتَرُ',text_uthmani:'ٱلْأَبْتَرُ'}]}
];}
async function fetchChapterPages(route, token){
 const all=[];
 for(let page=1;page<=8;page++){
  const qcfV2Url=`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/${route}?language=ar&words=true&word_fields=char_type_name,position,code_v2,v2_page,page_number,line_number,text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,audio_url&fields=code_v2,v2_page,text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,verse_key,rub_el_hizb_number,sajdah_type,sajdah_number,juz_number,hizb_number,page_number,ruku_number&per_page=50&page=${page}`;
  const fontUrl=`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/${route}?language=ar&words=true&word_fields=char_type_name,position,code_v2,page_number,line_number,text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,audio_url&fields=text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,verse_key,rub_el_hizb_number,sajdah_type,sajdah_number,juz_number,hizb_number,page_number,ruku_number&per_page=50&page=${page}`;
  const fallbackUrl=`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/${route}?language=ar&words=true&word_fields=char_type_name,position,page_number,line_number,text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,audio_url&fields=text_qpc_hafs,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,verse_key,rub_el_hizb_number,sajdah_type,sajdah_number,juz_number,hizb_number,page_number,ruku_number&per_page=50&page=${page}`;
  const compatibleUrl=`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/${route}?language=ar&words=true&word_fields=char_type_name,position,text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,audio_url,v2_page,page_number,line_number&fields=text_uthmani,text_uthmani_tajweed,verse_key,page_number,v2_page&per_page=50&page=${page}`;
  let data=null,lastError=null;
  for(const url of [qcfV2Url,fontUrl,fallbackUrl,compatibleUrl]){try{data=await fetchJson(url,8000);break;}catch(err){lastError=err;}}
  if(!data){if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return null;if(all.length)break;throw(lastError||new Error('Verse request failed'));}
  if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return null;
  const rows=data.verses||data.ayahs||[];
  if(!rows.length)break;
  all.push(...rows);
  const pagination=data.pagination||data.meta?.pagination||{};
  if(pagination.total_pages&&page>=Number(pagination.total_pages))break;
  if(rows.length<50)break;
 }
 return all;
}
async function supplementChapterCodeV2(chapter, rows, token){
 if(!Array.isArray(rows)||!rows.length)return rows;
 const needsGlyphs=rows.some(v=>!v.code_v2&&!(Array.isArray(v.words)&&v.words.some(w=>w?.code_v2)));
 if(!needsGlyphs)return rows;
 try{
  const glyphData=await fetchJson(`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/quran/verses/code_v2?chapter_number=${chapter}`,7000);
  if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return rows;
  const byKey=new Map((glyphData?.verses||[]).map(item=>[String(item.verse_key||''),item]));
  return rows.map((v,idx)=>{
   const key=String(v.verse_key||`${chapter}:${v.verse_number||idx+1}`);
   const match=byKey.get(key);
   if(!match)return v;
   return {...v,code_v2:v.code_v2||match.code_v2,v2_page:v.v2_page||match.v2_page||v.page_number};
  });
 }catch(_){return rows;}
}
async function loadChapter(chapter, preferredVerse=1){
 const token=++chapterLoadToken;currentChapter=Number(chapter);currentVerse=Number(preferredVerse)||1;state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=currentVerse;verses=[];fullChapterVerses=[];previousChapterLastRubNumber=null;currentIndex=0;loadedFromApi=false;
 $('surahTitle').textContent=surahNames[currentChapter-1];$('verseText').textContent='جارٍ تحميل الآيات…';$('apiNote').textContent='جارٍ الاتصال بخدمات Quran.com…';
 const initialFallback=getFallbackChapterVerses(currentChapter);
 if(initialFallback){verses=initialFallback;fullChapterVerses=verses.slice();if(chosenRange&&chosenRange.chapter===currentChapter)verses=verses.filter(v=>v.verse_number>=chosenRange.from&&v.verse_number<=chosenRange.to);currentIndex=Math.max(0,verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===currentVerse));currentVerse=verseNumber(verses[currentIndex]||verses[0]);$('apiNote').textContent='عُرضت نسخة أولية من السورة؛ يجري محاولة تحديثها من Quran.com.';ensureTajweedV4FontsForVerses(verses);renderVerseNav();renderCurrentVerse();}else{renderVerseNav();}
 const routes=[`verses/by_chapter/${currentChapter}`,`chapters/${currentChapter}/verses`];
 for(const route of routes){try{let rows=await fetchChapterPages(route,token);if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return;if(rows&&rows.length){rows=await supplementChapterCodeV2(currentChapter,rows,token);if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return;loadedFromApi=true;verses=rows.map((v,i)=>applyVerseLevelCodeV2({...v,verse_number:v.verse_number||Number((v.verse_key||'').split(':')[1])||i+1}));fullChapterVerses=verses.slice();break;}}catch(e){if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return;/* try the next compatible public endpoint */}}
 if(token!==chapterLoadToken)return;
 if(chosenRange&&chosenRange.chapter===currentChapter&&verses.length){verses=verses.filter(v=>{const n=Number(v.verse_number)||Number((v.verse_key||'').split(':')[1]);return n>=chosenRange.from&&n<=chosenRange.to;});}
 if(!verses.length){const fb=getFallbackChapterVerses(currentChapter);if(fb){fullChapterVerses=fb.slice();verses=fb.filter(v=>!chosenRange||chosenRange.chapter!==currentChapter||(v.verse_number>=chosenRange.from&&v.verse_number<=chosenRange.to));}}
 if(verses.length)verses.sort((a,b)=>verseNumber(a)-verseNumber(b));
 if(fullChapterVerses.length)fullChapterVerses.sort((a,b)=>verseNumber(a)-verseNumber(b));
 if(!verses.length){$('verseText').textContent='تعذر تحميل السورة الآن. تحقق من اتصال الإنترنت ثم اضغط على اسم السورة والنطاق لإعادة المحاولة.';$('apiNote').textContent='تعذر الاتصال بواجهة Quran.com العامة. بعض خدمات Quran Foundation الحديثة تتطلب بيانات اعتماد من خادم وسيط.';renderVerseNav();return;}
 currentIndex=Math.max(0,verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===currentVerse));if(currentIndex<0)currentIndex=0;currentVerse=verseNumber(verses[currentIndex])||1;
 $('apiNote').textContent=loadedFromApi?`تم تحميل نص سورة ${surahNames[currentChapter-1]} من واجهة Quran.com. تشغيل الصوت يتطلب اتصالًا بالإنترنت.`:'الاتصال بخدمة Quran.com غير متاح الآن؛ تُعرض نسخة احتياطية لسورة الكوثر. بقية السور تحتاج اتصالًا بالإنترنت.';
 ensureTajweedV4FontsForVerses(verses);
 renderVerseNav();renderCurrentVerse();
 if(currentChapter>1){const loadedChapter=currentChapter;const loadedToken=token;const prevCount=Number(verseCounts[loadedChapter-2])||0;if(prevCount){fetchJson(`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/verses/by_key/${loadedChapter-1}%3A${prevCount}?fields=verse_key,rub_el_hizb_number`,7000).then(payload=>{if(loadedToken!==chapterLoadToken||loadedChapter!==currentChapter)return;const prior=payload?.verse||payload?.verses?.[0]||payload;const rub=Number(prior?.rub_el_hizb_number);previousChapterLastRubNumber=Number.isInteger(rub)&&rub>0?rub:null;if((state.verseDisplayMode==='all'||currentVerse===1)&&!audioBusy)renderCurrentVerse();}).catch(()=>{});}}
}
function verseNumber(v){return Number(v?.verse_number)||Number((v?.verse_key||'').split(':')[1])||1;}
function renderVerseNav(){
 const nav=$('verseNav'),popup=$('verseNumberPopup');if(!nav)return;
 nav.innerHTML='';if(popup)popup.innerHTML='';
 const rawAvailable=verses.length?verses.map(verseNumber):Array.from({length:verseCounts[currentChapter-1]||1},(_,i)=>i+1);
 const available=[...new Set(rawAvailable.map(n=>Number(n)).filter(n=>Number.isInteger(n)&&n>0))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
 const count=available.length;
 const visibleSlice=available.slice(0,Math.min(6,count));
 visibleSlice.forEach((n,idx)=>{
  const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='verse-bubble'+(n===currentVerse?' active':'');
  b.style.order=String(idx+1);
  b.textContent=arabicNum(n);b.setAttribute('aria-label','الآية '+n);b.addEventListener('click',()=>goToVerse(n));nav.appendChild(b);
 });
 const toggle=document.createElement('button');toggle.type='button';toggle.className='more-btn more-circle';
 toggle.style.order='9999';
 toggle.textContent=showAllNumbers?'×':'+';toggle.title=showAllNumbers?'إغلاق اختيار الآيات':'إظهار جميع أرقام الآيات';toggle.setAttribute('aria-label',toggle.title);toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(showAllNumbers));
 toggle.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();showAllNumbers=!showAllNumbers;renderVerseNav();});nav.appendChild(toggle);
 if(popup){
  popup.hidden=!showAllNumbers;
  if(showAllNumbers){
   popup.setAttribute('tabindex','-1');
   available.forEach((n,idx)=>{
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='verse-picker-number'+(n===currentVerse?' active':'');
    b.style.order=String(idx+1);
    b.textContent=arabicNum(n);b.setAttribute('aria-label','اختيار الآية '+n);
    b.addEventListener('click',()=>{showAllNumbers=false;popup.hidden=true;goToVerse(n);});
    popup.appendChild(b);
   });
  }
 }
}
function currentVerseObj(){return verses[currentIndex]||verses.find(v=>verseNumber(v)===currentVerse)||null;}
const TAJWEED_ALLOWED_CLASSES=new Set(['ham_wasl','slnt','silent','sukoon','madda_normal','madda_permissible','madda_necessary','madda_obligatory','madda_obligatory_mottasel','madda_obligatory_monfasel','madda_muttasil','madda_badal','qalaqah','ikhafa','ikhafa_shafawi','idgham_ghunnah','idgham_wo_ghunnah','idgham_shafawi','idgham_mutajanisayn','idgham_mutaqaribayn','iqlab','ghunnah','laam_shamsiyah','tafkhim','ra']);
const BISMILLAH_TAJWEED_HTML='بِسْمِ <span class="tajweed ham_wasl">ٱ</span>للَّهِ <span class="tajweed ham_wasl">ٱ</span><span class="tajweed laam_shamsiyah">ل</span><span class="tajweed tafkhim">رَّ</span>حْمَ<span class="tajweed madda_normal">ـٰ</span>نِ <span class="tajweed ham_wasl">ٱ</span><span class="tajweed laam_shamsiyah">ل</span><span class="tajweed tafkhim">رَّ</span>ح<span class="tajweed madda_permissible">ِي</span>مِ';
function sanitizeTajweedMarkup(raw){if(!raw)return '';const parser=new DOMParser();const doc=parser.parseFromString('<body>'+String(raw)+'</body>','text/html');function clean(node){let out='';node.childNodes.forEach(child=>{if(child.nodeType===Node.TEXT_NODE){out+=safeText(child.textContent);}else if(child.nodeType===Node.ELEMENT_NODE){if((child.getAttribute('class')||'').split(/\s+/).includes('end'))return;const cls=(child.getAttribute('class')||'').split(/\s+/).filter(c=>TAJWEED_ALLOWED_CLASSES.has(c));const inner=clean(child);out+=cls.length?'<span class="tajweed '+cls.join(' ')+'">'+inner+'</span>':inner;}});return out;}return clean(doc.body);}
function hasArabicText(text){return /[\u0600-\u06FF\u0750-\u077F\u08A0-\u08FF]/.test(String(text||''));}
function hasArabicLetters(text){return /[\u0621-\u063A\u0641-\u064A\u066E-\u06D3\u06FA-\u06FF]/.test(String(text||''));}
function isEndMarkerWord(word){
 if(!word||typeof word!=='object')return false;
 const kind=String(word.char_type_name||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 if(kind==='end')return true;
 const raw=String(word.text_uthmani_tajweed||'');
 const doc=raw?new DOMParser().parseFromString('<body>'+raw+'</body>','text/html'):null;
 const plainWord=String(word.text_qpc_hafs||word.text_uthmani||'').trim();
 const markupSansEnd=doc?safePlainTajweedText(raw):'';
 if(doc&&doc.querySelector('.end')&&!hasArabicLetters(plainWord)&&!hasArabicLetters(markupSansEnd))return true;
 const markupText=doc?(doc.body.textContent||''):'';
 const combined=plainWord+' '+markupText;
 if(/[۝۞\u06DD\u06DE]/u.test(combined))return !hasArabicLetters(plainWord)&&!hasArabicLetters(markupSansEnd);
 return !hasArabicLetters(plainWord||markupSansEnd)&&/[٠-٩۰-۹0-9]/.test(combined);
}
function stripQuranEndMarkers(text){
 return String(text||'').replace(/(?:[۝۞\u06DD\u06DE])\s*[٠-٩۰-۹0-9]*/gu,' ').replace(/[۝۞\u06DD\u06DE]/gu,' ').replace(/[\u200e\u200f\u202a-\u202e]/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
}
function safePlainTajweedText(raw){
 if(!raw)return '';
 const d=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<body>'+String(raw)+'</body>','text/html');
 d.querySelectorAll('.end').forEach(el=>el.remove());
 return stripQuranEndMarkers(d.body.textContent||'');
}
function plainArabicFromMarkup(raw){return safePlainTajweedText(raw);}
function isCombiningQuranMark(ch){return /^\p{M}$/u.test(ch)||/[\u0670\u06D6-\u06ED\u08F0-\u08FF]/u.test(ch);}
function parseTajweedWords(raw){
 if(!raw)return [];
 const d=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<body>'+String(raw)+'</body>','text/html');
 const out=[];let clusters=[];
 const buildMarkup=(wordClusters)=>{
  let html='',runClass=null,runText='';
  const flushRun=()=>{if(!runText)return;html+=runClass?'<span class="tajweed '+runClass+'">'+safeText(runText)+'</span>':safeText(runText);runText='';};
  for(let idx=0;idx<wordClusters.length;idx++){
   const c=wordClusters[idx];
   let clsList=[...new Set((c.classes||[]).filter(x=>TAJWEED_ALLOWED_CLASSES.has(x)))];
   if(!clsList.length&&c.text){
    const base=c.text[0],marks=c.text.slice(1);
    const hasSukoon=/[\u0652\u06E1]/u.test(marks);
    const hasKasrah=/\u0650/u.test(marks);
    const prevMarks=idx>0?wordClusters[idx-1].text.slice(1):'';
    if(/[قطبجد]/u.test(base)&&hasSukoon)clsList=['qalaqah'];
    else if(/[خصضغطقظ]/u.test(base))clsList=['tafkhim'];
    else if(base==='ر'&&!hasKasrah&&!(hasSukoon&&/\u0650/u.test(prevMarks)))clsList=['tafkhim'];
    else if(/\u0653/u.test(marks))clsList=['madda_obligatory'];
    else if(/\u0670/u.test(marks))clsList=['madda_normal'];
   }
   const cls=clsList.sort().join(' ');
   if(cls!==runClass){flushRun();runClass=cls;}
   runText+=c.text;
  }
  flushRun();
  return html.replace(/[۝۞\u06DD\u06DE]/gu,'');
 };
 const flushWord=()=>{
  if(!clusters.length)return;
  const wordClusters=clusters;const plain=wordClusters.map(c=>c.text).join('');clusters=[];
  const cleanPlain=stripQuranEndMarkers(plain);
  if(!cleanPlain)return;
  const outHtml=buildMarkup(wordClusters);
  if(!hasArabicLetters(cleanPlain)){
   if(/[\u06D6-\u06ED]/u.test(cleanPlain)&&out.length){
    out[out.length-1].text+=cleanPlain;
    out[out.length-1].markup+=outHtml;
   }
   return;
  }
  out.push({text:cleanPlain,markup:outHtml});
 };
 const addText=(text,classes)=>{
  const validClasses=[...new Set(classes.filter(c=>TAJWEED_ALLOWED_CLASSES.has(c)))];
  for(const ch of Array.from(String(text||''))){
   if(/[\s\u200e\u200f\u202a-\u202e]/u.test(ch)){flushWord();continue;}
   if(/[۝۞\u06DD\u06DE]/u.test(ch)){flushWord();continue;}
   if(isCombiningQuranMark(ch)&&clusters.length){
    clusters[clusters.length-1].text+=ch;
    if(validClasses.length){
     clusters[clusters.length-1].classes=[...new Set([...(clusters[clusters.length-1].classes||[]),...validClasses])];
    }
    continue;
   }
   clusters.push({text:ch,classes:validClasses.slice()});
  }
 };
 const walk=(node,active=[])=>{
  if(node.nodeType===Node.TEXT_NODE){addText(node.nodeValue,active);return;}
  if(node.nodeType!==Node.ELEMENT_NODE)return;
  const names=(node.getAttribute('class')||'').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if(names.includes('end'))return;
  const added=names.filter(c=>TAJWEED_ALLOWED_CLASSES.has(c));
  const next=[...new Set([...active,...added])];
  node.childNodes.forEach(child=>walk(child,next));
 };
 d.body.childNodes.forEach(node=>walk(node,[]));flushWord();return out;
}
function comparableQuranWord(text){
 return String(text||'').normalize('NFC')
  .replace(/\u0670/gu,'\u0627')
  .replace(/[\p{M}\u0640\u06D6-\u06ED\u08F0-\u08FF\u06E5\u06E6]/gu,'')
  .replace(/[\u0622\u0623\u0625\u0671\u0672\u0673\u0675]/gu,'\u0627')
  .replace(/\u0627+/gu,'\u0627')
  .replace(/[\u0624]/gu,'\u0648')
  .replace(/[\u0626\u0649\u06CC]/gu,'\u064A')
  .replace(/[\u0621۝۞\u06DD\u06DE\s]+/gu,'')
  .trim();
}
function findTajweedWordForText(list,text,start=0){
 const key=comparableQuranWord(text);if(!key||!Array.isArray(list)||!list.length)return null;
 const candidates=[start,start+1,start-1,start+2];
 for(const idx of candidates){
  if(idx>=0&&idx<list.length&&comparableQuranWord(list[idx]?.text)===key){
   return {word:list[idx],index:idx};
  }
 }
 return null;
}
function cleanTajweedMarkupForWord(raw){
 const parsed=parseTajweedWords(raw||'');
 return parsed.length&&hasArabicLetters(parsed[0].text)?parsed[0].markup:'';
}


const SAJDAH_VERSE_KEYS=new Set(['7:206','13:15','16:50','17:109','19:58','22:18','22:77','25:60','27:26','32:15','38:24','41:38','53:62','84:21','96:19']);
function verseHasSajdah(v,chapter=currentChapter,number=verseNumber(v)){const type=String(v?.sajdah_type||'').toLowerCase();return type==='recommended'||type==='obligatory'||Number(v?.sajdah_number)>0||SAJDAH_VERSE_KEYS.has(String(chapter)+':'+String(number));}
function isRubElHizbStart(v,index,arr=verses){
 const n=verseNumber(v);
 // لا تُعرض علامة الربع (۞) في بداية أي سورة (الآية ١).
 if(n<=1)return false;
 const rub=Number(v?.rub_el_hizb_number);
 if(!Number.isInteger(rub)||rub<1)return false;
 const source=fullChapterVerses.length?fullChapterVerses:arr;
 const prev=source.find(item=>verseNumber(item)===n-1);
 if(prev){
  const prevRub=Number(prev?.rub_el_hizb_number);
  return Number.isInteger(prevRub)&&prevRub>0&&prevRub!==rub;
 }
 return false;
}
function firstWordHasEmbeddedRubGlyph(v){
 const ws=Array.isArray(v?.words)?v.words:[];
 return ws.slice(0,2).some(w=>/[۞\u06DE]/u.test(String(w?.text_qpc_hafs||w?.text_uthmani||w?.text_uthmani_tajweed||'')));
}
function createQuranSectionMarker(){const wrap=document.createElement('span');wrap.className='quran-section-marker';wrap.title='بداية ربع حزب';wrap.setAttribute('aria-label','علامة ربع الحزب');const symbol=document.createElement('span');symbol.className='rub-symbol';symbol.textContent='۞';wrap.appendChild(symbol);return wrap;}
function createSajdahMarker(){const el=document.createElement('span');el.className='quran-sajdah-marker';el.textContent='۩';el.title='موضع سجود التلاوة';el.setAttribute('aria-label','موضع سجود التلاوة');return el;}

let ayahHoverMenuEl = null, ayahHoverHideTimer = null, activeHoverAyahNumber = null, activeHoverVerseObj = null, activeHoverMeaningHandler = null;
let reciterActive = false;
function getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(vNo=currentVerse){
 const max=verseCounts[currentChapter-1]||vNo||1;
 if(chosenRange&&Number(chosenRange.chapter)===currentChapter&&vNo>=Number(chosenRange.from)&&vNo<=Number(chosenRange.to)){
  return Number(chosenRange.to);
 }
 return max;
}
async function playAyahFromMarker(verseNo){
 const targetN=Number(verseNo)||currentVerse;
 if(currentVerse!==targetN){
  if(state.verseDisplayMode!=='single'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden){
   currentVerse=targetN;
   currentIndex=Math.max(0,verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===targetN));
   state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=targetN;
   $('allVersesCard').querySelectorAll('.all-verse-row').forEach(r=>r.classList.toggle('current-row',Number(r.dataset.verse)===targetN));
   renderVerseNav();
   saveState();
  }else{
   await goToVerse(targetN, false);
  }
 }
 const selectedEnd=getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(targetN);
 rangePlayback={chapter:currentChapter,from:targetN,to:Math.max(targetN,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
 reciterActive=true;
 await playVerse(true);
}
function ensureAyahHoverMenu(){
 if(ayahHoverMenuEl)return ayahHoverMenuEl;
 const menu=document.createElement('div');
 menu.id='ayahHoverMenu';
 menu.className='ayah-hover-menu';
 menu.setAttribute('role','menu');
 menu.hidden=true;
 menu.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="ayah-hover-choice" data-ayah-action="meaning" role="menuitem" title="معنى الآية" aria-label="معنى الآية">•••</button>`;
 document.body.appendChild(menu);
 menu.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>clearTimeout(ayahHoverHideTimer));
 menu.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{
  clearTimeout(ayahHoverHideTimer);
  ayahHoverHideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{menu.hidden=true;},160);
 });
 menu.querySelector('[data-ayah-action="meaning"]').addEventListener('click',async e=>{
  e.stopPropagation();
  menu.hidden=true;
  if(typeof activeHoverMeaningHandler==='function'){
   await activeHoverMeaningHandler(e);
  }
 });
 $('allVersesCard')?.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{if(ayahHoverMenuEl)ayahHoverMenuEl.hidden=true;},{passive:true});
 ayahHoverMenuEl=menu;
 return menu;
}
function showAyahHoverMenuForMarker(anchorEl, number, handler, verseObj){
 if(!anchorEl||!anchorEl.classList?.contains('ayah-end'))return;
 const menu=ensureAyahHoverMenu();
 clearTimeout(ayahHoverHideTimer);
 const targetVerse=Number(number)||currentVerse;
 activeHoverAyahNumber=targetVerse;
 activeHoverVerseObj=verseObj;
 activeHoverMeaningHandler=handler;
 menu.dataset.verse=String(targetVerse);
 menu.hidden=false;
 const glyphEl=anchorEl.querySelector('.ayah-end-number')||anchorEl;
 const gRect=glyphEl.getBoundingClientRect();
 const aRect=anchorEl.getBoundingClientRect();
 const rect=(gRect.width>2&&gRect.height>2)?gRect:aRect;
 const menuW=menu.offsetWidth||36,menuH=menu.offsetHeight||22;
 let left=Math.round(rect.left+(rect.width/2)-(menuW/2));
 left=Math.max(4,Math.min(window.innerWidth-menuW-4,left));
 let top=Math.round(rect.top-menuH-2);
 if(top<2)top=2;
 menu.style.left=left+'px';
 menu.style.top=top+'px';
}
function scheduleHideAyahHoverMenu(){
 clearTimeout(ayahHoverHideTimer);
 ayahHoverHideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(ayahHoverMenuEl)ayahHoverMenuEl.hidden=true;},140);
}
function createAyahEndMarker(number,handler,verseObj=null){
 const end=document.createElement('button');end.type='button';end.className='ayah-end';end.dataset.verse=String(number);end.title='اضغط للاستماع إلى الآية — أو مرّر المؤشر لعرض معنى الآية';end.setAttribute('aria-label','علامة الآية '+number+' — اضغط للاستماع إلى الآية');
 const num=document.createElement('span');num.className='ayah-end-number';
 const fallbackText=arabicNum(number);
 num.textContent=fallbackText;
 end.dataset.unicodeText=fallbackText;
 end.appendChild(num);
 const endWord=verseObj?.end_word||null;
 const page=Number(endWord?.v2_page||endWord?.page_number||verseObj?.v2_page||verseObj?.page_number)||0;
 const codeV2=String(endWord?.code_v2||'').trim();
 if(codeV2&&page>=1&&page<=604){
  end.dataset.codeV2=codeV2;
  end.dataset.qcfPage=String(page);
  if(readyQcfV4Pages.has(page)&&!isCustomMushafFontSelected()){
   applyQcfGlyphToElement(end,page,codeV2);
  }else{
   loadTajweedV4PageFont(page);
  }
 }
 const meaningFn=handler||(()=>toggleTafsirPanel());
 end.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>showAyahHoverMenuForMarker(end,number,meaningFn,verseObj));
 end.addEventListener('pointerenter',()=>showAyahHoverMenuForMarker(end,number,meaningFn,verseObj));
 end.addEventListener('mouseleave',scheduleHideAyahHoverMenu);
 end.addEventListener('click',async e=>{
  e.stopPropagation();
  if(ayahHoverMenuEl)ayahHoverMenuEl.hidden=true;
  await playAyahFromMarker(number);
 });
 return end;
}
let recitingInlineColorSnapshots=[];
function clearRecitingVerseInlineColors(){for(const entry of recitingInlineColorSnapshots){const el=entry.el;if(!el||!el.style)continue;if(entry.colorValue)el.style.setProperty('color',entry.colorValue,entry.colorPriority||'');else el.style.removeProperty('color');if(entry.shadowValue)el.style.setProperty('text-shadow',entry.shadowValue,entry.shadowPriority||'');else el.style.removeProperty('text-shadow');}recitingInlineColorSnapshots=[];}
function setRecitingVerseHighlight(verseNo){document.querySelectorAll('.reciting-verse').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('reciting-verse'));clearRecitingVerseInlineColors();const n=Number(verseNo);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<1)return;const targets=(state.verseDisplayMode==='all'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden)?Array.from($('allVersesCard')?.querySelectorAll('.all-verse-row[data-verse="'+n+'"]')||[]):[$('verseText')].filter(Boolean);for(const target of targets){target.classList.add('reciting-verse');target.querySelectorAll('.word').forEach(word=>{[word,...word.querySelectorAll('*')].forEach(el=>{recitingInlineColorSnapshots.push({el,colorValue:el.style.getPropertyValue('color'),colorPriority:el.style.getPropertyPriority('color'),shadowValue:el.style.getPropertyValue('text-shadow'),shadowPriority:el.style.getPropertyPriority('text-shadow')});el.style.setProperty('color','#555650','important');el.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none','important');});});}}

function renderCurrentVerse(){
 const v=currentVerseObj();if(!v)return;
 currentVerse=verseNumber(v);
 $('surahTitle').textContent=surahNames[currentChapter-1];
 $('verseMeta').textContent=`الآية ${arabicNum(currentVerse)} من سورة ${surahNames[currentChapter-1]}`;
 const bEl=$('bismillah');
 if(bEl){
  bEl.innerHTML=BISMILLAH_TAJWEED_HTML;
  bEl.classList.toggle('show',currentVerse===1&&currentChapter!==1&&currentChapter!==9);
 }
 const textEl=$('verseText');textEl.innerHTML='';
 ensureTajweedV4FontsForVerses([v]);
 if(isRubElHizbStart(v,currentIndex,verses)&&!firstWordHasEmbeddedRubGlyph(v)){
  textEl.appendChild(createQuranSectionMarker());
  textEl.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
 }
 const renderCount=appendVerseWords(textEl,v);
 if(!renderCount){textEl.textContent='تعذّر عرض نص الآية العربي من المصدر. أعد المحاولة عند توفر الاتصال.';showToast('تم تجاهل نص غير عربي أو غير مكتمل من المصدر.');}
 const endMarkerEl=createAyahEndMarker(currentVerse,toggleTafsirPanel,v);
 const lastWordEl=textEl.querySelector('.word:last-of-type');
 if(lastWordEl&&lastWordEl.parentElement===textEl){
  const pairWrap=document.createElement('span');
  pairWrap.className='mushaf-word-end-pair';
  textEl.replaceChild(pairWrap,lastWordEl);
  pairWrap.appendChild(lastWordEl);
  pairWrap.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
  pairWrap.appendChild(endMarkerEl);
 }else{
  textEl.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
  textEl.appendChild(endMarkerEl);
 }
 $('prevVerse').disabled=currentIndex<=0;
 $('prevVerse').innerHTML='<svg width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9 5 16 12 9 19" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
 $('prevVerse').setAttribute('aria-label','الآية السابقة');$('prevVerse').title=currentIndex<=0?'أنت عند أول آية محمّلة':'الآية السابقة';
 $('nextVerse').innerHTML='<svg width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M15 5 8 12l7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';
 $('nextVerse').setAttribute('aria-label','الآية التالية');$('nextVerse').title=currentIndex>=verses.length-1?'أنت عند آخر آية محمّلة':'الآية التالية';
 $('playVerse').disabled=false;renderVerseNav();
}
async function goToVerse(n, autoResumeReciter=true){
 const targetN=Number(n);
 const wasReciterActive=Boolean(reciterActive||(['verse','range','chapter-seek'].includes($('audioPlayer')?.dataset?.mode||'')&&audioBusy));
 if(targetN===currentVerse){
  renderCurrentVerse();
  if(wasReciterActive&&autoResumeReciter){
   const selectedEnd=getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(currentVerse);
   rangePlayback={chapter:currentChapter,from:currentVerse,to:Math.max(currentVerse,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
   reciterActive=true;
   await playVerse(true);
  }
  return;
 }
 const idx=verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===targetN);
 if(idx<0){showToast('رقم الآية غير موجود في السورة المحمّلة.');return;}
 currentIndex=idx;currentVerse=targetN;state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=currentVerse;showAllNumbers=false;
 stopAudio();
 $('tafsirPanel').classList.remove('show');$('tafsirPanel').hidden=true;$('editTafsirButton').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
 renderCurrentVerse();saveState();
 if(wasReciterActive&&autoResumeReciter){
  const selectedEnd=getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(currentVerse);
  rangePlayback={chapter:currentChapter,from:currentVerse,to:Math.max(currentVerse,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
  reciterActive=true;
  await playVerse(true);
 }
}
$('prevVerse').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(currentIndex>0)goToVerse(verseNumber(verses[currentIndex-1]));else showToast('هذه أول آية في السورة.');});
$('nextVerse').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(currentIndex<verses.length-1)goToVerse(verseNumber(verses[currentIndex+1]));else showToast('هذه آخر آية في السورة.');});
function syncPlayVerseButton(playing){
 const b=$('playVerse');if(!b)return;
 const svg=b.querySelector('svg');
 if(svg){svg.innerHTML=playing?'<rect x="6" y="5" width="4" height="14" rx="1" fill="currentColor"></rect><rect x="14" y="5" width="4" height="14" rx="1" fill="currentColor"></rect>':'<path d="M8 5v14l11-7z" fill="currentColor"></path>';}
 b.classList.toggle('active',!!playing);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!playing));b.setAttribute('aria-label',playing?'إيقاف التلاوة':'استمع للآية');b.title=playing?'إيقاف التلاوة':'استمع للآية';
}
function stopAudio(){
 setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);
 audioRequestToken++;
 reciterActive=false;
 chapterAudioSession=null;verseWordSeekSession=null;
 const a=$('audioPlayer');
 try{a.pause();a.currentTime=0;if(a.dataset)a.dataset.mode='';}catch(_){}
 audioBusy=false;
 syncPlayVerseButton(false);
 document.querySelectorAll('.word.speaking').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('speaking'));
}
async function playAudioCandidate(url,requestToken){
 const a=$('audioPlayer');
 return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
  let settled=false,started=false,timer=0;
  const cleanup=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);a.removeEventListener('canplay',onCanPlay);a.removeEventListener('error',onError);};
  const finish=(fn,value)=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;cleanup();fn(value);};
  const onError=()=>finish(reject,new Error('Audio source failed'));
  const onCanPlay=()=>{
   if(started)return;started=true;
   if(requestToken!==audioRequestToken){finish(resolve,false);return;}
   a.play().then(()=>finish(resolve,true)).catch(err=>finish(reject,err));
  };
  a.addEventListener('canplay',onCanPlay);
  a.addEventListener('error',onError);
  timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(reject,new Error('Audio source timeout')),8500);
  a.src=url;
  a.load();
  if(a.readyState>=2)onCanPlay();
 });
}
function getReciterVerseAudioFallbackUrls(chapter,verse,reciter){
 const padded=String(chapter).padStart(3,'0')+String(verse).padStart(3,'0');
 if(Number(reciter)===6){
  return [`https://verses.quran.com/Husary/mp3/${padded}.mp3`,`https://everyayah.com/data/Husary_128kbps/${padded}.mp3`];
 }
 return [`https://verses.quran.com/Minshawy/mp3/${padded}.mp3`,`https://everyayah.com/data/Minshawy_Murattal_128kbps/${padded}.mp3`];
}
async function playVerse(fromRange=false){
 const a=$('audioPlayer');
 // زر الاستماع يعمل كمفتاح تشغيل/إيقاف عند ضغطه مرة ثانية.
 if(!fromRange&&(audioBusy||reciterActive)){rangePlayback=null;stopAudio();showToast('تم إيقاف التلاوة.');return;}
 const v=currentVerseObj();if(!v)return;
 if(!fromRange){
  const selectedEnd=getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(currentVerse);
  rangePlayback={chapter:currentChapter,from:currentVerse,to:Math.max(currentVerse,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
  fromRange=true;
 }
 stopAudio();
 const requestToken=++audioRequestToken;
 const reciter=Number(state.reciter)||6;
 const urls=[];
 reciterActive=true;audioBusy=true;syncPlayVerseButton(true);
 try{
  const timingKey=`${currentChapter}:${currentVerse}:${reciter}`;
  let timing=verseAudioTimingCache.get(timingKey)||null;
  if(!timing){
   try{
    timing=await Promise.race([
     getVerseAudioTiming(currentChapter,currentVerse,reciter),
     new Promise(r=>setTimeout(()=>r(null),2200))
    ]);
   }catch(_){}
  }
  if(requestToken!==audioRequestToken)return;
  if(timing?.audioUrl)urls.push(timing.audioUrl);
  urls.push(...getReciterVerseAudioFallbackUrls(currentChapter,currentVerse,reciter));
  if(!timing){
   getVerseAudioTiming(currentChapter,currentVerse,reciter).then(t=>{
    if(requestToken===audioRequestToken&&t?.segments?.length&&audioBusy&&['verse','range'].includes(a.dataset.mode)){
     verseWordSeekSession={chapter:Number(currentChapter),verse:Number(currentVerse),segments:normalizeTimingSegments(t.segments,getVerseSpokenWordCount(currentVerse)),position:1,targetSec:0};
     startAudioSyncRaf();
    }
   }).catch(()=>{});
  }
  if(currentVerse<verseCounts[currentChapter-1]){
   getVerseAudioTiming(currentChapter,currentVerse+1,reciter).catch(()=>{});
  }
  const unique=[...new Set(urls.filter(Boolean))];
  let played=false;
  for(const url of unique){
   if(requestToken!==audioRequestToken)return;
   try{played=await playAudioCandidate(url,requestToken);if(played)break;}catch(_){/* الانتقال تلقائيًا للمصدر الاحتياطي التالي */}
  }
  if(requestToken!==audioRequestToken)return;
  if(!played)throw new Error('No audio source could play');
  a.dataset.mode=fromRange?'range':'verse';
  a.loop=fromRange?false:audioRepeat;
  if(timing?.segments?.length){
   const normSegs=normalizeTimingSegments(timing.segments,getVerseSpokenWordCount(currentVerse));
   verseWordSeekSession={chapter:Number(currentChapter),verse:Number(currentVerse),segments:normSegs,position:1,targetSec:0};
   startAudioSyncRaf();
  }
  reciterActive=true;audioBusy=true;syncPlayVerseButton(true);setRecitingVerseHighlight(currentVerse);scrollVerseRowIntoView(currentVerse,true);
 }catch(e){
  if(requestToken!==audioRequestToken)return;
  reciterActive=false;audioBusy=false;syncPlayVerseButton(false);setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);
  if(fromRange)rangePlayback=null;
  showToast('تعذر تشغيل التلاوة. تحقق من الاتصال بالإنترنت ثم أعد المحاولة.');
 }
}
$('playVerse').addEventListener('click',()=>playVerse(false));
$('audioPlayer').addEventListener('ended',()=>{
 const a=$('audioPlayer');
 if(a.dataset.mode==='chapter-seek'&&chapterAudioSession){finishChapterAudioSession(true);return;}
 if(verseWordSeekSession){verseWordSeekSession=null;document.querySelectorAll('.word.speaking').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('speaking'));}
 if(a.dataset.mode==='range'&&rangePlayback){
  if(audioRepeat){a.currentTime=0;a.play().catch(()=>{});return;}
  const next=currentVerse+1;
  if(next<=rangePlayback.to){goToVerse(next,false);setTimeout(()=>playVerse(true),60);return;}
  if(rangePlayback.repeat){goToVerse(rangePlayback.from,false);setTimeout(()=>playVerse(true),60);return;}
  rangePlayback=null;reciterActive=false;audioBusy=false;syncPlayVerseButton(false);setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);showToast('اكتملت قراءة الآيات المحددة.');return;
 }
 if(audioRepeat&&a.dataset.mode!=='word'){a.currentTime=0;a.play().catch(()=>{});}
 else{reciterActive=false;audioBusy=false;syncPlayVerseButton(false);setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);}
});
$('audioPlayer').addEventListener('pause',()=>{if(!audioBusy&&!$('audioPlayer').ended)syncPlayVerseButton(false);});
audioRepeat=!!state.repeatAudio;
function scrollVerseRowIntoView(verseNo, smooth=true){
 const all=$('allVersesCard');
 if(state.verseDisplayMode!=='all'||!all||all.hidden)return;
 const row=all.querySelector('.word[data-verse="'+Number(verseNo)+'"],.ayah-end[data-verse="'+Number(verseNo)+'"]')||all.querySelector('.all-verse-row[data-verse="'+Number(verseNo)+'"]');
 if(!row)return;
 const cr=all.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=row.getBoundingClientRect(),pad=28;
 let top=all.scrollTop;
 if(rr.top<cr.top+pad)top-=cr.top+pad-rr.top;
 else if(rr.bottom>cr.bottom-pad)top+=rr.bottom-(cr.bottom-pad);
 if(Math.abs(top-all.scrollTop)>2)all.scrollTo({top:Math.max(0,top),behavior:smooth?'smooth':'auto'});
}
function setActiveVerseDuringAudio(verseNo){
 const n=Number(verseNo);if(!n)return;
 if(currentVerse!==n){currentVerse=n;currentIndex=verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===n);if(currentIndex<0)currentIndex=0;state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=n;
  if(state.verseDisplayMode==='all'&&!$('allVersesCard').hidden){$('allVersesCard').querySelectorAll('.all-verse-row').forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('current-row',Number(row.dataset.verse)===n));renderVerseNav();scrollVerseRowIntoView(n,true);}
  else renderCurrentVerse();
  saveState();
 }else scrollVerseRowIntoView(n,true);
 setRecitingVerseHighlight(n);
}
async function getChapterAudioTiming(chapter, reciter){
 const key=String(reciter)+':'+String(chapter);
 if(chapterTimingCache.has(key)){const cached=chapterTimingCache.get(key);if(cached&&cached.__unavailableAt){if(Date.now()-cached.__unavailableAt<45000)return null;chapterTimingCache.delete(key);}else return cached;}
 try{
  const catalog=await fetchJson('https://api.quran.com/api/v4/resources/chapter_reciters?language=en',4200);
  const records=Array.isArray(catalog.reciters)?catalog.reciters:(Array.isArray(catalog.chapter_reciters)?catalog.chapter_reciters:[]);
  const wanted=Number(reciter)===6?/(husary|husari|حصري)/i:/(minshawi|minshawy|minshawi|منشاوي)/i;
  const choices=records.filter(r=>wanted.test([r.name,r.reciter_name,r.reciterName,r.translated_name?.name,r.translatedName?.name,r.style?.name].join(' ')));
  choices.sort((a,b)=>{
   const aa=/murattal/i.test([a.style?.name,a.style?.language_name].join(' '))?0:1;
   const bb=/murattal/i.test([b.style?.name,b.style?.language_name].join(' '))?0:1;
   return aa-bb;
  });
  if(!choices.length){chapterTimingCache.set(key,{__unavailableAt:Date.now()});return null;}
  for(const candidate of choices.slice(0,2)){
   try{
    const data=await fetchJson(`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/chapter_recitations/${candidate.id}/${chapter}?segments=true`,4200);
    const file=data.audio_file||data.audioFile||data.chapter_recitation||data;
    const audioUrl=normalizeUrl(file.audio_url||file.audioUrl||file.url||'');
    const timestamps=Array.isArray(file.timestamps)?file.timestamps:[];
    if(audioUrl&&timestamps.length){const result={audioUrl,timestamps,reciterId:candidate.id};chapterTimingCache.set(key,result);return result;}
   }catch(_){}
  }
 }catch(_){}
 chapterTimingCache.set(key,{__unavailableAt:Date.now()});
 return null;
}
function verseKeyNumber(timestamp){const key=String(timestamp?.verse_key||timestamp?.verseKey||'');const m=key.match(/\d+:(\d+)/);return m?Number(m[1]):Number(timestamp?.verse_number||timestamp?.verseNumber||0);}
function getVerseSpokenWordCount(verseNo){
 const vNo=Number(verseNo)||currentVerse;
 const domWords=document.querySelectorAll((state.verseDisplayMode!=='single'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden)?`#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="${vNo}"]`:'#verseText .word');
 if(domWords.length)return domWords.length;
 const vObj=verses.find(v=>verseNumber(v)===vNo);
 if(vObj&&Array.isArray(vObj.words)){
  return vObj.words.filter(w=>!isEndMarkerWord(w)&&!isPauseMarkWord(w)).length;
 }
 return 0;
}
function normalizeTimingSegments(rawSegments, spokenWordCount=0){
 if(!Array.isArray(rawSegments))return [];
 const fourTuples=[];
 const threeTuples=[];
 for(const seg of rawSegments){
  if(!Array.isArray(seg)||seg.length<3)continue;
  if(seg.length>=4&&Number.isFinite(Number(seg[2]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(seg[3]))){
   const a=Number(seg[0]),b=Number(seg[1]),startMs=Number(seg[2]),endMs=Number(seg[3]);
   if(Number.isFinite(a)&&Number.isFinite(b)&&endMs>startMs)fourTuples.push({a,b,startMs,endMs});
  }else if(Number.isFinite(Number(seg[0]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(seg[1]))&&Number.isFinite(Number(seg[2]))){
   const w=Number(seg[0]),startMs=Number(seg[1]),endMs=Number(seg[2]);
   if(Number.isFinite(w)&&endMs>startMs)threeTuples.push({w,startMs,endMs});
  }
 }
 let norm=[];
 if(fourTuples.length){
  // استبعاد مقاطع الصمت في بداية أو نهاية الملف الصوتي مثل [0, 0, 0, 120]
  const wordTuples=fourTuples.filter(s=>!(s.a<=0&&s.b<=0));
  if(!wordTuples.length)return [];
  // إذا كان أول مقطع كلمة يبدأ من a===0 و b===1 فالمؤشر الأول صفرى (0,1,2...) ورقم الكلمة هو a+1
  // أما إذا كان يبدأ من a>=1 (مثل [1, 2, start, end]) فرقم الكلمة الفعلي هو a (لأن b=a+1 هو الحد الحصري)
  const zeroBased=(wordTuples[0].a===0&&wordTuples[0].b>=1);
  norm=wordTuples.map(s=>{
   const pos=zeroBased?(s.a+1):Math.max(1,s.a);
   return {wordPos:pos,rawPos:pos,wordEndPos:pos,startMs:s.startMs,endMs:s.endMs};
  });
 }else if(threeTuples.length){
  // في المقاطع الثلاثية [wordPos, startMs, endMs]: الرقم 0 يمثل صمت البداية مثل [0, 0, 120]، والكلمات دائماً تبدأ من 1
  const nonZero=threeTuples.filter(s=>s.w>=1);
  const list=nonZero.length?nonZero:threeTuples;
  const zeroBased=(!nonZero.length&&list[0]?.w===0);
  norm=list.map(s=>{
   const pos=zeroBased?(s.w+1):Math.max(1,s.w);
   return {wordPos:pos,rawPos:pos,wordEndPos:pos,startMs:s.startMs,endMs:s.endMs};
  });
 }
 norm.sort((x,y)=>x.startMs-y.startMs);
 const merged=[];
 for(const item of norm){
  const prev=merged[merged.length-1];
  if(prev&&prev.rawPos===item.rawPos){
   prev.endMs=Math.max(prev.endMs,item.endMs);
  }else{
   merged.push({...item});
  }
 }
 if(spokenWordCount>0&&merged.length===spokenWordCount){
  merged.forEach((m,idx)=>{m.wordPos=idx+1;});
 }
 return merged;
}
function findWordSegment(timestamp, position, spokenWordCount=0, tokenPosition=0){
 const norm=normalizeTimingSegments(timestamp?.segments||timestamp, spokenWordCount);
 if(!norm.length)return null;
 const pos=Number(position)||1;
 const tokPos=Number(tokenPosition)||pos;
 const exact=norm.find(s=>s.wordPos===pos)
  ||(tokPos!==pos?norm.find(s=>s.rawPos===tokPos):null)
  ||norm.find(s=>pos>=s.wordPos&&pos<=s.wordEndPos);
 if(!exact)return null;
 return [exact.wordPos,Math.max(0,exact.startMs),exact.endMs];
}
function setSpeakingWord(verseNo, wordPosition, preferWbwPos=false){
 document.querySelectorAll('.word.speaking').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('speaking'));
 const pos=Number(wordPosition);if(!Number.isFinite(pos)||pos<1)return;
 const vNo=Number(verseNo);
 let target=null;
 const attrPrimary=preferWbwPos?'data-token-position':'data-spoken-word-index';
 const attrSecondary=preferWbwPos?'data-wbw-position':'data-token-position';
 if(state.verseDisplayMode!=='single'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden){
  target=document.querySelector('#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="'+vNo+'"]['+attrPrimary+'="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="'+vNo+'"]['+attrSecondary+'="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="'+vNo+'"][data-spoken-word-index="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="'+vNo+'"][data-chapter-word-index="'+pos+'"]');
 }else{
  target=document.querySelector('#verseText .word['+attrPrimary+'="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#verseText .word['+attrSecondary+'="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#verseText .word[data-spoken-word-index="'+pos+'"]')
   ||document.querySelector('#verseText .word[data-chapter-word-index="'+pos+'"]');
 }
 if(target){target.classList.add('speaking');currentWord=target;}
}
function finishChapterAudioSession(reachedEnd=false){
 const session=chapterAudioSession;if(!session)return;
 chapterAudioSession=null;
 const a=$('audioPlayer');audioBusy=false;
 try{a.pause();}catch(_){}
 document.querySelectorAll('.word.speaking').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('speaking'));
 setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);syncPlayVerseButton(false);
 if(reachedEnd&&session.range&&session.repeat){
  rangePlayback={...session.range};reciterActive=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{goToVerse(session.range.from,false);playVerse(true);},80);return;
 }
 if(reachedEnd&&!session.range&&session.repeatSingle){
  rangePlayback=null;reciterActive=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{goToVerse(session.targetVerse,false);playVerse(false);},80);return;
 }
 reciterActive=false;
 if(session.range){rangePlayback=null;if(reachedEnd)showToast('اكتملت قراءة الآيات المحددة.');}
}
function handleChapterAudioTime(){
 const session=chapterAudioSession,a=$('audioPlayer');if(!session||a.dataset.mode!=='chapter-seek')return;
 const ms=a.currentTime*1000;
 if(session.endMs&&ms>=session.endMs-80){finishChapterAudioSession(true);return;}
 const time=session.timestamps.find(t=>{
  const from=Number(t.timestamp_from??t.timestampFrom),to=Number(t.timestamp_to??t.timestampTo);
  return Number.isFinite(from)&&Number.isFinite(to)&&ms>=from&&ms<to;
 });
 if(!time)return;
 const n=verseKeyNumber(time);
 if(n&&n>=session.startVerse&&n<=session.stopAfterVerse){
  setActiveVerseDuringAudio(n);
  const vFrom=Number(time.timestamp_from??time.timestampFrom)||0;
  const norm=normalizeTimingSegments(time.segments,getVerseSpokenWordCount(n));
  const seg=norm.find(s=>{
   const absStart=(s.startMs<vFrom&&vFrom>0)?vFrom+s.startMs:s.startMs;
   const absEnd=(s.endMs<vFrom&&vFrom>0)?vFrom+s.endMs:s.endMs;
   return ms>=absStart-45&&ms<=absEnd+45;
  });
  if(seg)setSpeakingWord(n,seg.wordPos);
 }
}
$('audioPlayer').addEventListener('timeupdate',handleChapterAudioTime);
const wbwVerseWordsCache=new Map();
async function fetchVerifiedWordAudioUrl(chapter,verse,spokenIdx,arabicTxt){
 const key=`${Number(chapter)}:${Number(verse)}`;
 let words=wbwVerseWordsCache.get(key);
 if(!words){
  try{
   const data=await fetchJson(`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/verses/by_key/${Number(chapter)}%3A${Number(verse)}?words=true&word_fields=audio_url,position,text_uthmani,text_qpc_hafs,char_type_name`,2500);
   words=data?.verse?.words||data?.verses?.[0]?.words||[];
   if(Array.isArray(words)&&words.length)wbwVerseWordsCache.set(key,words);
  }catch(_){}
 }
 if(!Array.isArray(words)||!words.length)return '';
 const spokenList=words.filter(w=>!isEndMarkerWord(w)&&!isPauseMarkWord(w)&&w?.audio_url);
 const idx=Math.max(0,(Number(spokenIdx)||1)-1);
 if(spokenList[idx]?.audio_url){
  const cmpTarget=comparableQuranWord(arabicTxt||'');
  const cmpCand=comparableQuranWord(spokenList[idx].text_qpc_hafs||spokenList[idx].text_uthmani||'');
  if(!cmpTarget||!cmpCand||cmpTarget===cmpCand)return normalizeUrl(spokenList[idx].audio_url);
 }
 if(arabicTxt){
  const cmpTarget=comparableQuranWord(arabicTxt);
  const found=spokenList.find((w,i)=>Math.abs(i-idx)<=2&&comparableQuranWord(w.text_qpc_hafs||w.text_uthmani||'')===cmpTarget);
  if(found?.audio_url)return normalizeUrl(found.audio_url);
 }
 return spokenList[idx]?.audio_url?normalizeUrl(spokenList[idx].audio_url):'';
}
async function playStandaloneWord(span,word,targetVerse){
 const a=$('audioPlayer');const reqToken=++audioRequestToken;reciterActive=false;chapterAudioSession=null;verseWordSeekSession=null;rangePlayback=null;audioBusy=false;syncPlayVerseButton(false);
 try{a.pause();a.currentTime=0;}catch(_){}
 a.onended=null;
 document.querySelectorAll('.word.speaking').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('speaking'));
 setRecitingVerseHighlight(null);span.classList.add('speaking');currentWord=span;
 const chPad=String(currentChapter).padStart(3,'0');
 const vPad=String(targetVerse).padStart(3,'0');
 const spokenIdx=Number(span.dataset.spokenWordIndex)||1;
 const sPad=String(spokenIdx).padStart(3,'0');
 const candidateUrls=[
  `https://audio.qurancdn.com/wbw/${chPad}_${vPad}_${sPad}.mp3`,
  `https://verses.quran.foundation/wbw/${chPad}_${vPad}_${sPad}.mp3`
 ];
 if(word?.audio_url&&!/^(?:null|undefined)$/i.test(String(word.audio_url).trim())){
  const rawPos=resolveSpokenWordPosition(word,spokenIdx);
  if(rawPos===spokenIdx)candidateUrls.unshift(normalizeUrl(word.audio_url));
 }
 const uniqueUrls=[...new Set(candidateUrls.filter(Boolean))];
 for(const url of uniqueUrls){
  if(reqToken!==audioRequestToken)return false;
  a.dataset.mode='word';a.loop=false;a.onended=()=>{if(currentWord===span)span.classList.remove('speaking');};
  try{
   const started=await new Promise(resolve=>{
    let settled=false,timer=null;
    const finish=ok=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);a.removeEventListener('playing',onPlaying);a.removeEventListener('canplay',onCanPlay);a.removeEventListener('error',onError);resolve(ok);};
    const onPlaying=()=>finish(true);
    const onCanPlay=()=>{a.play().then(()=>finish(true)).catch(()=>finish(false));};
    const onError=()=>finish(false);
    a.addEventListener('playing',onPlaying,{once:true});
    a.addEventListener('canplay',onCanPlay,{once:true});
    a.addEventListener('error',onError,{once:true});
    timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(!a.paused&&!a.error&&a.readyState>=2),3600);
    a.src=url;a.load();
    try{Promise.resolve(a.play()).then(()=>finish(true)).catch(()=>{});}catch(_){}
   });
   if(started)return true;
  }catch(_){}
 }
 try{a.pause();}catch(_){}
 a.onended=null;
 span.classList.remove('speaking');
 showToast('النطق المنفرد لهذه الكلمة غير متاح حاليًا من المصدر الصوتي.');
 return false;
}
async function getVerseAudioTiming(chapter,verse,reciter,timeoutMs=4200){
 const key=`${Number(chapter)}:${Number(verse)}:${Number(reciter)}`;
 if(verseAudioTimingCache.has(key)){const v=verseAudioTimingCache.get(key);if(v&&Array.isArray(v.segments)&&v.segments.length)return v;}
 const endpoints=[
  `https://api.quran.com/api/v4/recitations/${Number(reciter)}/by_ayah/${Number(chapter)}%3A${Number(verse)}?fields=segments`,
  `https://api.quran.com/api/v4/quran/recitations/${Number(reciter)}?verse_key=${Number(chapter)}%3A${Number(verse)}&fields=segments`
 ];
 for(const endpoint of endpoints){
  try{
   const data=await fetchJson(endpoint,timeoutMs);
   const file=data.audio_file||data.audio_files?.[0]||{};
   const audioUrl=normalizeUrl(file.audio_url||file.url||'');
   const segments=Array.isArray(file.segments)?file.segments:[];
   if(audioUrl&&segments.length){
    const result={audioUrl,segments};
    verseAudioTimingCache.set(key,result);
    return result;
   }
  }catch(_){}
 }
 return null;
}
function estimateWordProportionalSeekSeconds(targetVerse, pos, totalDurationSec){
 if(pos<=1||!Number.isFinite(totalDurationSec)||totalDurationSec<=0.4)return 0;
 const scope=(state.verseDisplayMode!=='single'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden)
  ?Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#allVersesCard .word[data-verse="'+Number(targetVerse)+'"]'))
  :Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#verseText .word'));
 if(!scope.length)return 0;
 const weights=scope.map(el=>Math.max(2,comparableQuranWord(el.dataset.arabicText||el.textContent||'').length));
 const totalW=weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)||1;
 const beforeW=weights.slice(0,Math.max(0,pos-1)).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 const lead=Math.min(0.2,totalDurationSec*0.05);
 const tail=Math.min(0.45,totalDurationSec*0.1);
 const usable=Math.max(0.2,totalDurationSec-lead-tail);
 return Math.max(0,Math.min(totalDurationSec-0.2,lead+usable*(beforeW/totalW)));
}
let audioSyncRafId=0;
function startAudioSyncRaf(){
 if(audioSyncRafId)return;
 const tick=()=>{
  audioSyncRafId=0;
  const a=$('audioPlayer');
  if(!a||a.paused||!audioBusy)return;
  if(verseWordSeekSession)handleVerseWordSeekTime();
  if(chapterAudioSession)handleChapterAudioTime();
  audioSyncRafId=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 };
 audioSyncRafId=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
async function playVerseWordSegment(span,word,targetVerse,resumeContext){
 const reciter=Number(state.reciter)||6;
 const pos=Number(span.dataset.spokenWordIndex)||Number(span.dataset.chapterWordIndex)||resolveSpokenWordPosition(word,Number(span.dataset.wordIndex)+1);
 const tokPos=Number(span.dataset.tokenPosition)||pos;
 const spokenCount=getVerseSpokenWordCount(targetVerse);
 let timing=null;
 try{
  timing=await Promise.race([
   getVerseAudioTiming(currentChapter,targetVerse,reciter,3000),
   new Promise(r=>setTimeout(()=>r(null),3000))
  ]);
 }catch(_){}
 const segment=timing?.segments?.length?findWordSegment({segments:timing.segments},pos,spokenCount,tokPos):null;
 const a=$('audioPlayer'),range=resumeContext?.range?{...resumeContext.range}:null;
 const candidateUrls=[];
 if(timing?.audioUrl)candidateUrls.push(timing.audioUrl);
 candidateUrls.push(...getReciterVerseAudioFallbackUrls(currentChapter,targetVerse,reciter));
 const uniqueUrls=[...new Set(candidateUrls.filter(Boolean))];
 const currentSrcClean=a.src?a.src.split('?')[0]:'';
 const padded=String(currentChapter).padStart(3,'0')+String(targetVerse).padStart(3,'0');
 const isSameVerseLoaded=Boolean(currentSrcClean&&(uniqueUrls.some(u=>u.split('?')[0]===currentSrcClean)||currentSrcClean.endsWith('/'+padded+'.mp3'))&&a.readyState>=1);
 const token=++audioRequestToken;
 chapterAudioSession=null;
 a.onended=null;a.loop=range?false:Boolean(resumeContext?.repeatSingle??audioRepeat);
 a.dataset.mode=range?'range':'verse';
 try{
  if(!isSameVerseLoaded){
   let loadedOk=false;
   for(const u of uniqueUrls){
    if(token!==audioRequestToken)return true;
    try{
     a.src=u;a.load();
     await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      let done=false;
      const fin=(fn,v)=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearTimeout(timer);a.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata',ok);a.removeEventListener('canplay',ok);a.removeEventListener('error',bad);fn(v);};
      const ok=()=>fin(resolve,true),bad=()=>fin(reject,new Error('Audio metadata unavailable'));
      const timer=setTimeout(()=>fin(reject,new Error('Audio metadata timeout')),6500);
      a.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',ok,{once:true});
      a.addEventListener('canplay',ok,{once:true});
      a.addEventListener('error',bad,{once:true});
      if(a.readyState>=1)ok();
     });
     loadedOk=true;break;
    }catch(_){}
   }
   if(!loadedOk)return false;
  }
  if(token!==audioRequestToken)return true;
  let targetSec=0;
  if(segment&&Number.isFinite(Number(segment[1]))&&Number(segment[1])>=0){
   targetSec=Math.max(0,Number(segment[1])/1000);
  }else{
   targetSec=estimateWordProportionalSeekSeconds(targetVerse,pos,a.duration);
  }
  try{a.currentTime=targetSec;}catch(_){}
  rangePlayback=range?{...range}:null;
  const normSegs=timing?.segments?.length?normalizeTimingSegments(timing.segments,spokenCount):[];
  verseWordSeekSession={chapter:Number(currentChapter),verse:Number(targetVerse),segments:normSegs,position:pos,targetSec};
  reciterActive=true;audioBusy=true;syncPlayVerseButton(true);setRecitingVerseHighlight(targetVerse);
  setSpeakingWord(targetVerse,pos);scrollVerseRowIntoView(targetVerse,true);
  if(a.paused)await a.play();
  startAudioSyncRaf();
  return true;
 }catch(_){
  if(token===audioRequestToken){verseWordSeekSession=null;audioBusy=false;syncPlayVerseButton(false);}
  return false;
 }
}
async function handleVerseWordSeekTime(){
 const session=verseWordSeekSession,a=$('audioPlayer');if(!session||!['verse','range'].includes(a.dataset.mode))return;
 if(!Array.isArray(session.segments)||!session.segments.length)return;
 const ms=a.currentTime*1000;
 const seg=session.segments.find(x=>ms>=Number(x.startMs)&&ms<Number(x.endMs))
  ||session.segments.find(x=>ms>=Number(x.startMs)-35&&ms<=Number(x.endMs)+35);
 if(seg){
  const pos=Number(seg.wordPos);
  if(session.preferWbwPos===undefined){
   const maxSegPos=Math.max(...session.segments.map(s=>Number(s.wordPos)||0));
   const spokenCount=getVerseSpokenWordCount(session.verse);
   session.preferWbwPos=(spokenCount>0&&maxSegPos>spokenCount);
  }
  const targetPos=session.preferWbwPos?(Number(seg.rawPos)||pos):pos;
  if(session.lastHighlightedPos!==targetPos){
   session.lastHighlightedPos=targetPos;
   setSpeakingWord(session.verse,targetPos,Boolean(session.preferWbwPos));
  }
 }
}
$('audioPlayer').addEventListener('timeupdate',handleVerseWordSeekTime);
async function playFromWordInContinuousRecitation(span,word,targetVerse,resumeContext){
 return playVerseWordSegment(span,word,targetVerse,resumeContext);
}
async function playWord(span,word,targetVerse=currentVerse){
 const a=$('audioPlayer');
 const target=Number(targetVerse)||currentVerse;
 const pos=Number(span.dataset.spokenWordIndex)||1;
 const wasReciterActive=Boolean(reciterActive||chapterAudioSession||verseWordSeekSession||rangePlayback||(['verse','range','chapter-seek'].includes(a?.dataset?.mode||'')&&(audioBusy||!a.paused)));
 const selectedEnd=getSelectedRangeEndForVerse(target);
 const activeRange=rangePlayback?{...rangePlayback,from:target,to:Math.max(target,Number(rangePlayback.to)||selectedEnd)}:{chapter:currentChapter,from:target,to:Math.max(target,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
 const resumeContext={wasReciterActive,range:activeRange,repeatSingle:audioRepeat};
 if(target!==currentVerse){
  if(state.verseDisplayMode!=='single'&&!$('allVersesCard')?.hidden){
   currentVerse=target;currentIndex=Math.max(0,verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===target));state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=target;
   $('allVersesCard').querySelectorAll('.all-verse-row').forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('current-row',Number(row.dataset.verse)===target));
   renderVerseNav();saveState();
  }else{
   currentVerse=target;currentIndex=Math.max(0,verses.findIndex(v=>verseNumber(v)===target));state.lastChapter=currentChapter;state.lastVerse=target;renderCurrentVerse();saveState();
   const fresh=$('verseText')?.querySelector('.word[data-spoken-word-index="'+pos+'"]')||$('verseText')?.querySelector('.word[data-chapter-word-index="'+pos+'"]');
   if(fresh)span=fresh;
  }
 }
 if(wasReciterActive){
  reciterActive=true;
  const started=await playVerseWordSegment(span,word,target,resumeContext);
  if(started)return;
 }
 rangePlayback=null;
 reciterActive=false;
 await playStandaloneWord(span,word,target);
}
async function startRecording(){if(!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia||!window.MediaRecorder){showToast('التسجيل يتطلب متصفحًا حديثًا واتصالًا آمنًا (HTTPS أو localhost).');return;}try{const stream=await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true});recordingChunks=[];recorder=new MediaRecorder(stream);recorder.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data.size)recordingChunks.push(e.data);};recorder.onstop=()=>{recordingBlob=new Blob(recordingChunks,{type:recorder.mimeType||'audio/webm'});if(recordingUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(recordingUrl);recordingUrl=URL.createObjectURL(recordingBlob);$('recordedPlayer').src=recordingUrl;$('listenRecording').disabled=false;writeLocalRecord('student-recording',{blob:recordingBlob,type:recordingBlob.type||'audio/webm',savedAt:Date.now()}).then(()=>{if(typeof scheduleDeviceBackupSave==='function')scheduleDeviceBackupSave();}).catch(()=>{});stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());$('recordIndicator').classList.remove('show');$('recordButton').classList.remove('active');$('recordButton').innerHTML='<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><rect x="9" y="2" width="6" height="12" rx="3"/><path d="M5 10v2a7 7 0 0 0 14 0v-2M12 19v3m-4 0h8"/></svg>'; $('recordButton').title='تسجيل صوت الطالب';$('recordButton').setAttribute('aria-label','تسجيل صوت الطالب');showToast('تم حفظ التسجيل محليًا؛ سيبقى متاحًا بعد إغلاق الصفحة في هذا المتصفح.');};recorder.start();$('recordIndicator').classList.add('show');$('recordButton').classList.add('active');$('recordButton').innerHTML='<svg width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><rect x="6" y="6" width="12" height="12" rx="2"/></svg>'; $('recordButton').title='إيقاف التسجيل';$('recordButton').setAttribute('aria-label','إيقاف التسجيل');}catch(e){showToast('لم يتم منح إذن الميكروفون. اسمح بالوصول للميكروفون ثم حاول مجددًا.');}}
$('recordButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(recorder&&recorder.state==='recording'){recorder.stop();}else startRecording();});$('listenRecording').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(recordingUrl)$('recordedPlayer').play().catch(()=>showToast('تعذر تشغيل التسجيل.'));});
let activeModalCancelHandler = null;
function cancelQuranDisplayNonDestructive(){
 if(typeof setVersesTrayHidden==='function')setVersesTrayHidden(true);
 stopAudio();
 saveState();
 if(typeof renderScreenTray==='function')renderScreenTray();
 showToast('تم إخفاء عرض القرآن مع الاحتفاظ بجميع اختيارات الدرس والصور والرسومات.');
}
function mountRangeModalTopActions(modal){
 if(!modal)return;
 modal.querySelector('.range-modal-top-actions')?.remove();
 const closeX=modal.querySelector('#modalCloseX');
 if(closeX)closeX.hidden=true;
 const group=document.createElement('div');group.className='range-modal-top-actions';group.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="control-btn primary range-top-save">عرض</button>';
 const titleEl=modal.querySelector('#modalTitle');
 if(titleEl){titleEl.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin',group);}else{modal.prepend(group);}
 group.querySelector('.range-top-save').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('editForm').requestSubmit();});
}
function openModal(title,description,body,onSubmit,onCancel=null){activeModal=onSubmit;activeModalCancelHandler=onCancel;$('editForm').closest('.modal').querySelector('.range-modal-top-actions')?.remove();$('editForm').closest('.modal').classList.remove('range-modal','mushaf-modal');$('editForm').querySelector('[type=submit]').textContent='حفظ التعديل';$('cancelModal').textContent='إلغاء';$('editForm').querySelector('.modal-actions').style.display='flex';$('modalTitle').textContent=title;$('modalDescription').textContent=description;$('modalBody').innerHTML=body;$('editModal').classList.add('show');const field=$('modalBody').querySelector('input,textarea,select');if(field)setTimeout(()=>field.focus(),30);}
function closeModal(fromCancel=false){$('editModal').classList.remove('show');const cancelFn=activeModalCancelHandler;activeModal=null;activeModalCancelHandler=null;activeTafsirKey=null;if(fromCancel&&typeof cancelFn==='function'){try{cancelFn();}catch(_){}}}
$('cancelModal').addEventListener('click',()=>{if($('editForm').closest('.modal').classList.contains('range-modal'))cancelQuranDisplayNonDestructive();closeModal(true);});$('modalCloseX').addEventListener('click',()=>closeModal(true));$('editModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===$('editModal')){if(!$('editForm').closest('.modal').classList.contains('range-modal'))closeModal(true);}});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){if(!$('editForm').closest('.modal').classList.contains('range-modal'))closeModal(true);}});
$('editForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(activeModal){const fn=activeModal;const ok=fn(new FormData(e.target));if(ok!==false)closeModal();}});
async function fetchTafsirForKey(key){let ids=[];try{const resources=await fetchJson('https://api.quran.com/api/v4/resources/tafsirs?language=ar',6500);const list=resources.tafsirs||resources.resources||[];const selected=list.find(x=>/الميسّر|الميسر|muyassar/i.test((x.name||'')+' '+(x.name_ar||'')+' '+(x.slug||'')));if(selected?.id)ids.push(selected.id);}catch(_){}ids.push(16);const candidates=[...ids.map(id=>`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/tafsirs/${id}/by_ayah/${key}`),`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/verses/by_key/${key}?tafsirs=${ids[0]||16}`];for(const url of candidates){try{const d=await fetchJson(url,6500);const t=d.tafsir?.text||d.tafsir?.text_html||d.verse?.tafsirs?.[0]?.text||d.verse?.tafsirs?.[0]?.text_html||d.verses?.[0]?.tafsirs?.[0]?.text;if(t&&typeof t==='string')return t.replace(/<[^>]*>/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}catch(_){}}return '';}
function openTafsir(){const key=currentChapter+':'+currentVerse;activeTafsirKey=key;const defaultTafsir=state.customTafsirs[key]??state.tafsir[key]??'';openModal('معنى الآية وتفسيرها',`السورة: ${surahNames[currentChapter-1]} — الآية: ${arabicNum(currentVerse)}. يمكنك تعديل النص وحفظه على هذا الجهاز.`,`<label for="tafsirValue">التفسير أو المعنى</label><textarea id="tafsirValue" name="value" maxlength="5000" placeholder="اكتب معنى الآية هنا…">${safeText(defaultTafsir)}</textarea><div class="small-note">التفسير الذي تعدّله يُحفظ محليًا ولا يغيّر مصدر التفسير الأصلي.</div>`,fd=>{state.customTafsirs[key]=String(fd.get('value')||'').trim();saveState();refreshTafsirPanel();showToast('تم حفظ معنى الآية على هذا الجهاز.');});if(!defaultTafsir){const area=$('tafsirValue');area.placeholder='جارٍ محاولة جلب التفسير الميسر من Quran.com…';fetchTafsirForKey(key).then(t=>{if(t&&$('editModal').classList.contains('show')&&activeTafsirKey===key&&$('modalTitle').textContent==='معنى الآية وتفسيرها'&&$('tafsirValue')&&!$('tafsirValue').value){$('tafsirValue').value=t;state.tafsir[key]=t;saveState();}else if(!t&&$('tafsirValue')&&!$('tafsirValue').value)$('tafsirValue').placeholder='لم يتوفر التفسير تلقائيًا. اكتب المعنى الذي تريد حفظه.';});}}
function refreshTafsirPanel(){const panel=$('tafsirPanel');if(!panel)return;const key=currentChapter+':'+currentVerse;const value=state.customTafsirs[key]??state.tafsir[key]??'';$('tafsirPanelText').textContent=value||'لا يتوفر معنى محفوظ لهذه الآية بعد.';}
async function toggleTafsirPanel(){const panel=$('tafsirPanel'),button=$('editTafsirButton');const showing=panel.classList.contains('show');if(showing){panel.classList.remove('show');panel.hidden=true;button.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');return;}refreshTafsirPanel();panel.hidden=false;panel.classList.add('show');button.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');const key=currentChapter+':'+currentVerse;const hasText=!!(state.customTafsirs[key]??state.tafsir[key]);if(!hasText){$('tafsirPanelText').textContent='جارٍ محاولة جلب التفسير الميسر…';const fetched=await fetchTafsirForKey(key);if(fetched&&currentChapter+':'+currentVerse===key){state.tafsir[key]=fetched;saveState();refreshTafsirPanel();}else if(!fetched&&currentChapter+':'+currentVerse===key)$('tafsirPanelText').textContent='لم يتوفر التفسير تلقائيًا. يمكنك إضافة المعنى أو تعديله من علامة القلم الصغيرة.';}}
$('editTafsirButton').addEventListener('click',toggleTafsirPanel);$('editTafsirInline').addEventListener('click',openTafsir);
function normalizeDigitsToAscii(str){
 return String(str||'').replace(/[٠-٩]/g,d=>String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d))).replace(/[۰-۹]/g,d=>String('۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'.indexOf(d)));
}
function normalizeArabicSearch(value){
 return normalizeDigitsToAscii(value)
  .normalize('NFC')
  .replace(/([^اآأإٱ])ٰ/gu,'$1ا')
  .replace(/[ً-ٰٟۖ-ࣰۭ-ࣿـ]/gu,'')
  .replace(/[أإآٱٲٳٵ]/gu,'ا')
  .replace(/ة/gu,'ه')
  .replace(/[ىئېی]/gu,'ي')
  .replace(/ؤ/gu,'و')
  .replace(/[۝۞۝۞]/gu,' ')
  .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
  .toLowerCase()
  .trim();
}
function normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(value){
 return normalizeDigitsToAscii(value)
  .normalize('NFC')
  .replace(/[ً-ٰٟۖ-ࣰۭ-ࣿـ]/gu,'')
  .replace(/[أإآٱٲٳٵ]/gu,'ا')
  .replace(/ة/gu,'ه')
  .replace(/[ىئېی]/gu,'ي')
  .replace(/ؤ/gu,'و')
  .replace(/[۝۞۝۞]/gu,' ')
  .replace(/\s+/g,' ')
  .toLowerCase()
  .trim();
}
