let fullQuranCorpusCache = null, fullQuranCorpusPromise = null;
async function ensureFullQuranCorpus(){
 if(fullQuranCorpusCache&&fullQuranCorpusCache.length>6000)return fullQuranCorpusCache;
 if(fullQuranCorpusPromise)return fullQuranCorpusPromise;
 fullQuranCorpusPromise=(async()=>{
  try{
   const saved=await readLocalRecord('full-quran-corpus-v2');
   if(Array.isArray(saved)&&saved.length>6000){
    fullQuranCorpusCache=saved;
    return saved;
   }
  }catch(_){}
  try{
   const [imlaeiRes,uthmaniRes]=await Promise.all([
    fetchJson('https://api.quran.com/api/v4/quran/verses/imlaei',12000).catch(()=>null),
    fetchJson('https://api.quran.com/api/v4/quran/verses/uthmani',12000).catch(()=>null)
   ]);
   const imlaeiList=imlaeiRes?.verses||[];
   const uthmaniList=uthmaniRes?.verses||[];
   if(imlaeiList.length>6000||uthmaniList.length>6000){
    const uthMap=new Map();
    for(const u of uthmaniList){if(u?.verse_key)uthMap.set(u.verse_key,u.text_uthmani||'');}
    const baseList=imlaeiList.length>=uthmaniList.length?imlaeiList:uthmaniList;
    const compiled=[];
    for(const item of baseList){
     const key=String(item?.verse_key||'');
     const parts=key.split(':');
     const ch=Number(parts[0]),v=Number(parts[1]);
     if(!ch||!v)continue;
     const imlaeiTxt=String(item.text_imlaei||item.text_uthmani||'').trim();
     const uthTxt=String(uthMap.get(key)||item.text_uthmani||imlaeiTxt).trim();
     compiled.push({
      c:ch,
      v:v,
      t:uthTxt||imlaeiTxt,
      n1:normalizeArabicSearch(imlaeiTxt),
      n2:normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(uthTxt)
     });
    }
    if(compiled.length>6000){
     fullQuranCorpusCache=compiled;
     writeLocalRecord('full-quran-corpus-v2',compiled).catch(()=>{});
     return compiled;
    }
   }
  }catch(_){}
  fullQuranCorpusPromise=null;
  return null;
 })();
 return fullQuranCorpusPromise;
}
setTimeout(()=>{ensureFullQuranCorpus().catch(()=>{});},1200);

function parseSurahAndVerseQuery(rawQuery){
 const ascii=normalizeDigitsToAscii(rawQuery).trim();
 if(!ascii)return null;
 // 1) Numeric pattern like "55:42" or "55:1-10"
 const numMatch=ascii.match(/^\s*(\d{1,3})\s*[:\/،,\-]\s*(\d{1,3})(?:\s*[-–إلىالىto]+\s*(\d{1,3}))?\s*$/i);
 if(numMatch){
  const ch=Number(numMatch[1]),v1=Number(numMatch[2]),v2=numMatch[3]?Number(numMatch[3]):v1;
  if(ch>=1&&ch<=114){
   const max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;
   const from=Math.min(max,Math.max(1,v1));
   const to=Math.min(max,Math.max(from,v2));
   return {chapter:ch,from,to,explicitVerse:true};
  }
 }
 // 2) Surah name + verse number (e.g. "سورة الرحمن 42", "الرحمن 42", "سورة المائدة 43", "المائدة آية 43", "البقرة 1-5")
 const numberTail=ascii.match(/^(.*?)\s*(?:[:\-،,]|(?:آية|الآية|ايه|الايه|آيه|الاية|من\s*الآية|من\s*ايه|من))?\s*(\d{1,3})(?:\s*(?:[-–]|إلى|الى|حتي|حتى)\s*(\d{1,3}))?\s*$/);
 if(numberTail){
  const namePart=numberTail[1].replace(/^\s*(?:سورة|سوره)\s+/,'').trim();
  const normName=normalizeArabicSearch(namePart).replace(/\s+/g,'');
  const normNoDag=normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(namePart).replace(/\s+/g,'');
  if(normName){
   let matchedCh=0;
   for(let i=0;i<surahNames.length;i++){
    const sNorm1=normalizeArabicSearch(surahNames[i]).replace(/\s+/g,'');
    const sNorm2=normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(surahNames[i]).replace(/\s+/g,'');
    if(sNorm1===normName||sNorm2===normNoDag||sNorm1===normNoDag||sNorm2===normName){
     matchedCh=i+1;break;
    }
   }
   if(!matchedCh){
    for(let i=0;i<surahNames.length;i++){
     const sNorm1=normalizeArabicSearch(surahNames[i]).replace(/\s+/g,'');
     if(normName.length>=2&&(sNorm1.startsWith(normName)||normName.startsWith(sNorm1))){
      matchedCh=i+1;break;
     }
    }
   }
   if(matchedCh>=1&&matchedCh<=114){
    const max=verseCounts[matchedCh-1]||1;
    const v1=Number(numberTail[2])||1;
    const v2=numberTail[3]?Number(numberTail[3]):v1;
    if(v1>=1&&v1<=max){
     const from=Math.min(max,Math.max(1,v1));
     const to=Math.min(max,Math.max(from,v2));
     return {chapter:matchedCh,from,to,explicitVerse:true};
    }
   }
  }
 }
 // 3) Exact Surah name only (e.g. "سورة الرحمن" or "المائدة")
 const cleanOnly=ascii.replace(/^\s*(?:سورة|سوره)\s+/,'').trim();
 const normOnly=normalizeArabicSearch(cleanOnly).replace(/\s+/g,'');
 if(normOnly){
  for(let i=0;i<surahNames.length;i++){
   const sNorm1=normalizeArabicSearch(surahNames[i]).replace(/\s+/g,'');
   const sNorm2=normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(surahNames[i]).replace(/\s+/g,'');
   if(sNorm1===normOnly||sNorm2===normOnly){
    return {chapter:i+1,from:1,to:verseCounts[i]||1,explicitVerse:false};
   }
  }
 }
 return null;
}

function syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs(){
 state.lessonSetup=state.lessonSetup||{chapter:108,surahName:'الكوثر',from:1,to:3,studentAge:'',designType:DEFAULT_DESIGN_TYPE,reciter:6};
 const rawSurah=String($('surahSearch')?.value??state.lessonSetup.surahName??'').trim();
 state.lessonSetup.surahName=rawSurah;
 const cleaned=rawSurah.replace(/^سورة\s*/,'').replace(/\s+/g,'');
 const idx=surahNames.findIndex(n=>n.replace(/\s+/g,'')===cleaned||normalizeArabicSearch(n).replace(/\s+/g,'')===normalizeArabicSearch(cleaned).replace(/\s+/g,''));
 if(idx>=0){
  state.lessonSetup.chapter=idx+1;
  if($('chapterChoice'))$('chapterChoice').value=String(idx+1);
 }else if($('chapterChoice')?.value){
  const chVal=Number($('chapterChoice').value);
  if(chVal>=1&&chVal<=114)state.lessonSetup.chapter=chVal;
 }
 const maxV=verseCounts[(Number(state.lessonSetup.chapter)||108)-1]||286;
 const fromVal=Number($('fromVerse')?.value);
 const toVal=Number($('toVerse')?.value);
 if(Number.isFinite(fromVal)&&fromVal>=1)state.lessonSetup.from=Math.min(maxV,Math.max(1,Math.trunc(fromVal)));
 if(Number.isFinite(toVal)&&toVal>=1)state.lessonSetup.to=Math.min(maxV,Math.max(state.lessonSetup.from||1,Math.trunc(toVal)));
 if($('studentAgeChoice')){
  state.lessonSetup.studentAge=String($('studentAgeChoice').value||'').trim();
  state.studentAge=state.lessonSetup.studentAge;
 }
 if($('designTypeChoice')){
  const dt=String($('designTypeChoice').value||'').trim();
  if(DESIGN_TYPE_OPTIONS.includes(dt)){
   state.lessonSetup.designType=dt;
   state.designType=dt;
  }
 }
 if($('rangeReciterSelect')){
  const rec=Number($('rangeReciterSelect').value)||6;
  state.lessonSetup.reciter=rec;
  state.reciter=rec;
 }
 saveState();
}
function chooseSearchResult(ch,verse,endVerseOverride=null){
 const max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;
 const fromV=Math.min(max,Math.max(1,Number(verse)||1));
 const toV=endVerseOverride!==null?Math.min(max,Math.max(fromV,Number(endVerseOverride)||fromV)):fromV;
 if($('surahSearch'))$('surahSearch').value=surahNames[ch-1]||'';
 if($('chapterChoice'))$('chapterChoice').value=String(ch);
 if($('fromVerse')){$('fromVerse').max=String(max);$('fromVerse').value=String(fromV);}
 if($('toVerse')){$('toVerse').max=String(max);$('toVerse').value=String(toV);}
 if($('quranSearchResults'))$('quranSearchResults').innerHTML='';
 if($('surahSearchResults')){$('surahSearchResults').innerHTML='';$('surahSearchResults').classList.remove('show');}
 if($('surahSearch'))$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
 syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
 chosenRange={chapter:ch,from:fromV,to:toV};state.chosenRange=chosenRange;state.lastChapter=ch;state.lastVerse=fromV;
 saveState();
 loadChapter(ch,fromV);
 if(fromV===toV){
  showToast('تم اختيار وعرض سورة '+surahNames[ch-1]+' — الآية '+arabicNum(fromV)+'.');
 }else{
  showToast('تم اختيار وعرض سورة '+surahNames[ch-1]+' من الآية '+arabicNum(fromV)+' إلى '+arabicNum(toV)+'.');
 }
}
function paintQuranSearchResults(results,query){
 const host=$('quranSearchResults');if(!host)return;host.innerHTML='';
 if(!results.length){
  const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='quran-search-empty';empty.textContent='لا توجد نتائج مطابقة حتى الآن.';host.appendChild(empty);return;
 }
 results.slice(0,25).forEach(r=>{
  const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='quran-search-result';
  const title=document.createElement('strong');
  const toV=r.toVerse&&r.toVerse!==r.verse?r.toVerse:null;
  title.textContent='سورة '+(surahNames[r.chapter-1]||'')+' — '+(toV?('من الآية '+arabicNum(r.verse)+' إلى '+arabicNum(toV)):('الآية '+arabicNum(r.verse)));
  const snippet=document.createElement('span');
  snippet.textContent=String(r.text||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'').slice(0,220);
  btn.append(title,snippet);
  btn.addEventListener('click',()=>chooseSearchResult(r.chapter,r.verse,r.toVerse||r.verse));
  host.appendChild(btn);
 });
}
async function fetchSingleVerseTextForSearch(ch,vNo){
 if(fullQuranCorpusCache){
  const found=fullQuranCorpusCache.find(x=>x.c===ch&&x.v===vNo);
  if(found?.t)return found.t;
 }
 const localHit=LOCAL_QURAN_SEARCH_INDEX.find(x=>x.chapter===ch&&x.verse===vNo);
 if(localHit?.text)return localHit.text;
 if(currentChapter===ch&&Array.isArray(fullChapterVerses)){
  const cv=fullChapterVerses.find(x=>verseNumber(x)===vNo);
  if(cv)return stripQuranEndMarkers(cv.text_uthmani||cv.text_qpc_hafs||plainArabicFromMarkup(cv.text_uthmani_tajweed||''));
 }
 try{
  const data=await fetchJson(`https://api.quran.com/api/v4/verses/by_key/${ch}%3A${vNo}?fields=text_uthmani,text_imlaei,verse_key`,4500);
  const v=data?.verse||data?.verses?.[0]||data;
  const txt=stripQuranEndMarkers(v?.text_uthmani||v?.text_imlaei||'');
  if(txt)return txt;
 }catch(_){}
 try{
  const data=await fetchJson(`https://api.alquran.cloud/v1/ayah/${ch}:${vNo}/quran-uthmani`,4500);
  const txt=stripQuranEndMarkers(data?.data?.text||'');
  if(txt)return txt;
 }catch(_){}
 return `سورة ${surahNames[ch-1]||''} — الآية ${arabicNum(vNo)}`;
}
async function searchQuranVerses(query){
 const token=++verseSearchToken,host=$('quranSearchResults');if(!host)return;
 const raw=String(query||'').trim();
 const norm1=normalizeArabicSearch(raw);
 const norm2=normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(raw);
 if(!norm1){host.innerHTML='';return;}
 host.innerHTML='';
 const loading=document.createElement('div');loading.className='quran-search-empty';loading.textContent='جارٍ البحث الشامل في آيات القرآن الكريم…';host.appendChild(loading);
 const results=[];
 const seen=new Set();
 const addHit=(ch,vNo,txt,toVerse=null)=>{
  const c=Number(ch),v=Number(vNo);
  if(!c||c<1||c>114||!v||v<1||v>(verseCounts[c-1]||286))return;
  const id=`${c}:${v}:${toVerse||v}`;
  if(seen.has(id))return;
  seen.add(id);
  results.push({chapter:c,verse:v,toVerse:toVerse||v,text:stripQuranEndMarkers(txt||'')});
 };

 // 1) فحص ما إذا كان المستخدم كتب اسم سورة ورقم آية مثل: "سورة الرحمن 42" أو "المائدة 43"
 const parsedRef=parseSurahAndVerseQuery(raw);
 if(parsedRef){
  const vText=await fetchSingleVerseTextForSearch(parsedRef.chapter,parsedRef.from);
  if(token!==verseSearchToken)return;
  addHit(parsedRef.chapter,parsedRef.from,vText,parsedRef.explicitVerse?parsedRef.to:(verseCounts[parsedRef.chapter-1]||1));
  paintQuranSearchResults(results,raw);
  if(parsedRef.explicitVerse)return;
 }

 // 2) البحث في الفهرس المحلي والآيات المحملة حاليًا
 const matchText=(txt)=>{
  if(!txt)return false;
  const a=normalizeArabicSearch(txt),b=normalizeArabicSearchNoDagger(txt);
  return a.includes(norm1)||b.includes(norm2)||a.includes(norm2)||b.includes(norm1);
 };
 const localSource=Array.isArray(fullChapterVerses)&&fullChapterVerses.length?fullChapterVerses:(Array.isArray(verses)?verses:[]);
 for(const v of localSource){
  const text=String(v.text_qpc_hafs||v.text_uthmani||v.text_uthmani_tajweed||v.text||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,' ').trim();
  const bits=String(v.verse_key||`${currentChapter}:${verseNumber(v)}`).split(':');
  if(matchText(text))addHit(Number(bits[0])||currentChapter,Number(bits[1])||verseNumber(v),text);
 }
 for(const item of LOCAL_QURAN_SEARCH_INDEX){
  if(matchText(item.text))addHit(item.chapter,item.verse,item.text);
 }
 if(results.length)paintQuranSearchResults(results,raw);

 // 3) البحث في المصحف الكامل (6236 آية) + واجهات البحث المباشرة بالتوازي
 const parseGlobalResults=(data)=>{
  const candidates=data?.result?.verses||data?.result?.results||data?.verses||data?.search?.results||data?.results||[];
  const out=[];
  for(const r of candidates){
   let keyValue=r?.verse_key||r?.verseKey||r?.key||r?.verse_key_value||'';
   if(keyValue&&typeof keyValue==='object')keyValue=keyValue.verse_key||keyValue.key||keyValue.value||'';
   if(!keyValue&&r?.chapter_number&&r?.verse_number)keyValue=String(r.chapter_number)+':'+String(r.verse_number);
   const parts=String(keyValue).match(/(\d{1,3})\s*:\s*(\d{1,3})/);if(!parts)continue;
   const chapter=Number(parts[1]),verse=Number(parts[2]);
   let text=String(r?.text||r?.text_uthmani||r?.name||r?.arabic||r?.highlight||r?.highlighted||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,' ').trim();
   if(!text&&Array.isArray(r?.words)){
    text=r.words.map(w=>String(w?.text||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'')).join(' ').trim();
   }
   out.push({chapter,verse,text});
  }
  return out;
 };

 const corpusTask=ensureFullQuranCorpus().then(corpus=>{
  if(!Array.isArray(corpus)||!corpus.length||token!==verseSearchToken)return;
  for(const item of corpus){
   if(item.n1.includes(norm1)||item.n2.includes(norm2)||item.n1.includes(norm2)||item.n2.includes(norm1)){
    addHit(item.c,item.v,item.t);
    if(results.length>=30)break;
   }
  }
  if(results.length&&token===verseSearchToken)paintQuranSearchResults(results,raw);
 }).catch(()=>{});

 const apiTask=(async()=>{
  const endpoints=[
   'https://api.quran.com/api/v4/search?q='+encodeURIComponent(raw)+'&size=25&language=ar',
   'https://api.alquran.cloud/v1/search/'+encodeURIComponent(raw)+'/all/quran-simple-clean',
   'https://api.alquran.cloud/v1/search/'+encodeURIComponent(norm1)+'/all/quran-simple-clean'
  ];
  for(const url of endpoints){
   if(results.length>=25||token!==verseSearchToken)break;
   try{
    const data=await fetchJson(url,4500);
    if(token!==verseSearchToken)return;
    let rows=parseGlobalResults(data);
    if(!rows.length&&Array.isArray(data?.data?.matches)){
     rows=data.data.matches.map(m=>({chapter:Number(m?.surah?.number)||0,verse:Number(m?.numberInSurah)||0,text:String(m?.text||'').trim()})).filter(m=>m.chapter>=1&&m.chapter<=114&&m.verse>=1);
    }
    for(const r of rows)addHit(r.chapter,r.verse,r.text);
    if(results.length&&token===verseSearchToken)paintQuranSearchResults(results,raw);
    if(rows.length)break;
   }catch(_){}
  }
 })();

 await Promise.allSettled([corpusTask,apiTask]);
 if(token!==verseSearchToken)return;
 paintQuranSearchResults(results,raw);
 if(!results.length){
  host.innerHTML='';
  const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='quran-search-empty';
  empty.textContent='لم يعثر البحث على نتائج مطابقة. جرّب كتابة كلمة قرآنية (مثل: كل الطعام) أو اسم السورة ورقم الآية (مثل: سورة الرحمن 42).';
  host.appendChild(empty);
 }
}
async function copyTextToClipboard(text, successMessage){
 if(!text||!String(text).trim()){showToast('تعذّر إنشاء نص البرومبت؛ تحقق من بيانات السورة والآيات.');return false;}
 let copied=false;
 try{
  if(navigator.clipboard&&typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText==='function'){
   await navigator.clipboard.writeText(String(text));
   copied=true;
  }
 }catch(_){copied=false;}
 if(!copied){
  try{
   const ta=document.createElement('textarea');
   ta.value=String(text);
   ta.setAttribute('readonly','');
   ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.top='-9999px';ta.style.opacity='0';
   document.body.appendChild(ta);
   ta.focus();ta.select();ta.setSelectionRange(0,ta.value.length);
   copied=Boolean(document.execCommand('copy'));
   ta.remove();
  }catch(_){copied=false;}
 }
 if(copied){
  showToast(successMessage||'تم نسخ البرومبت بنجاح.');
  return true;
 }
 showToast('تعذّر النسخ التلقائي إلى الحافظة في هذا المتصفح؛ تأكد من منح صلاحية الحافظة.');
 return false;
}
function openRangeModal(){
 state.lessonSetup=state.lessonSetup||{chapter:currentChapter||108,surahName:surahNames[(currentChapter||108)-1]||'الكوثر',from:1,to:verseCounts[(currentChapter||108)-1]||3,studentAge:state.studentAge||'',designType:state.designType||DEFAULT_DESIGN_TYPE,reciter:Number(state.reciter)||6};
 const savedSetup=state.lessonSetup;
 const savedCh=(Number(savedSetup.chapter)>=1&&Number(savedSetup.chapter)<=114)?Number(savedSetup.chapter):(currentChapter||108);
 const maxSaved=verseCounts[savedCh-1]||1;
 const savedSurahText=savedSetup.surahName!==undefined&&savedSetup.surahName!==null?String(savedSetup.surahName):(surahNames[savedCh-1]||'');
 const defaultFrom=Math.min(maxSaved,Math.max(1,Number(savedSetup.from)||1));
 const defaultTo=Math.min(maxSaved,Math.max(defaultFrom,Number(savedSetup.to)||maxSaved));
 const savedAge=String(savedSetup.studentAge??state.studentAge??'').trim();
 const savedDesign=DESIGN_TYPE_OPTIONS.includes(savedSetup.designType)?savedSetup.designType:(DESIGN_TYPE_OPTIONS.includes(state.designType)?state.designType:DEFAULT_DESIGN_TYPE);
 const savedReciter=Number(savedSetup.reciter||state.reciter||6);
 const resolveSurahInput=()=>{
  const raw=String($('surahSearch')?.value||'').trim().replace(/^سورة\s*/,'').replace(/\s+/g,'');
  const idx=surahNames.findIndex(n=>n.replace(/\s+/g,'')===raw||normalizeArabicSearch(n)===normalizeArabicSearch(raw));
  if(idx>=0){$('chapterChoice').value=String(idx+1);return idx+1;}
  const chVal=Number($('chapterChoice')?.value);
  if(raw&&chVal>=1&&chVal<=114)return chVal;
  return 0;
 };
 const options=surahNames.map((n,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}" ${i+1===savedCh?'selected':''}>${arabicNum(i+1)}. سورة ${n}</option>`).join('');
 const designOptionsHtml=DESIGN_TYPE_OPTIONS.map(opt=>`<option value="${safeText(opt)}" ${opt===savedDesign?'selected':''}>${safeText(opt)}</option>`).join('');
 openModal('اختيار السورة ونطاق الآيات','اختر السورة ونطاق الآيات وعمر الطالب. تُحفظ اختياراتك تلقائيًا فور تعديلها.',
 `<div class="chapter-grid range-top-three-cols">
  <div class="field-group surah-compact-field"><label for="surahSearch">السورة</label><div class="surah-input-wrap"><input id="surahSearch" type="text" placeholder="اسم السورة…" autocomplete="off" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="surahSearchResults" value="${safeText(savedSurahText)}"><button type="button" id="clearSurahSearch" title="مسح اسم السورة" aria-label="مسح اسم السورة">×</button></div><div id="surahSearchResults" class="surah-search-results" role="listbox" aria-label="السور المطابقة"></div><select id="chapterChoice" name="chapter" aria-label="السورة" style="display:none!important">${options}</select></div>
  <div class="field-group"><label for="fromVerse">من الآية</label><input type="number" id="fromVerse" name="from" min="1" max="${maxSaved}" value="${defaultFrom}" required></div>
  <div class="field-group"><label for="toVerse">إلى الآية</label><input type="number" id="toVerse" name="to" min="1" max="${maxSaved}" value="${defaultTo}" required></div>
 </div>
 <div class="field-group quran-search-group"><label for="quranVerseSearch">بحث في جميع آيات القرآن الكريم</label><input id="quranVerseSearch" type="search" placeholder="اكتب كلمة أو آية (مثل: كل الطعام) أو اسم سورة ورقم آية (مثل: سورة الرحمن 42)…" autocomplete="off"><div id="quranSearchResults" class="quran-search-results" aria-live="polite"></div></div>
 <div class="age-and-prompt-row">
  <div class="field-group age-field-group"><label for="studentAgeChoice">عمر الطالب</label><input id="studentAgeChoice" name="studentAge" type="number" min="1" max="110" step="1" inputmode="numeric" value="${safeText(savedAge)}" placeholder="مثال: 8"></div>
 </div>
 <div class="specialized-prompts-row" role="group" aria-label="خيارات المحتوى التعليمي">
  <button type="button" class="control-btn mini-prompt-btn" id="promptVersesBtn" data-prompt-kind="verses"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 19.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 17H20"/><path d="M6.5 2H20v20H6.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 4 19.5v-15A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 2z"/></svg><span>الآيات</span></button>
  <button type="button" class="control-btn mini-prompt-btn" id="promptTajweedBtn" data-prompt-kind="tajweed"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v7a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V5a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3"/></svg><span>أحكام التجويد</span></button>
  <button type="button" class="control-btn mini-prompt-btn" id="promptValuesBtn" data-prompt-kind="values"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 21.35l-1.45-1.32C5.4 15.36 2 12.28 2 8.5 2 5.42 4.42 3 7.5 3c1.74 0 3.41.81 4.5 2.09C13.09 3.81 14.76 3 16.5 3 19.58 3 22 5.42 22 8.5c0 3.78-3.4 6.86-8.55 11.54L12 21.35z"/></svg><span>القيم والآداب</span></button>
  <button type="button" class="control-btn mini-prompt-btn" id="promptAllBtn" data-prompt-kind="all"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/><rect x="3" y="14" width="7" height="7" rx="1"/></svg><span>الجميع</span></button>
 </div>
 <div class="range-action-row compact-playback-row three-buttons-row"><button id="rangeRepeatButton" type="button" class="control-btn ${audioRepeat?'active':''}" aria-pressed="${audioRepeat}">${audioRepeat?'تكرار الآية مفعّل':'تكرار الآية'}</button><button type="button" class="control-btn ${state.repeatAllAudio?'active':''}" id="rangeRepeatAllButton" aria-pressed="${!!state.repeatAllAudio}">${state.repeatAllAudio?'تكرار جميع الآيات مفعّل':'تكرار جميع الآيات'}</button><button type="button" class="control-btn" id="playRangeButton">▶ قراءة الآيات كاملة</button></div>
 <div class="range-extra-grid range-reciter-last"><div class="field-group"><label for="rangeReciterSelect">القارئ</label><select id="rangeReciterSelect"><option value="6" ${savedReciter===6?'selected':''}>الشيخ محمود خليل الحصري</option><option value="9" ${savedReciter===9?'selected':''}>الشيخ محمد صديق المنشاوي</option></select></div></div>
 <div class="small-note">تُحفظ اختيارات السورة والنطاق وعمر الطالب تلقائيًا. اضغط على أي زر من أزرار البرومبتات الأربعة لنسخ البرومبت المخصص مباشرة إلى الحافظة.</div>`,
 fd=>{
  const ch=resolveSurahInput();
  if(!ch){showToast('اكتب اسم السورة كاملًا أو اختره من الاقتراحات.');$('surahSearch').focus();return false;}
  const from=Math.max(1,Number(fd.get('from'))||1),to=Math.max(from,Number(fd.get('to'))||from),max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;
  if(from>max||to>max){showToast('رقم الآية خارج نطاق هذه السورة.');return false;}
  syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
  chosenRange={chapter:ch,from,to};state.chosenRange=chosenRange;state.lastChapter=ch;state.lastVerse=from;
  state.studentAge=String(fd.get('studentAge')||'').trim();
  state.designType=String(fd.get('designType')||state.designType||DEFAULT_DESIGN_TYPE);
  state.reciter=Number($('rangeReciterSelect')?.value||state.reciter||6);
  state.repeatAudio=audioRepeat;
  if(typeof setVersesTrayHidden==='function')setVersesTrayHidden(false);
  saveState();setBlankDisplay(false,false);loadChapter(ch,from);
  showToast('تم عرض سورة '+surahNames[ch-1]+' من الآية '+arabicNum(from)+' إلى '+arabicNum(to)+'.');
 });
 const modal=$('editForm').closest('.modal');modal.classList.add('range-modal');mountRangeModalTopActions(modal);$('editForm').querySelector('[type=submit]').textContent='عرض';$('cancelModal').textContent='إلغاء العرض';$('cancelModal').classList.add('range-cancel-small');
 let activeModalChapter=savedCh;
 function updateSurahRange(chapter, forceReset=false){
  const max=verseCounts[chapter-1]||1;
  const changed=Number(chapter)!==Number(activeModalChapter);
  activeModalChapter=Number(chapter);
  $('chapterChoice').value=String(chapter);
  $('fromVerse').max=String(max);$('toVerse').max=String(max);
  if(changed||forceReset){
   $('fromVerse').value='1';$('toVerse').value=String(max);
  }else{
   const curFrom=Math.min(max,Math.max(1,Number($('fromVerse').value)||1));
   const curTo=Math.min(max,Math.max(curFrom,Number($('toVerse').value)||max));
   $('fromVerse').value=String(curFrom);$('toVerse').value=String(curTo);
  }
  syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
 }
 function paintSurahSuggestions(query, fromUserTyping=false){
  const host=$('surahSearchResults');if(!host)return;
  const raw=String(query||'').trim().replace(/^سورة\s*/,'');
  const norm=normalizeArabicSearch(raw);
  if(!norm){host.innerHTML='';host.classList.remove('show');$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(fromUserTyping)syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();return;}
  const matches=surahNames.map((name,i)=>({name,i})).filter(x=>normalizeArabicSearch(x.name).includes(norm)).slice(0,12);
  host.innerHTML='';
  for(const item of matches){
   const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='surah-search-result';b.setAttribute('role','option');
   b.innerHTML='<span>سورة '+safeText(item.name)+'</span><small>'+arabicNum(item.i+1)+' · '+arabicNum(verseCounts[item.i])+' آية</small>';
   b.addEventListener('click',()=>{$('surahSearch').value=item.name;updateSurahRange(item.i+1,true);host.innerHTML='';host.classList.remove('show');$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');});
   host.appendChild(b);
  }
  host.classList.toggle('show',matches.length>0);$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(matches.length>0));
  if(fromUserTyping){
   const exact=surahNames.findIndex(n=>normalizeArabicSearch(n)===norm);
   if(exact>=0)updateSurahRange(exact+1,false);
   else{$('chapterChoice').value='';syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();}
  }
 }
 $('surahSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>paintSurahSuggestions(e.target.value,true));
 $('clearSurahSearch')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const input=$('surahSearch');if(!input)return;
  input.value='';$('chapterChoice').value='';
  $('surahSearchResults').innerHTML='';$('surahSearchResults').classList.remove('show');
  input.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
  input.focus();
 });
 $('surahSearch').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){$('surahSearchResults').classList.remove('show');$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}if(e.key==='Enter'){const exact=surahNames.findIndex(n=>normalizeArabicSearch(n)===normalizeArabicSearch(String($('surahSearch').value||'').trim().replace(/^سورة\s*/,'')));const first=$('surahSearchResults').querySelector('button');if(exact>=0){e.preventDefault();updateSurahRange(exact+1);$('surahSearchResults').classList.remove('show');$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}else if(first&&$('surahSearchResults').classList.contains('show')){e.preventDefault();first.click();}}if(e.key==='ArrowDown'){const first=$('surahSearchResults').querySelector('button');if(first){e.preventDefault();first.focus();}}});
 $('surahSearch').addEventListener('blur',()=>setTimeout(()=>{const host=$('surahSearchResults');if(host&&!host.contains(document.activeElement)){host.classList.remove('show');$('surahSearch').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}},130));
 $('fromVerse').addEventListener('input',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('fromVerse').addEventListener('change',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('toVerse').addEventListener('input',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('toVerse').addEventListener('change',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('quranVerseSearch').addEventListener('input',()=>{clearTimeout(verseSearchTimer);const query=$('quranVerseSearch').value.trim();if(!query){$('quranSearchResults').innerHTML='';return;}verseSearchTimer=setTimeout(()=>searchQuranVerses(query),150);});
 $('quranVerseSearch').addEventListener('focus',()=>{const query=$('quranVerseSearch').value.trim();if(query&&!$('quranSearchResults').children.length)searchQuranVerses(query);});
 $('studentAgeChoice').addEventListener('input',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('studentAgeChoice').addEventListener('change',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('designTypeChoice')?.addEventListener('change',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('rangeReciterSelect').addEventListener('change',syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs);
 $('rangeRepeatButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{audioRepeat=!audioRepeat;state.repeatAudio=audioRepeat;$('audioPlayer').loop=audioRepeat;$('rangeRepeatButton').classList.toggle('active',audioRepeat);$('rangeRepeatButton').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(audioRepeat));$('rangeRepeatButton').textContent=audioRepeat?'تكرار الآية مفعّل':'تكرار الآية';saveState();});
 $('rangeRepeatAllButton').addEventListener('click',()=>{state.repeatAllAudio=!state.repeatAllAudio;if(rangePlayback)rangePlayback.repeat=state.repeatAllAudio;$('rangeRepeatAllButton').classList.toggle('active',state.repeatAllAudio);$('rangeRepeatAllButton').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(state.repeatAllAudio));$('rangeRepeatAllButton').textContent=state.repeatAllAudio?'تكرار جميع الآيات مفعّل':'تكرار جميع الآيات';saveState();});
 $('playRangeButton').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  const ch=resolveSurahInput();
  if(!ch){showToast('اكتب اسم السورة كاملًا أو اختره من الاقتراحات.');$('surahSearch').focus();return;}
  const from=Math.max(1,Number($('fromVerse').value)||1),to=Math.max(from,Number($('toVerse').value)||from),max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;
  if(from>to||to>max){showToast('راجع نطاق الآيات قبل القراءة.');return;}
  syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
  chosenRange={chapter:ch,from,to};state.chosenRange=chosenRange;state.lastChapter=ch;state.lastVerse=from;
  if(typeof setVersesTrayHidden==='function')setVersesTrayHidden(false);
  saveState();setBlankDisplay(false,false);
  rangePlayback={chapter:ch,from,to,repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
  closeModal();
  await loadChapter(ch,from);
  if(currentVerseObj()&&currentChapter===ch){rangePlayback={chapter:ch,from,to,repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};goToVerse(from);await playVerse(true);showToast('بدأت قراءة الآيات المحددة.');}
 });
 const validateAndCopyPrompt=async(kind, toastSuccess)=>{
  const ch=resolveSurahInput();
  if(!ch){showToast('اكتب اسم السورة كاملًا أو اختره من الاقتراحات.');$('surahSearch')?.focus();return;}
  const from=Number($('fromVerse')?.value)||1,to=Number($('toVerse')?.value)||from;
  const max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;
  if(from<1||from>to||to>max){showToast('راجع نطاق الآيات قبل نسخ البرومبت.');return;}
  syncLessonSetupFromModalInputs();
  const age=String($('studentAgeChoice')?.value||'').trim()||'لم يحدد العمر';
  const designType=String($('designTypeChoice')?.value||state.designType||DEFAULT_DESIGN_TYPE).trim();
  const promptText=kind==='general'
   ? buildLessonPrompt(ch,from,to,age,designType)
   : buildSpecializedPrompt(kind,ch,from,to,age,designType);
  await copyTextToClipboard(promptText,toastSuccess);
 };
 $('copyLessonPrompt')?.addEventListener('click',()=>validateAndCopyPrompt('general','تم نسخ البرومبت العام لإنشاء الصورة التعليمية إلى الحافظة.'));
 $('promptVersesBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>validateAndCopyPrompt('verses','تم نسخ برومبت الآيات'));
 $('promptTajweedBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>validateAndCopyPrompt('tajweed','تم نسخ برومبت أحكام التجويد'));
 $('promptValuesBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>validateAndCopyPrompt('values','تم نسخ برومبت القيم والآداب'));
 $('promptAllBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>validateAndCopyPrompt('all','تم نسخ برومبت الجميع'));
}
$('openRange').addEventListener('click',openRangeModal);
$('surahPill').addEventListener('click',()=>openRangeModal());$('surahPill').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();openRangeModal();}});

// إخفاء المصحف من الشاشة دون مسح النطاق أو البيانات المحفوظة.


document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target?.id==='chapterChoice'){const ch=Number(e.target.value);const max=verseCounts[ch-1]||1;const from=$('fromVerse'),to=$('toVerse'),search=$('surahSearch');if(search)search.value=surahNames[ch-1]||'';if(from&&to){from.max=String(max);to.max=String(max);from.value='1';to.value=String(max);}}});
function readOriginalImageDataUrl(file){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!file||!file.type.startsWith('image/')){reject(new Error('اختر ملف صورة.'));return;}const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(String(reader.result||''));reader.onerror=()=>reject(reader.error||new Error('تعذرت قراءة الصورة.'));reader.readAsDataURL(file);});}
function fileToDataUrl(file,maxSide=850,quality=.82,preserveTransparency=false){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!file||!file.type.startsWith('image/')){reject(new Error('اختر ملف صورة.'));return;}const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=reject;reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=reject;img.onload=()=>{const scale=Math.min(1,maxSide/Math.max(img.width,img.height));const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.width*scale));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.height*scale));c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,c.width,c.height);const outType=preserveTransparency&&/png|webp|gif/i.test(file.type)?'image/png':'image/jpeg';resolve(c.toDataURL(outType,quality));};img.src=reader.result;};reader.readAsDataURL(file);});}
function applyPhoto(which,data){if(which==='logo'){state.logo=data;$('logoImage').src=data;$('logoImage').hidden=false;$('logoFallback').hidden=true;}else{state.portrait=data;$('teacherImage').src=data;$('teacherImage').hidden=false;$('teacherFallback').hidden=true;}saveState();showToast('تم تحديث الصورة وحفظها على هذا الجهاز.');}
$('logoButton').addEventListener('click',openMushafModal);
$('logoFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{try{const data=await fileToDataUrl(e.target.files[0],500,.78,true);applyPhoto('logo',data);}catch(_){showToast('تعذر فتح الصورة المحددة.');}e.target.value='';});
$('teacherFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{try{const data=await fileToDataUrl(e.target.files[0],500,.78,true);applyPhoto('portrait',data);}catch(_){showToast('تعذر فتح الصورة المحددة.');}e.target.value='';});
const BUILTIN_BACKGROUNDS=[];
function backgroundLabel(name){return ({paper:'ورقي',green:'أخضر',blue:'أزرق',rose:'وردي',night:'ليلي',custom:'صورة من الجهاز'})[name]||'الخلفية';}
function paintCustomBackgroundButtons(){const host=$('customBackgroundButtons');if(!host)return;host.innerHTML='';(state.customBackgrounds||[]).forEach((src,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='bg-btn custom-thumb'+(state.background==='custom'&&i===state.customBackgroundIndex?' active':'');b.title='خلفية مضافة '+(i+1);b.setAttribute('aria-label','اختيار الخلفية المضافة '+(i+1));b.style.backgroundImage='url("'+src+'")';b.dataset.bg='custom';b.dataset.customIndex=String(i);b.addEventListener('click',()=>{setBackground('custom',src);saveState();});host.appendChild(b);});}
function setBackground(name,custom=''){state.background=name;const app=$('app');app.classList.remove('dark-mode','background-image');app.style.background='';document.body.style.background='';document.body.style.backgroundImage='';document.body.style.backgroundColor='';const clarity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(state.ageBackgroundOpacity?.[state.ageMode]??78)))/100;const fade=Number((.88*(1-clarity)).toFixed(3));const tint=name==='night'?`rgba(7,18,13,${Math.min(.48,fade)})`:`rgba(255,255,255,${fade})`;const overlay=`linear-gradient(${tint},${tint})`;if(name==='paper'){document.body.style.background=`${overlay},radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%,rgba(217,194,137,.17),transparent 28%),linear-gradient(135deg,#f7f4e9,#edf5ef)`;}else if(name==='green'){document.body.style.background=`${overlay},radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#f7f7e8,#d9ebd5 55%,#b9d5c0)`;}else if(name==='blue'){document.body.style.background=`${overlay},radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#f7fbff,#deeff4 52%,#c8dfea)`;}else if(name==='rose'){document.body.style.background=`${overlay},radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#fff9f4,#f6e1d7 55%,#edd2c5)`;}else if(name==='night'){app.classList.add('dark-mode');app.style.background=`${overlay},radial-gradient(circle at 18% 15%,#29463a,#111e19 65%)`;}else if(name==='custom'&&custom){document.body.style.background=`${overlay},url("${custom}") center/cover fixed no-repeat`;app.classList.add('background-image');}document.querySelectorAll('.bg-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.bg===name&&(name!=='custom'||Number(b.dataset.customIndex)===state.customBackgroundIndex)));paintCustomBackgroundButtons();}
function cycleBackground(direction){const choices=[...BUILTIN_BACKGROUNDS,...(state.customBackgrounds||[]).map((src,i)=>({name:'custom',src,index:i}))];let current=BUILTIN_BACKGROUNDS.indexOf(state.background);if(state.background==='custom')current=BUILTIN_BACKGROUNDS.length+Math.max(0,Number(state.customBackgroundIndex)||0);if(current<0)current=0;let next=(current+direction+choices.length)%choices.length;const choice=choices[next];if(typeof choice==='string')setBackground(choice);else setBackground('custom',choice.src);saveState();}
/* تتم إدارة خلفيات الكبار والأطفال من نافذة الإعدادات فقط. */
/* إدارة الصور المضافة: تحريك حر فوق واجهة البرنامج، والحذف من زر Delete فقط. */
function syncStickerState(){state.stickers=[...$('stickerLayer').querySelectorAll('.sticker-item')].map(item=>{const kind=item.dataset.mediaKind==='video'?'video':'image';return {kind,src:kind==='image'?(item.querySelector('img')?.src||''):'',mediaKey:item.dataset.mediaKey||'',left:parseFloat(item.style.left)||0,top:parseFloat(item.style.top)||0,width:parseFloat(item.style.width)||150,height:parseFloat(item.style.height)||150,hidden:item.dataset.trayHidden==='1'};}).filter(item=>item.src||(item.kind==='video'&&item.mediaKey));saveState();}
let selectedSticker=null,stickerZCounter=10011,boardTextZCounter=10013;
function selectSticker(item){document.querySelectorAll('.sticker-item.selected-sticker').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected-sticker'));selectedSticker=item;if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.classList.remove('selected');selectedBoardText=null;}if(item){$('stickerLayer').style.setProperty('z-index','10016','important');$('boardTextLayer').style.setProperty('z-index','10012','important');item.classList.add('selected-sticker');item.style.setProperty('--sticker-z',String(++stickerZCounter));item.focus({preventScroll:true});}}
function removeSticker(item,persist=true){if(!item)return;if(selectedSticker===item)selectedSticker=null;item.remove();if(persist)syncStickerState();}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('.sticker-item')){document.querySelectorAll('.sticker-item.selected-sticker').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected-sticker'));selectedSticker=null;}},true);
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
 const target=e.target,tag=target?.tagName||'';
 const insideTextControls=!!target?.closest?.('.board-text-font-panel,.settings-panel,.modal,.settings-backdrop,.modal-backdrop');
 if(selectedBoardText&&e.key==='Delete'&&!/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(tag)&&!target?.isContentEditable&&!insideTextControls){e.preventDefault();removeBoardText(selectedBoardText,true);return;}
 if(!selectedSticker||$('editModal')?.classList.contains('show')||$('settingsBackdrop')?.classList.contains('show')||$('rangeBackdrop')?.classList.contains('show'))return;
 if(/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(tag)||target?.isContentEditable)return;
 if(e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();removeSticker(selectedSticker,true);return;}
 const bigger=e.key==='+'||e.key==='='||e.code==='NumpadAdd',smaller=e.key==='-'||e.key==='_'||e.code==='NumpadSubtract';if(!bigger&&!smaller)return;
 e.preventDefault();const r=selectedSticker.getBoundingClientRect(),factor=bigger?1.12:(1/1.12),w=Math.max(24,Math.min(10000,r.width*factor)),h=Math.max(24,Math.min(10000,r.height*factor));selectedSticker.style.width=w+'px';selectedSticker.style.height=h+'px';syncStickerState();
});
function addSticker(dataUrl,index=0,config=null,persist=true){
 const layer=$('stickerLayer'),item=document.createElement('div');item.className='sticker-item';item.tabIndex=0;item.setAttribute('role','group');
 const mediaKind=config?.kind==='video'?'video':'image';item.dataset.mediaKind=mediaKind;if(mediaKind==='video'&&config?.mediaKey)item.dataset.mediaKey=config.mediaKey;
 item.setAttribute('aria-label',mediaKind==='video'?'فيديو قابل للتحريك وتغيير الحجم':'صورة قابلة للتحريك وتغيير الحجم');
 const initialWidth=Math.max(24,Number(config?.width)||150),initialHeight=Math.max(24,Number(config?.height)||150);item.style.width=initialWidth+'px';item.style.height=initialHeight+'px';
 item.style.left=(config&&Number.isFinite(Number(config.left))?Number(config.left):Math.max(12,Math.min(window.innerWidth-initialWidth-12,window.innerWidth*.35+index*14)))+'px';
 item.style.top=(config&&Number.isFinite(Number(config.top))?Number(config.top):Math.max(24,Math.min(window.innerHeight-initialHeight-12,window.innerHeight*.30+index*12)))+'px';
 item.style.setProperty('--sticker-z',String(++stickerZCounter));
 let media,dragSurface;
 if(mediaKind==='video'){
  media=document.createElement('video');media.src=dataUrl;media.controls=true;media.playsInline=true;media.preload='metadata';media.loop=false;media.setAttribute('aria-label','فيديو مضاف');
  const moveHandle=document.createElement('button');moveHandle.type='button';moveHandle.className='sticker-video-move-handle';moveHandle.title='اسحب لتحريك الفيديو';moveHandle.setAttribute('aria-label','تحريك الفيديو');moveHandle.innerHTML='<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 3v18M3 12h18M8 7l4-4 4 4M8 17l4 4 4-4M7 8l-4 4 4 4M17 8l4 4-4 4"/></svg>';
  dragSurface=moveHandle;item.append(media,moveHandle);
 }else{
  media=document.createElement('img');media.src=dataUrl;media.alt='صورة مضافة';media.draggable=false;dragSurface=media;item.appendChild(media);
 }
 const resizeHandle=document.createElement('button');resizeHandle.type='button';resizeHandle.className='sticker-resize-handle corner-se';resizeHandle.dataset.corner='se';resizeHandle.title=mediaKind==='video'?'اسحب لتغيير حجم الفيديو':'اسحب لتكبير أو تصغير العنصر';resizeHandle.setAttribute('aria-label',mediaKind==='video'?'تغيير حجم الفيديو':'تغيير حجم الصورة');resizeHandle.innerHTML='<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 16 18 6M10 6h8v8"/></svg>';
 const cornerNW=document.createElement('button');cornerNW.type='button';cornerNW.className='sticker-corner-handle corner-nw';cornerNW.dataset.corner='nw';cornerNW.tabIndex=-1;cornerNW.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير من الزاوية العلوية اليسرى');
 const cornerNE=document.createElement('button');cornerNE.type='button';cornerNE.className='sticker-corner-handle corner-ne';cornerNE.dataset.corner='ne';cornerNE.tabIndex=-1;cornerNE.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير من الزاوية العلوية اليمنى');
 const cornerSW=document.createElement('button');cornerSW.type='button';cornerSW.className='sticker-corner-handle corner-sw';cornerSW.dataset.corner='sw';cornerSW.tabIndex=-1;cornerSW.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير من الزاوية السفلية اليسرى');
 item.append(cornerNW,cornerNE,cornerSW,resizeHandle);layer.appendChild(item);
 item.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>{if(isPenActive())return;selectSticker(item);});item.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(isPenActive())return;selectSticker(item);});
 let dragging=false,offsetX=0,offsetY=0,pointerId=null,imageResizing=false,resizePointerId=null,baseWidth=initialWidth,baseHeight=initialHeight,anchorX=0,anchorY=0,startDiagonal=1;
 dragSurface.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(isPenActive())return;if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;selectSticker(item);dragging=true;pointerId=e.pointerId;const r=item.getBoundingClientRect();offsetX=e.clientX-r.left;offsetY=e.clientY-r.top;try{dragSurface.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
 dragSurface.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!dragging||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;e.preventDefault();const w=item.offsetWidth,h=item.offsetHeight;const minLeft=24-w,maxLeft=window.innerWidth-24,minTop=24-h,maxTop=window.innerHeight-24;item.style.left=Math.min(maxLeft,Math.max(minLeft,e.clientX-offsetX))+'px';item.style.top=Math.min(maxTop,Math.max(minTop,e.clientY-offsetY))+'px';});
 const finishDrag=e=>{if(!dragging||(e&&pointerId!==null&&e.pointerId!==pointerId))return;dragging=false;pointerId=null;if(persist)syncStickerState();};dragSurface.addEventListener('pointerup',finishDrag);dragSurface.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishDrag);dragSurface.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{if(dragging){dragging=false;if(persist)syncStickerState();}});
 resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;selectSticker(item);imageResizing=true;resizePointerId=e.pointerId;baseWidth=item.offsetWidth;baseHeight=item.offsetHeight;anchorX=parseFloat(item.style.left)||0;anchorY=parseFloat(item.style.top)||0;startDiagonal=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(e.clientX-anchorX,e.clientY-anchorY));try{resizeHandle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
 resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!imageResizing||e.pointerId!==resizePointerId)return;e.preventDefault();const scale=Math.max(.12,Math.min(12,Math.hypot(e.clientX-anchorX,e.clientY-anchorY)/startDiagonal));item.style.width=Math.max(24,Math.min(10000,baseWidth*scale))+'px';item.style.height=Math.max(24,Math.min(10000,baseHeight*scale))+'px';});
 const finishResize=e=>{if(!imageResizing||(e&&e.pointerId!==resizePointerId))return;imageResizing=false;resizePointerId=null;if(persist)syncStickerState();};resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointerup',finishResize);resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishResize);resizeHandle.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{if(imageResizing){imageResizing=false;if(persist)syncStickerState();}});
 [cornerNW,cornerNE,cornerSW].forEach(handle=>{
  let cResizing=false,cPid=null,startW=0,startH=0,startL=0,startT=0,oppX=0,oppY=0,startDist=1;
  const corner=handle.dataset.corner;
  handle.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
   if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;
   selectSticker(item);cResizing=true;cPid=e.pointerId;
   startW=item.offsetWidth;startH=item.offsetHeight;
   startL=parseFloat(item.style.left)||0;startT=parseFloat(item.style.top)||0;
   oppX=(corner==='nw'||corner==='sw')?(startL+startW):startL;
   oppY=(corner==='nw'||corner==='ne')?(startT+startH):startT;
   startDist=Math.max(12,Math.hypot(e.clientX-oppX,e.clientY-oppY));
   try{handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}
   e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  });
  handle.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
   if(!cResizing||e.pointerId!==cPid)return;
   e.preventDefault();
   const scale=Math.max(0.15,Math.min(12,Math.hypot(e.clientX-oppX,e.clientY-oppY)/startDist));
   const newW=Math.max(28,Math.min(10000,Math.round(startW*scale)));
   const newH=Math.max(28,Math.min(10000,Math.round(startH*scale)));
   item.style.width=newW+'px';item.style.height=newH+'px';
   if(corner==='nw'||corner==='sw')item.style.left=Math.round(oppX-newW)+'px';
   if(corner==='nw'||corner==='ne')item.style.top=Math.round(oppY-newH)+'px';
  });
  const endCorner=e=>{if(!cResizing||(e&&e.pointerId!==cPid))return;cResizing=false;cPid=null;if(persist)syncStickerState();};
  handle.addEventListener('pointerup',endCorner);handle.addEventListener('pointercancel',endCorner);
  handle.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{if(cResizing){cResizing=false;if(persist)syncStickerState();}});
 });
 item.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{
  if(!item.classList.contains('selected-sticker'))return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  const factor=e.deltaY<0?1.08:(1/1.08);
  const rect=item.getBoundingClientRect();
  const curL=parseFloat(item.style.left)||rect.left;
  const curT=parseFloat(item.style.top)||rect.top;
  const newW=Math.max(28,Math.min(10000,Math.round(rect.width*factor)));
  const newH=Math.max(28,Math.min(10000,Math.round(rect.height*factor)));
  item.style.left=Math.round(curL-(newW-rect.width)/2)+'px';
  item.style.top=Math.round(curT-(newH-rect.height)/2)+'px';
  item.style.width=newW+'px';item.style.height=newH+'px';
  if(persist)syncStickerState();
 },{passive:false});
 if(config?.hidden)item.dataset.trayHidden='1';
 if(persist){delete item.dataset.trayHidden;syncStickerState();selectSticker(item);if(typeof renderScreenTray==='function')renderScreenTray();}return item;
}
async function restoreStickers(){const saved=Array.isArray(state.stickers)?state.stickers.slice():[];for(let i=0;i<saved.length;i++){const item=saved[i];if(!item)continue;if(item.kind==='video'&&item.mediaKey){try{const record=await readLocalRecord(item.mediaKey);if(record?.blob)addSticker(URL.createObjectURL(record.blob),i,item,false);}catch(_){/* يظل البرنامج قابلًا للاستخدام إن تعذر استرجاع فيديو */}}else if(item.src)addSticker(item.src,i,item,false);}}
window.addSticker=addSticker;window.selectSticker=selectSticker;window.removeSticker=removeSticker;window.syncStickerState=syncStickerState;window.showToast=showToast;window.removeBoardText=removeBoardText;window.selectBoardText=selectBoardText;window.syncBoardTextState=syncBoardTextState;window.setPenTool=setPenTool;window.syncCanvasDrawingState=(hasPixels)=>{try{state.drawingData=hasPixels?canvas.toDataURL('image/png'):'';saveState();}catch(_){}};
$('addStickerButton').addEventListener('click',()=>$('stickerFile').click());
$('stickerFile').addEventListener('change',async e=>{try{const files=[...e.target.files].filter(f=>f.type.startsWith('image/')||f.type.startsWith('video/')).slice(0,8);let addedImages=0,addedVideos=0;for(let i=0;i<files.length;i++){const file=files[i];if(file.type.startsWith('image/')){addSticker(await readOriginalImageDataUrl(file),i);addedImages++;}else if(file.type.startsWith('video/')){const mediaKey='board-video-'+Date.now()+'-'+i;await writeLocalRecord(mediaKey,{blob:file,name:file.name,type:file.type,savedAt:Date.now()});addSticker(URL.createObjectURL(file),i,{kind:'video',mediaKey},true);addedVideos++;}}if(files.length)showToast('تمت إضافة '+(addedImages?' '+addedImages+' صورة':'')+(addedVideos?' و'+addedVideos+' فيديو':'')+'؛ يمكنك تحريك العنصر وتغيير حجمه.');}catch(_){showToast('تعذرت إضافة الملف. اختر صورة أو فيديو مدعومًا.');}e.target.value='';});
function applyLoadedSettings(){ $('programTitleButton').textContent=state.title;$('teacherNameButton').textContent=state.teacher;document.title=state.title+' — '+state.teacher;if(state.logo){$('logoImage').src=state.logo;$('logoImage').hidden=false;$('logoFallback').hidden=true;}if(state.portrait){$('teacherImage').src=state.portrait;$('teacherImage').hidden=false;$('teacherFallback').hidden=true;}applyFonts();applyFrameStyles();applyDisplayMode(state.displayMode||state.ageMode||'adult',false);paintCustomBackgroundButtons();if(state.blankDisplay){isBlankDisplay=true;document.body.classList.add('blank-display');$('surahTitle').textContent='اختر السورة';}}
let boardDrawings=Array.isArray(state.vectorDrawings)?state.vectorDrawings:[];
let activeStrokePoints=[];
window.boardDrawings=boardDrawings;
function drawStoredStrokeObject(d){
 if(!d)return;
 ctx.save();
 if(d.tool==='marker'){
  ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0.18,Math.min(0.42,(Number(d.opacity)??1)*0.32));
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(18,(Number(d.width)||5)*3.5);
  ctx.strokeStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.fillStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  drawMarkerPath(d.points||[]);
 }else if(d.tool==='free'){
  ctx.globalAlpha=Number(d.opacity)??1;
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.lineWidth=Number(d.width)||5;
  ctx.strokeStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.fillStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  const pts=d.points||[];
  if(pts.length){
   ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x,pts[0].y);
   if(pts.length===1){
    ctx.lineTo(pts[0].x+0.1,pts[0].y);ctx.stroke();
   }else if(Number(d.smooth)===0){
    for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(pts[i].x,pts[i].y);
    ctx.stroke();
   }else{
    const sf=(Number(d.smooth)>=2)?0.32:0.62;
    let lp=pts[0];
    for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++){
     const p=pts[i];
     const ctrl={x:lp.x+(p.x-lp.x)*sf,y:lp.y+(p.y-lp.y)*sf};
     const mid={x:(ctrl.x+p.x)/2,y:(ctrl.y+p.y)/2};
     ctx.quadraticCurveTo(ctrl.x,ctrl.y,mid.x,mid.y);
     lp=p;
    }
    ctx.lineTo(pts[pts.length-1].x,pts[pts.length-1].y);
    ctx.stroke();
   }
  }
 }else if(d.tool==='line'||d.tool==='rect'||d.tool==='circle'){
  ctx.globalAlpha=Number(d.opacity)??1;
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.lineWidth=Number(d.width)||5;
  ctx.strokeStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.fillStyle=d.color||'#dc5148';
  ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  if(d.from&&d.to)drawShape(d.from,d.to,d.tool);
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
function redrawVectorCanvas(persist=true){
 const dpr=Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
 ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.restore();
 ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
 for(const d of boardDrawings)drawStoredStrokeObject(d);
 ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
 if(persist){
  state.vectorDrawings=boardDrawings;
  if(!boardDrawings.length)state.drawingData='';
  scheduleCanvasSnapshotSave();
  saveState();
 }
}
window.redrawVectorCanvas=redrawVectorCanvas;
function restoreDrawingFromState(){
 boardDrawings=Array.isArray(state.vectorDrawings)?state.vectorDrawings:[];
 window.boardDrawings=boardDrawings;
 if(boardDrawings.length){redrawVectorCanvas(false);return;}
 if(!state.drawingData)return;
 const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0,Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,innerWidth,innerHeight);ctx.restore();ctx.setTransform(Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0,Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0);};img.src=state.drawingData;
}
function canvasPoint(e){const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:e.clientX-r.left,y:e.clientY-r.top};}
let markerPoints=[];
function drawMarkerPath(points){
 if(!points||!points.length)return;
 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(points[0].x,points[0].y);
 if(points.length===1){ctx.lineTo(points[0].x+0.1,points[0].y);ctx.stroke();return;}
 for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++){
  const prev=points[i-1],cur=points[i];
  const mid={x:(prev.x+cur.x)/2,y:(prev.y+cur.y)/2};
  ctx.quadraticCurveTo(prev.x,prev.y,mid.x,mid.y);
 }
 const last=points[points.length-1];
 ctx.lineTo(last.x,last.y);
 ctx.stroke();
}
function configStroke(){
 if(penTool==='marker'){
  ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0.18,Math.min(0.42,penOpacity*0.32));
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(18,penWidth*3.5);
  ctx.strokeStyle=penColor;
  ctx.fillStyle=penColor;
  ctx.lineCap='round';
  ctx.lineJoin='round';
  return;
 }
 ctx.globalAlpha=penTool==='eraser'?1:penOpacity;ctx.globalCompositeOperation=penTool==='eraser'?'destination-out':'source-over';ctx.lineWidth=penTool==='eraser'?Math.max(12,penWidth*3):penWidth;ctx.strokeStyle=penTool==='eraser'?'rgba(0,0,0,1)':penColor;ctx.fillStyle=penColor;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
}
function drawShape(from,to,tool){ctx.beginPath();if(tool==='line'){ctx.moveTo(from.x,from.y);ctx.lineTo(to.x,to.y);ctx.stroke();}else if(tool==='rect'){ctx.strokeRect(from.x,from.y,to.x-from.x,to.y-from.y);}else if(tool==='circle'){const rx=(to.x-from.x)/2,ry=(to.y-from.y)/2;ctx.ellipse(from.x+rx,from.y+ry,Math.max(1,Math.abs(rx)),Math.max(1,Math.abs(ry)),0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();}}
function getSnapshot(){snapshot=document.createElement('canvas');snapshot.width=canvas.width;snapshot.height=canvas.height;snapshot.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas,0,0);}
function restoreSnapshot(){if(snapshot){ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.drawImage(snapshot,0,0);ctx.restore();ctx.setTransform(Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0,Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0);}}
canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(penTool==='stop')return;if(!isPenActive())return;e.preventDefault();canvas.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);isDrawing=true;drawStart=canvasPoint(e);lastPoint=drawStart;activeStrokePoints=[{x:drawStart.x,y:drawStart.y}];if(penTool!=='free'&&penTool!=='eraser')getSnapshot();configStroke();if(penTool==='marker'){markerPoints=[drawStart];drawMarkerPath(markerPoints);}else if(penTool==='free'||penTool==='eraser'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(drawStart.x,drawStart.y);}});
canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!isDrawing)return;e.preventDefault();const p=canvasPoint(e);activeStrokePoints.push({x:p.x,y:p.y});configStroke();if(penTool==='marker'){markerPoints.push(p);restoreSnapshot();configStroke();drawMarkerPath(markerPoints);lastPoint=p;}else if(penTool==='free'||penTool==='eraser'){if(penSmooth===0){ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);}else{const smoothFactor=penSmooth>=2?0.32:0.62;const control={x:lastPoint.x+(p.x-lastPoint.x)*smoothFactor,y:lastPoint.y+(p.y-lastPoint.y)*smoothFactor};const mid={x:(control.x+p.x)/2,y:(control.y+p.y)/2};ctx.quadraticCurveTo(control.x,control.y,mid.x,mid.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(mid.x,mid.y);}lastPoint=p;}else{restoreSnapshot();configStroke();drawShape(drawStart,p,penTool);}});
function endDrawing(e){
 if(!isDrawing)return;isDrawing=false;
 const endPt=e?canvasPoint(e):(lastPoint||drawStart);
 if(endPt)activeStrokePoints.push({x:endPt.x,y:endPt.y});
 if(penTool==='marker'){
  if(e){markerPoints.push(endPt);}restoreSnapshot();configStroke();drawMarkerPath(markerPoints);
  boardDrawings.push({id:'drw_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),tool:'marker',color:penColor,width:penWidth,opacity:penOpacity,smooth:penSmooth,points:markerPoints.map(pt=>({x:pt.x,y:pt.y}))});
  markerPoints=[];
 }else if(penTool==='free'){
  ctx.closePath();
  boardDrawings.push({id:'drw_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),tool:'free',color:penColor,width:penWidth,opacity:penOpacity,smooth:penSmooth,points:activeStrokePoints.map(pt=>({x:pt.x,y:pt.y}))});
 }else if(penTool==='eraser'){
  ctx.closePath();
  if(boardDrawings.length&&typeof window.eraseVectorStrokesByPath==='function'){
   window.eraseVectorStrokesByPath(activeStrokePoints,Math.max(12,penWidth*3));
  }
 }else if(['line','rect','circle'].includes(penTool)){
  restoreSnapshot();configStroke();drawShape(drawStart,endPt,penTool);
  boardDrawings.push({id:'drw_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),tool:penTool,color:penColor,width:penWidth,opacity:penOpacity,smooth:penSmooth,from:{x:drawStart.x,y:drawStart.y},to:{x:endPt.x,y:endPt.y}});
 }
 activeStrokePoints=[];
 ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';snapshot=null;lastPoint=null;
 state.vectorDrawings=boardDrawings;scheduleCanvasSnapshotSave();saveState();
}
canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',endDrawing);canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',endDrawing);canvas.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{isDrawing=false;});
function isPenActive(){return Boolean($('penToolbar')?.classList.contains('show'))&&penTool!=='stop'&&penTool!=='text';}
function isArrowToolActive(){return !Boolean($('penToolbar')?.classList.contains('show'))||penTool==='stop';}
const BOARD_TEXT_FONT_STACKS={
 'Amiri':"'Amiri',serif",'Amiri Quran':"'Amiri Quran','Amiri',serif",'Cairo':"'Cairo',sans-serif",'Tajawal':"'Tajawal','Cairo',sans-serif",'Noto Naskh Arabic':"'Noto Naskh Arabic','Amiri',serif",'Almarai':"'Almarai','Cairo',sans-serif",'Reem Kufi':"'Reem Kufi','Cairo',sans-serif",'Changa':"'Changa','Cairo',sans-serif"
};
let selectedBoardText=null,boardTextDefaultFont='Amiri',boardTextDefaultSize=36,boardTextDefaultBold=false,boardTextSaveTimer=null;
function boardTextData(item){const content=item.querySelector('.board-text-content');return {text:(content?.innerText||content?.textContent||'').replace(/\u00a0/g,' ').trimEnd(),left:parseFloat(item.style.left)||0,top:parseFloat(item.style.top)||0,fontFamily:item.dataset.fontFamily||boardTextDefaultFont,fontSize:Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize,color:item.dataset.color||penColor,opacity:Number(item.dataset.opacity??1),fontWeight:item.dataset.fontWeight||'400',hidden:item.dataset.trayHidden==='1'};}
function applyBoardTextStyle(item){const content=item?.querySelector('.board-text-content');if(!content)return;const font=item.dataset.fontFamily||boardTextDefaultFont;content.style.fontFamily=BOARD_TEXT_FONT_STACKS[font]||BOARD_TEXT_FONT_STACKS.Amiri;content.style.fontSize=(Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize)+'px';content.style.color=item.dataset.color||penColor;content.style.opacity=String(Math.max(.1,Math.min(1,Number(item.dataset.opacity??1))));content.style.fontWeight=item.dataset.fontWeight||'400';}
function syncBoardTextState(){if(!state)return;state.boardTexts=[...$('boardTextLayer').querySelectorAll('.board-text-item')].map(boardTextData).filter(item=>item.text.trim());saveState();}
function scheduleBoardTextSave(){clearTimeout(boardTextSaveTimer);boardTextSaveTimer=setTimeout(syncBoardTextState,250);}
function updateBoardTextControls(item=selectedBoardText){if(item){$('boardTextFont').value=item.dataset.fontFamily||boardTextDefaultFont;$('boardTextSize').value=Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize;$('boardTextSizeValue').value=(Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize)+' px';$('boardTextBold').checked=(item.dataset.fontWeight||'400')==='700';}else{$('boardTextFont').value=boardTextDefaultFont;$('boardTextSize').value=boardTextDefaultSize;$('boardTextSizeValue').value=boardTextDefaultSize+' px';$('boardTextBold').checked=boardTextDefaultBold;}}
function selectBoardText(item,focusEditor=false){document.querySelectorAll('.board-text-item.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));selectedBoardText=item;if(selectedSticker){selectedSticker.classList.remove('selected-sticker');selectedSticker=null;}$('boardTextLayer').style.setProperty('z-index','10016','important');$('stickerLayer').style.setProperty('z-index','10010','important');if(item){item.style.setProperty('--board-text-z',String(++boardTextZCounter));item.classList.add('selected');updateBoardTextControls(item);if(focusEditor){const editor=item.querySelector('.board-text-content');editor?.focus({preventScroll:true});const range=document.createRange();range.selectNodeContents(editor);range.collapse(false);const sel=window.getSelection();sel?.removeAllRanges();sel?.addRange(range);}}}
function removeBoardText(item,persist=true){if(!item)return;if(selectedBoardText===item)selectedBoardText=null;item.remove();if(persist)syncBoardTextState();}
function addBoardText(config={},persist=false){
 const layer=$('boardTextLayer'),item=document.createElement('div');item.className='board-text-item';item.tabIndex=0;item.setAttribute('role','group');item.setAttribute('aria-label','نص شفاف قابل للتحرير والتحريك');
 const defaultLeft=Math.max(12,Math.round((window.innerWidth-300)/2)),defaultTop=Math.max(90,Math.round(window.innerHeight*.32));
 item.style.left=(Number.isFinite(Number(config.left))?Number(config.left):defaultLeft)+'px';item.style.top=(Number.isFinite(Number(config.top))?Number(config.top):defaultTop)+'px';
 item.dataset.fontFamily=config.fontFamily||boardTextDefaultFont;item.dataset.fontSize=String(Number(config.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize);item.dataset.color=config.color||penColor;item.dataset.opacity=String(Number.isFinite(Number(config.opacity))?Number(config.opacity):penOpacity);item.dataset.fontWeight=config.fontWeight||((config.bold??boardTextDefaultBold)?'700':'400');if(config.hidden)item.dataset.trayHidden='1';
 const move=document.createElement('button');move.type='button';move.className='board-text-drag';move.title='اسحب لتحريك النص';move.setAttribute('aria-label','تحريك النص');move.innerHTML='<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2v20M2 12h20M12 2l-3 3m3-3 3 3M12 22l-3-3m3 3 3-3M2 12l3-3m-3 3 3 3M22 12l-3-3m3 3-3 3"/></svg>';
 const resizeText=document.createElement('button');resizeText.type='button';resizeText.className='board-text-resize';resizeText.title='اسحب لتكبير النص أو تصغيره';resizeText.setAttribute('aria-label','تغيير حجم النص باليد');resizeText.innerHTML='<svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.1" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 17 17 7M9 7h8v8"/></svg>';
 const tCornerNW=document.createElement('button');tCornerNW.type='button';tCornerNW.className='text-corner-handle corner-nw';tCornerNW.dataset.corner='nw';tCornerNW.tabIndex=-1;tCornerNW.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير النص');
 const tCornerNE=document.createElement('button');tCornerNE.type='button';tCornerNE.className='text-corner-handle corner-ne';tCornerNE.dataset.corner='ne';tCornerNE.tabIndex=-1;tCornerNE.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير النص');
 const tCornerSW=document.createElement('button');tCornerSW.type='button';tCornerSW.className='text-corner-handle corner-sw';tCornerSW.dataset.corner='sw';tCornerSW.tabIndex=-1;tCornerSW.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير النص');
 const tCornerSE=document.createElement('button');tCornerSE.type='button';tCornerSE.className='text-corner-handle corner-se';tCornerSE.dataset.corner='se';tCornerSE.tabIndex=-1;tCornerSE.setAttribute('aria-label','تكبير وتصغير النص');
 const content=document.createElement('div');content.className='board-text-content';content.contentEditable='true';content.spellcheck=false;content.dir='rtl';content.setAttribute('role','textbox');content.setAttribute('aria-multiline','true');content.setAttribute('data-placeholder','اكتب ما تريد هنا…');content.textContent=String(config.text||'');
 item.append(move,resizeText,tCornerNW,tCornerNE,tCornerSW,tCornerSE,content);layer.appendChild(item);applyBoardTextStyle(item);
 [tCornerNW,tCornerNE,tCornerSW,tCornerSE].forEach(h=>{
  let cRes=false,cPid=null,sSize=36,sDist=1,oppX=0,oppY=0;
  const corner=h.dataset.corner;
  h.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
   if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;
   selectBoardText(item,false);cRes=true;cPid=e.pointerId;
   sSize=Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize;
   const r=item.getBoundingClientRect();
   oppX=(corner==='nw'||corner==='sw')?r.right:r.left;
   oppY=(corner==='nw'||corner==='ne')?r.bottom:r.top;
   sDist=Math.max(16,Math.hypot(e.clientX-oppX,e.clientY-oppY));
   try{h.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}
   e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  });
  h.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
   if(!cRes||e.pointerId!==cPid)return;
   e.preventDefault();
   const scale=Math.max(0.3,Math.min(6,Math.hypot(e.clientX-oppX,e.clientY-oppY)/sDist));
   const nextSize=Math.round(Math.max(12,Math.min(240,sSize*scale)));
   item.dataset.fontSize=String(nextSize);
   applyBoardTextStyle(item);
   if(selectedBoardText===item)updateBoardTextControls(item);
  });
  const endC=e=>{if(!cRes||(e&&e.pointerId!==cPid))return;cRes=false;cPid=null;syncBoardTextState();};
  h.addEventListener('pointerup',endC);h.addEventListener('pointercancel',endC);
 });
 item.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{
  if(!item.classList.contains('selected')&&!item.classList.contains('multi-selected'))return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  const curSize=Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize;
  const nextSize=Math.round(Math.max(12,Math.min(240,curSize*(e.deltaY<0?1.08:0.92))));
  item.dataset.fontSize=String(nextSize);
  applyBoardTextStyle(item);
  if(selectedBoardText===item)updateBoardTextControls(item);
  scheduleBoardTextSave();
 },{passive:false});
 item.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(isPenActive())return;if(e.target===move||e.target===resizeText)return;selectBoardText(item,false);});item.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(isPenActive())return;selectBoardText(item,false);});
 content.addEventListener('focus',()=>selectBoardText(item,false));content.addEventListener('input',scheduleBoardTextSave);content.addEventListener('blur',()=>{if(content.textContent.trim())syncBoardTextState();});
 let moving=false,startX=0,startY=0,baseLeft=0,baseTop=0,pointerId=null,resizingText=false,resizeTextPointerId=null,textResizeStartX=0,textResizeStartY=0,textBaseSize=36;
 move.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;selectBoardText(item,false);move.focus({preventScroll:true});moving=true;pointerId=e.pointerId;startX=e.clientX;startY=e.clientY;baseLeft=parseFloat(item.style.left)||0;baseTop=parseFloat(item.style.top)||0;try{move.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
 move.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!moving||e.pointerId!==pointerId)return;e.preventDefault();const r=item.getBoundingClientRect(),nextLeft=baseLeft+e.clientX-startX,nextTop=baseTop+e.clientY-startY,left=Math.min(window.innerWidth-24,Math.max(24-r.width,nextLeft)),top=Math.min(window.innerHeight-24,Math.max(24-r.height,nextTop));item.style.left=left+'px';item.style.top=top+'px';});
 const finishMove=e=>{if(!moving||(e&&e.pointerId!==pointerId))return;moving=false;pointerId=null;syncBoardTextState();};move.addEventListener('pointerup',finishMove);move.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishMove);
 resizeText.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==undefined&&e.button!==0)return;selectBoardText(item,false);resizeText.focus({preventScroll:true});resizingText=true;resizeTextPointerId=e.pointerId;textResizeStartX=e.clientX;textResizeStartY=e.clientY;textBaseSize=Number(item.dataset.fontSize)||boardTextDefaultSize;try{resizeText.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
 resizeText.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!resizingText||e.pointerId!==resizeTextPointerId)return;e.preventDefault();const delta=((textResizeStartX-e.clientX)+(e.clientY-textResizeStartY))/2,newSize=Math.round(Math.max(12,Math.min(240,textBaseSize+delta*.55)));item.dataset.fontSize=String(newSize);applyBoardTextStyle(item);if(selectedBoardText===item)updateBoardTextControls(item);scheduleBoardTextSave();});
 const finishTextResize=e=>{if(!resizingText||(e&&e.pointerId!==resizeTextPointerId))return;resizingText=false;resizeTextPointerId=null;syncBoardTextState();};resizeText.addEventListener('pointerup',finishTextResize);resizeText.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishTextResize);resizeText.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{if(resizingText){resizingText=false;syncBoardTextState();}});
 if(persist&&content.textContent.trim())syncBoardTextState();return item;
}
function createBoardText(){penTool='text';document.querySelectorAll('[data-tool]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.tool==='text'));canvas.style.pointerEvents='none';const item=addBoardText({fontFamily:boardTextDefaultFont,fontSize:boardTextDefaultSize,color:penColor,opacity:penOpacity,fontWeight:boardTextDefaultBold?'700':'400'},false);selectBoardText(item,true);showToast('اكتب في النص الشفاف. اسحب علامة الصليب لتحريكه، واضغط عليها ثم Delete لحذفه. اسحب السهم الصغير لتغيير حجمه، واختر Aa لتغيير الخط.');}
function restoreBoardTexts(){(Array.isArray(state.boardTexts)?state.boardTexts:[]).forEach(config=>{if(config&&String(config.text||'').trim())addBoardText(config,false);});}
function syncPdfDrawingMode(){
 const drawingNow=isPenActive();
 const frame=$('pdfSideFrame');if(frame)frame.style.pointerEvents=drawingNow?'none':'auto';
 document.body.classList.toggle('pen-drawing-active',drawingNow);
 if(drawingNow&&typeof window.clearAllSelections==='function')window.clearAllSelections();
}
function setPenTool(tool){
 penTool=tool;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-tool]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.tool===tool));
 $('stopPen')?.classList.toggle('active',tool==='stop');
 canvas.style.pointerEvents='none';
 syncPdfDrawingMode();
 if(tool==='stop'){showToast('تم تفعيل أداة السهم لتحديد العناصر وتحريكها والتحكم في حجمها.');}
}
document.querySelectorAll('[data-tool]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{setPenTool(b.dataset.tool);if(b.dataset.tool==='text')createBoardText();}));
$('boardTextFontToggle').addEventListener('click',()=>{const panel=$('boardTextFontPanel');panel.hidden=!panel.hidden;$('boardTextFontToggle').classList.toggle('active',!panel.hidden);if(!panel.hidden){updateBoardTextControls();if(selectedBoardText)selectedBoardText.classList.add('selected');}});
$('closeBoardTextFontPanel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('boardTextFontPanel').hidden=true;$('boardTextFontToggle').classList.remove('active');});
$('boardTextFont').addEventListener('change',e=>{boardTextDefaultFont=e.target.value;if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.fontFamily=boardTextDefaultFont;applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}});
$('boardTextSize').addEventListener('input',e=>{boardTextDefaultSize=Number(e.target.value)||36;$('boardTextSizeValue').value=boardTextDefaultSize+' px';if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.fontSize=String(boardTextDefaultSize);applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}});
$('boardTextBold').addEventListener('change',e=>{boardTextDefaultBold=e.target.checked;if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.fontWeight=boardTextDefaultBold?'700':'400';applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const target=e.target;if(target.closest('.board-text-item')||target.closest('#boardTextFontPanel,.toolset,.colors,.pen-settings'))return;if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.classList.remove('selected');selectedBoardText=null;}},true);
document.querySelectorAll('[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{penColor=b.dataset.color;$('customColor').value=penColor;document.querySelectorAll('.color-dot').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.color=penColor;applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}}));$('customColor').addEventListener('input',e=>{penColor=e.target.value;document.querySelectorAll('.color-dot').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.color===penColor));if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.color=penColor;applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}});
$('penWidth').addEventListener('input',e=>{penWidth=Number(e.target.value);$('widthValue').textContent=penWidth;});$('penOpacity').addEventListener('input',e=>{penOpacity=Number(e.target.value)/100;$('opacityValue').textContent=e.target.value+'%';if(selectedBoardText){selectedBoardText.dataset.opacity=String(penOpacity);applyBoardTextStyle(selectedBoardText);scheduleBoardTextSave();}});$('penSmooth').addEventListener('input',e=>{penSmooth=Number(e.target.value);$('smoothValue').textContent=['سريعة','متوسطة','ناعمة'][penSmooth];});
function hideSecondaryPenPopupsOnly(){
 const sp=$('stickerPackPopup');if(sp)sp.hidden=true;
 const hp=$('stickerHoverPreview');if(hp)hp.hidden=true;
 $('stickerPackToggle')?.classList.remove('active');
 $('stickerPackToggle')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
 const fp=$('boardTextFontPanel');if(fp)fp.hidden=true;
 $('boardTextFontToggle')?.classList.remove('active');
}
function showPenToolbar(){
 $('penToolbar').classList.add('show');
 $('penToggle').classList.add('active');
 $('penToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
 $('penToggle').setAttribute('aria-label','إخفاء أدوات القلم');
 canvas.style.pointerEvents='none';
 syncPdfDrawingMode();
}
function hidePenToolbar(){
 hideSecondaryPenPopupsOnly();
 $('penToolbar')?.classList.remove('show');
 $('penToggle')?.classList.remove('active');
 $('penToggle')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
 $('penToggle')?.setAttribute('aria-label','فتح أدوات القلم');
 canvas.style.pointerEvents='none';
 syncPdfDrawingMode();
}
$('penToggle').addEventListener('click',()=>{
 if($('penToolbar').classList.contains('show')){hidePenToolbar();}
 else{showPenToolbar();}
});
$('closePenToolbar').addEventListener('click',hidePenToolbar);$('clearCanvas').addEventListener('click',()=>{boardDrawings.length=0;state.vectorDrawings=[];ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.restore();ctx.setTransform(Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0,Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1),0,0);state.drawingData='';if(typeof window.clearAllSelections==='function')window.clearAllSelections();saveState();showToast('تم مسح جميع الرسومات.');});$('stopPen').addEventListener('click',()=>setPenTool('stop'));


const LESSON_PROMPT_TEMPLATE = `# البيانات المطلوبة للتصميم
