 const validWidths=lineMetrics.map(m=>m.w).filter(w=>w>20);
 if(bismW>20)validWidths.push(bismW);
 if(validWidths.length){
  const maxNaturalW=Math.max(...validWidths);
  if(maxNaturalW>maxInnerW){
   unifiedFont=Math.max(14,unifiedFont*(maxInnerW/maxNaturalW));
   all.style.setProperty('--mushaf-all-font-size',unifiedFont.toFixed(2)+'px');
   all.style.setProperty('--mushaf-line-font-size',unifiedFont.toFixed(2)+'px');
   lineMetrics=measureAllLines();
   bismW=getBismWidth();
  }
 }

 // اعتماد أكبر عرض طبيعي للأسطر لكي يتسع الإطار لجميع الآيات في السور القصيرة والطويلة دون قص أو تغيير لحجم خط أي سطر
 const finalWidths=lineMetrics.map(m=>m.w).filter(w=>w>20);
 if(bismW>20)finalWidths.push(bismW);
 let targetColumnW=finalWidths.length?Math.min(maxInnerW,Math.ceil(Math.max(...finalWidths))+4):maxInnerW;
 targetColumnW=Math.max(180,targetColumnW);

 const exactCardW=Math.min(maxCardW,Math.round(targetColumnW+frameSidePad*2));
 all.style.setProperty('width',exactCardW+'px','important');
 all.style.setProperty('max-width',exactCardW+'px','important');
 all.style.setProperty('flex-basis',exactCardW+'px','important');
 all.style.setProperty('padding-left',frameSidePad+'px','important');
 all.style.setProperty('padding-right',frameSidePad+'px','important');
 all.style.setProperty('--mushaf-target-width',targetColumnW+'px');

 const totalSurahVerses=Number(verseCounts[currentChapter-1])||0;
 for(let sIdx=0;sIdx<containers.length;sIdx++){
  const sheet=containers[sIdx];
  const lines=Array.from(sheet.querySelectorAll('.mushaf-line'));
  if(!lines.length)continue;
  const wrap=sheet.querySelector('.mushaf-page-lines')||sheet;
  sheet.style.setProperty('--mushaf-target-width',targetColumnW+'px');
  wrap.style.setProperty('--mushaf-target-width',targetColumnW+'px');

  for(let mIdx=0;mIdx<lines.length;mIdx++){
   const line=lines[mIdx];
   const m=lineMetrics.find(x=>x.line===line)||{w:targetColumnW,tokenCount:5,visibleCount:5,hasGhost:false};
   const {w,tokenCount}=m;
   const isLastLineInSheet=(mIdx===lines.length-1);
   const hasSurahFinalAyah=totalSurahVerses>0&&Boolean(line.querySelector(`.ayah-end[data-verse="${totalSurahVerses}"],.mushaf-ghost-end[data-ghost-verse="${totalSurahVerses}"]`));
   const midStart=line.dataset.midStart==='1';
   const midEnd=line.dataset.midEnd==='1';
   const midBoth=line.dataset.midBoth==='1';

   if(w<targetColumnW*0.90){
    if(midBoth){
     line.classList.add('mushaf-line-short');
    }else if(midStart&&!hasSurahFinalAyah){
     line.classList.add('mushaf-line-mid-start');
    }else if(midEnd){
     line.classList.add('mushaf-line-mid-end');
    }else if((isLastLineInSheet&&hasSurahFinalAyah)||(allLines.length===1&&tokenCount<=4)||tokenCount<=2){
     line.classList.add('mushaf-line-short');
    }
   }
   line.style.removeProperty('--mushaf-line-font-size');
  }
 }
 all.classList.remove('mushaf-measuring');
}
window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(state?.verseDisplayMode==='all')requestAnimationFrame(fitMushafPageLines);},{passive:true});
if(document.fonts?.ready){document.fonts.ready.then(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitMushafPageLines)).catch(()=>{});}

function buildWordSpanForVerse(v,w,index,tajWords,tajCursorRef,sourceTokens,targetVerseNo,versePage,meaningHandler=null,spokenIndexOverride=null){
 const rawMarkup=String(w?.text_uthmani_tajweed||'');
 let txt=String(w?.text_qpc_hafs||w?.text_uthmani||'').trim();
 if(!hasArabicLetters(txt)&&rawMarkup)txt=plainArabicFromMarkup(rawMarkup);
 txt=stripQuranEndMarkers(txt);
 const codeV2=String(w?.code_v2||'').trim();
 const pauseOnly=isPauseMarkWord(w)||(!hasArabicLetters(txt)&&/[ۖ-ۭ]/u.test(txt));
 if(pauseOnly){
  if(!txt&&!codeV2)return null;
  const pSpan=document.createElement('span');
  pSpan.className='quran-pause-mark';
  pSpan.textContent=txt||' ';
  pSpan.dataset.unicodeHtml=pSpan.innerHTML;
  const page=Number(w?.v2_page||w?.page_number||versePage)||0;
  if(codeV2&&page>=1&&page<=604){
   pSpan.dataset.codeV2=codeV2;
   pSpan.dataset.qcfPage=String(page);
   if(readyQcfV4Pages.has(page)&&!isCustomMushafFontSelected()){
    applyQcfGlyphToElement(pSpan,page,codeV2);
   }else{
    loadTajweedV4PageFont(page).then(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitMushafPageLines)).catch(()=>{});
   }
  }
  return pSpan;
 }
 if(!hasArabicLetters(txt)&&!w)txt=sourceTokens[index]||'';
 txt=stripQuranEndMarkers(txt);
 if(!hasArabicLetters(txt)&&!codeV2)return null;
 const span=document.createElement('span');span.className='word';
 const cleanMarkup=cleanTajweedMarkupForWord(rawMarkup||'');
 const matched=findTajweedWordForText(tajWords,txt,tajCursorRef.value);if(matched)tajCursorRef.value=matched.index+1;
 const cleanText=cleanMarkup?safePlainTajweedText(cleanMarkup):'';
 const matchedPlain=matched?.word?.markup?safePlainTajweedText(matched.word.markup):'';
 const autoMarkup=cleanTajweedMarkupForWord(txt);
 if(cleanMarkup&&comparableQuranWord(cleanText)===comparableQuranWord(txt))span.innerHTML=cleanMarkup;
 else if(matched&&matched.word?.markup&&comparableQuranWord(matchedPlain)===comparableQuranWord(txt))span.innerHTML=matched.word.markup;
 else if(autoMarkup)span.innerHTML=autoMarkup;
 else span.textContent=txt;
 if(!hasArabicLetters(span.textContent||span.innerText)&&!codeV2)return null;
 span.dataset.arabicText=txt||plainArabicFromMarkup(span.innerHTML)||'';
 span.dataset.unicodeHtml=span.innerHTML;
 const page=Number(w?.v2_page||w?.page_number||versePage)||0;
 if(codeV2&&page>=1&&page<=604){
  span.dataset.codeV2=codeV2;
  span.dataset.qcfPage=String(page);
  if(readyQcfV4Pages.has(page)&&!isCustomMushafFontSelected()){
   applyQcfGlyphToElement(span,page,codeV2);
  }else{
   loadTajweedV4PageFont(page).then(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitMushafPageLines)).catch(()=>{});
  }
 }
 const seqSpoken=Number(spokenIndexOverride)||(Number(index)+1);
 const tokPos=Number(w?.position)||(Number(index)+1);
 const exactWbwUrl='https://audio.qurancdn.com/wbw/'+String(currentChapter).padStart(3,'0')+'_'+String(targetVerseNo).padStart(3,'0')+'_'+String(seqSpoken).padStart(3,'0')+'.mp3';
 span.title='اضغط للاستماع إلى الكلمة';
 span.dataset.verse=String(targetVerseNo);
 span.dataset.wordIndex=String(index);
 span.dataset.spokenWordIndex=String(seqSpoken);
 span.dataset.wbwPosition=String(seqSpoken);
 span.dataset.tokenPosition=String(tokPos);
 span.dataset.chapterWordIndex=String(seqSpoken);
 span.dataset.audio=exactWbwUrl;
 span.addEventListener('click',()=>playWord(span,w||{},targetVerseNo));
 return span;
}
function appendVerseWords(host,v){
 const tajWords=parseTajweedWords(v.text_uthmani_tajweed||'');
 const sourceRaw=stripQuranEndMarkers(hasArabicText(v.text_qpc_hafs)?v.text_qpc_hafs:(hasArabicText(v.text_uthmani)?v.text_uthmani:plainArabicFromMarkup(v.text_uthmani_tajweed||'')));
 const sourceTokens=sourceRaw.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
 const words=Array.isArray(v.words)&&v.words.length?v.words:null;
 const targetVerseNo=Number(host.closest('.all-verse-row')?.dataset.verse)||verseNumber(v)||currentVerse;
 const versePage=Number(v?.v2_page||v?.page_number)||0;
 let count=0,spokenCount=0,lastWordSpan=null;
 const tajCursorRef={value:0};
 if(words){
  let prevWordPage=0;
  const isAllRow=host.classList?.contains('all-verse-row');
  for(const [i,w] of words.entries()){
   if(isEndMarkerWord(w))continue;
   const pauseOnly=isPauseMarkWord(w);
   if(!pauseOnly)spokenCount++;
   const wPage=Number(w?.v2_page||w?.page_number||versePage)||0;
   if(isAllRow&&prevWordPage>0&&wPage>0&&wPage!==prevWordPage){
    const intraDivider=document.createElement('div');intraDivider.className='mushaf-page-divider';intraDivider.setAttribute('role','separator');
    const badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='mushaf-page-divider-badge';badge.textContent='صفحة '+arabicNum(prevWordPage);
    intraDivider.appendChild(badge);host.appendChild(intraDivider);
   }
   if(wPage>0)prevWordPage=wPage;
   const span=buildWordSpanForVerse(v,w,i,tajWords,tajCursorRef,sourceTokens,targetVerseNo,versePage,null,spokenCount);
   if(span){
    if(pauseOnly&&lastWordSpan&&lastWordSpan.parentElement===host){
     const pair=document.createElement('span');
     pair.className='mushaf-word-end-pair';
     host.replaceChild(pair,lastWordSpan);
     pair.appendChild(lastWordSpan);
     pair.appendChild(span);
     lastWordSpan=pair;
    }else{
     if(count)host.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
     host.appendChild(span);
     lastWordSpan=span;
     count++;
    }
   }
  }
 }
 if(!count){let cursor=0;for(const [i,txt] of sourceTokens.entries()){
  if(!hasArabicLetters(txt)||/[۝۞۝۞]/u.test(txt))continue;
  spokenCount++;
  const matched=findTajweedWordForText(tajWords,txt,cursor);if(matched)cursor=matched.index+1;
  const span=document.createElement('span');span.className='word';if(matched?.word?.markup)span.innerHTML=matched.word.markup;else span.textContent=txt;
  if(!hasArabicLetters(span.textContent||span.innerText))span.textContent=txt;
  span.dataset.arabicText=txt;
  span.dataset.unicodeHtml=span.innerHTML;
  if(count)host.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));span.dataset.verse=String(targetVerseNo);span.dataset.wordIndex=String(i);span.dataset.spokenWordIndex=String(spokenCount);span.dataset.chapterWordIndex=String(spokenCount);span.dataset.audio=wordAudioUrl(null,currentChapter,targetVerseNo,spokenCount);span.title='اضغط للاستماع إلى الكلمة';span.addEventListener('click',()=>playWord(span,{},targetVerseNo));
  host.appendChild(span);count++;
 }}
 return count;
}
function toggleAllViewTafsir(row,v){
 const allCard=$('allVersesCard');if(!allCard)return;
 const n=verseNumber(v),key=currentChapter+':'+n;
 const existing=allCard.querySelector(`.all-verse-tafsir[data-tafsir-verse="${n}"]`);
 if(existing){existing.remove();return;}
 allCard.querySelectorAll('.all-verse-tafsir').forEach(el=>el.remove());
 const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='all-verse-tafsir';box.dataset.tafsirVerse=String(n);
 const text=document.createElement('span');text.textContent=state.customTafsirs[key]??state.tafsir[key]??'جارٍ محاولة جلب التفسير الميسر…';box.appendChild(text);
 const edit=document.createElement('button');edit.type='button';edit.textContent='تعديل المعنى';edit.addEventListener('click',()=>{currentVerse=n;currentIndex=verses.findIndex(x=>verseNumber(x)===n);openTafsir();});box.appendChild(edit);
 const lineHost=row?.classList?.contains('mushaf-line')?row:row?.closest?.('.mushaf-line');
 if(lineHost&&lineHost.parentElement){lineHost.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',box);}
 else{(row||allCard).appendChild(box);}
 if(!(state.customTafsirs[key]??state.tafsir[key]))fetchTafsirForKey(key).then(t=>{if(t){state.tafsir[key]=t;saveState();if(box.isConnected)text.textContent=t;}else if(box.isConnected)text.textContent='لم يتوفر التفسير تلقائيًا لهذا الاتصال.';});
}
renderCurrentVerse = function(){
 const single=document.querySelector('.verse-stage .verse-card'),all=$('allVersesCard');
 if(state.verseDisplayMode==='single'||document.body.classList.contains('blank-display')){
  all.hidden=true;single.hidden=false;document.querySelector('.verse-stage').classList.remove('all-verses-mode');document.querySelector('.verse-stage').style.removeProperty('--all-verses-top');document.querySelector('.center-wrap').classList.remove('all-view-wrap');
  singleVerseRenderer();updateVerseViewButton();return;
 }
 const v=currentVerseObj();if(!v){singleVerseRenderer();return;}
 ensureTajweedV4FontsForVerses(verses);
 const oldScroll=all.scrollTop;all.tabIndex=0;all.setAttribute('role','region');single.hidden=true;all.hidden=false;document.querySelector('.verse-stage').classList.add('all-verses-mode');document.querySelector('.center-wrap').classList.add('all-view-wrap');updateAllVerseStagePosition();
 $('surahTitle').textContent=surahNames[currentChapter-1];
 all.innerHTML='';
 const firstN=verses.length?verseNumber(verses[0]):currentVerse;
 const createMushafPageDivider=(pageNo)=>{
  const p=Number(pageNo);if(!Number.isInteger(p)||p<1)return null;
  const divider=document.createElement('div');divider.className='mushaf-page-divider';divider.setAttribute('role','separator');divider.setAttribute('aria-label','صفحة '+arabicNum(p));
  const badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='mushaf-page-divider-badge';badge.textContent=arabicNum(p);
  divider.appendChild(badge);return divider;
 };
 const makeMushafGhostNode=(node,verseNo)=>{
  if(!node)return null;
  const ghost=node.cloneNode(true);
  const isEnd=ghost.classList.contains('ayah-end');
  ghost.classList.remove('word','ayah-end','all-ayah-end','current-row','speaking');
  ghost.classList.add('mushaf-ghost-token',isEnd?'mushaf-ghost-end':'mushaf-ghost-word');
  if(verseNo)ghost.dataset.ghostVerse=String(verseNo);
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-verse');
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-spoken-word-index');
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-wbw-position');
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-token-position');
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-chapter-word-index');
  ghost.removeAttribute('data-audio');
  ghost.removeAttribute('title');
  ghost.removeAttribute('aria-label');
  ghost.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  ghost.tabIndex=-1;
  ghost.querySelectorAll('.word,.ayah-end,[data-verse]').forEach(el=>{
   const childEnd=el.classList.contains('ayah-end');
   el.classList.remove('word','ayah-end','all-ayah-end','speaking');
   el.classList.add('mushaf-ghost-token',childEnd?'mushaf-ghost-end':'mushaf-ghost-word');
   el.removeAttribute('data-verse');
   el.removeAttribute('title');
   el.removeAttribute('aria-label');
   el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  });
  return ghost;
 };
 const hasLineNumbers=verses.some(item=>Array.isArray(item.words)&&item.words.some(w=>Number(w?.line_number)>0));
 if(state.verseDisplayMode==='all'&&hasLineNumbers){
  const selectedVerseSet=new Set(verses.map(vItem=>verseNumber(vItem)));
  const firstSelectedVerse=verses.length?verseNumber(verses[0]):1;
  const lastSelectedVerse=verses.length?verseNumber(verses[verses.length-1]):firstSelectedVerse;
  const activeLineKeys=new Set();
  verses.forEach(item=>{
   const versePage=Number(item?.v2_page||item?.page_number)||602;
   const rawWords=Array.isArray(item.words)?item.words:[];
   let lastP=versePage,lastL=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(rawWords[0]?.line_number)||1));
   rawWords.forEach(w=>{
    if(isEndMarkerWord(w))return;
    lastP=Number(w?.v2_page||w?.page_number||versePage)||versePage;
    lastL=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(w?.line_number)||lastL||1));
    activeLineKeys.add(`${lastP}:${lastL}`);
   });
   const endP=Number(item.end_word?.v2_page||item.end_word?.page_number||lastP)||lastP;
   const endL=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(item.end_word?.line_number||lastL)||lastL));
   activeLineKeys.add(`${endP}:${endL}`);
  });
  const sourceVerses=(Array.isArray(fullChapterVerses)&&fullChapterVerses.length>=verses.length)?fullChapterVerses:verses;
  const pagesMap=new Map();
  const ensurePageEntry=(pNo,vItem)=>{
   const p=pNo||Number(vItem?.v2_page||vItem?.page_number)||602;
   const juzNo=resolveJuzForPage(p,vItem?.juz_number);
   if(!pagesMap.has(p))pagesMap.set(p,{page:p,juz:juzNo,hizb:Number(vItem?.hizb_number)||0,lines:new Map(),firstVerse:verseNumber(vItem)});
   return pagesMap.get(p);
  };
  const ensureLineEntry=(pageObj,lineNo)=>{
   const ln=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(lineNo)||1));
   if(!pageObj.lines.has(ln))pageObj.lines.set(ln,[]);
   return pageObj.lines.get(ln);
  };
  sourceVerses.forEach((item,vIdx)=>{
   const n=verseNumber(item);
   const isSelected=selectedVerseSet.has(n);
   const versePage=Number(item?.v2_page||item?.page_number)||602;
   const tajWords=parseTajweedWords(item.text_uthmani_tajweed||'');
   const sourceRaw=stripQuranEndMarkers(hasArabicText(item.text_qpc_hafs)?item.text_qpc_hafs:(hasArabicText(item.text_uthmani)?item.text_uthmani:plainArabicFromMarkup(item.text_uthmani_tajweed||'')));
   const sourceTokens=sourceRaw.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
   const tajCursorRef={value:0};
   const rawWords=Array.isArray(item.words)?item.words:[];
   let lastPageNo=versePage;
   let lastLineNo=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(rawWords[0]?.line_number)||1));
   const meaningHandlerForVerse=isSelected?(async e=>{
    if(e)e.stopPropagation();
    if(currentVerse!==n)await goToVerse(n);
    const activeLine=$('allVersesCard')?.querySelector(`.mushaf-line .ayah-end[data-verse="${n}"]`)?.closest('.mushaf-line')
     ||$('allVersesCard')?.querySelector(`.mushaf-line .word[data-verse="${n}"]`)?.closest('.mushaf-line')
     ||$('allVersesCard')?.querySelector(`.all-verse-row[data-verse="${n}"]`);
    if(activeLine)toggleAllViewTafsir(activeLine,item);
   }):null;
   if(isRubElHizbStart(item,vIdx,sourceVerses)&&!firstWordHasEmbeddedRubGlyph(item)){
    if(activeLineKeys.has(`${lastPageNo}:${lastLineNo}`)){
     const pageObj=ensurePageEntry(lastPageNo,item);
     const rubNode=createQuranSectionMarker();
     ensureLineEntry(pageObj,lastLineNo).push({type:'rub',verse:n,item,ghost:!isSelected,node:isSelected?rubNode:makeMushafGhostNode(rubNode,n)});
    }
   }
   let spokenIdxInVerse=0;
   rawWords.forEach((w,wIdx)=>{
    if(isEndMarkerWord(w))return;
    const pauseOnly=isPauseMarkWord(w);
    if(!pauseOnly)spokenIdxInVerse++;
    const wPage=Number(w?.v2_page||w?.page_number||versePage)||versePage;
    const wLine=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(w?.line_number)||lastLineNo||1));
    lastPageNo=wPage;
    lastLineNo=wLine;
    if(!activeLineKeys.has(`${wPage}:${wLine}`))return;
    const lastPageObj=ensurePageEntry(wPage,item);
    const span=buildWordSpanForVerse(item,w,wIdx,tajWords,tajCursorRef,sourceTokens,n,wPage,meaningHandlerForVerse,spokenIdxInVerse);
    if(span){
     const finalSpan=isSelected?span:makeMushafGhostNode(span,n);
     const lineArr=ensureLineEntry(lastPageObj,wLine);
     if(pauseOnly&&lineArr.length&&lineArr[lineArr.length-1].type==='word'&&lineArr[lineArr.length-1].ghost===!isSelected){
      const prevTok=lineArr[lineArr.length-1];
      const pair=document.createElement('span');
      pair.className='mushaf-word-end-pair'+(isSelected?'':' mushaf-ghost-token');
      pair.appendChild(prevTok.node);
      pair.appendChild(finalSpan);
      prevTok.node=pair;
     }else{
      lineArr.push({type:pauseOnly?'pause':'word',verse:n,item,ghost:!isSelected,node:finalSpan});
     }
    }
   });
   // يوضع رقم الآية في موضعه المعتمد في المصحف
   const endPageNo=Number(item.end_word?.v2_page||item.end_word?.page_number||lastPageNo)||lastPageNo;
   const endLineNo=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,Number(item.end_word?.line_number||lastLineNo)||lastLineNo));
   if(activeLineKeys.has(`${endPageNo}:${endLineNo}`)){
    const endBtn=createAyahEndMarker(n,meaningHandlerForVerse,item);
    endBtn.classList.add('all-ayah-end');
    endBtn.setAttribute('aria-label','عرض تفسير الآية '+n);
    const endPageObj=ensurePageEntry(endPageNo,item);
    ensureLineEntry(endPageObj,endLineNo).push({type:'end',verse:n,item,ghost:!isSelected,node:isSelected?endBtn:makeMushafGhostNode(endBtn,n)});
   }
  });
  const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();
  const pageList=[...pagesMap.values()];
  pageList.forEach((pageObj,pIdx)=>{
   const sheet=document.createElement('article');
   sheet.className='mushaf-page-sheet';
   sheet.dataset.mushafPage=String(pageObj.page);
   const pageHead=document.createElement('header');
   pageHead.className='mushaf-page-header';
   const juzVal=resolveJuzForPage(pageObj.page,pageObj.juz);
   pageHead.innerHTML=`<span>سورة ${safeText(surahNames[currentChapter-1]||'')}</span><span>الجزء ${arabicNum(juzVal)}</span><span>صفحة ${arabicNum(pageObj.page)}</span>`;
   sheet.appendChild(pageHead);
   if(pIdx===0&&firstN===1&&currentChapter!==1&&currentChapter!==9){
    const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='all-bismillah mushaf-page-bismillah';b.innerHTML=BISMILLAH_TAJWEED_HTML;sheet.appendChild(b);
   }
   const linesWrap=document.createElement('div');
   linesWrap.className='mushaf-page-lines';
   const sortedLines=[...pageObj.lines.keys()].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
   sortedLines.forEach(lineNo=>{
    const tokens=pageObj.lines.get(lineNo)||[];
    if(!tokens.length)return;
    const lineEl=document.createElement('div');
    lineEl.className='mushaf-line';
    lineEl.dataset.line=String(lineNo);
    const hasLeadingGhost=tokens.some(t=>t.ghost&&t.verse<firstSelectedVerse);
    const hasTrailingGhost=tokens.some(t=>t.ghost&&t.verse>lastSelectedVerse);
    if(hasLeadingGhost&&hasTrailingGhost)lineEl.dataset.midBoth='1';
    else if(hasLeadingGhost)lineEl.dataset.midStart='1';
    else if(hasTrailingGhost)lineEl.dataset.midEnd='1';
    let currentRunVerse=null,currentRunSpan=null,currentRunGhost=null;
    for(let tIdx=0;tIdx<tokens.length;tIdx++){
     const tok=tokens[tIdx];
     if(tok.ghost){
      currentRunVerse=null;
      if(!currentRunGhost){
       currentRunGhost=document.createElement('span');
       currentRunGhost.className='mushaf-ghost-segment';
       currentRunGhost.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
       lineEl.appendChild(currentRunGhost);
      }
      if(tok.node)currentRunGhost.appendChild(tok.node);
      continue;
     }
     currentRunGhost=null;
     if(tok.verse!==currentRunVerse||!currentRunSpan){
      currentRunVerse=tok.verse;
      currentRunSpan=document.createElement('span');
      currentRunSpan.className='all-verse-row mushaf-verse-segment'+(tok.verse===currentVerse?' current-row':'');
      currentRunSpan.dataset.verse=String(tok.verse);
      currentRunSpan.addEventListener('click',e=>{
       if(e.target.closest('button,.word,.all-verse-tafsir,.quran-section-marker,.quran-sajdah-marker'))return;
       if(currentVerse!==tok.verse)goToVerse(tok.verse);
      });
      lineEl.appendChild(currentRunSpan);
     }
     if(tok.node){
      currentRunSpan.appendChild(tok.node);
     }
    }
    linesWrap.appendChild(lineEl);
   });
   sheet.appendChild(linesWrap);
   fragment.appendChild(sheet);
   if(pIdx<pageList.length-1){
    const divider=createMushafPageDivider(pageObj.page);
    if(divider)fragment.appendChild(divider);
   }
  });
  all.appendChild(fragment);
  if(typeof refreshRenderedQcfFonts==='function')refreshRenderedQcfFonts();
  requestAnimationFrame(fitMushafPageLines);
  setTimeout(fitMushafPageLines,90);
  setTimeout(fitMushafPageLines,220);
 }else{
  if(firstN===1&&currentChapter!==1&&currentChapter!==9){const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='all-bismillah';b.innerHTML=BISMILLAH_TAJWEED_HTML;all.appendChild(b);}
  const getVerseStartPage=(vObj)=>Number(vObj?.words?.[0]?.v2_page||vObj?.words?.[0]?.page_number||vObj?.v2_page||vObj?.page_number)||0;
  const getVerseEndPage=(vObj)=>{
   const ws=Array.isArray(vObj?.words)?vObj.words:[];
   const lastW=ws.length?ws[ws.length-1]:null;
   return Number(vObj?.end_word?.v2_page||vObj?.end_word?.page_number||lastW?.v2_page||lastW?.page_number||vObj?.v2_page||vObj?.page_number)||0;
  };
  const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();
  verses.forEach((item,index)=>{
   const n=verseNumber(item),row=document.createElement('div');row.className='all-verse-row'+(n===currentVerse?' current-row':'');row.dataset.verse=String(n);
   if(isRubElHizbStart(item,index,verses)&&!firstWordHasEmbeddedRubGlyph(item)){row.appendChild(createQuranSectionMarker());row.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));}
   appendVerseWords(row,item);
   const end=createAyahEndMarker(n,async e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(currentVerse!==n)await goToVerse(n);const newRow=$('allVersesCard').querySelector('.all-verse-row[data-verse="'+n+'"]');if(newRow)toggleAllViewTafsir(newRow,item);},item);end.classList.add('all-ayah-end');end.setAttribute('aria-label','عرض تفسير الآية '+n);
   const lastWEl=row.querySelector('.word:last-of-type');
   if(lastWEl&&lastWEl.parentElement===row){
    const pair=document.createElement('span');pair.className='mushaf-word-end-pair';
    row.replaceChild(pair,lastWEl);pair.appendChild(lastWEl);pair.appendChild(end);
   }else{row.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));row.appendChild(end);}
   row.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('button,.word,.all-verse-tafsir,.quran-section-marker,.quran-sajdah-marker'))return;if(currentVerse!==n)goToVerse(n);});fragment.appendChild(row);fragment.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
   const endPage=getVerseEndPage(item);
   const nextItem=verses[index+1];
   const nextStartPage=nextItem?getVerseStartPage(nextItem):0;
   if(endPage>0&&(!nextItem||(nextStartPage>0&&nextStartPage!==endPage))){
    const divider=createMushafPageDivider(endPage);
    if(divider)fragment.appendChild(divider);
   }
  });
  all.appendChild(fragment);
 }
 all.scrollTop=oldScroll;
 const active=all.querySelector('.all-verse-row.current-row');if(active&&currentIndex>=0)requestAnimationFrame(()=>scrollVerseRowIntoView(currentVerse,false));
 $('prevVerse').disabled=currentIndex<=0;$('nextVerse').disabled=currentIndex>=verses.length-1;$('playVerse').disabled=false;renderVerseNav();updateVerseViewButton();
};
function setVerseDisplayMode(mode){state.verseDisplayMode=mode==='single'?'single':'all';$('verseViewMenu').hidden=true;$('verseViewToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');stopAudio();renderCurrentVerse();saveState();showToast(state.verseDisplayMode==='all'?'تم عرض جميع الآيات.':'تم عرض آية واحدة.');}
$('verseViewToggle').addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();$('verseViewMenu').hidden=!$('verseViewMenu').hidden;$('verseViewToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!$('verseViewMenu').hidden));});
$('viewSingleVerse').addEventListener('click',()=>setVerseDisplayMode('single'));
$('viewAllVerses').addEventListener('click',()=>setVerseDisplayMode('all'));
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('#verseViewWrap')){$('verseViewMenu').hidden=true;$('verseViewToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}});

// ضمّ اختيارات وضع العرض في زر واحد بقائمة فوقه.
updateAgeModeButton = function(){
 const mode=state.displayMode||state.ageMode||'adult';
 const buttons=[['adultModeButton','adult','وضع الكبار'],['childModeButton','child','وضع الأطفال'],['boardModeButton','board','وضع السبورة البيضاء']];
 for(const [id,key,label] of buttons){const b=$(id);if(b){b.classList.toggle('active',mode===key);b.title=label;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(mode===key));}}
 const trigger=$('modeMenuToggle'),source=$(mode==='child'?'childModeButton':mode==='board'?'boardModeButton':'adultModeButton');
 if(trigger&&source){$('modeCurrentIcon').innerHTML=source.querySelector('svg').outerHTML;trigger.title='الوضع الحالي: '+(mode==='child'?'الأطفال':mode==='board'?'السبورة البيضاء':'الكبار')+' — اضغط لاختيار وضع آخر';trigger.setAttribute('aria-label',trigger.title);}
 [['modeAdultChoice','adult'],['modeChildChoice','child'],['modeBoardChoice','board']].forEach(([id,key])=>$(id)?.classList.toggle('active',mode===key));
};
$('modeMenuToggle').addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();$('modeMenu').hidden=!$('modeMenu').hidden;$('modeMenuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!$('modeMenu').hidden));});
$('modeAdultChoice').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('modeMenu').hidden=true;$('modeMenuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');applyDisplayMode('adult',true);});
$('modeChildChoice').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('modeMenu').hidden=true;$('modeMenuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');applyDisplayMode('child',true);});
$('modeBoardChoice').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('modeMenu').hidden=true;$('modeMenuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');applyDisplayMode('board',true);});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('#modePickerWrap')){$('modeMenu').hidden=true;$('modeMenuToggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');}});

// عرض الصورة بخلفيتها وأبعادها الأصلية؛ درجة الوضوح تُتحكم بها عبر طبقة التبييض فقط.
setBackground = function(name,custom=''){
 state.background=name;const app=$('app');app.classList.remove('dark-mode','background-image');app.style.background='';
 document.body.style.backgroundImage='';document.body.style.backgroundPosition='';document.body.style.backgroundSize='';document.body.style.backgroundAttachment='';document.body.style.backgroundRepeat='';document.body.style.backgroundColor='';document.body.style.background='';
 const clarity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(state.ageBackgroundOpacity?.[state.ageMode]??100)))/100;
 const fade=Number((1-clarity).toFixed(3));
 if(name==='paper'){document.body.style.background='radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%,rgba(217,194,137,.17),transparent 28%),linear-gradient(135deg,#f7f4e9,#edf5ef)';}
 else if(name==='green'){document.body.style.background='radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#f7f7e8,#d9ebd5 55%,#b9d5c0)';}
 else if(name==='blue'){document.body.style.background='radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#f7fbff,#deeff4 52%,#c8dfea)';}
 else if(name==='rose'){document.body.style.background='radial-gradient(circle at 20% 0%,#fff9f4,#f6e1d7 55%,#edd2c5)';}
 else if(name==='night'){app.classList.add('dark-mode');app.style.background='radial-gradient(circle at 18% 15%,#29463a,#111e19 65%)';}
 else if(name==='custom'&&custom){const white=`rgba(255,255,255,${fade})`;document.body.style.backgroundColor='#fff';document.body.style.backgroundImage=`linear-gradient(${white},${white}),url("${custom}")`;document.body.style.backgroundPosition='center center,center center';document.body.style.backgroundSize='cover,cover';document.body.style.backgroundAttachment='fixed,fixed';document.body.style.backgroundRepeat='no-repeat,no-repeat';app.classList.add('background-image');}
 document.querySelectorAll('.bg-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.bg===name&&(name!=='custom'||Number(b.dataset.customIndex)===state.customBackgroundIndex)));paintCustomBackgroundButtons();if(typeof refreshRenderedQcfFonts==='function')refreshRenderedQcfFonts();
};

// الرسم يبدأ فقط عندما تكون أدوات القلم مفتوحة، ولا يمنع العناصر من استقبال النقرات.
function fixPenToolbarSizing(){const bar=$('penToolbar');if(!bar)return;bar.style.width='max-content';bar.style.maxWidth='calc(100vw - 145px)';}
fixPenToolbarSizing();

// ضم كبسولة السورة وشريط التحكم في صف علوي واحد لتوفير مساحة أكبر للمصحف.
function arrangeControlCardInHeader(){
 const topbar=document.querySelector('.topbar'),pill=$('surahPill'),card=document.querySelector('.control-card');
 if(!topbar||!pill||!card||pill.parentElement?.id==='surahControlGroup')return;
 const group=document.createElement('div');group.className='surah-control-group';group.id='surahControlGroup';
 topbar.insertBefore(group,pill);group.appendChild(pill);group.appendChild(card);
}
arrangeControlCardInHeader();
// إغلاق نافذة أرقام الآيات بالنقر خارجها فقط؛ الضغط داخلها لا يغلقها قبل الاختيار.
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('#verseNavWrap')&&showAllNumbers){showAllNumbers=false;renderVerseNav();}});

// لصق الصور والنصوص مباشرة فوق مساحة البرنامج باستخدام Ctrl+V.
let lastBoardPastePoint=null,boardInternalClipboard=null;
function isBoardPasteUi(target){return !!target?.closest?.('button,input,select,textarea,a,.pen-toolbar,.sticker-pack-popup,.board-text-font-panel,.settings-panel,.modal,.settings-backdrop,.modal-backdrop');}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!isBoardPasteUi(e.target))lastBoardPastePoint={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};},true);
document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!isBoardPasteUi(e.target))lastBoardPastePoint={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};},{capture:true,passive:true});
function boardPastePosition(width=260,height=120){
 const p=lastBoardPastePoint||{x:window.innerWidth*.5,y:window.innerHeight*.42};
 return {left:Math.max(8,Math.min(Math.max(8,window.innerWidth-width-8),p.x+10)),top:Math.max(76,Math.min(Math.max(76,window.innerHeight-height-8),p.y+10))};
}
function clipboardImageDimensions(src){return new Promise(resolve=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>resolve({width:img.naturalWidth||640,height:img.naturalHeight||480});img.onerror=()=>resolve({width:640,height:480});img.src=src;});}
async function pasteImageSource(src,label='الصورة المنسوخة'){
 if(!src)return;
 try{
  const dim=await clipboardImageDimensions(src),maxW=Math.max(160,Math.min(window.innerWidth*.76,1100)),maxH=Math.max(140,Math.min(window.innerHeight*.70,900));
  const scale=Math.min(1,maxW/dim.width,maxH/dim.height),width=Math.max(32,Math.round(dim.width*scale)),height=Math.max(32,Math.round(dim.height*scale)),pos=boardPastePosition(width,height);
  const item=addSticker(src,0,{...pos,width,height},true);selectSticker(item);showToast('تم لصق الصورة بجودتها الأصلية. اسحبها إلى المكان الذي تريده أو غيّر حجمها من المقبض.');
 }catch(_){showToast('تعذر لصق الصورة من الحافظة؛ جرّب نسخ الصورة نفسها مرة أخرى.');}
}
function clipboardHtmlImage(html){
 if(!html)return '';
 try{
  const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html'),img=doc.querySelector('img');
  let src=img?.getAttribute('src')||'';
  if(!src&&img?.getAttribute('srcset'))src=img.getAttribute('srcset').split(',')[0].trim().split(/\s+/)[0];
  if(/^data:image\//i.test(src))return src;
  const url=new URL(src,location.href);return /^https?:$/.test(url.protocol)?url.href:'';
 }catch(_){return '';}
}
function pasteBoardText(text){
 text=String(text||'').replace(/\u0000/g,'');if(!text.trim())return;
 const pos=boardPastePosition(Math.min(320,window.innerWidth*.5),80);
 const item=addBoardText({text,left:pos.left,top:pos.top,fontFamily:boardTextDefaultFont,fontSize:boardTextDefaultSize,color:penColor,opacity:penOpacity,fontWeight:boardTextDefaultBold?'700':'400'},false);
 selectBoardText(item,true);syncBoardTextState();showToast('تم لصق النص كعنصر شفاف قابل للتحرير والتحريك.');
}
function duplicateBoardClipboard(){
 if(!boardInternalClipboard)return false;
 const data=boardInternalClipboard;
 if(data.type==='text'&&data.config){const cfg={...data.config,left:(Number(data.config.left)||0)+24,top:(Number(data.config.top)||0)+24};const item=addBoardText(cfg,false);selectBoardText(item,true);syncBoardTextState();showToast('تم لصق نسخة من النص.');return true;}
 return false;
}
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
 if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)||e.altKey)return;
 const target=e.target;
 if(e.key.toLowerCase()==='c'&&selectedBoardText&&!target?.isContentEditable&&!isBoardPasteUi(target)){
  const config=boardTextData(selectedBoardText),text=config.text;
  if(text){boardInternalClipboard={type:'text',config:{...config},text};try{navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text).catch(()=>{});}catch(_){} }
  return;
 }
 if(e.key.toLowerCase()==='v')return; // handled in the paste event, which can read image clipboard items.
});
document.addEventListener('paste',e=>{
 const clip=e.clipboardData;if(!clip)return;
 const target=e.target;
 const items=Array.from(clip.items||[]),imageFiles=[];
 for(const item of items){if(item.kind==='file'&&/^image\//i.test(item.type)){const file=item.getAsFile();if(file)imageFiles.push(file);}}
 if(imageFiles.length){
  e.preventDefault();
  imageFiles.slice(0,4).forEach(async(file,i)=>{try{const src=await readOriginalImageDataUrl(file);await pasteImageSource(src,'الصورة المنسوخة');}catch(_){showToast('تعذر قراءة الصورة المنسوخة من الحافظة.');}});
  return;
 }
 const html=(()=>{try{return clip.getData('text/html')||'';}catch(_){return '';}})();
 const htmlSrc=clipboardHtmlImage(html);
 if(htmlSrc){e.preventDefault();pasteImageSource(htmlSrc);return;}
 let text='';try{text=clip.getData('text/plain')||'';}catch(_){}
 if(!text&&html){try{text=new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html').body?.textContent||'';}catch(_){}}
 if(/^https?:\/\/\S+\.(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|bmp|svg)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(text.trim())){e.preventDefault();pasteImageSource(text.trim());return;}
 if(!text.trim())return;
 // داخل محرر نص نشط، اترك المتصفح يلصق النص داخل المحرر؛ خلاف ذلك أضفه على الشاشة كعنصر شفاف.
 if(target?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(target?.tagName||''))return;
 e.preventDefault();
 if(boardInternalClipboard?.type==='text'&&boardInternalClipboard.text===text.trim()&&duplicateBoardClipboard())return;
 pasteBoardText(text);
});

try{if(navigator.storage?.persist)navigator.storage.persist().catch(()=>{});}catch(_){}
restorePersistentState().then(async()=>{applyLoadedSettings();restoreDrawingFromState();await restoreStickers();restoreBoardTexts();await restoreSavedRecording();if(typeof setVersesTrayHidden==='function')setVersesTrayHidden(!!state.versesHidden);chosenRange=state.chosenRange||null;const startChapter=Number(state.lastChapter)||108;const startVerse=Number(state.lastVerse)||1;await loadChapter(startChapter,startVerse);if(state.blankDisplay){document.body.classList.add('blank-display');$('surahTitle').textContent='اختر السورة';}if(state.displayMode==='board')applyDisplayMode('board',false);if(state.pdfPanelWasOpen){try{await openPdfSidePanel();const panel=$('pdfSidePanel'),g=state.pdfPanelGeometry;if(panel&&g){panel.style.left=Number(g.left)+'px';panel.style.top=Number(g.top)+'px';panel.style.width=Number(g.width)+'px';panel.style.height=Number(g.height)+'px';panel.style.right='auto';}if($('pdfStretchX'))$('pdfStretchX').value=String(state.pdfStretchX||100);if($('pdfStretchY'))$('pdfStretchY').value=String(state.pdfStretchY||100);applyPdfStretch();}catch(_){}}if(typeof renderScreenTray==='function')renderScreenTray();}).catch(()=>{applyLoadedSettings();loadChapter(Number(state.lastChapter)||108,Number(state.lastVerse)||1);});

/* === تعديلات المستخدم: شعار ثابت، PDF جانبي، خلفيات السبورة، قائمة العناصر، وترتيب نافذة النطاق === */
// 1) شعار البرنامج للعرض فقط؛ لا يفتح نافذة المصحف.
try{
 const oldLogo=$('logoButton');
 if(oldLogo){
  const logo=document.createElement('div');logo.id='logoButton';logo.className=oldLogo.className;logo.innerHTML=oldLogo.innerHTML;
  logo.setAttribute('role','img');logo.setAttribute('aria-label','شعار رحلة القرآن');logo.title='شعار رحلة القرآن';
  oldLogo.replaceWith(logo);
 }
}catch(_){ }
// أخفِ اختيارات رابط المصحف من الإعدادات مع الإبقاء على البيانات القديمة والملف المحفوظ.
try{
 document.querySelector('[data-settings-tab="mushaf"]')?.setAttribute('hidden','');
 document.querySelector('[data-settings-category="mushaf"]')?.setAttribute('hidden','');
 if(activeSettingsCategory==='mushaf')activeSettingsCategory='parts';
}catch(_){ }

// الإعدادات الافتراضية للسبورة: نعومة 100% وشفافية كاملة.
try{
 penSmooth=2;penOpacity=1;
 const oldSmooth=$('penSmooth');
 if(oldSmooth){const smooth=oldSmooth.cloneNode(true);smooth.value='2';oldSmooth.replaceWith(smooth);smooth.addEventListener('input',e=>{penSmooth=Number(e.target.value);$('smoothValue').textContent=Math.round(penSmooth/2*100)+'%';});}
 if($('smoothValue'))$('smoothValue').textContent='100%';
 if($('penOpacity'))$('penOpacity').value='100';
 if($('opacityValue'))$('opacityValue').textContent='100%';
}catch(_){ }

// إعداد بيانات خلفية السبورة دون المساس بخلفيات الكبار والأطفال.
state.ageBackgrounds={adult:[],child:[],board:[],...(state.ageBackgrounds||{})};
state.ageBackgroundIndex={adult:0,child:0,board:0,...(state.ageBackgroundIndex||{})};
state.ageBackgroundOpacity={adult:100,child:100,board:100,...(state.ageBackgroundOpacity||{})};
state.boardBackgroundColor=state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc';
state.boardBackgroundType=state.boardBackgroundType||'color';
state.frameShapes={...(state.frameShapes||{})};

function applyBoardBackground(save=true){
 const images=Array.isArray(state.ageBackgrounds?.board)?state.ageBackgrounds.board:[];
 const idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(images.length-1,Number(state.ageBackgroundIndex?.board)||0));
 state.ageBackgroundIndex.board=idx;
 const opacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(state.ageBackgroundOpacity?.board??100)))/100;
 document.body.style.setProperty('--board-background-color',state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc');
 let image='none';
 if(state.boardBackgroundType==='image'&&images[idx]){
  const wash=(1-opacity).toFixed(3);
  image=`linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,${wash}),rgba(255,255,255,${wash})),url("${images[idx]}")`;
 }
 document.body.style.setProperty('--board-background-image',image);
 document.body.style.backgroundColor=state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc';
 if(state.displayMode==='board'){
  document.body.classList.add('whiteboard-mode');
  document.body.style.setProperty('background-color',state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc','important');
  document.body.style.setProperty('background-image',image,'important');
  document.body.style.setProperty('background-size','cover','important');
  document.body.style.setProperty('background-position','center center','important');
 }
 if(save)saveState();
}
const baseApplyDisplayModeForBoard=applyDisplayMode;
applyDisplayMode=function(mode,save=true){
 if(mode!=='board'){baseApplyDisplayModeForBoard(mode,save);return;}
 baseApplyDisplayModeForBoard('board',false);applyBoardBackground(false);updateAgeModeButton();if(save)saveState();
};
const baseCycleAgeBackgroundForBoard=cycleAgeBackground;
cycleAgeBackground=function(delta){
 if(state.displayMode!=='board'){baseCycleAgeBackgroundForBoard(delta);return;}
 const images=state.ageBackgrounds.board||[];
 if(images.length){state.ageBackgroundType='image';state.boardBackgroundType='image';state.ageBackgroundIndex.board=(Number(state.ageBackgroundIndex.board||0)+delta+images.length)%images.length;applyBoardBackground(true);}
 else{const colors=['#f0e2cc','#ffffff','#f7f4e9','#e8f0e6','#e4eff7','#f8e9ee','#1f332a'];let i=colors.indexOf(state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc');i=(i+delta+colors.length)%colors.length;state.boardBackgroundColor=colors[i];state.boardBackgroundType='color';applyBoardBackground(true);}
 renderSettingsBackgrounds();
};

// خلفيات الإعدادات: كل صورة لها زر حذف منفرد، مع مجموعة ثالثة خاصة بالسبورة.
function ensureBoardBackgroundControls(){
 const section=document.querySelector('[data-settings-category="backgrounds"]');if(!section||$('boardColorOptions'))return;
 const pickerWrap=document.createElement('div');pickerWrap.id='boardColorOptions';pickerWrap.hidden=true;
 pickerWrap.innerHTML=`<strong style="font-size:11px;color:#536d5c;width:100%">لون خلفية السبورة</strong>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#f0e2cc" title="بيج دافئ" style="background:#f0e2cc"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#ffffff" title="أبيض" style="background:#ffffff"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#f7f4e9" title="ورقي فاتح" style="background:#f7f4e9"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#e8f0e6" title="أخضر فاتح" style="background:#e8f0e6"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#e4eff7" title="أزرق فاتح" style="background:#e4eff7"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#f8e9ee" title="وردي فاتح" style="background:#f8e9ee"></button>
 <button type="button" class="board-color-chip" data-board-color="#1f332a" title="داكن" style="background:#1f332a"></button>
 <input type="color" id="boardBackgroundColorPicker" value="#f0e2cc" aria-label="اختيار لون مخصص لخلفية السبورة">`;
 const bgStrength=section.querySelector('.background-strength');bgStrength?.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin',pickerWrap);
 const select=$('settingsBgProfile');if(select&&!select.querySelector('option[value="board"]')){const opt=document.createElement('option');opt.value='board';opt.textContent='خلفيات السبورة';select.appendChild(opt);}
 pickerWrap.querySelectorAll('[data-board-color]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{state.boardBackgroundColor=btn.dataset.boardColor;state.boardBackgroundType='color';if($('boardBackgroundColorPicker'))$('boardBackgroundColorPicker').value=state.boardBackgroundColor;applyBoardBackground(true);updateBoardColorChips();}));
 $('boardBackgroundColorPicker').addEventListener('input',e=>{state.boardBackgroundColor=e.target.value;state.boardBackgroundType='color';applyBoardBackground(true);updateBoardColorChips();});
}
function updateBoardColorChips(){document.querySelectorAll('.board-color-chip').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.boardColor===state.boardBackgroundColor&&state.boardBackgroundType==='color'));if($('boardBackgroundColorPicker'))$('boardBackgroundColorPicker').value=state.boardBackgroundColor||'#f0e2cc';}
ensureBoardBackgroundControls();
renderSettingsBackgrounds=function(){
 ensureBoardBackgroundControls();
 const host=$('settingsBackgroundGallery');if(!host)return;
 const mode=$('settingsBgProfile')?.value||activeSettingsBgProfile||'adult';activeSettingsBgProfile=mode;
 const arrays=state.ageBackgrounds||(state.ageBackgrounds={adult:[],child:[],board:[]});if(!Array.isArray(arrays[mode]))arrays[mode]=[];
 const arr=arrays[mode],idx=Number(state.ageBackgroundIndex?.[mode]||0);host.innerHTML='';
 $('boardColorOptions').hidden=mode!=='board';
 const title=document.querySelector('[data-settings-category="backgrounds"] h3');if(title)title.innerHTML='<span>✦</span> خلفيات الكبار والأطفال والسبورة';
 const msg=$('settingsBgMessage');if(msg)msg.textContent=mode==='board'?'اختر لونًا أو صورة لخلفية السبورة. يمكنك حذف أي صورة منفردة من علامة ×.':'اختر خلفية لتطبيقها، أو احذف صورة بعينها من علامة ×.';
 if(!arr.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='settings-help';empty.textContent=mode==='board'?'لا توجد صور للسبورة بعد؛ اختر لونًا أعلاه أو أضف صورًا من الجهاز.':'لا توجد خلفيات مضافة لهذه المجموعة بعد. يمكنك رفع صور جديدة.';host.appendChild(empty);}
 arr.forEach((src,i)=>{
  const tile=document.createElement('div');tile.className='settings-bg-tile'+(i===idx&&((mode!=='board')||state.boardBackgroundType==='image')?' active':'');tile.setAttribute('role','button');tile.tabIndex=0;tile.setAttribute('aria-label','اختيار الخلفية '+(i+1));tile.title='خلفية مضافة '+(i+1);tile.style.backgroundImage=`url("${src}")`;tile.dataset.backgroundIndex=String(i);
  const lab=document.createElement('span');lab.textContent='خلفية '+(i+1);tile.appendChild(lab);
  const del=document.createElement('button');del.type='button';del.className='settings-bg-delete';del.textContent='×';del.title='حذف هذه الخلفية';del.setAttribute('aria-label','حذف الخلفية '+(i+1));
  del.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();arr.splice(i,1);if((Number(state.ageBackgroundIndex?.[mode])||0)>=arr.length)state.ageBackgroundIndex[mode]=Math.max(0,arr.length-1);if(mode==='board'&&state.boardBackgroundType==='image'){if(arr.length)state.ageBackgroundIndex.board=Math.min(state.ageBackgroundIndex.board,arr.length-1);else state.boardBackgroundType='color';if(state.displayMode==='board')applyBoardBackground(false);}else if(mode===state.ageMode&&state.displayMode!=='board')applyAgeBackground(mode,false);saveState();renderSettingsBackgrounds();showToast('تم حذف الخلفية المحددة.');});
  tile.appendChild(del);
  tile.addEventListener('click',()=>{state.ageBackgroundIndex[mode]=i;if(mode==='board'){state.boardBackgroundType='image';if(state.displayMode==='board')applyBoardBackground(false);}else if(state.ageMode===mode&&state.displayMode!=='board')applyAgeBackground(mode,false);saveState();renderSettingsBackgrounds();showToast('تم تطبيق الخلفية المحددة.');});tile.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if((e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' ')&&e.target===tile){e.preventDefault();tile.click();}});host.appendChild(tile);
 });
 syncBackgroundStrength();updateBoardColorChips();
};
// اختيار الخلفيات من الإعدادات يعرض مجموعة الوضع الحالي تلقائيًا.
$('settingsToggle').addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(()=>{if($('settingsBackdrop').classList.contains('show')){$('settingsBgProfile').value=state.displayMode==='board'?'board':(state.ageMode||'adult');activeSettingsBgProfile=$('settingsBgProfile').value;syncBackgroundStrength();renderSettingsBackgrounds();}},0));
$('backgroundStrength').addEventListener('input',()=>{if($('settingsBgProfile').value==='board'){state.ageBackgroundOpacity.board=Number($('backgroundStrength').value);applyBoardBackground(true);}});
$('backgroundFile').addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(()=>{if($('settingsBgProfile')?.value==='board'){const images=state.ageBackgrounds.board||[];if(images.length){state.boardBackgroundType='image';state.ageBackgroundIndex.board=Math.min(images.length-1,Math.max(0,Number(state.ageBackgroundIndex.board)||0));if(state.displayMode==='board')applyBoardBackground(true);else saveState();}renderSettingsBackgrounds();}},450));
$('resetModeBackground').addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(()=>{if($('settingsBgProfile')?.value==='board'){state.ageBackgrounds.board=[];state.ageBackgroundIndex.board=0;state.boardBackgroundType='color';applyBoardBackground(state.displayMode==='board');renderSettingsBackgrounds();}},0));

// أشكال هندسية إضافية للإطارات، وتخزين الاختيار مستقلًا عن نوع الحد.
function frameShapeRadius(shape,key){const st={...(DEFAULT_STATE.frameSettings[key]||{}),...(state.frameSettings?.[key]||{})};const defaults={brand:28,surah:28,teacher:28,controls:24,verse:28,screen:0};return ({default:`${Number(st.radius??defaults[key]??24)}px`,rounded:'32px',soft:'14px',capsule:'999px',arched:'42% 42% 14px 14px',asymmetric:'34px 10px 34px 10px',bevel:'20px 5px 20px 5px'})[shape]||`${Number(st.radius??defaults[key]??24)}px`;}
function applyFrameGeometry(){
 state.frameShapes=state.frameShapes||{};
 for(const [key,cfg] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){const el=document.querySelector(cfg.selector);if(!el)continue;const shape=state.frameShapes[key]||'default';el.dataset.frameShape=shape;el.style.setProperty('border-radius',frameShapeRadius(shape,key),'important');}
}
function addFrameShapeControls(){
 for(const [key,cfg] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){
  const select=$(cfg.select);if(!select||select.parentElement.querySelector('.frame-shape-field'))continue;
  const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='frame-shape-field';const label=document.createElement('label');label.textContent='الشكل الهندسي للإطار';label.htmlFor='frameShape'+key[0].toUpperCase()+key.slice(1);
  const shape=document.createElement('select');shape.id=label.htmlFor;shape.innerHTML='<option value="default">الشكل الافتراضي</option><option value="rounded">مستدير ناعم</option><option value="soft">زوايا دائرية خفيفة</option><option value="capsule">بيضاوي / كبسولة</option><option value="arched">محدب علوي</option><option value="asymmetric">زوايا متبادلة</option><option value="bevel">زوايا هندسية</option>';
  shape.value=state.frameShapes[key]||'default';shape.addEventListener('change',()=>{state.frameShapes[key]=shape.value;applyFrameGeometry();saveState();});wrap.append(label,shape);select.parentElement.appendChild(wrap);
 }
}
addFrameShapeControls();
const baseCaptureFrameSettings=captureFrameSettings;
captureFrameSettings=function(){baseCaptureFrameSettings();state.frameShapes=state.frameShapes||{};for(const [key] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){const s=$('frameShape'+key[0].toUpperCase()+key.slice(1));if(s)state.frameShapes[key]=s.value;}};
const baseLoadFrameControlsFromState=loadFrameControlsFromState;
loadFrameControlsFromState=function(){baseLoadFrameControlsFromState();addFrameShapeControls();for(const [key] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){const s=$('frameShape'+key[0].toUpperCase()+key.slice(1));if(s)s.value=state.frameShapes?.[key]||'default';}applyFrameGeometry();};
const baseApplyFrameStylesForShapes=applyFrameStyles;
applyFrameStyles=function(){baseApplyFrameStylesForShapes();applyFrameGeometry();};

// أدوات أيقونية داخل شريط القلم لاختيار السورة وفتح PDF.
function iconButton(id,title,svg){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id=id;b.className='tool-btn';b.title=title;b.setAttribute('aria-label',title);b.innerHTML=svg;return b;}
const svgSurah='<svg aria-hidden="true" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 3.5h10l4 4V21H5z"/><path d="M15 3.5V8h4M8 12h8M8 15h5"/><path d="M18.5 15.5v5M16 18h5"/></svg>';
const svgPdf='<svg aria-hidden="true" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M5 3h10l4 4v14H5z"/><path d="M15 3v5h4"/><path d="M7.5 15.5h2a1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0-3h-2v5M13 17.5v-5h1.5a2.5 2.5 0 0 1 0 5H13M18 17.5v-5h3"/></svg>';
const toolbarTools=$('toolset');
/* تم حذف رمز إضافة السورة من شريط أدوات القلم بناءً على طلب المستخدم */
const stickerActions=document.querySelector('.sticker-actions');
if(stickerActions&&!$('penPdfButton')){const b=iconButton('penPdfButton','فتح ملف PDF بجانب السبورة',svgPdf);b.classList.add('pen-pdf-action');stickerActions.insertBefore(b,stickerActions.firstChild);}

// نافذة PDF جانبية قابلة للسحب والتغيير بالحجم، مع تكبير وتصغير.
let pdfPendingOpen=false,pdfSideUrl='',pdfSideZ=10025;
function createPdfSidePanel(){
 if($('pdfSidePanel'))return;
 const panel=document.createElement('section');panel.id='pdfSidePanel';panel.setAttribute('role','dialog');panel.setAttribute('aria-label','عارض ملف PDF');
 const initialLeft=Math.min(36,Math.max(12,window.innerWidth*.03));const availableW=Math.max(200,window.innerWidth-initialLeft-12);const availableH=Math.max(220,window.innerHeight-4);const initialWidth=Math.min(760,availableW,Math.max(Math.min(300,availableW),window.innerWidth*.48));const initialHeight=availableH;panel.style.left=initialLeft+'px';panel.style.right='auto';panel.style.top='2px';panel.style.bottom='0px';panel.style.width=initialWidth+'px';panel.style.height=initialHeight+'px';panel.style.maxWidth=availableW+'px';panel.style.maxHeight=availableH+'px';
 panel.innerHTML=`<div id="pdfSideHeader"><strong id="pdfSideTitle">ملف PDF</strong><div class="pdf-head-actions"><button type="button" id="pdfChooseFile" title="اختيار ملف PDF من الجهاز" aria-label="اختيار ملف PDF من الجهاز">+</button><button type="button" id="pdfPanelClose" title="إغلاق نافذة PDF">×</button></div></div><div class="pdf-stretch-controls"><label>العرض <input type="range" id="pdfStretchX" min="60" max="160" step="5" value="100"><output id="pdfStretchXValue">100%</output></label><label>الطول <input type="range" id="pdfStretchY" min="60" max="160" step="5" value="100"><output id="pdfStretchYValue">100%</output></label></div><div id="pdfSideViewport"><iframe id="pdfSideFrame" title="مصحف PDF" src="about:blank"></iframe></div><div class="pdf-panel-note">اسحب الشريط العلوي لتحريك النافذة، واسحب السهم في الزاوية لتغيير حجمها. يمكن مطّ العرض والطول من أدوات التحكم.</div><button type="button" id="pdfResizeHandle" aria-label="اسحب لتكبير أو تصغير نافذة PDF" title="اسحب لتكبير أو تصغير نافذة PDF"><svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18"><path d="M5 19 19 5M10 19h9V10M5 14v5h5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></button>`;
 document.body.appendChild(panel);
 let picker=$('pdfSideFilePicker');if(!picker){picker=document.createElement('input');picker.type='file';picker.id='pdfSideFilePicker';picker.accept='application/pdf,.pdf';picker.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(picker);picker.addEventListener('change',async e=>{const file=e.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;if(file.type!=='application/pdf'&&!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf')){showToast('اختر ملفًا بصيغة PDF.');e.target.value='';return;}try{mushafPdfObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(file);state.mushafPdfName=file.name;saveState();showPdfPanelWithUrl(mushafPdfObjectUrl,file.name);try{await savePdfToDb(file);}catch(_){/* يظل الملف متاحًا في الجلسة الحالية */}}catch(_){showToast('تعذر فتح ملف PDF.');}e.target.value='';});}
 $('pdfPanelClose').addEventListener('click',closePdfSidePanel);
 $('pdfChooseFile').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('pdfSideFilePicker').click();});

 $('pdfStretchX').addEventListener('input',e=>setPdfStretch('x',Number(e.target.value)));$('pdfStretchY').addEventListener('input',e=>setPdfStretch('y',Number(e.target.value)));
 const header=$('pdfSideHeader');let drag=null;
 header.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.target.closest('button'))return;const r=panel.getBoundingClientRect();drag={x:e.clientX-r.left,y:e.clientY-r.top,id:e.pointerId};try{header.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();});
 header.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!drag||e.pointerId!==drag.id)return;const left=Math.min(window.innerWidth-90,Math.max(0,e.clientX-drag.x));const top=Math.min(window.innerHeight-80,Math.max(0,e.clientY-drag.y));panel.style.left=left+'px';panel.style.top=top+'px';panel.style.right='auto';});
 const end=()=>{drag=null;savePdfPanelGeometry();};header.addEventListener('pointerup',end);header.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
 const resizeHandle=$('pdfResizeHandle');let resizeDrag=null;
 resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{const r=panel.getBoundingClientRect();resizeDrag={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,left:r.left,top:r.top,width:r.width,height:r.height,id:e.pointerId};panel.style.left=r.left+'px';panel.style.top=r.top+'px';panel.style.right='auto';panel.style.bottom='auto';try{resizeHandle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
 resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!resizeDrag||e.pointerId!==resizeDrag.id)return;const maxW=Math.max(220,window.innerWidth-resizeDrag.left-4),maxH=Math.max(200,window.innerHeight-resizeDrag.top);const minW=Math.min(240,maxW),minH=Math.min(200,maxH);const nextW=Math.max(minW,Math.min(maxW,resizeDrag.width+e.clientX-resizeDrag.x));const nextH=Math.max(minH,Math.min(maxH,resizeDrag.height+e.clientY-resizeDrag.y));panel.style.maxWidth=maxW+'px';panel.style.maxHeight=maxH+'px';panel.style.width=nextW+'px';panel.style.height=nextH+'px';});
 const endResize=()=>{resizeDrag=null;savePdfPanelGeometry();};resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointerup',endResize);resizeHandle.addEventListener('pointercancel',endResize);
 if('ResizeObserver' in window){const pdfResizeObserver=new ResizeObserver(()=>{if(panel.classList.contains('show'))savePdfPanelGeometry();});pdfResizeObserver.observe(panel);}
}
function applyPdfStretch(){const frame=$('pdfSideFrame');if(!frame)return;const sx=Math.max(.6,Math.min(1.6,(Number($('pdfStretchX')?.value)||100)/100)),sy=Math.max(.6,Math.min(1.6,(Number($('pdfStretchY')?.value)||100)/100));if($('pdfStretchXValue'))$('pdfStretchXValue').textContent=Math.round(sx*100)+'%';if($('pdfStretchYValue'))$('pdfStretchYValue').textContent=Math.round(sy*100)+'%';frame.style.width='100%';frame.style.height='100%';frame.style.transform=`scale(${sx},${sy})`;frame.style.transformOrigin='top left';}
function setPdfStretch(axis,value){const slider=$(axis==='x'?'pdfStretchX':'pdfStretchY');if(slider)slider.value=String(Math.max(60,Math.min(160,Number(value)||100)));if(axis==='x')state.pdfStretchX=Number(slider?.value||100);else state.pdfStretchY=Number(slider?.value||100);applyPdfStretch();saveState();}

function closePdfSidePanel(){savePdfPanelGeometry();const panel=$('pdfSidePanel');if(panel)panel.classList.remove('show');state.pdfPanelWasOpen=false;saveState();renderScreenTray();}
function showPdfPanelWithUrl(url,name='مصحف PDF'){
 createPdfSidePanel();pdfSideUrl=url;pdfAvailableInTray=true;$('pdfSideTitle').textContent=name;
 const panel=$('pdfSidePanel');
 if(panel){
  const fullH=Math.max(240,window.innerHeight-4);
  panel.style.top='2px';
  panel.style.bottom='0px';
  panel.style.height=fullH+'px';
  panel.style.maxHeight=fullH+'px';
 }
 const urlWithoutHash=String(url).split('#')[0];$('pdfSideFrame').src=urlWithoutHash+'#toolbar=0&navpanes=0';$('pdfStretchX').value=String(state.pdfStretchX||100);$('pdfStretchY').value=String(state.pdfStretchY||100);applyPdfStretch();$('pdfSidePanel').classList.add('show');$('pdfSidePanel').style.zIndex=String(++pdfSideZ);state.pdfPanelWasOpen=true;saveState();applyPdfStretch();renderScreenTray();
}
async function openPdfSidePanel(){
 createPdfSidePanel();
 try{
  let url=mushafPdfObjectUrl;
  if(!url){const record=await readPdfFromDb();if(record?.blob){url=URL.createObjectURL(record.blob);mushafPdfObjectUrl=url;}}
  if(url){showPdfPanelWithUrl(url,state.mushafPdfName||'مصحف PDF');return;}
 }catch(_){ }
 $('pdfSideFilePicker').click();
}
$('penPdfButton')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{createPdfSidePanel();const picker=$('pdfSideFilePicker');if(picker){picker.value='';picker.click();}});

// فهرس جانبي للعناصر المعروضة: تحكم مستقل في إظهار/إخفاء القرآن وكل صورة وملصق ونص وملف PDF دون الاختيار الحصري.
function createScreenTray(){if($('screenItemTray'))return;const tray=document.createElement('aside');tray.id='screenItemTray';tray.setAttribute('aria-label','العناصر المفتوحة على الشاشة');document.body.appendChild(tray);}
function makeTrayButton({kind,id,label,glyph,src,onClick,onDelete,active=false}){
 const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='screen-tray-item'+(active?' active':'');b.title=label;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!active));b.dataset.trayKind=kind;if(id!==undefined)b.dataset.trayId=String(id);
 if(src){const im=document.createElement('img');im.src=src;im.alt='';b.appendChild(im);}else{const g=document.createElement('span');g.className='tray-glyph';g.textContent=glyph||'▣';b.appendChild(g);}
 if(typeof onDelete==='function'){
  const del=document.createElement('span');
  del.className='screen-tray-delete';
  del.setAttribute('role','button');
  del.tabIndex=-1;
  del.title='حذف '+label;
  del.setAttribute('aria-label','حذف '+label);
  del.textContent='×';
  del.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();});
  del.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();onDelete(b,e);});
  b.appendChild(del);
 }
 const tip=document.createElement('span');tip.className='tray-label';tip.textContent=label;b.appendChild(tip);
 b.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('.screen-tray-delete'))return;e.preventDefault();onClick?.(b,e);});
 return b;
}
let versesHiddenByTray=Boolean(state?.versesHidden);
let pdfAvailableInTray=false;
function setVersesTrayHidden(hidden){
 versesHiddenByTray=!!hidden;
 if(state)state.versesHidden=versesHiddenByTray;
 document.body.classList.toggle('verses-hidden-by-tray',versesHiddenByTray);
}
function isVersesTrayVisible(){
 return !state.blankDisplay && !versesHiddenByTray;
}
function isPdfTrayVisible(){
 return Boolean($('pdfSidePanel')?.classList.contains('show'));
}
function toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem(type, index=-1){
 const stickers=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item'));
 const texts=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item'));
 if(type==='verses'){
  if(isVersesTrayVisible()){
   setVersesTrayHidden(true);
  }else{
   if(state.blankDisplay)setBlankDisplay(false,false);
   setVersesTrayHidden(false);
   renderCurrentVerse();
  }
  saveState();
  renderScreenTray();
  return;
 }
 if(type==='pdf'){
  const panel=$('pdfSidePanel');
  if(panel){
   if(panel.classList.contains('show')){
    savePdfPanelGeometry();
    panel.classList.remove('show');
    state.pdfPanelWasOpen=false;
   }else{
    panel.classList.add('show');
    panel.style.zIndex=String(++pdfSideZ);
    state.pdfPanelWasOpen=true;
    applyPdfStretch();
   }
  }
  saveState();
  renderScreenTray();
  return;
 }
 if(type==='sticker'&&stickers[index]){
  const item=stickers[index];
  if(item.dataset.trayHidden==='1'){
   delete item.dataset.trayHidden;
   selectSticker(item);
   item.style.setProperty('--sticker-z',String(++stickerZCounter));
   item.style.zIndex=String(stickerZCounter);
  }else{
   item.dataset.trayHidden='1';
   item.querySelector('video')?.pause?.();
   if(selectedSticker===item){item.classList.remove('selected-sticker');selectedSticker=null;}
  }
  syncStickerState();
  renderScreenTray();
  return;
 }
 if(type==='text'&&texts[index]){
  const item=texts[index];
  if(item.dataset.trayHidden==='1'){
   delete item.dataset.trayHidden;
   selectBoardText(item,false);
   item.style.zIndex=String(++boardTextZCounter);
  }else{
   item.dataset.trayHidden='1';
   if(selectedBoardText===item){item.classList.remove('selected');selectedBoardText=null;}
  }
  syncBoardTextState();
  renderScreenTray();
 }
}
const toggleExclusiveScreenTrayItem = toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem;
async function navigateMushafSurah(step){
 const cur=Math.max(1,Math.min(114,Number(currentChapter)||108));
 const nextCh=((cur-1+step+114)%114)+1;
 const maxV=verseCounts[nextCh-1]||1;
 stopAudio();
 chosenRange={chapter:nextCh,from:1,to:maxV};
 state.chosenRange=chosenRange;
 state.lastChapter=nextCh;
 state.lastVerse=1;
 state.lessonSetup=state.lessonSetup||{};
 state.lessonSetup.chapter=nextCh;
 state.lessonSetup.surahName=surahNames[nextCh-1]||'';
 state.lessonSetup.from=1;
 state.lessonSetup.to=maxV;
 if(typeof setVersesTrayHidden==='function')setVersesTrayHidden(false);
 setBlankDisplay(false,false);
 saveState();
 await loadChapter(nextCh,1);
 showToast('سورة '+surahNames[nextCh-1]+' (من الآية ١)');
}
$('prevImageBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>navigateMushafSurah(-1));
$('nextImageBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>navigateMushafSurah(1));
function renderScreenTray(){
 createScreenTray();const tray=$('screenItemTray');if(!tray)return;tray.innerHTML='';
 const versesVisible=isVersesTrayVisible();
 tray.appendChild(makeTrayButton({kind:'verses',label:'الآيات الحالية',glyph:'۞',active:versesVisible,onClick:()=>{
  toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem('verses');
 }}));
 const hasPdf=pdfAvailableInTray||Boolean(pdfSideUrl||mushafPdfObjectUrl||state.pdfPanelWasOpen||$('pdfSidePanel')?.classList.contains('show'));
 if(hasPdf){
  pdfAvailableInTray=true;
  tray.appendChild(makeTrayButton({kind:'pdf',label:'مصحف PDF',glyph:'▤',active:isPdfTrayVisible(),onClick:()=>{
   toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem('pdf');
  },onDelete:()=>{
   const panel=$('pdfSidePanel');
   if(panel)panel.classList.remove('show');
   const frame=$('pdfSideFrame');
   if(frame)frame.src='about:blank';
   pdfAvailableInTray=false;
   pdfSideUrl='';
   if(mushafPdfObjectUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(mushafPdfObjectUrl);}catch(_){}mushafPdfObjectUrl='';}
   state.pdfPanelWasOpen=false;
   state.mushafPdfName='';
   if(typeof clearStoredPdf==='function')clearStoredPdf().catch(()=>{});
   saveState();
   renderScreenTray();
  }}));
 }
 document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item').forEach((item,i)=>{
  const img=item.querySelector('img'),isVideo=item.dataset.mediaKind==='video';
  const isVisible=item.dataset.trayHidden!=='1';
  tray.appendChild(makeTrayButton({kind:isVideo?'video':'image',id:String(i),label:(isVideo?'فيديو مضاف ':'صورة مضافة ')+(i+1),src:img?.src,glyph:isVideo?'▶':'▧',active:isVisible,onClick:()=>{
   toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem('sticker',i);
  },onDelete:()=>{
   removeSticker(item,true);
   renderScreenTray();
  }}));
 });
 document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item').forEach((item,i)=>{
  const txt=(item.querySelector('.board-text-content')?.innerText||'نص').trim();
  const isVisible=item.dataset.trayHidden!=='1';
  tray.appendChild(makeTrayButton({kind:'text',id:String(i),label:txt.slice(0,45)||'نص جديد',glyph:'T',active:isVisible,onClick:()=>{
   toggleIndependentScreenTrayItem('text',i);
  },onDelete:()=>{
   removeBoardText(item,true);
   renderScreenTray();
  }}));
 });
}
window.renderScreenTray=renderScreenTray;createScreenTray();setVersesTrayHidden(!!state?.versesHidden);renderScreenTray();
const trayLayerObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(!trayLayerObserver._queued){trayLayerObserver._queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{trayLayerObserver._queued=false;renderScreenTray();});}});
trayLayerObserver.observe($('stickerLayer'),{childList:true});trayLayerObserver.observe($('boardTextLayer'),{childList:true});

let lastOrganizedRangeSearch=null;
function organizeRangeModal(){
 const search=$('surahSearch');if(!search||search===lastOrganizedRangeSearch)return;
 lastOrganizedRangeSearch=search;
 const play=$('playRangeButton');if(play)play.classList.remove('primary');
 const submitBtn=$('editForm')?.querySelector('[type=submit]');if(submitBtn&&$('editForm')?.closest('.modal')?.classList.contains('range-modal'))submitBtn.textContent='عرض';
 const topSave=document.querySelector('.range-modal-top-actions .range-top-save');if(topSave)topSave.textContent='عرض';
}
const rangeModalObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(!rangeModalObserver._timer){rangeModalObserver._timer=setTimeout(()=>{rangeModalObserver._timer=0;organizeRangeModal();},0);}});
rangeModalObserver.observe($('modalBody'),{childList:true});

if(state.verseDisplayDefaultVersion!==1){state.verseDisplayMode='all';state.verseDisplayDefaultVersion=1;saveState();}

const oldPlayVerseButton=$('playVerse');
if(oldPlayVerseButton){const playButton=oldPlayVerseButton.cloneNode(true);oldPlayVerseButton.replaceWith(playButton);playButton.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
 if(audioBusy||reciterActive){rangePlayback=null;stopAudio();showToast('تم إيقاف التلاوة.');return;}
 const max=verseCounts[currentChapter-1]||currentVerse;
 const selectedEnd=chosenRange&&Number(chosenRange.chapter)===currentChapter&&currentVerse>=Number(chosenRange.from)&&currentVerse<=Number(chosenRange.to)?Number(chosenRange.to):max;
 rangePlayback={chapter:currentChapter,from:currentVerse,to:Math.max(currentVerse,selectedEnd),repeat:!!state.repeatAllAudio};
 reciterActive=true;
 await playVerse(true);
});}


// النسخ الاحتياطي الكامل: يشمل الحالة والخلفيات والصور والنصوص والرسم وملفات PDF والتسجيلات المحلية.
function blobToDataUrl(blob){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{if(!blob){resolve(null);return;}const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>resolve(String(r.result||''));r.onerror=()=>reject(r.error||new Error('تعذر قراءة الملف'));r.readAsDataURL(blob);});}
function dataUrlToBlob(dataUrl){const match=String(dataUrl||'').match(/^data:([^;,]+)?(;base64)?,([\s\S]*)$/);if(!match)throw new Error('ملف نسخة غير صالح');const type=match[1]||'application/octet-stream',body=match[3]||'';if(match[2]){const binary=atob(body),bytes=new Uint8Array(binary.length);for(let i=0;i<binary.length;i++)bytes[i]=binary.charCodeAt(i);return new Blob([bytes],{type});}return new Blob([decodeURIComponent(body)],{type});}
function savePdfPanelGeometry(){const panel=$('pdfSidePanel');if(!panel||!panel.classList.contains('show'))return;const r=panel.getBoundingClientRect();state.pdfPanelGeometry={left:Math.max(0,Math.round(r.left)),top:Math.max(0,Math.round(r.top)),width:Math.round(r.width),height:Math.round(r.height)};state.pdfPanelWasOpen=true;state.pdfStretchX=Number($('pdfStretchX')?.value||state.pdfStretchX||100);state.pdfStretchY=Number($('pdfStretchY')?.value||state.pdfStretchY||100);}
// نسخ احتياطي محمول ومتوافق بين المتصفحات، مع تضمين ملفات PDF والتسجيلات والفيديوهات.
function setBackupStatus(message){const el=$('backupStatus');if(el)el.textContent=message;}
function scheduleDeviceBackupSave(delay=3500){
 clearTimeout(deviceBackupTimer);
 deviceBackupTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{
  let handle=null;try{handle=await readLocalRecord(DEVICE_BACKUP_HANDLE_KEY);}catch(_){}
  if(!handle||typeof handle.createWritable!=='function')return;
  try{const permission=typeof handle.queryPermission==='function'?await handle.queryPermission({mode:'readwrite'}):'granted';
   if(permission!=='granted'){setBackupStatus('تغيّرت البيانات محليًا، لكن المتصفح يحتاج إذن الكتابة للملف. اضغط «ربط ملف حفظ تلقائي» وأعد منح الإذن، أو استخدم «تنزيل نسخة».');return;}
   await writeBackupToDeviceHandle(handle,false);
  }catch(err){console.warn('Auto backup to device file failed',err);setBackupStatus('تعذّر تحديث ملف النسخة التلقائية. افتح «النسخ الاحتياطي» واضغط «ربط ملف حفظ تلقائي» لإعادة منح الإذن، أو نزّل نسخة احتياطية.');}
 },delay);
}
async function buildCompleteBackupPayload(){
 state.pdfPanelWasOpen=!!$('pdfSidePanel')?.classList.contains('show');savePdfPanelGeometry();
 const snapshot=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state));
 let pdf=null,recording=null;const videos=[];
 try{const p=await readPdfFromDb();if(p?.blob)pdf={name:p.name||state.mushafPdfName||'mushaf.pdf',type:p.blob.type||'application/pdf',data:await blobToDataUrl(p.blob)};}catch(err){console.warn('Could not include saved PDF in backup',err);}
 try{const r=await readLocalRecord('student-recording');if(r?.blob)recording={type:r.type||r.blob.type||'audio/webm',data:await blobToDataUrl(r.blob)};}catch(err){console.warn('Could not include student recording in backup',err);}
 const videoKeys=[...new Set((snapshot.stickers||[]).filter(item=>item?.kind==='video'&&item.mediaKey).map(item=>String(item.mediaKey)))];
 for(const key of videoKeys){try{const v=await readLocalRecord(key);if(v?.blob)videos.push({key,name:v.name||'video',type:v.type||v.blob.type||'video/mp4',data:await blobToDataUrl(v.blob)});}catch(err){console.warn('Could not include a board video in backup',key,err);}}
 return {format:'rihlat-alquran-backup',version:3,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),appVersion:'rihlat-html-single-file',state:snapshot,files:{pdf,recording,videos},ui:{pdfOpen:state.pdfPanelWasOpen,pdfGeometry:state.pdfPanelGeometry||null,pdfStretchX:state.pdfStretchX||100,pdfStretchY:state.pdfStretchY||100}};
}
function backupFileName(){return 'نسخة-رحلة-القرآن-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';}
function backupPayloadBlob(payload){
 const jsonText=JSON.stringify(payload,null,2);
 if(!jsonText||jsonText.length<20)throw new Error('تعذر تحويل بيانات النسخة الاحتياطية إلى JSON');
 return new Blob([jsonText],{type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'});
}
async function downloadCompleteBackup(){
 const btn=$('downloadBackup');if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='جارٍ تجهيز النسخة…';}
 try{
  const payload=await buildCompleteBackupPayload();
  if(!payload||!payload.state||typeof payload.state!=='object')throw new Error('بيانات النسخة غير مكتملة');
  const blob=backupPayloadBlob(payload);
  if(!blob||blob.size<20)throw new Error('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية فارغ');
  const fileName=backupFileName();
  window.__lastBackupPayload=payload;
  window.__lastBackupFileName=fileName;
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;a.download=fileName;a.rel='noopener';a.style.display='none';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  setTimeout(()=>{try{a.remove();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}catch(_){}},60000);
  setBackupStatus('تم إنشاء وتنزيل ملف النسخة الاحتياطية الكاملة ('+fileName+'). يحتفظ الملف بجميع الإعدادات والخلفيات والصور والملصقات والرسومات وملف PDF والتسجيلات.');
  showToast('تم تنزيل النسخة الاحتياطية الكاملة.');
  return payload;
 }catch(err){
  console.warn(err);
  setBackupStatus('تعذّر إنشاء النسخة الاحتياطية: '+(err?.message||'خطأ غير متوقع'));
  showToast('تعذر إنشاء النسخة الاحتياطية.');
  return null;
 }finally{if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='تنزيل نسخة';}}
}
async function writeBackupToDeviceHandle(handle,requestPermission=false){
 if(deviceBackupBusy){deviceBackupQueued=true;return false;}
 deviceBackupBusy=true;
 try{
  let permission=typeof handle.queryPermission==='function'?await handle.queryPermission({mode:'readwrite'}):'granted';
  if(permission!=='granted'&&requestPermission&&typeof handle.requestPermission==='function')permission=await handle.requestPermission({mode:'readwrite'});
  if(permission!=='granted'){setBackupStatus('لم يمنح المتصفح إذن الكتابة إلى الملف. اضغط «ربط ملف حفظ تلقائي» وأعد منح الإذن، أو استخدم «تنزيل نسخة».');return false;}
  const payload=await buildCompleteBackupPayload(),blob=backupPayloadBlob(payload),writable=await handle.createWritable();
  await writable.write(blob);await writable.close();
  setBackupStatus('تم حفظ أحدث البيانات تلقائيًا في ملف على الجهاز: '+(handle.name||'ملف النسخة المحلية')+' — '+new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}));
  return true;
 }catch(err){console.error(err);setBackupStatus('تعذّر الكتابة إلى ملف النسخة المحلية: '+(err?.message||'خطأ غير معروف')+'. استخدم «تنزيل نسخة» كبديل آمن.');return false;}
 finally{deviceBackupBusy=false;if(deviceBackupQueued){deviceBackupQueued=false;scheduleDeviceBackupSave(1200);}}
}
async function connectDeviceBackupFile(){
 // إن كان هناك ملف مرتبط بالفعل، استخدم هذا الزر لإعادة طلب إذن الكتابة إليه.
 try{
  const existing=await readLocalRecord(DEVICE_BACKUP_HANDLE_KEY);
  if(existing&&typeof existing.createWritable==='function'){
   let permission=typeof existing.queryPermission==='function'?await existing.queryPermission({mode:'readwrite'}):'granted';
   if(permission!=='granted'&&typeof existing.requestPermission==='function')permission=await existing.requestPermission({mode:'readwrite'});
   if(permission==='granted'){
    const ok=await writeBackupToDeviceHandle(existing,false);
    if(ok){showToast('تم تحديث ملف النسخة المرتبط على الجهاز.');return;}
   }
  }
 }catch(err){console.warn('Could not reuse the existing backup file handle; ask for a new one if needed.',err);}
 if(typeof window.showSaveFilePicker!=='function'){
  setBackupStatus('هذا المتصفح لا يدعم الربط التلقائي لملف على الجهاز. يمكنك تنزيل نسخة كاملة يدويًا وستكون قابلة للاستعادة في متصفح آخر.');
  await downloadCompleteBackup();return;
 }
 try{
  const handle=await window.showSaveFilePicker({suggestedName:'نسخة-رحلة-القرآن-تلقائية.json',types:[{description:'نسخة احتياطية لرحلة القرآن',accept:{'application/json':['.json']}}]});
  await writeLocalRecord(DEVICE_BACKUP_HANDLE_KEY,handle);
  const ok=await writeBackupToDeviceHandle(handle,true);
  if(ok)showToast('تم ربط ملف النسخة المحلية؛ ستُحدّثه التغييرات تلقائيًا عند توفر إذن الكتابة.');
 }catch(err){if(err?.name==='AbortError')return;console.error(err);setBackupStatus('لم يتم ربط الملف. يمكنك إتمام النسخ عبر زر «تنزيل نسخة». '+(err?.message||''));showToast('تعذر ربط ملف الحفظ التلقائي.');}
}
function isStateObject(value){
 if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||Array.isArray(value))return false;
 return ['title','teacher','fonts','fontSizes','fontFiles','stickers','boardTexts','customBackgrounds','ageBackgrounds','ageMode','displayMode','background','customBackground','logo','portrait','drawingData','lastChapter','lastVerse','verseDisplayMode','mushafPdfName','frameSettings','chosenRange'].some(key=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value,key));
}
function maybeParseJson(value){if(typeof value!=='string')return value;const text=value.trim();if(!text||(!text.startsWith('{')&&!text.startsWith('[')))return value;try{return JSON.parse(text);}catch(_){return value;}}
function extractBackupParts(parsed){
 const visited=new Set(),queue=[parsed];let foundState=null,foundFiles=null,foundUi=null;
 while(queue.length){let obj=maybeParseJson(queue.shift());if(!obj||typeof obj!=='object'||Array.isArray(obj)||visited.has(obj))continue;visited.add(obj);
  if(!foundFiles&&obj.files&&typeof obj.files==='object')foundFiles=obj.files;
  if(!foundUi&&obj.ui&&typeof obj.ui==='object')foundUi=obj.ui;
  const candidates=[obj.state,obj.appState,obj.programState,obj.applicationState,obj.snapshot,obj.dataState,obj.backupState,obj.programData,obj.applicationData,obj.stateData,obj.preferences];
  for(let candidate of candidates){candidate=maybeParseJson(candidate);if(isStateObject(candidate)){foundState=candidate;break;}}
  if(!foundState){const stored=maybeParseJson(obj[STORE_KEY]);if(isStateObject(stored))foundState=stored;}
  if(!foundState&&isStateObject(obj))foundState=obj;
  if(foundState&&foundFiles)break;
  for(const key of ['payload','backup','backupData','data','program','export','snapshotData','application']){const child=obj[key];if(child&&typeof child==='object'&&!visited.has(child))queue.push(child);else if(typeof child==='string'&&child.trim().startsWith('{'))queue.push(child);}
 }
 return {state:foundState,files:foundFiles,ui:foundUi,hasFileBundle:!!foundFiles};
}
