async function deleteLocalRecord(key){try{const db=await openLocalDataDb();await withIdbTimeout(new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('items','readwrite');tx.objectStore('items').delete(key);tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('تعذر حذف سجل محلي'));}),2800);try{db.close();}catch(_){}}catch(_){} }
async function clearStoredPdf(){try{const db=await openMushafDb();await withIdbTimeout(new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('files','readwrite');tx.objectStore('files').delete('mushaf');tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('تعذر حذف PDF'));}),2800);try{db.close();}catch(_){}}catch(_){}if(mushafPdfObjectUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(mushafPdfObjectUrl);}catch(_){}mushafPdfObjectUrl='';}}
async function clearStoredRecording(){await deleteLocalRecord('student-recording');}
function validateBackupPayloadStructure(parsed){
 if(!parsed||typeof parsed!=='object'||Array.isArray(parsed))throw new Error('بنية ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير صالحة (يجب أن يكون كائن JSON).');
 if(parsed.format&&parsed.format!=='rihlat-alquran-backup')throw new Error('هذا الملف ليس نسخة احتياطية مدعومة لبرنامج رحلة القرآن.');
 if(parsed.version!==undefined&&(!Number.isFinite(Number(parsed.version))||Number(parsed.version)<1||Number(parsed.version)>10)){
  throw new Error('إصدار ملف النسخة الاحتياطية غير مدعوم.');
 }
 const parts=extractBackupParts(parsed);
 if(!parts.state||typeof parts.state!=='object'||Array.isArray(parts.state)){
  throw new Error('لم يتم العثور على بيانات صالحة للبرنامج داخل هذا الملف.');
 }
 const files=parts.files||{};
 if(files.pdf?.data)dataUrlToBlob(files.pdf.data);
 if(files.recording?.data)dataUrlToBlob(files.recording.data);
 const videos=Array.isArray(files.videos)?files.videos:(files.videos&&typeof files.videos==='object'?Object.entries(files.videos).map(([key,v])=>({key,...v})):[]);
 for(const v of videos){if(v?.data)dataUrlToBlob(v.data);}
 return {parts,videos};
}
async function applyRestoredStateLive(){
 applyLoadedSettings();
 if(!state.logo){$('logoImage').hidden=true;$('logoImage').removeAttribute('src');$('logoFallback').hidden=false;}
 if(!state.portrait){$('teacherImage').hidden=true;$('teacherImage').removeAttribute('src');$('teacherFallback').hidden=false;}
 if(ctx&&canvas){
  ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.restore();
  const dpr=Math.max(1,window.devicePixelRatio||1);ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
 }
 restoreDrawingFromState();
 $('stickerLayer').innerHTML='';selectedSticker=null;
 await restoreStickers();
 $('boardTextLayer').innerHTML='';selectedBoardText=null;
 restoreBoardTexts();
 await restoreSavedRecording();
 setVersesTrayHidden(!!state.versesHidden);
 chosenRange=state.chosenRange||null;
 audioRepeat=!!state.repeatAudio;
 const startChapter=Number(state.lastChapter)||108;
 const startVerse=Number(state.lastVerse)||1;
 await loadChapter(startChapter,startVerse);
 if(state.blankDisplay){
  isBlankDisplay=true;
  document.body.classList.add('blank-display');
  $('surahTitle').textContent='اختر السورة';
 }else{
  isBlankDisplay=false;
  document.body.classList.remove('blank-display');
 }
 if(state.displayMode==='board')applyDisplayMode('board',false);
 if(state.pdfPanelWasOpen){
  try{
   await openPdfSidePanel();
   const panel=$('pdfSidePanel'),g=state.pdfPanelGeometry;
   if(panel&&g){panel.style.left=Number(g.left)+'px';panel.style.top=Number(g.top)+'px';panel.style.width=Number(g.width)+'px';panel.style.height=Number(g.height)+'px';panel.style.right='auto';}
   if($('pdfStretchX'))$('pdfStretchX').value=String(state.pdfStretchX||100);
   if($('pdfStretchY'))$('pdfStretchY').value=String(state.pdfStretchY||100);
   applyPdfStretch();
  }catch(_){}
 }else if($('pdfSidePanel')){
  $('pdfSidePanel').classList.remove('show');
 }
 if($('settingsBackdrop')?.classList.contains('show'))openSettings();
 renderScreenTray();
}
async function restoreCompleteBackup(file, skipConfirm=false){
 if(!file)return false;
 const previousSnapshot=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state));
 try{
  const rawText=await file.text();
  let parsed=null;
  try{parsed=JSON.parse(rawText);}catch(_){throw new Error('ملف النسخة الاحتياطية تالف أو ليس بصيغة JSON صالحة.');}
  const {parts,videos}=validateBackupPayloadStructure(parsed);
  const restored=parts.state,files=parts.files||{};
  if(!skipConfirm&&!window.__autoConfirmBackupRestore){
   const confirmed=await new Promise(resolve=>{
    let settled=false;const finish=ok=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;resolve(ok);};
    openModal(
     'تأكيد استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية',
     'ستُستبدل البيانات الحالية بما في ملف النسخة الاحتياطية بعد التحقق من سلامتها، بما يشمل الإعدادات والخلفيات والصور والملصقات والنصوص والرسومات. هل تريد المتابعة؟',
     '<div class="small-note">اضغط «حفظ التعديل» للمتابعة واستعادة النسخة الاحتياطية، أو «إلغاء» للتراجع والاحتفاظ ببياناتك الحالية.</div>',
     ()=>{finish(true);return true;},
     ()=>finish(false)
    );
   });
   if(!confirmed){
    setBackupStatus('تم إلغاء عملية الاستعادة، وبقيت البيانات الحالية كما هي.');
    return false;
   }
  }
  backupRestoreInProgress=true;
  stopAudio();
  const oldVideoKeys=(state.stickers||[]).filter(x=>x?.kind==='video'&&x.mediaKey).map(x=>String(x.mediaKey));
  const next=normalizeState(restored);next.__updatedAt=Date.now();
  if(parts.ui?.pdfGeometry)next.pdfPanelGeometry=parts.ui.pdfGeometry;
  if(parts.ui?.pdfOpen!==undefined)next.pdfPanelWasOpen=!!parts.ui.pdfOpen;
  if(parts.ui?.pdfStretchX!==undefined)next.pdfStretchX=Number(parts.ui.pdfStretchX)||100;
  if(parts.ui?.pdfStretchY!==undefined)next.pdfStretchY=Number(parts.ui.pdfStretchY)||100;
  const restoredVideoKeySet=new Set((next.stickers||[]).filter(x=>x?.kind==='video'&&x.mediaKey).map(x=>String(x.mediaKey)));
  const importedVideoKeys=new Set();
  for(const video of videos){
   if(!video?.data)continue;
   const key=String(video.key||video.mediaKey||'');if(!key)continue;
   const blob=dataUrlToBlob(video.data);
   try{await writeLocalRecord(key,{blob,name:video.name||'video',type:video.type||blob.type||'video/mp4',savedAt:Date.now()});importedVideoKeys.add(key);}catch(_){}
  }
  for(const key of oldVideoKeys)if(!restoredVideoKeySet.has(key))await deleteLocalRecord(key);
  if(parts.hasFileBundle){
   if(files.pdf?.data){
    const b=dataUrlToBlob(files.pdf.data);
    const pdfFile=new File([b],files.pdf.name||next.mushafPdfName||'mushaf.pdf',{type:files.pdf.type||b.type||'application/pdf'});
    try{await savePdfToDb(pdfFile);}catch(_){}
    if(mushafPdfObjectUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(mushafPdfObjectUrl);}catch(_){}}
    mushafPdfObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(pdfFile);
    next.mushafPdfName=files.pdf.name||next.mushafPdfName||'mushaf.pdf';
   }else{
    next.mushafPdfName='';next.pdfPanelWasOpen=false;await clearStoredPdf();
   }
   if(files.recording?.data){
    const b=dataUrlToBlob(files.recording.data);
    try{await writeLocalRecord('student-recording',{blob:b,type:files.recording.type||b.type||'audio/webm',savedAt:Date.now()});}catch(_){}
    recordingBlob=b;
    if(recordingUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(recordingUrl);}catch(_){}}
    recordingUrl=URL.createObjectURL(b);
    if($('recordedPlayer'))$('recordedPlayer').src=recordingUrl;
    if($('listenRecording'))$('listenRecording').disabled=false;
   }else{
    await clearStoredRecording();
    recordingBlob=null;
    if(recordingUrl){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(recordingUrl);}catch(_){}recordingUrl=null;}
    if($('listenRecording'))$('listenRecording').disabled=true;
   }
  }
  state=next;chosenRange=state.chosenRange||null;audioRepeat=!!state.repeatAudio;
  let savedIdb=false,savedLocal=false;
  try{await writeLocalRecord('state',{savedAt:next.__updatedAt,state:next});savedIdb=true;}catch(err){console.warn('IndexedDB state write warning',err);}
  try{safeLocalSet(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(next));savedLocal=true;}catch(err){console.warn('localStorage state write warning',err);}
  if(!savedIdb&&!savedLocal)throw new Error('تعذر حفظ البيانات المستعادة في تخزين المتصفح.');
  backupRestoreInProgress=false;
  await applyRestoredStateLive();
  let message='تمت استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية بنجاح وتحديث جميع العناصر والإعدادات فورًا.';
  if(!parts.hasFileBundle)message+=' (نسخة سابقة: تم الاحتفاظ بملفات PDF والتسجيل المحلية الحالية).';
  setBackupStatus(message);
  showToast('تمت استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية بنجاح.');
  return true;
 }catch(err){
  console.warn(err);
  state=normalizeState(previousSnapshot);
  backupRestoreInProgress=false;
  const message=err?.message||'ملف غير صالح أو تالف';
  setBackupStatus('فشلت الاستعادة: '+message+' — تم الحفاظ على بيانات البرنامج الحالية.');
  showToast('تعذرت استعادة النسخة: '+message);
  return false;
 }finally{
  backupRestoreInProgress=false;
  const input=$('backupRestoreFile');if(input)input.value='';
 }
}
$('downloadBackup')?.addEventListener('click',downloadCompleteBackup);
$('restoreBackup')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$('backupRestoreFile')?.click());
$('backupRestoreFile')?.addEventListener('change',e=>restoreCompleteBackup(e.target.files?.[0]));
$('connectDeviceBackup')?.addEventListener('click',connectDeviceBackupFile);

(async function refreshDeviceBackupLinkStatus(){try{const handle=await readLocalRecord(DEVICE_BACKUP_HANDLE_KEY);if(handle?.name)setBackupStatus('ملف الحفظ التلقائي المرتبط: '+handle.name+'. إذا طلب المتصفح الإذن، اضغط «ربط ملف حفظ تلقائي» وأعد منح الإذن.');}catch(_){}})();


// قوائم خطوط عربية افتراضية ظاهرة بجوار كل بند، مع إبقاء الاسم قابلًا للتحرير ورفع خط خاص.
(function addFontPresetChoices(){
 const presets=['Cairo','Tajawal','Amiri','Noto Naskh Arabic','Almarai','Changa','Reem Kufi','UthmanicHafs','SurahNameCalligraphy'];
 const mapping={fontTitleName:'titleFontPreset',fontSurahName:'surahFontPreset',fontTeacherName:'teacherFontPreset',fontMushafName:'mushafFontPreset'};
 const sync=()=>{
  for(const [id,selectId] of Object.entries(mapping)){
   const input=$(id),select=$(selectId);if(!input||!select)continue;
   select.value=presets.includes(String(input.value||'').trim())?String(input.value).trim():'';
  }
 };
 for(const [id,selectId] of Object.entries(mapping)){
  const input=$(id),row=input?.closest('.font-row');if(!input||!row)continue;
  let select=$(selectId);
  if(!select){
   select=document.createElement('select');select.id=selectId;select.className='font-presets-select settings-select';select.setAttribute('aria-label','اختيار خط عربي افتراضي');
   const first=document.createElement('option');first.value='';first.textContent='خطوط جاهزة';select.appendChild(first);
   presets.forEach(name=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=name;o.textContent=name;select.appendChild(o);});
   const upload=row.querySelector('[data-font-upload]');row.insertBefore(select,upload||null);
   select.addEventListener('change',()=>{if(!select.value)return;input.value=select.value;input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));});
   input.addEventListener('input',()=>{select.value=presets.includes(String(input.value||'').trim())?String(input.value).trim():'';});
  }
 }
 sync();
 const backdrop=$('settingsBackdrop');if(backdrop){new MutationObserver(sync).observe(backdrop,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});}
 for(const id of ['fontFileTitle','fontFileSurah','fontFileTeacher','fontFileMushaf'])$(id)?.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(sync,120));
})();
// تحكم مستقل في شفافية خلفية الآيات والمصحف، مع حفظ الإعداد.
(function addVerseCardOpacityControl(){
 const root=document.documentElement;
 const savedOpacity=state.verseCardOpacity;
 const initial=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(savedOpacity==null||Number(savedOpacity)===96?82:savedOpacity)));
 state.verseCardOpacity=initial;
 root.style.setProperty('--verse-card-opacity',String(initial/100));
 const backgroundSection=document.querySelector('.settings-section[data-settings-category="backgrounds"]');
 const originalStrength=backgroundSection?.querySelector('.background-strength');
 if(!backgroundSection||!originalStrength)return;
 let panel=$('verseCardOpacitySetting');
 if(!panel){
  panel=document.createElement('div');panel.className='background-strength';panel.id='verseCardOpacitySetting';
  panel.innerHTML='<label for="verseCardOpacityRange">شفافية خلفية الآيات</label><div class="strength-row"><span>شفافة</span><input id="verseCardOpacityRange" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="82"><output id="verseCardOpacityValue">82%</output><span>معتمة</span></div><p class="settings-help">غيّر شفافية خلفية بطاقة الآية أو عرض جميع الآيات دون التأثير على وضوح النص القرآني.</p>';
  originalStrength.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',panel);
 }
 const slider=$('verseCardOpacityRange');if(!slider)return;slider.value=String(initial);$('verseCardOpacityValue').textContent=initial+'%';
 slider.addEventListener('input',()=>{const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(slider.value)||0));root.style.setProperty('--verse-card-opacity',String(v/100));$('verseCardOpacityValue').textContent=v+'%';state.verseCardOpacity=v;saveState();});
})();



// === تثبيت تحميل خطوط النظام وتجاهل خيار شفافية إطار رقم الآية القديم بأمان ===
(function activateFontsAndAyahFrameControl(){
 const root=document.documentElement;
 root.style.setProperty('--ayah-marker-frame-opacity','1');
 $('ayahMarkerFrameOpacityPanel')?.remove();
 const inputs={title:'fontTitleName',surah:'fontSurahName'};
 const selects={title:'titleFontPreset',surah:'surahFontPreset'};
 const uploadedNames={title:'RihlaTitleUploaded',surah:'RihlaSurahUploaded'};
 for(const key of Object.keys(inputs)){
  const input=$(inputs[key]),select=$(selects[key]);if(!input)continue;
  if(select&&!select.dataset.fontApplyFix){select.dataset.fontApplyFix='1';select.addEventListener('change',()=>{
   if(!select.value)return;input.value=select.value;state.fonts=state.fonts||{...DEFAULT_STATE.fonts};state.fonts[key]=select.value;
   if(key==='title')state.fonts.teacher=select.value;
   if(state.fontFiles&&state.fontFiles[key]&&select.value!==uploadedNames[key])delete state.fontFiles[key];
   applyFonts();saveState();try{document.fonts.load(`20px "${select.value}"`);}catch(_){}
   showToast('تم تطبيق خط النظام المحدد.');
  });}
  if(!input.dataset.fontApplyFix){input.dataset.fontApplyFix='1';input.addEventListener('change',()=>{
   const typed=String(input.value||'').trim();if(!typed)return;
   if(typed!==uploadedNames[key]&&state.fontFiles?.[key])delete state.fontFiles[key];
   state.fonts=state.fonts||{...DEFAULT_STATE.fonts};state.fonts[key]=typed;
   if(key==='title')state.fonts.teacher=typed;
   applyFonts();saveState();
  });}
 }
 const saveButton=$('saveSettings');if(saveButton&&!saveButton.dataset.fontSaveFix){saveButton.dataset.fontSaveFix='1';saveButton.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  for(const key of Object.keys(inputs)){const chosen=String($(inputs[key])?.value||'').trim();if(chosen&&chosen!==uploadedNames[key]&&state.fontFiles?.[key])delete state.fontFiles[key];}
  applyFonts();saveState();
 },true);}
})();


/* إصلاح مباشر لشفافية الآية وإعدادات الإطارات داخل نطاق النظام الأصلي. */

(function repairVerseOpacityAndFrameSettings(){
  const clamp=(value,min,max)=>Math.min(max,Math.max(min,Number.isFinite(Number(value))?Number(value):min));
  const root=document.documentElement;
  const get=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // أعد لون الخلفية الكريمي الأصلي، وطبّق الشفافية نفسها على الوضعين.
  function applyVerseSurface(value){
    const slider=get('verseCardOpacityRange');
    const raw=value!==undefined ? value : (slider ? slider.value : (state.verseCardOpacity??82));
    const percent=clamp(raw,0,100);
    const alpha=percent/100;
    const rgba=`rgba(255, 247, 234, ${alpha.toFixed(2)})`;
    root.style.setProperty('--verse-card-opacity',String(alpha));
    for(const el of document.querySelectorAll('.verse-stage > .verse-card, .verse-stage > #allVersesCard')){
      el.style.setProperty('background',rgba,'important');
      el.style.setProperty('background-color',rgba,'important');
      el.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
    }
    const out=get('verseCardOpacityValue');
    if(out)out.textContent=percent+'%';
    if(slider && String(slider.value)!==String(percent))slider.value=String(percent);
    state.verseCardOpacity=percent;
    return percent;
  }

  const opacitySlider=get('verseCardOpacityRange');
  if(opacitySlider && !opacitySlider.dataset.directSurfaceRepair){
    opacitySlider.dataset.directSurfaceRepair='1';
    opacitySlider.addEventListener('input',()=>{applyVerseSurface(opacitySlider.value);saveState();});
    opacitySlider.addEventListener('change',()=>{applyVerseSurface(opacitySlider.value);saveState();});
  }

  // بطاقة إعدادات إطار الآية يجب أن تؤثر على بطاقة الآية المفردة وبطاقة جميع الآيات.
  if(typeof FRAME_TARGETS!=='undefined' && FRAME_TARGETS.verse){
    FRAME_TARGETS.verse.selector='.verse-card, #allVersesCard';
  }

  function allFrameElements(selector){
    try{return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector));}catch(_){return [];}
  }

  // إصلاح تطبيق أشكال الإطارات على جميع العناصر المستهدفة، وليس أول عنصر فقط.
  if(typeof applyFrameGeometry==='function' && typeof frameShapeRadius==='function'){
    applyFrameGeometry=function applyFrameGeometryFixed(){
      state.frameShapes=state.frameShapes||{};
      for(const [key,cfg] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){
        const shape=state.frameShapes[key]||'default';
        for(const el of allFrameElements(cfg.selector)){
          el.dataset.frameShape=shape;
          el.style.setProperty('border-radius',frameShapeRadius(shape,key),'important');
          if(el.id==='allVersesCard'){
            el.style.setProperty('border-bottom-left-radius','0px','important');
            el.style.setProperty('border-bottom-right-radius','0px','important');
          }
        }
      }
    };
  }

  if(typeof applyFrameStyles==='function' && typeof frameColorWithOpacity==='function'){
    applyFrameStyles=function applyFrameStylesFixed(){
      if(typeof mountFrameCustomControls==='function')mountFrameCustomControls();
      const styles={...DEFAULT_STATE.frameStyles,...(state.frameStyles||{})};
      for(const [key,cfg] of Object.entries(FRAME_TARGETS)){
        const preset=styles[key]||'default';
        const st={...DEFAULT_STATE.frameSettings[key],...(state.frameSettings?.[key]||{})};
        const width=clamp(st.width,0,10);
        const radius=clamp(st.radius,0,60);
        const opacity=clamp(st.opacity??100,0,100);
        const line=['solid','double','dashed','dotted'].includes(st.style)?st.style:'solid';
        const hidden=preset==='none'||width===0||opacity===0;
        const color=frameColorWithOpacity(st.color||'#d3e0d4',opacity);
        for(const el of allFrameElements(cfg.selector)){
          el.setAttribute('data-frame-style',preset);
          if(key==='screen'){
            el.style.setProperty('outline',hidden?'none':`${width}px ${line} ${color}`,'important');
            el.style.setProperty('outline-offset','-8px','important');
            el.style.setProperty('border-radius',radius+'px','important');
          }else{
            el.style.setProperty('border-width',hidden?'0px':width+'px','important');
            el.style.setProperty('border-style',line,'important');
            el.style.setProperty('border-color',hidden?'transparent':color,'important');
            el.style.setProperty('border-radius',radius+'px','important');
            if(el.id==='allVersesCard'){
              el.style.setProperty('border-bottom-left-radius','0px','important');
              el.style.setProperty('border-bottom-right-radius','0px','important');
              el.style.setProperty('border-bottom-width','0px','important');
            }
          }
          if(hidden){
            el.style.setProperty('box-shadow','none','important');
          }else if(preset==='classic'){
            el.style.setProperty('box-shadow',`0 0 0 3px ${frameColorWithOpacity(st.color||'#c5a34b',opacity*.14)},0 8px 20px rgba(24,65,48,${(0.08*opacity/100).toFixed(3)})`,'important');
          }else if(preset==='ornate'){
            el.style.setProperty('box-shadow',`inset 0 0 0 3px ${frameColorWithOpacity(st.color||'#bd9a4a',opacity*.11)},0 8px 24px rgba(31,76,53,${(0.11*opacity/100).toFixed(3)})`,'important');
          }else if(preset==='glow'){
            el.style.setProperty('box-shadow',`0 0 0 3px ${frameColorWithOpacity(st.color||'#83b985',opacity*.14)},0 0 22px ${frameColorWithOpacity(st.color||'#83b985',opacity*.17)}`,'important');
          }else{
            el.style.setProperty('box-shadow','none','important');
          }
        }
      }
      if(typeof applyFrameGeometry==='function')applyFrameGeometry();
    };
  }

  // بعد فتح الإعدادات أو تبديل طريقة عرض الآيات، أعد تطبيق الخلفية والإطارات.
  if(typeof loadFrameControlsFromState==='function' && !loadFrameControlsFromState.__finalFrameRepair){
    const oldLoad=loadFrameControlsFromState;
    const fixedLoad=function(...args){const result=oldLoad.apply(this,args);applyFrameStyles();applyVerseSurface();return result;};
    fixedLoad.__finalFrameRepair=true;
    loadFrameControlsFromState=fixedLoad;
  }
  if(typeof renderCurrentVerse==='function' && !renderCurrentVerse.__finalSurfaceRepair){
    const oldRender=renderCurrentVerse;
    const fixedRender=function(...args){const result=oldRender.apply(this,args);applyVerseSurface();applyFrameStyles();return result;};
    fixedRender.__finalSurfaceRepair=true;
    renderCurrentVerse=fixedRender;
  }

  // استدعاء أولي بعد تثبيت الإصلاحات، مع الإبقاء على الإعدادات المحفوظة.
  applyVerseSurface(state.verseCardOpacity??opacitySlider?.value??82);
  if(typeof applyFrameStyles==='function')applyFrameStyles();
})();

// === توحيد بطاقة إطارات الشاشة، أزرار الإعدادات العلوية الثابتة، وخط أسماء السور المزخرف ===
(function setupUnifiedFramesTopSettingsAndSurahCalligraphy(){
  // 1) ربط زري الحفظ والإغلاق العلويين الثابتين في نافذة الإعدادات
  $('saveSettingsTop')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$('saveSettings')?.click());
  $('cancelSettingsTop')?.addEventListener('click',()=>$('cancelSettings')?.click());

  // 2) توحيد التحكم في إطارات الشاشة من بطاقة واحدة (frameBrand) ليطبق على كل إطارات الشاشة
  const allTargets=['brand','surah','teacher','controls','verse'];
  function syncUnifiedFramesFromBrand(){
    const brandPreset=$('frameBrand')?.value||'default';
    state.frameStyles=state.frameStyles||{};
    state.frameSettings=state.frameSettings||{};
    state.frameShapes=state.frameShapes||{};
    const w=Number($('frameBrandWidth')?.value??1);
    const c=$('frameBrandColor')?.value||'#d3e0d4';
    const o=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number($('frameBrandOpacity')?.value??100)));
    const r=Number($('frameBrandRadius')?.value??28);
    const l=$('frameBrandLine')?.value||'solid';
    const sh=$('frameShapeBrand')?.value||'default';
    for(const key of allTargets){
      const cap=key[0].toUpperCase()+key.slice(1);
      state.frameStyles[key]=brandPreset;
      state.frameSettings[key]={width:w,color:c,opacity:o,radius:r,style:l};
      state.frameShapes[key]=sh;
      if($('frame'+cap))$('frame'+cap).value=brandPreset;
      if($('frame'+cap+'Width'))$('frame'+cap+'Width').value=String(w);
      if($('frame'+cap+'Color'))$('frame'+cap+'Color').value=c;
      if($('frame'+cap+'Opacity'))$('frame'+cap+'Opacity').value=String(o);
      if($('frame'+cap+'OpacityValue'))$('frame'+cap+'OpacityValue').textContent=o+'%';
      if($('frame'+cap+'Radius'))$('frame'+cap+'Radius').value=String(r);
      if($('frame'+cap+'Line'))$('frame'+cap+'Line').value=l;
      if($('frameShape'+cap))$('frameShape'+cap).value=sh;
    }
    captureFrameSettings();
    applyFrameStyles();
    saveState();
  }
  const bindUnifiedFrameListeners=()=>{
    const presetEl=$('frameBrand');
    if(presetEl&&!presetEl.dataset.unifiedBound){
      presetEl.dataset.unifiedBound='1';
      presetEl.addEventListener('change',()=>setTimeout(syncUnifiedFramesFromBrand,0));
    }
    for(const id of ['frameBrandWidth','frameBrandColor','frameBrandOpacity','frameBrandRadius','frameBrandLine','frameShapeBrand']){
      const el=$(id);
      if(el&&!el.dataset.unifiedBound){
        el.dataset.unifiedBound='1';
        el.addEventListener('input',syncUnifiedFramesFromBrand);
        el.addEventListener('change',syncUnifiedFramesFromBrand);
      }
    }
  };
  bindUnifiedFrameListeners();
  $('settingsToggle')?.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(bindUnifiedFrameListeners,30));

  // 3) خط أسماء السور المزخرف (SurahNameCalligraphy) لبطاقة اسم السورة
  if(state.surahCalligraphyDefaultV1!==1){
    state.fonts=state.fonts||{...DEFAULT_STATE.fonts};
    if(!state.fontFiles?.surah && (!state.fonts.surah || state.fonts.surah==='Amiri')){
      state.fonts.surah='SurahNameCalligraphy';
    }
    state.surahCalligraphyDefaultV1=1;
    saveState();
  }
  const SURAH_CALLIGRAPHY_URLS=[
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v4/surah-name-v4.woff2",
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v4/surah-name-v4.ttf",
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v2/surah-name-v2.woff2",
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v2/surah-name-v2.ttf",
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v1/surah-name-v1.woff2",
    "https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v1/surah-name-v1.ttf"
  ];
  let surahCalligraphyReady=false;
  let syncingSurahTitle=false;
  function isCalligraphySurahFontSelected(){
    if(state?.fontFiles?.surah)return false;
    const f=String(state?.fonts?.surah||'SurahNameCalligraphy').trim();
    return !f || f==='SurahNameCalligraphy' || f==='Amiri';
  }
  function updateSurahTitleDisplay(){
    const el=$('surahTitle');
    if(!el||syncingSurahTitle)return;
    syncingSurahTitle=true;
    try{
      const blank=isBlankDisplay||document.body.classList.contains('blank-display');
      if(blank){
        el.classList.remove('surah-calligraphy-ligature');
        if(el.textContent!=='اختر السورة')el.textContent='اختر السورة';
        el.setAttribute('aria-label','اختر السورة');
        return;
      }
      const ch=Math.max(1,Math.min(114,Number(currentChapter)||108));
      const arName=surahNames[ch-1]||'الكوثر';
      el.setAttribute('aria-label','سورة '+arName);
      if(surahCalligraphyReady && isCalligraphySurahFontSelected()){
        const code='surah'+String(ch).padStart(3,'0');
        el.classList.add('surah-calligraphy-ligature');
        if(el.textContent!==code)el.textContent=code;
      }else{
        el.classList.remove('surah-calligraphy-ligature');
        if(el.textContent!==arName)el.textContent=arName;
      }
    }finally{
      syncingSurahTitle=false;
    }
  }
  async function loadSurahCalligraphyFont(){
    if(typeof FontFace!=='function')return;
    for(const url of SURAH_CALLIGRAPHY_URLS){
      try{
        const face=new FontFace('SurahNameCalligraphy',`url('${url}')`,{display:'swap'});
        await face.load();
        document.fonts.add(face);
        surahCalligraphyReady=true;
        updateSurahTitleDisplay();
        return;
      }catch(_){}
    }
  }
  const titleEl=$('surahTitle');
  if(titleEl){
    new MutationObserver(()=>{if(!syncingSurahTitle)updateSurahTitleDisplay();}).observe(titleEl,{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true});
  }
  loadSurahCalligraphyFont();
  updateSurahTitleDisplay();
  $('saveSettings')?.addEventListener('click',()=>setTimeout(updateSurahTitleDisplay,20));
})();

})();
</script>

<style id="requested-media-icons-and-single-verse-frame">
/* شريط الوسائط: أيقونة الصورة/الفيديو وأيقونة PDF فقط، بلا نص بجوارهما. */
.sticker-actions{display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;gap:5px!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
.sticker-actions small{display:none!important;}
.sticker-actions .tool-btn{display:inline-grid!important;place-items:center!important;width:33px!important;min-width:33px!important;height:32px!important;padding:0!important;border-radius:9px!important;}
.sticker-item img,.sticker-item video{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;user-select:none;touch-action:none;}
.sticker-item video{background:#101010;touch-action:auto;}
.sticker-video-move-handle{position:absolute;left:4px;top:4px;z-index:6;width:28px;height:28px;border:1px solid #b9cdbb;border-radius:9px;display:grid;place-items:center;background:rgba(255,253,246,.94);color:#245640;box-shadow:0 3px 9px #0002;cursor:move;touch-action:none;padding:0;}
/* في وضع الآية الواحدة يُزال الإطار الخارجي الكبير وزخارف البطاقة، مع إبقاء علامة رقم الآية نفسها. */
.verse-stage>.verse-card:has(+ #allVersesCard[hidden]){border-radius:24px!important;}
.verse-stage>.verse-card:has(+ #allVersesCard[hidden])::before,.verse-stage>.verse-card:has(+ #allVersesCard[hidden])::after{display:none!important;content:none!important;}
</style>
<style id="user-single-verse-clear-stage-and-device-media">
/* في عرض الآية الواحدة أزل مساحة المصحف الكريمية الممتدة إلى حواف الشاشة؛ اترك الخلفية العامة المختارة كما هي. */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode){background:transparent!important;background-color:transparent!important;background-image:none!important;}
/* لون الخلفية الموحد يحدده إعداد الشفافية في النمط النهائي أدناه. */
/* أيقونة الصورة مرئية بجوار أيقونة PDF وتفتح منتقي الصور/الفيديو من الجهاز؛ لا تعرض أي زر للصورة القرآنية. */
.sticker-actions{display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;gap:5px!important;flex:0 0 auto!important;}
#addStickerButton{display:inline-grid!important;visibility:visible!important;opacity:1!important;}
.sticker-actions .tool-btn{display:inline-grid!important;place-items:center!important;width:33px!important;min-width:33px!important;height:32px!important;padding:0!important;}
</style>
<script id="preserve-image-add-buttons">
(function(){
 // أبقِ أزرار إضافة الصور الموجودة كما هي، ولا تحذف أي زر من شريط القلم.
 const mediaButton=document.getElementById('addStickerButton');
 if(mediaButton){mediaButton.setAttribute('aria-label','إضافة صورة أو فيديو');mediaButton.title='إضافة صورة أو فيديو';mediaButton.style.display='inline-grid';mediaButton.style.visibility='visible';mediaButton.style.opacity='1';}
})();
</script>
<style id="local-device-backup-ui">.backup-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}.backup-actions .control-btn{min-height:39px}.backup-section #backupStatus{overflow-wrap:anywhere}.backup-section #connectDeviceBackup{background:#edf4e9;color:#245840;border:1px solid #d7e8d7}</style>

<style id="sticky-window-headers-final">
/* إبقاء رأس كل نافذة ظاهرًا أثناء تمرير محتواها، من غير تغيير تخطيط بقية المحتوى. */
#settingsBackdrop .settings-panel{overscroll-behavior:contain;}
#settingsBackdrop .settings-header{
 position:sticky!important;top:-22px!important;z-index:40!important;
 margin-top:-22px!important;padding:22px 0 12px!important;
 background:#fcfdf9!important;border-bottom:1px solid #e1e9df!important;
 box-shadow:0 5px 12px rgba(20,55,35,.045)!important;
}
#settingsBackdrop .settings-tabs{top:88px!important;z-index:39!important;background:rgba(252,253,249,.98)!important;}
.modal-backdrop .modal{
 max-height:min(92vh,950px)!important;overflow-y:auto!important;overflow-x:hidden!important;
 overscroll-behavior:contain!important;scrollbar-gutter:stable;position:relative!important;
}
.modal-backdrop .modal>h2{
 position:sticky!important;top:-24px!important;z-index:20!important;
 margin-top:-24px!important;padding:24px 44px 10px 0!important;
 background:#fffdf7!important;border-bottom:1px solid #e9e2d1!important;
}
.modal-backdrop .modal-close-x{
 position:sticky!important;float:left!important;left:auto!important;top:8px!important;
 margin:0 0 -34px 0!important;z-index:30!important;
}
.modal-backdrop .range-modal-top-actions{z-index:22!important;}
#pdfSideHeader,#pdfSidePanel .pdf-side-header{position:sticky!important;top:0!important;z-index:30!important;}
#boardTextFontPanel{max-height:min(76vh,680px)!important;overflow-y:auto!important;overscroll-behavior:contain!important;}
#boardTextFontPanel .board-text-font-head{position:sticky!important;top:-14px!important;z-index:12!important;background:rgba(255,255,250,.99)!important;padding:4px 0 8px!important;}
.tafsir-panel .tafsir-panel-head{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:5;background:inherit;padding:2px 0 7px;}
@media(max-width:760px){
 #settingsBackdrop .settings-tabs{top:76px!important;}
 #settingsBackdrop .settings-header{padding-top:18px!important;}
 .modal-backdrop .modal>h2{padding-right:42px!important;font-size:19px!important;}
}
</style>


<style id="requested-ayah-background-opacity-and-range-close-alignment">
/* محاذاة علامة الإغلاق أفقيًا مع زري «حفظ» و«إلغاء العرض» في نافذة السورة ونطاق الآيات. */
#editModal .modal.range-modal .modal-close-x{top:12px!important;}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions{top:12px!important;align-items:center!important;height:34px!important;}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions .control-btn{height:34px!important;min-height:34px!important;}
</style>
<style id="final-verse-opacity-and-frame-repair">
/* اللون الأصلي الكريمي #fff7ea في وضعي الآية الواحدة وجميع الآيات؛ المنزلق يتحكم في شفافيته. */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) > .verse-card,
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard {
  background: rgba(255, 247, 234, var(--verse-card-opacity, .82)) !important;
  background-color: rgba(255, 247, 234, var(--verse-card-opacity, .82)) !important;
  background-image: none !important;
}
</style>
<style id="qurancom-qcf-v4-tajweed-mushaf-unified">
/* تطبيق مصحف التجويد الملون من Quran.com (QCF V4 Tajweed + UthmanicHafs) بنفس الشكل والرسم والألوان في وضعي الآية الواحدة وجميع الآيات */
/* في وضع الآية الواحدة: الإطار على حجم الآية القصيرة، وله حد أقصى ثابت عند الآيات الطويلة ليكمل في السطر التالي */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode)>.verse-card,
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{
  display:block!important;
  width:fit-content!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  max-width:min(65vw,860px)!important;
  padding:clamp(14px,1.8vw,22px) clamp(18px,2.4vw,30px)!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
#verseText.verse-text,
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #verseText,
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #verseText.verse-text{
  display:block!important;
  width:auto!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  line-height:2.22!important;
  word-spacing:normal!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
  white-space:normal!important;
  overflow-wrap:break-word!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
}
@media(max-width:900px){
  .verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode)>.verse-card,
  .verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{
    max-width:min(82vw,680px)!important;
  }
}
@media(max-width:560px){
  .verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode)>.verse-card,
  .verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{
    max-width:calc(100vw - 84px)!important;
  }
}
#allVersesCard,.all-verses-card{
  text-align:justify!important;
  text-justify:inter-word!important;
  text-align-last:justify!important;
}
#verseText .word,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row .word,
.all-verse-row .word{
  display:inline!important;
  vertical-align:baseline!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  font-synthesis:none!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
  word-spacing:normal!important;
  padding:0 1px!important;
  margin:0!important;
}
#verseText .word.qcf-v4-ready,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row .word.qcf-v4-ready,
.all-verse-row .word.qcf-v4-ready{
  font-family:var(--qcf-page-font,var(--mushaf-font,'UthmanicHafs',serif))!important;
  font-palette:var(--qcf-font-palette,normal)!important;
}
/* عندما يتم تحميل رمز نهاية الآية من خط مصحف التجويد الأصلي (qcf-v4-ready) يعرض الرمز الملون الأصلي مباشرة دون ازدواجية الخلفية */
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready,
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:hover,
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:focus,
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:active,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:hover,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:focus,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready:active,
button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  position:relative!important;
  vertical-align:baseline!important;
  width:auto!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  height:auto!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  max-height:none!important;
  flex:none!important;
  padding:0 .04em!important;
  margin:0 .04em!important;
  border:0!important;
  outline:0!important;
  border-radius:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  background-image:none!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  color:var(--quran-ink,#11100e)!important;
  font-family:var(--qcf-page-font,'UthmanicHafs',serif)!important;
  font-palette:var(--qcf-font-palette,normal)!important;
  font-size:.95em!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  opacity:var(--ayah-marker-frame-opacity,1)!important;
}
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::before,
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::after,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::before,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::after,
button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::before,
button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready::after{
  content:none!important;
  display:none!important;
  background:none!important;
  background-image:none!important;
  border:0!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
}
#verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready .ayah-end-number,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready .ayah-end-number,
button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready .ayah-end-number{
  display:inline!important;
  position:static!important;
  font-family:var(--qcf-page-font,'UthmanicHafs',serif)!important;
  font-palette:var(--qcf-font-palette,normal)!important;
  font-size:1em!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
  color:inherit!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  padding:0!important;
  margin:0!important;
}
.app.dark-mode #verseText button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready,
.app.dark-mode #allVersesCard .all-verse-row button.ayah-end.qcf-v4-ready{
  color:#f0f4dc!important;
}
#verseText.reciting-verse .word.qcf-v4-ready,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row.reciting-verse .word.qcf-v4-ready{
  filter:grayscale(.85) brightness(.75)!important;
  opacity:.82!important;
}
#verseText.reciting-verse .word.qcf-v4-ready.speaking,
#allVersesCard .all-verse-row.reciting-verse .word.qcf-v4-ready.speaking{
  filter:none!important;
  opacity:1!important;
}
</style>

<style id="user-requested-v5-enhancements">
@font-face{
  font-family:'SurahNameCalligraphy';
  src:url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v4/surah-name-v4.woff2') format('woff2'),
      url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v4/surah-name-v4.ttf') format('truetype'),
      url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v2/surah-name-v2.woff2') format('woff2'),
      url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v2/surah-name-v2.ttf') format('truetype'),
      url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v1/surah-name-v1.woff2') format('woff2'),
      url('https://static-cdn.tarteel.ai/qul/fonts/surah-names/v1/surah-name-v1.ttf') format('truetype');
  font-weight:400;
  font-style:normal;
  font-display:swap;
}

/* 1) خط اسم السورة المزخرف في بطاقة اسم السورة */
#surahTitle.surah-calligraphy-ligature{
  font-family:'SurahNameCalligraphy','Aref Ruqaa','Reem Kufi','Amiri',serif!important;
  font-size:calc(var(--surah-size, 28px) * 1.38)!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
  line-height:1.08!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
  font-feature-settings:"liga" 1,"calt" 1,"dlig" 1!important;
  font-variant-ligatures:common-ligatures contextual discretionary-ligatures!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  display:inline-block!important;
  padding:2px 4px!important;
}
#surahTitle:not(.surah-calligraphy-ligature){
  font-family:'Aref Ruqaa',var(--surah-font,'Amiri',serif)!important;
}

/* 2) تخطيط صفحات المصحف (١٥ سطرًا) وخط فاصل نهاية الصفحة في وضع عرض جميع الآيات */
.mushaf-edition-banner{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:10px!important;
  flex-wrap:wrap!important;
  padding:6px 14px!important;
  margin:0 auto 10px!important;
  max-width:960px!important;
  border-radius:12px!important;
  background:rgba(22,75,59,.07)!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(22,75,59,.16)!important;
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  color:#1d513b!important;
}
.mushaf-edition-pages{
  padding:2px 10px!important;
  border-radius:999px!important;
  background:#fbf2d9!important;
  border:1px solid #d8c083!important;
  color:#7a581d!important;
  font-size:11px!important;
}
.mushaf-page-sheet{
  width:min(100%, 960px)!important;
  margin:0 auto 14px!important;
  padding:clamp(12px, 1.8vw, 22px) clamp(14px, 2.5vw, 32px)!important;
  border:2px solid #d5c18d!important;
  outline:1px solid rgba(197,163,75,.38)!important;
  outline-offset:-6px!important;
  border-radius:18px!important;
  background:rgba(255, 249, 238, var(--verse-card-opacity, .92))!important;
  box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(24,58,39,.07)!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
}
.app.dark-mode .mushaf-page-sheet{
  background:rgba(26, 46, 37, var(--verse-card-opacity, .94))!important;
  border-color:#5a755f!important;
}
.mushaf-page-header{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:8px!important;
  padding:0 4px 8px!important;
  margin-bottom:8px!important;
  border-bottom:1px solid rgba(197,163,75,.45)!important;
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  color:#7a581d!important;
}
.app.dark-mode .mushaf-page-header{
  color:#e8d399!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines{
  display:block!important;
  width:100%!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  text-align:justify!important;
  text-justify:inter-word!important;
  text-align-last:justify!important;
  line-height:2.28!important;
  padding:4px 6px!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines .all-verse-row{
  display:inline!important;
  text-align:justify!important;
  text-align-last:justify!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines .word{
  display:inline-block!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines .all-verse-tafsir{
  display:block!important;
  text-align:right!important;
  text-align-last:right!important;
}
.ayah-hover-menu{
  position:fixed!important;
  z-index:10120!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:6px!important;
  padding:5px 7px!important;
  border-radius:12px!important;
  background:rgba(20,58,43,.97)!important;
  border:1px solid #d6b36a!important;
  box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(10,32,22,.28)!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  backdrop-filter:blur(8px)!important;
}
.ayah-hover-menu[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}
.ayah-hover-choice{
  padding:5px 11px!important;
  border-radius:8px!important;
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.14)!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.28)!important;
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;
  font-size:11.5px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
  transition:background .14s,transform .14s!important;
}
.ayah-hover-choice:hover{
  background:#d6b36a!important;
  color:#143829!important;
  border-color:#f2dfaa!important;
  transform:translateY(-1px)!important;
}
.mushaf-line:last-child{
  border-bottom:0!important;
}
.mushaf-Page-bismillah,.mushaf-page-bismillah{
  margin:4px 0 8px!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  gap:14px!important;
  width:100%!important;
  margin:14px 0 18px!important;
  padding:4px 0!important;
  clear:both!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  user-select:none!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider::before,
.mushaf-page-divider::after{
  content:""!important;
  flex:1 1 auto!important;
  height:1.5px!important;
  background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(189,154,74,.14),rgba(189,154,74,.85),rgba(189,154,74,.14))!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider-badge{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  padding:2px 15px!important;
  border-radius:999px!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(189,154,74,.68)!important;
  background:rgba(251,242,217,.88)!important;
  color:#7a581d!important;
  font-family:'Cairo','Amiri',sans-serif!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  line-height:1.45!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(139,107,44,.08)!important;
}
.app.dark-mode .mushaf-page-divider-badge{
  background:rgba(42,67,52,.92)!important;
  color:#f0dfaa!important;
  border-color:rgba(214,179,106,.55)!important;
}

/* 3) رفع زر «الآيات الحالية» قليلًا إلى الأعلى والتحكم المستقل في إخفاء/إظهار العناصر */
#screenItemTray{
  top:20%!important;
}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item[data-tray-kind="verses"]{
  margin-top:-2px!important;
  margin-bottom:2px!important;
}
body.verses-hidden-by-tray .center-wrap{
  display:none!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item[data-tray-hidden="1"]{
  display:none!important;
}
#boardTextLayer .board-text-item[data-tray-hidden="1"]{
  display:none!important;
}

/* 4) توسيع لوحة التحكم قليلًا لاستيعاب السهمين المزدوجين للتنقل بين الصور مع الحفاظ على ألوانها */
.surah-control-group>.control-card{
  max-width:min(100%, 670px)!important;
  padding:6px 10px!important;
}
.surah-control-group .verse-nav{
  margin-top:-2px!important;
}
.control-card .image-nav-arrow{
  width:32px!important;
  min-width:32px!important;
  height:32px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border-radius:50%!important;
}
body:not(.child-theme) .control-card,
body.adult-theme .control-card{
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,#133c31,#245a43 60%,#94773e)!important;
  border-color:#c1a664!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(22,75,59,.18)!important;
}
body.child-theme .control-card{
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,#a64b91,#7253bc 55%,#428cc5)!important;
  border-color:#c895e2!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(119,80,165,.2)!important;
}
.control-card .verse-bubble,
.control-card .more-btn,
.control-card .audio-row .control-btn,
.control-card .audio-row #playVerse{
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.16)!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.32)!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
}
.control-card .verse-bubble:hover,
.control-card .more-btn:hover,
.control-card .audio-row .control-btn:hover,
.control-card .audio-row #playVerse:hover{
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.28)!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
}
.control-card .verse-bubble.active,
.control-card .audio-row .control-btn.active,
.control-card .audio-row #playVerse.active{
  background:rgba(255,255,255,.34)!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  border:1.5px solid #ffffff!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(255,255,255,.22)!important;
}
.control-card .audio-row .control-btn svg,
.control-card .audio-row #playVerse svg{
  color:#ffffff!important;
}
.control-card .audio-row #playVerse svg,
.control-card .audio-row #listenRecording svg{
  fill:#ffffff!important;
}
.control-card .record-indicator{
  color:#ffffff!important;
}

/* 5) تنسيق نافذة اختيار السورة والآيات والبرومبتات الأربعة ونوع التصميم */
#editModal .modal.range-modal{
  width:min(760px, 100%)!important;
  padding:18px 20px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal > h2{
  position:sticky!important;
  top:-18px!important;
  z-index:25!important;
  margin:-18px 0 6px 0!important;
  padding:16px 0 10px 218px!important;
  background:#fffdf7!important;
  border-bottom:1px solid #e9e2d1!important;
  font-size:19px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal > p#modalDescription{
  margin:0 0 8px!important;
  font-size:11.5px!important;
  line-height:1.55!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions{
  position:sticky!important;
  float:left!important;
  top:8px!important;
  left:auto!important;
  margin:0 0 -34px 8px!important;
  z-index:30!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:6px!important;
  height:34px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions .control-btn{
  height:34px!important;
  min-height:34px!important;
  padding:0 12px!important;
  border-radius:10px!important;
  font-size:11.5px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .modal-close-x{
  position:sticky!important;
  float:left!important;
  top:8px!important;
  left:auto!important;
  width:34px!important;
  height:34px!important;
  min-height:34px!important;
  margin:0 0 -34px 0!important;
  z-index:31!important;
}
.range-modal .chapter-grid.range-top-three-cols{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:minmax(150px,1.35fr) minmax(88px,.82fr) minmax(88px,.82fr)!important;
  gap:9px!important;
  align-items:start!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .chapter-grid.range-top-three-cols .field-group{
  position:relative!important;
}
.range-modal #surahSearchResults{
  position:absolute!important;
  top:calc(100% + 4px)!important;
  right:0!important;
  left:0!important;
  z-index:45!important;
  margin-top:0!important;
}
.range-modal .chapter-grid.range-top-three-cols input,
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row input,
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row select,
.range-modal #quranVerseSearch,
.range-modal #rangeReciterSelect{
  height:40px!important;
  min-height:40px!important;
  padding:7px 10px!important;
  font-size:12.5px!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
.range-modal .quran-search-group{
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:minmax(110px,.75fr) minmax(180px,1.45fr)!important;
  gap:9px!important;
  align-items:end!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row .range-action-row.prompt-row{
  margin:0!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row #copyLessonPrompt{
  height:40px!important;
  min-height:40px!important;
  padding:0 15px!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  background:#edf4e9!important;
  color:#174b3b!important;
  border:1px solid #c8dac5!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row #copyLessonPrompt:hover{
  background:#dfece0!important;
}
.range-modal .specialized-prompts-row{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))!important;
  gap:6px!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .specialized-prompts-row .mini-prompt-btn{
  height:35px!important;
  min-height:35px!important;
  padding:4px 6px!important;
  border-radius:10px!important;
  font-size:11px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  gap:4px!important;
  background:linear-gradient(145deg,#fbfcf9,#eef5ec)!important;
  color:#1d523c!important;
  border:1px solid #d2e1d0!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  overflow:hidden!important;
  text-overflow:ellipsis!important;
}
.range-modal .specialized-prompts-row .mini-prompt-btn:hover{
  background:#e1efe0!important;
  border-color:#adc8ac!important;
}
.range-modal .specialized-prompts-row .mini-prompt-btn svg{
  flex:0 0 auto!important;
  color:#9c7b34!important;
}
.range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))!important;
  gap:7px!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row .control-btn{
  height:37px!important;
  min-height:37px!important;
  padding:6px 8px!important;
  font-size:11.5px!important;
}
.range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row #playRangeButton{
  grid-column:auto!important;
  background:#f2f6f0!important;
  color:#2b5845!important;
  border:1px solid #e0e9df!important;
}
.range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row #playRangeButton:hover{
  background:#e6f0e5!important;
}
.range-modal .range-extra-grid.range-reciter-last{
  margin:4px 0 6px!important;
}
.range-modal .small-note{
  margin-top:6px!important;
  font-size:10.5px!important;
  line-height:1.5!important;
}
.range-modal .modal-actions{
  margin-top:10px!important;
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:8px!important;
  flex-wrap:wrap!important;
}
.range-modal .modal-actions .control-btn{
  height:36px!important;
  min-height:36px!important;
  padding:6px 16px!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
}
.range-modal .modal-actions #cancelModal.range-cancel-small{
  min-width:95px!important;
  height:36px!important;
  min-height:36px!important;
  padding:6px 14px!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  background:#fff4ed!important;
  color:#8d4233!important;
  border-color:#efd3c6!important;
}

/* 6) الإعدادات وخطوط النظام الموحدة */
body, button, input, select, textarea{
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',Tahoma,sans-serif);
}
#programTitleButton, #teacherNameButton{
  color:var(--system-font-color,var(--green))!important;
}
.system-font-size-row{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:36px 1fr 36px 52px!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:7px!important;
}
.font-zoom-btn{
  width:34px!important;
  height:32px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  display:grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  font-size:17px!important;
  font-weight:800!important;
}
.system-font-color-row{
  align-items:center!important;
}
.system-font-color-row input[type="color"]{
  width:56px!important;
  height:36px!important;
  padding:2px!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
}
#settingsBackdrop .settings-header{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:flex-start!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:12px!important;
  position:sticky!important;
  top:-22px!important;
  z-index:45!important;
  background:#fcfdf9!important;
  padding:18px 0 12px!important;
  border-bottom:1px solid #e1e9df!important;
}
.settings-title-group{
  flex:1 1 auto!important;
  min-width:0!important;
}
.settings-title-bar{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:12px!important;
  flex-wrap:wrap!important;
}
.settings-top-actions{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:7px!important;
}
.settings-top-actions .control-btn{
  padding:7px 13px!important;
  min-height:34px!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  border-radius:11px!important;
}
.frame-settings-grid.unified-frame-grid{
  grid-template-columns:1fr!important;
}
.frame-settings-grid.unified-frame-grid .field-group[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}

@media(max-width:640px){
  #editModal .modal.range-modal{
    padding:14px 12px!important;
  }
  #editModal .modal.range-modal > h2{
    padding-left:188px!important;
    font-size:16px!important;
  }
  .range-modal .chapter-grid.range-top-three-cols{
    grid-template-columns:minmax(105px,1.25fr) minmax(68px,.85fr) minmax(68px,.85fr)!important;
    gap:6px!important;
  }
  .range-modal .age-and-prompt-row{
    grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr!important;
    gap:6px!important;
  }
  .range-modal .age-and-prompt-row .range-action-row.prompt-row{
    grid-column:1 / -1!important;
  }
  .range-modal .age-and-prompt-row #copyLessonPrompt{
    width:100%!important;
    justify-content:center!important;
  }
  .range-modal .specialized-prompts-row{
    display:grid!important;
    grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(112px,1fr))!important;
    overflow-x:auto!important;
    padding-bottom:3px!important;
    scrollbar-width:thin!important;
  }
  .range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row{
    grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr))!important;
    gap:5px!important;
  }
  .range-modal .range-action-row.compact-playback-row.three-buttons-row .control-btn{
    font-size:10px!important;
    padding:5px 4px!important;
  }
}
</style>

<style id="mushaf-v8-master-layout">
/* 1) إطار خارجي واحد فقط في عرض جميع الآيات مطابق لتصميم إطار الآية الواحدة، وإزالة أي إطار داخلي */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) > .verse-card,
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
  border:1.5px solid #e4ddc4!important;
  border-radius:24px!important;
  box-shadow:0 10px 28px rgba(17,49,38,.09)!important;
  background:rgba(255, 247, 234, var(--verse-card-opacity, .82))!important;
  background-color:rgba(255, 247, 234, var(--verse-card-opacity, .82))!important;
}
.app.dark-mode .verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) > .verse-card,
.app.dark-mode .verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
  border-color:#57745b!important;
  background:rgba(26, 46, 37, var(--verse-card-opacity, .92))!important;
  background-color:rgba(26, 46, 37, var(--verse-card-opacity, .92))!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode{
  bottom:0!important;
  padding:0 12px 0!important;
  margin-bottom:0!important;
  align-items:stretch!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
  width:min(1020px, calc(100vw - 28px))!important;
  max-width:1020px!important;
  flex:0 1 1020px!important;
  height:100%!important;
  min-height:100%!important;
  max-height:100%!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
  padding:clamp(18px, 2.2vw, 28px) clamp(20px, 3vw, 40px) 96px!important;
  border-radius:24px 24px 0 0!important;
  border-bottom:0!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  text-align-last:center!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
.mushaf-page-sheet,
.app.dark-mode .mushaf-page-sheet{
  width:100%!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:0 auto 6px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border:0!important;
  outline:0!important;
  outline-offset:0!important;
  border-radius:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  background-color:transparent!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
}
.mushaf-edition-banner{
  display:none!important;
}

/* 2) رأس الصفحة: اسم السورة ورقم الجزء ورقم الصفحة */
.mushaf-page-header{
  display:flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:10px!important;
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 100%)!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  padding:0 2px 6px!important;
  margin:0 auto 8px!important;
  border-bottom:1px solid rgba(197,163,75,.38)!important;
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;
  font-size:12.5px!important;
  font-weight:700!important;
  color:#7a581d!important;
}
.mushaf-page-header span{
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}

/* 3) البسملة متوازنة في الوسط */
.all-bismillah,
.mushaf-page-bismillah{
  display:block!important;
  width:fit-content!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:2px auto 8px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  text-align-last:center!important;
  word-spacing:.06em!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
  line-height:1.5!important;
  font-size:calc(var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px) * 0.86)!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}

/* 4) أسطر المصحف الدقيقة (.mushaf-line): متساوية تمامًا من الجهتين (اليمين واليسار) بلا أي سطر طالع وبمسافات طبيعية */
.mushaf-page-lines{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:column!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:2px!important;
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 100%)!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
  padding:2px 0!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
.mushaf-line{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  flex-wrap:nowrap!important;
  align-items:baseline!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 100%)!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
  padding:1px 0!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  font-size:var(--mushaf-line-font-size, var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px))!important;
  line-height:1.95!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  word-spacing:normal!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
.mushaf-line.mushaf-line-short{
  justify-content:center!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  text-align-last:center!important;
  gap:.14em!important;
}
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-line{
  width:max-content!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  justify-content:flex-start!important;
  gap:.14em!important;
  font-size:var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px)!important;
}
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line,
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line > .all-verse-row,
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line .word{
  font-size:var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px)!important;
}
.mushaf-line > .all-verse-row,
.mushaf-page-lines .all-verse-row.mushaf-verse-segment{
  display:contents!important;
  font-size:inherit!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
}
.mushaf-line .word,
.mushaf-line .quran-pause-mark,
.mushaf-line .quran-section-marker,
.mushaf-line .quran-sajdah-marker{
  display:inline-block!important;
  flex:0 0 auto!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  font-size:inherit!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
}
.quran-pause-mark{
  margin-right:.03em!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
.mushaf-word-end-pair{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  flex-wrap:nowrap!important;
  align-items:baseline!important;
  gap:.04em!important;
  flex:0 0 auto!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines .all-verse-tafsir{
  display:block!important;
  width:100%!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
  text-align:right!important;
  text-align-last:right!important;
  white-space:normal!important;
}

/* 5) ترتيب أرقام الآيات في لوحة التحكم تصاعديًا ووضع علامة (+) في النهاية */
#verseNav{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
}
#verseNumberPopup{
  direction:rtl!important;
}

/* 6) نافذة اختيار السورة ونطاق الآيات: عمر الطالب بطول الصف كاملًا وحذف زر X */
#editModal .modal.range-modal .modal-close-x,
.range-modal #modalCloseX{
  display:none!important;
  visibility:hidden!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions{
  left:14px!important;
  margin:0 0 -34px 0!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal > h2{
  padding-left:176px!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row{
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:1fr!important;
  width:100%!important;
  gap:8px!important;
  margin:6px 0 8px!important;
}
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row .age-field-group,
.range-modal .age-and-prompt-row #studentAgeChoice{
  width:100%!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
}

/* 7) زر الثلاث نقاط (•••) عند الإشارة إلى رقم الآية: خلفية بيضاء ونقاط سوداء قريب جدًا من رقم الآية */
.ayah-hover-menu{
  position:fixed!important;
  z-index:10120!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  gap:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  border-radius:999px!important;
  background:#ffffff!important;
  border:1px solid #c8c8c8!important;
  box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.16)!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  backdrop-filter:none!important;
  pointer-events:auto!important;
}
.ayah-hover-menu[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}
.ayah-hover-choice{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  width:auto!important;
  min-width:34px!important;
  height:22px!important;
  padding:0 8px!important;
  border-radius:999px!important;
  background:#ffffff!important;
  color:#000000!important;
  border:0!important;
  font-family:var(--system-ui-font,'Cairo',sans-serif)!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:900!important;
  letter-spacing:1.5px!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
  transition:background .12s,transform .12s!important;
}
.ayah-hover-choice:hover{
  background:#f0f0f0!important;
  color:#000000!important;
  transform:scale(1.04)!important;
}

@media(max-width:720px){
  .verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
    width:calc(100vw - 16px)!important;
    height:100%!important;
    min-height:100%!important;
    max-height:100%!important;
    padding:14px 12px 96px!important;
    border-radius:18px 18px 0 0!important;
    border-bottom:0!important;
  }
  .mushaf-page-header{
    font-size:11px!important;
  }
  .mushaf-line{
    line-height:1.85!important;
  }
}
</style>


<style id="glossy-sticker-pack-styles">
/* 1) استعادة الشكل الأصلي للمصحف بدون فواصل أو رؤوس إضافية داخلية (مطابق للصورة المرجعية) */
.mushaf-page-header,
.mushaf-edition-banner,
.all-verse-row .mushaf-page-divider{
  display:none!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:column!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  gap:6px!important;
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 92%)!important;
  margin:14px auto 18px!important;
  padding:4px 0 8px!important;
  border-bottom:1px solid rgba(210,192,148,.55)!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider::before,
.mushaf-page-divider::after{
  display:none!important;
  content:none!important;
}
.mushaf-page-divider-badge{
  background:transparent!important;
  border:0!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  padding:0!important;
  color:#718078!important;
  font-family:'Amiri','Cairo',serif!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:400!important;
}

/* 2) تاسعًا: تغيير لون الكلمة عند الضغط عليها للاستماع إلى خلفية خضراء فاتحة جدًا وهادئة وشفافة */
.word:hover,
#verseText .word:hover,
#allVersesCard .word:hover,
.all-verse-row .word:hover{
  background:rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.10)!important;
  border-radius:6px!important;
}
.word.speaking,
#verseText .word.speaking,
#allVersesCard .word.speaking,
.all-verse-row .word.speaking{
  background:rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.18)!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.11)!important;
  border-radius:6px!important;
  animation:pulseWordCalmGreen .9s ease-in-out infinite alternate!important;
}
@keyframes pulseWordCalmGreen{
  from{background:rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.14)!important;}
  to{background:rgba(46, 204, 113, 0.24)!important;}
}

/* 3) سابعًا: نافذة اختيار السورة ونطاق الآيات — حذف «إلغاء العرض» أعلى وأسفل، ونقل زري «عرض» لأقصى اليسار بنفس الحجم */
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-top-blank,
#editModal .modal.range-modal #cancelModal,
.range-modal .modal-actions #cancelModal{
  display:none!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions{
  position:sticky!important;
  float:left!important;
  left:0!important;
  top:6px!important;
  margin:0 0 -38px 0!important;
  z-index:32!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:flex-end!important;
  height:38px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .modal-actions{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  justify-content:flex-end!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  width:100%!important;
  margin-top:12px!important;
}
#editModal .modal.range-modal .range-modal-top-actions .range-top-save,
#editModal .modal.range-modal .modal-actions button[type="submit"]{
  min-width:114px!important;
  height:38px!important;
  min-height:38px!important;
  padding:7px 22px!important;
  border-radius:12px!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:800!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(22,75,59,.18)!important;
}

/* 4) ثالثًا: زوم واجهة البرنامج الثابت والمريح (70% افتراضيًا) وفصل زوم المصحف عن زوم الواجهة */
:root{
  --ui-zoom:0.70;
  --mushaf-zoom:1.00;
  --effective-mushaf-scale:1.00;
}
.topbar{
  width:100%!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding-inline:4px!important;
  display:grid!important;
  grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto minmax(0,1fr)!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:8px!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
}
.topbar > .brand{
  grid-column:1!important;
  grid-row:1!important;
  justify-self:start!important;
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:right top!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}
.topbar > .surah-control-group,
#surahControlGroup{
  grid-column:2!important;
  grid-row:1!important;
  justify-self:center!important;
  width:max-content!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  flex-wrap:nowrap!important;
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:center top!important;
}
.topbar > .teacher{
  grid-column:3!important;
  grid-row:1!important;
  justify-self:end!important;
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:left top!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
}
#screenItemTray{
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:left top!important;
  padding:7px 8px!important;
  overflow-y:auto!important;
  overflow-x:visible!important;
}
#boardNav{
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:bottom right!important;
}
#penToggle{
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:bottom left!important;
}
#settingsToggle{
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:bottom left!important;
  bottom:calc(14px + 58px * var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
}
#penToolbar{
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:bottom left!important;
  left:calc(16px + 62px * var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
}
/* تكبير وتصغير مساحة ومحتويات المصحف في وضع الآية الواحدة ووضع جميع الآيات */
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) > .verse-card,
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) .verse-card{
  max-width:min(calc(100vw - 24px), calc(860px * var(--effective-mushaf-scale, 1)))!important;
  padding:calc(18px * var(--effective-mushaf-scale, 1)) calc(26px * var(--effective-mushaf-scale, 1))!important;
}
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #verseText{
  font-size:calc(var(--mushaf-size, 52px) * var(--effective-mushaf-scale, 1))!important;
}
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #bismillah{
  font-size:calc(var(--mushaf-size, 52px) * 0.62 * var(--effective-mushaf-scale, 1))!important;
}
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line,
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line > .all-verse-row,
#allVersesCard .mushaf-line .word{
  font-size:var(--mushaf-line-font-size, var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px))!important;
}
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-line,
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-line > .all-verse-row,
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-line .word{
  font-size:var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px)!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
  scroll-padding-top:24px!important;
}

/* 5) رابعًا: قسم الملصقات التشجيعية (شريط قصير مثل شريط القلم مع تمرير أفقي) + معاينة التكبير التلقائي */
#stickerPackPopup.sticker-pack-popup{
  position:fixed!important;
  left:calc(16px + 62px * var(--ui-zoom, 0.70));
  bottom:calc(18px + 48px * var(--ui-zoom, 0.70));
  z-index:10075!important;
  width:var(--sticker-popup-width, 650px)!important;
  max-width:var(--sticker-popup-width, calc(100vw - 100px))!important;
  height:86px!important;
  min-height:86px!important;
  max-height:86px!important;
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:9px!important;
  padding:8px 12px!important;
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(255,255,255,.98),rgba(248,252,247,.97))!important;
  border:1.5px solid #cfe0ce!important;
  border-radius:20px!important;
  box-shadow:0 16px 40px rgba(16,52,35,.22),inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.95)!important;
  backdrop-filter:blur(16px)!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
  box-sizing:border-box!important;
  overflow:visible!important;
  user-select:none!important;
  transform:scale(var(--ui-zoom, 0.70))!important;
  transform-origin:bottom left!important;
}
#stickerPackPopup.sticker-pack-popup[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}
.app.dark-mode ~ #stickerPackPopup.sticker-pack-popup{
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(27,48,39,.98),rgba(21,38,30,.98))!important;
  border-color:#3e5d4b!important;
  box-shadow:0 16px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.42)!important;
}
#stickerPackToggle.active{
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f7d56e,#d49b27)!important;
  color:#1b3b2b!important;
  border-color:#c99222!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(218,165,32,.28),0 4px 10px rgba(180,125,18,.25)!important;
}
.sticker-pack-add-btn{
  flex:0 0 62px!important;
  width:62px!important;
  height:62px!important;
  min-width:62px!important;
  border-radius:15px!important;
  border:1.5px dashed #287658!important;
  background:linear-gradient(145deg,#f3faf4,#dff0e3)!important;
  color:#174b3a!important;
  display:grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  padding:0!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
  box-shadow:0 4px 10px rgba(22,75,59,.10),inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.85)!important;
  transition:transform .16s ease,background .16s ease,box-shadow .16s ease!important;
}
.sticker-pack-add-btn:hover{
  transform:scale(1.06)!important;
  background:linear-gradient(145deg,#e5f5e8,#cce8d3)!important;
  border-style:solid!important;
  box-shadow:0 6px 14px rgba(22,75,59,.18)!important;
}
.sticker-pack-list{
  flex:1 1 0!important;
  width:0!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  height:100%!important;
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:9px!important;
  overflow-x:auto!important;
  overflow-y:hidden!important;
  padding:4px 6px!important;
  scrollbar-width:thin!important;
  scrollbar-color:#c4d6c4 transparent!important;
  scroll-behavior:smooth!important;
}
.sticker-pack-list::-webkit-scrollbar{
  height:5px!important;
}
.sticker-pack-list::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{
  background:rgba(36,88,66,.26)!important;
  border-radius:99px!important;
}
.sticker-pack-thumb{
  position:relative!important;
  flex:0 0 66px!important;
  width:66px!important;
  height:66px!important;
  min-width:66px!important;
  padding:3px!important;
  border-radius:15px!important;
  border:1.5px solid rgba(210,224,210,.9)!important;
  background:radial-gradient(circle at 30% 22%,rgba(255,255,255,.99),rgba(242,248,241,.92))!important;
  box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(17,52,35,.10),inset 0 1px 0 #fff!important;
  display:grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
  transition:transform .18s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1.2),box-shadow .18s ease,border-color .18s ease!important;
}
.sticker-pack-thumb:hover,
.sticker-pack-thumb.is-hovered{
  transform:translateY(-4px) scale(1.18)!important;
  border-color:#d4af37!important;
  box-shadow:0 10px 22px rgba(184,134,11,.28),0 0 0 2.5px rgba(245,215,110,.55)!important;
  z-index:5!important;
}
.sticker-pack-thumb img{
  width:100%!important;
  height:100%!important;
  aspect-ratio:1/1!important;
  object-fit:contain!important;
  display:block!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  filter:drop-shadow(0 3px 5px rgba(0,0,0,.16))!important;
}
.sticker-pack-thumb-del{
  position:absolute!important;
  top:-4px!important;
  left:-4px!important;
  width:18px!important;
  height:18px!important;
  border-radius:50%!important;
  background:#fff0ed!important;
  color:#b71c1c!important;
  border:1px solid #e6a8a0!important;
  font-size:12px!important;
  font-weight:900!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  display:none!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  padding:0!important;
  box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.18)!important;
}
.sticker-pack-thumb.custom-pack-item:hover .sticker-pack-thumb-del{
  display:grid!important;
}
/* نافذة المعاينة المكبرة التلقائية للملصق عند الوقوف عليه بالمؤشر */
#stickerHoverPreview{
  position:fixed!important;
  z-index:10110!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:column!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  gap:6px!important;
  padding:12px 14px 10px!important;
  width:214px!important;
  background:radial-gradient(circle at 35% 25%,#ffffff,#f6faf5)!important;
  border:2px solid #d4af37!important;
  border-radius:24px!important;
  box-shadow:0 20px 50px rgba(12,40,28,.32),0 0 0 3px rgba(255,248,214,.75)!important;
  transform:translate(-50%, 0) scale(1)!important;
  transition:left .08s ease-out,top .08s ease-out,opacity .14s ease!important;
  direction:rtl!important;
}
#stickerHoverPreview[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}
#stickerHoverPreview img{
  width:182px!important;
  height:182px!important;
  aspect-ratio:1/1!important;
  object-fit:contain!important;
  display:block!important;
  filter:drop-shadow(0 8px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.22))!important;
}
#stickerHoverPreview span{
  display:inline-block!important;
  padding:3px 12px!important;
  border-radius:999px!important;
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,#174b3a,#246950)!important;
  color:#fff9db!important;
  font-family:'Cairo','Tajawal',sans-serif!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:800!important;
  line-height:1.35!important;
  text-align:center!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  max-width:194px!important;
  overflow:hidden!important;
  text-overflow:ellipsis!important;
  box-shadow:0 3px 8px rgba(22,75,59,.2)!important;
}

/* 6) تحديد وتحجيم الملصقات والصور والنصوص والرسومات: لا يظهر مربع التحجيم للملصق إلا عند الضغط عليه وتحديده */
#stickerLayer .sticker-item:not(.selected-sticker):not(.multi-selected){
  outline:none!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker,
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.multi-selected{
  outline:2px solid #8b5cf6!important;
  outline-offset:2px!important;
  border-radius:6px!important;
}
.board-text-item.selected,
.board-text-item.multi-selected{
  outline:2px solid #8b5cf6!important;
  outline-offset:4px!important;
  border-radius:6px!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-corner-handle,
.board-text-item .text-corner-handle,
#canvasSelectionBox .group-corner-handle{
  position:absolute!important;
  width:16px!important;
  height:16px!important;
  min-width:16px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border-radius:50%!important;
  background:#ffffff!important;
  border:2.5px solid #8b5cf6!important;
  box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(17,24,39,.28)!important;
  display:none!important;
  z-index:9!important;
  touch-action:none!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item:not(.selected-sticker):not(.multi-selected) .sticker-corner-handle,
#stickerLayer .sticker-item:not(.selected-sticker):not(.multi-selected) .sticker-resize-handle{
  display:none!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker .sticker-corner-handle,
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.multi-selected .sticker-corner-handle,
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker .sticker-resize-handle,
#stickerLayer .sticker-item.multi-selected .sticker-resize-handle,
.board-text-item.selected .text-corner-handle,
.board-text-item.multi-selected .text-corner-handle,
#canvasSelectionBox:not([hidden]) .group-corner-handle{
  display:flex!important;
}
.corner-nw{left:-9px!important;top:-9px!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;}
.corner-ne{right:-9px!important;top:-9px!important;cursor:nesw-resize!important;}
.corner-sw{left:-9px!important;bottom:-9px!important;cursor:nesw-resize!important;}
