.corner-se{right:-9px!important;bottom:-9px!important;cursor:nwse-resize!important;}
#stickerLayer .sticker-resize-handle{
  right:-11px!important;
  bottom:-11px!important;
  width:24px!important;
  height:24px!important;
  min-width:24px!important;
  border:2px solid #8b5cf6!important;
  background:#ffffff!important;
  color:#6d28d9!important;
  box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(17,24,39,.28)!important;
  z-index:8!important;
}
#stickerLayer .sticker-corner-handle:hover,
#stickerLayer .sticker-resize-handle:hover,
.board-text-item .text-corner-handle:hover,
#canvasSelectionBox .group-corner-handle:hover{
  transform:scale(1.18)!important;
  background:#f5f3ff!important;
}

/* علامة X صغيرة في الزاوية اليسرى لكل عنصر في القائمة الجانبية لا تظهر إلا عند الإشارة إليها */
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item{
  position:relative!important;
  overflow:visible!important;
}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-delete{
  position:absolute!important;
  left:-5px!important;
  top:-5px!important;
  right:auto!important;
  bottom:auto!important;
  width:18px!important;
  height:18px!important;
  min-width:18px!important;
  padding:0!important;
  border-radius:50%!important;
  background:#ef4444!important;
  color:#ffffff!important;
  border:1.5px solid #ffffff!important;
  font-size:13px!important;
  font-weight:900!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  display:grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  opacity:0!important;
  visibility:hidden!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  transform:scale(0.75)!important;
  transition:opacity .14s ease,transform .14s ease,visibility .14s ease,background .14s ease!important;
  z-index:12!important;
  box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.28)!important;
  cursor:pointer!important;
}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item:hover .screen-tray-delete,
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-item:focus-visible .screen-tray-delete{
  opacity:1!important;
  visibility:visible!important;
  pointer-events:auto!important;
  transform:scale(1)!important;
}
#screenItemTray .screen-tray-delete:hover{
  background:#dc2626!important;
  transform:scale(1.15)!important;
}

/* إطار التحديد الموحد للرسومات والمجموعات (#canvasSelectionBox) ومستطيل السحب (#boardSelectionMarquee) */
#boardSelectionMarquee{
  position:fixed!important;
  z-index:10085!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  border:1.8px solid #8b5cf6!important;
  background:rgba(139,92,246,.12)!important;
  border-radius:4px!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,.5)!important;
}
#boardSelectionMarquee[hidden],
#canvasSelectionBox[hidden]{
  display:none!important;
}
body.pen-drawing-active #stickerLayer .sticker-item,
body.pen-drawing-active #boardTextLayer .board-text-item,
body.pen-drawing-active #canvasSelectionBox{
  pointer-events:none!important;
}
#canvasSelectionBox{
  position:fixed!important;
  z-index:10080!important;
  pointer-events:auto!important;
  cursor:move!important;
  touch-action:none!important;
  user-select:none!important;
  border:2px dashed #8b5cf6!important;
  background:rgba(139,92,246,.05)!important;
  border-radius:6px!important;
  box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(255,255,255,.45)!important;
}

/* ضبط حواف أسطر المصحف من اليمين واليسار تمامًا مثل المصحف الأصلي، مع جعل الإطار على قد الآيات بالضبط */
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode{
  align-items:flex-start!important;
}
.verse-stage.all-verses-mode > #allVersesCard{
  height:fit-content!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  max-height:calc(100vh - var(--all-verses-top, 128px) - 8px)!important;
  padding-top:14px!important;
  padding-bottom:18px!important;
  border-radius:18px!important;
  border-bottom:1.5px solid #e4ddc4!important;
}
.mushaf-page-lines{
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 100%)!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  margin:0 auto!important;
}
#bismillah,#bismillah *,.all-bismillah,.all-bismillah *,#verseText,#verseText *,#allVersesCard .mushaf-line,#allVersesCard .mushaf-line *{
  font-weight:400!important;
  font-synthesis:none!important;
}
.mushaf-line{
  display:flex!important;
  flex-direction:row!important;
  flex-wrap:nowrap!important;
  align-items:baseline!important;
  justify-content:space-between!important;
  width:var(--mushaf-target-width, 100%)!important;
  max-width:100%!important;
  gap:0.03em!important;
  font-size:var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px)!important;
  word-spacing:normal!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
}
.mushaf-line.mushaf-line-short{
  justify-content:center!important;
  gap:0.10em!important;
}
.mushaf-line.mushaf-line-mid-start{
  justify-content:flex-end!important;
  gap:0.06em!important;
}
.mushaf-line.mushaf-line-mid-end{
  justify-content:flex-start!important;
  gap:0.06em!important;
}
.mushaf-ghost-segment{
  display:contents!important;
}
.mushaf-line .mushaf-ghost-token{
  display:inline-block!important;
  flex:0 0 auto!important;
  white-space:nowrap!important;
  font-family:var(--qcf-page-font,var(--mushaf-font,'UthmanicHafs',serif))!important;
  font-palette:var(--qcf-font-palette,normal)!important;
  font-size:inherit!important;
  line-height:inherit!important;
  margin:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
  visibility:hidden!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  user-select:none!important;
}
.mushaf-line button.mushaf-ghost-end{
  display:inline-grid!important;
  place-items:center!important;
  vertical-align:middle!important;
  width:1.36em!important;
  min-width:1.36em!important;
  max-width:1.36em!important;
  height:1.70em!important;
  min-height:1.70em!important;
  max-height:1.70em!important;
  padding:0!important;
  margin:0 .045em!important;
  border:0!important;
  background:transparent!important;
  background-image:none!important;
  box-shadow:none!important;
  font-size:.78em!important;
  line-height:1!important;
  visibility:hidden!important;
  pointer-events:none!important;
  user-select:none!important;
}
.mushaf-line button.mushaf-ghost-end.qcf-v4-ready{
  display:inline-flex!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  vertical-align:baseline!important;
  width:auto!important;
  min-width:0!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  height:auto!important;
  min-height:0!important;
  max-height:none!important;
  padding:0 .04em!important;
  margin:0 .04em!important;
  font-size:.95em!important;
}
.mushaf-line button.mushaf-ghost-end::before,
.mushaf-line button.mushaf-ghost-end::after{
  content:none!important;
  display:none!important;
}
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-page-sheet,
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-page-lines{
  width:max-content!important;
  max-width:none!important;
}
#allVersesCard.mushaf-measuring .mushaf-line{
  width:max-content!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  justify-content:flex-start!important;
  gap:0.04em!important;
  font-size:var(--mushaf-all-font-size, 42px)!important;
}
#verseText.verse-text,
.verse-stage:not(.all-verses-mode) #verseText{
  word-spacing:normal!important;
  letter-spacing:0!important;
}
#verseText .word,
.mushaf-line .word{
  margin-inline:0!important;
  padding:0!important;
}
/* نافذة PDF تمتد من أعلى الشاشة إلى أسفل جزء في الشاشة مع تحكم بالحجم من السهم الجانبي */
#pdfSidePanel{
  top:2px!important;
  bottom:0!important;
  height:calc(100vh - 4px)!important;
  max-height:calc(100vh - 2px)!important;
  max-width:calc(100vw - 16px)!important;
  border-radius:14px 14px 0 0!important;
}
#pdfChooseFile{
  font-size:20px!important;
  font-weight:900!important;
  line-height:1!important;
}
</style>
<script id="glossy-sticker-pack-logic">
(function(){
  const popup = document.getElementById('stickerPackPopup');
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('stickerPackToggle');
  const addCustomBtn = document.getElementById('addCustomStickerPackBtn');
  const customFileInput = document.getElementById('customStickerPackFile');
  const listHost = document.getElementById('stickerPackList');
  const penToolbar = document.getElementById('penToolbar');
  const penToggle = document.getElementById('penToggle');
  if(!popup || !toggleBtn || !listHost || !penToolbar) return;

  function svgToDataUrl(svgString){
    return 'data:image/svg+xml;utf8,' + encodeURIComponent(svgString.trim());
  }

  // نافذة المعاينة المكبرة التلقائية للملصق عند مرور المؤشر عليه
  const hoverPreview = document.createElement('div');
  hoverPreview.id = 'stickerHoverPreview';
  hoverPreview.hidden = true;
  hoverPreview.innerHTML = '<img alt="معاينة الملصق" src=""/><span id="stickerHoverPreviewTitle"></span>';
  document.body.appendChild(hoverPreview);
  const hoverPreviewImg = hoverPreview.querySelector('img');
  const hoverPreviewTitle = hoverPreview.querySelector('#stickerHoverPreviewTitle');

  function showStickerHoverPreview(thumbBtn, src, title){
    document.querySelectorAll('.sticker-pack-thumb.is-hovered').forEach(el=>{
      if(el !== thumbBtn) el.classList.remove('is-hovered');
    });
    thumbBtn.classList.add('is-hovered');
    hoverPreviewImg.src = src;
    hoverPreviewTitle.textContent = title || 'ملصق تشجيعي';
    hoverPreview.hidden = false;
    const r = thumbBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pw = 214, ph = 236;
    const centerX = Math.max(pw / 2 + 10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - pw / 2 - 10, r.left + r.width / 2));
    const topY = Math.max(10, r.top - ph - 12);
    hoverPreview.style.left = Math.round(centerX) + 'px';
    hoverPreview.style.top = Math.round(topY) + 'px';
  }

  function hideStickerHoverPreview(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.sticker-pack-thumb.is-hovered').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('is-hovered'));
    hoverPreview.hidden = true;
  }

  // صانع ملصقات عبارات تشجيعية احترافية فائقة الوضوح والجمال (خط عربي كبير وواضح جدًا داخل وسام ملكي ثلاثي الأبعاد)
  function makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id, title, line1, line2='', bg1, bg2, bg3, rim1='#fff8b8', rim2='#c88a12', ribbonColor='#b71c1c', icon='star'}){
    const hasLine2 = Boolean(line2);
    const y1 = hasLine2 ? 98 : 114;
    const fs1 = hasLine2 ? (line1.length > 9 ? 30 : 35) : (line1.length > 8 ? 34 : 42);
    const fs2 = line2.length > 10 ? 27 : 32;
    let topMotif = '';
    if(icon === 'crown'){
      topMotif = `<path d="M72 44 L85 22 L100 36 L115 22 L128 44 Z" fill="url(#gold_${id})" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.6" filter="url(#sh_${id})"/>
                  <circle cx="85" cy="20" r="3.8" fill="#fff59d"/><circle cx="100" cy="18" r="4.8" fill="#ff5252"/><circle cx="115" cy="20" r="3.8" fill="#fff59d"/>`;
    } else if(icon === 'quran'){
      topMotif = `<path d="M80 30 C89 25 96 27 100 31 C104 27 111 25 120 30 L120 47 C111 43 104 45 100 49 C96 45 89 43 80 47 Z" fill="url(#gold_${id})" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
                  <path d="M100 31 L100 49" stroke="#6d4500" stroke-width="1.6"/>`;
    } else {
      topMotif = `<polygon points="100,16 105.5,28 118,29.5 108.5,38 111,50 100,43.5 89,50 91.5,38 82,29.5 94.5,28" fill="url(#gold_${id})" stroke="#fffde7" stroke-width="1.4"/>`;
    }
    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sh_${id}" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%">
          <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="5" flood-color="#071a12" flood-opacity="0.40"/>
        </filter>
        <linearGradient id="gold_${id}" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
          <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fffde7"/>
          <stop offset="28%" stop-color="${rim1}"/>
          <stop offset="68%" stop-color="${rim2}"/>
          <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#fff59d"/>
        </linearGradient>
        <radialGradient id="bg_${id}" cx="35%" cy="28%" r="75%">
          <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${bg1}"/>
          <stop offset="52%" stop-color="${bg2}"/>
          <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${bg3}"/>
        </radialGradient>
        <linearGradient id="gloss_${id}" x1="20%" y1="0%" x2="65%" y2="85%">
          <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0.65"/>
          <stop offset="42%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0.14"/>
          <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffffff" stop-opacity="0"/>
        </linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <g filter="url(#sh_${id})">
        <path d="M54 146 L38 188 L64 178 L76 194 L88 152 Z" fill="${ribbonColor}" stroke="url(#gold_${id})" stroke-width="2.2"/>
        <path d="M146 146 L162 188 L136 178 L124 194 L112 152 Z" fill="${ribbonColor}" stroke="url(#gold_${id})" stroke-width="2.2"/>
        <polygon points="100,5 118,13 137,10 150,24 169,29 174,48 189,60 185,79 194,96 183,113 185,132 169,143 163,161 144,164 132,178 113,174 100,183 87,174 68,178 56,164 37,161 31,143 15,132 17,113 6,96 15,79 11,60 26,48 31,29 50,24 63,10 82,13" fill="url(#gold_${id})"/>
        <circle cx="100" cy="94" r="76" fill="url(#bg_${id})" stroke="url(#gold_${id})" stroke-width="5.5"/>
        <circle cx="100" cy="94" r="68" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.52" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/>
        <ellipse cx="86" cy="54" rx="54" ry="28" transform="rotate(-12 86 54)" fill="url(#gloss_${id})"/>
        ${topMotif}
        <text x="100" y="${y1}" text-anchor="middle" direction="rtl" font-family="'Cairo','Tajawal','Amiri',sans-serif" font-size="${fs1}" font-weight="900" fill="#ffffff" stroke="#081c14" stroke-width="4.2" stroke-linejoin="round" paint-order="stroke fill">${line1}</text>
        ${hasLine2 ? `<text x="100" y="136" text-anchor="middle" direction="rtl" font-family="'Cairo','Tajawal','Amiri',sans-serif" font-size="${fs2}" font-weight="900" fill="#fff59d" stroke="#081c14" stroke-width="3.6" stroke-linejoin="round" paint-order="stroke fill">${line2}</text>` : ''}
        <path d="M32 38 L35 47 L44 50 L35 53 L32 62 L29 53 L20 50 L29 47 Z" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.96"/>
        <path d="M168 42 L170 49 L177 51 L170 53 L168 60 L166 53 L159 51 L166 49 Z" fill="#fff59d" opacity="0.96"/>
      </g>
    </svg>`;
    return {id, title, src: svgToDataUrl(svg)};
  }

  function makeGlossyStarSticker({id, title, c1, c2, c3, rim='#fff8b5'}){
    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>

  function makeGlossyHeartSticker({id, title, c1, c2, c3, rim='#ffe4e9'}){
    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">

  function makeSpecialQuranStickers(){
    const quranMushafSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sp_q1" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#092218" flood-opacity="0.38"/></filter>
        <radialGradient id="sp_q1_bg" cx="50%" cy="40%" r="65%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#267356"/><stop offset="65%" stop-color="#134634"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#0a291e"/></radialGradient>
        <linearGradient id="sp_q1_gold" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fff9c4"/><stop offset="45%" stop-color="#ffd54f"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#b27d12"/></linearGradient>

        <path d="M44 74 C66 64 86 68 100 78 C114 68 134 64 156 74 L156 130 C134 120 114 124 100 134 C86 124 66 120 44 130 Z" fill="#fffdf4" stroke="url(#sp_q1_gold)" stroke-width="4" stroke-linejoin="round"/>

        <path d="M62 132 L100 150 L138 132 L146 142 L100 162 L54 142 Z" fill="url(#sp_q1_gold)"/>
        <text x="100" y="56" text-anchor="middle" direction="rtl" font-family="'Cairo','Tajawal',sans-serif" font-size="25" font-weight="900" fill="#fff59d" stroke="#081c14" stroke-width="3" paint-order="stroke fill">نور القرآن</text>
      </g>
    </svg>`;

    const crownStickerSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sp_cr" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#1c0d26" flood-opacity="0.38"/></filter>
        <linearGradient id="sp_cr_g" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fff9c4"/><stop offset="40%" stop-color="#ffd740"/><stop offset="80%" stop-color="#c68400"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffecb3"/></linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <g filter="url(#sp_cr)">
        <circle cx="100" cy="104" r="80" fill="#4a148c" stroke="url(#sp_cr_g)" stroke-width="6"/>
        <ellipse cx="86" cy="58" rx="48" ry="24" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.18"/>
        <path d="M42 122 L32 64 L70 90 L100 42 L130 90 L168 64 L158 122 Z" fill="url(#sp_cr_g)" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
        <rect x="42" y="122" width="116" height="18" rx="7" fill="url(#sp_cr_g)" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
        <circle cx="32" cy="60" r="7" fill="#00e676" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
        <circle cx="100" cy="36" r="9" fill="#ff1744" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2"/>
        <circle cx="168" cy="60" r="7" fill="#00e676" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
        <text x="100" y="168" text-anchor="middle" direction="rtl" font-family="'Cairo','Tajawal',sans-serif" font-size="27" font-weight="900" fill="#fff9c4" stroke="#220838" stroke-width="3.2" paint-order="stroke fill">تاج الوقار</text>
      </g>
    </svg>`;

    const trophyStickerSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sp_tr" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#1f1404" flood-opacity="0.38"/></filter>
        <linearGradient id="sp_tr_g" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fffde7"/><stop offset="35%" stop-color="#ffd54f"/><stop offset="75%" stop-color="#c78500"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ffe082"/></linearGradient>

      <g filter="url(#sp_tr)">
        <path d="M58 34 H142 V78 C142 106 124 124 100 128 C76 124 58 106 58 78 Z" fill="url(#sp_tr_g)" stroke="#fff8e1" stroke-width="2.5"/>

        <path d="M142 46 H164 C164 78 154 96 136 98" fill="none" stroke="url(#sp_tr_g)" stroke-width="9" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <rect x="88" y="126" width="24" height="26" rx="4" fill="url(#sp_tr_g)"/>
        <rect x="64" y="150" width="72" height="14" rx="6" fill="url(#sp_tr_g)" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5"/>
        <rect x="52" y="164" width="96" height="20" rx="7" fill="#5d4037" stroke="url(#sp_tr_g)" stroke-width="2.5"/>
        <polygon points="100,50 107,65 123,67 111,78 114,94 100,86 86,94 89,78 77,67 93,65" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.92"/>
      </g>

    const lanternStickerSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sp_ln" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#081b29" flood-opacity="0.38"/></filter>
        <radialGradient id="sp_ln_glow" cx="50%" cy="52%" r="50%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fff9c4"/><stop offset="55%" stop-color="#ffb300"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ff6f00" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
        <linearGradient id="sp_ln_g" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#fff8e1"/><stop offset="45%" stop-color="#ffd54f"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#b27800"/></linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <g filter="url(#sp_ln)">

        <circle cx="48" cy="116" r="3.5" fill="#fff9c4"/><circle cx="156" cy="56" r="4" fill="#fff9c4"/>
      </g>

    const diamondStickerSvg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 200 200" width="200" height="200">
      <defs>
        <filter id="sp_dm" x="-18%" y="-18%" width="136%" height="136%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="6" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#072235" flood-opacity="0.36"/></filter>
        <linearGradient id="sp_dm_1" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%"><stop offset="0%" stop-color="#e0f7fa"/><stop offset="45%" stop-color="#4dd0e1"/><stop offset="100%" stop-color="#00838f"/></linearGradient>
      </defs>
      <g filter="url(#sp_dm)">
        <polygon points="56,34 144,34 182,78 100,176 18,78" fill="url(#sp_dm_1)" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="4" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
        <polygon points="56,34 144,34 126,78 74,78" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.55"/>
        <polygon points="18,78 74,78 100,176" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.28"/>
        <polygon points="126,78 182,78 100,176" fill="#004d40" opacity="0.22"/>
        <polygon points="74,78 126,78 100,176" fill="#80deea" opacity="0.45"/>
        <line x1="18" y1="78" x2="182" y2="78" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="2.5"/>
        <path d="M40 20 L43 29 L52 32 L43 35 L40 44 L37 35 L28 32 L37 29 Z" fill="#ffffff"/>
        <path d="M164 122 L167 130 L175 133 L167 136 L164 144 L161 136 L153 133 L161 130 Z" fill="#e0f7fa"/>
      </g>
    </svg>`;

    return [
      {id:'quran_mushaf', title:'نور القرآن', src: svgToDataUrl(quranMushafSvg)},
      {id:'quran_crown', title:'تاج الوقار', src: svgToDataUrl(crownStickerSvg)},
      {id:'quran_trophy', title:'كأس التفوق الذهبي', src: svgToDataUrl(trophyStickerSvg)},
      {id:'quran_lantern', title:'فانوس وهلال القرآن', src: svgToDataUrl(lanternStickerSvg)},
      {id:'quran_diamond', title:'ماسة التألق', src: svgToDataUrl(diamondStickerSvg)}
    ];
  }

  const BUILTIN_GLOSSY_STICKERS = [
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'ahsant', title:'أحسنت', line1:'أحسنت', bg1:'#2ecc71', bg2:'#166b44', bg3:'#0b3b24', ribbonColor:'#c62828', icon:'star'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'mumtaz', title:'ممتاز', line1:'ممتاز', bg1:'#f59e0b', bg2:'#b45309', bg3:'#78350f', ribbonColor:'#1e40af', icon:'crown'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'barak_batal', title:'بارك الله فيك يا بطل', line1:'بارك الله فيك', line2:'يا بطل', bg1:'#3b82f6', bg2:'#1d4ed8', bg3:'#1e3a8a', ribbonColor:'#d97706', icon:'crown'}),

    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'barakallah', title:'بارك الله فيك', line1:'بارك الله', line2:'فيك', bg1:'#ec4899', bg2:'#be185d', bg3:'#831843', ribbonColor:'#1d4ed8', icon:'star'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'mumtaz_batal', title:'ممتاز يا بطل', line1:'ممتاز', line2:'يا بطل', bg1:'#a855f7', bg2:'#6d28d9', bg3:'#4c1d95', ribbonColor:'#e11d48', icon:'crown'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'qari_mutamayyiz', title:'قارئ متميز', line1:'قارئ', line2:'متميز', bg1:'#f59e0b', bg2:'#b45309', bg3:'#78350f', ribbonColor:'#15803d', icon:'quran'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'tilawa_raia', title:'تلاوة رائعة', line1:'تلاوة', line2:'رائعة', bg1:'#14b8a6', bg2:'#0f766e', bg3:'#134e4a', ribbonColor:'#b91c1c', icon:'quran'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'najm_halaqa', title:'نجم الحلقة', line1:'نجم', line2:'الحلقة', bg1:'#eab308', bg2:'#a16207', bg3:'#713f12', ribbonColor:'#1e40af', icon:'crown'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'zadak_allah', title:'زادك الله علمًا', line1:'زادك الله', line2:'علمًا', bg1:'#6366f1', bg2:'#4338ca', bg3:'#312e81', ribbonColor:'#b91c1c', icon:'star'}),
    makeGlossyBadgeSticker({id:'hifz_mutqan', title:'حفظ متقن', line1:'حفظ متقن', bg1:'#22c55e', bg2:'#15803d', bg3:'#14532d', ribbonColor:'#9333ea', icon:'quran'}),
    ...makeSpecialQuranStickers(),
    makeGlossyStarSticker({id:'star_gold', title:'نجمة ذهبية لامعة', c1:'#fffde7', c2:'#ffd600', c3:'#ff8f00', rim:'#fff9c4'}),
    makeGlossyStarSticker({id:'star_emerald', title:'نجمة زمردية لامعة', c1:'#b9f6ca', c2:'#00c853', c3:'#1b5e20', rim:'#e8f5e9'}),
    makeGlossyStarSticker({id:'star_royal', title:'نجمة زرقاء ملكية لامعة', c1:'#bbdefb', c2:'#2979ff', c3:'#0d47a1', rim:'#e3f2fd'}),
    makeGlossyStarSticker({id:'star_ruby', title:'نجمة حمراء ياقوتية لامعة', c1:'#ffcdd2', c2:'#ff1744', c3:'#b71c1c', rim:'#ffebee'}),

    makeGlossyHeartSticker({id:'heart_emerald', title:'قلب زمردي لامع', c1:'#a5d6a7', c2:'#2e7d32', c3:'#1b5e20', rim:'#c8e6c9'}),
    makeGlossyHeartSticker({id:'heart_blue', title:'قلب سماوي لامع', c1:'#80d8ff', c2:'#0288d1', c3:'#01579b', rim:'#b3e5fc'}),
    makeGlossyHeartSticker({id:'heart_purple', title:'قلب بنفسجي لامع', c1:'#ea80fc', c2:'#8e24aa', c3:'#4a148c', rim:'#e1bee7'})
  ];

  let spawnCascadeCount = 0;

  function loadCustomPackFromStorage(){
    try{
      const raw = localStorage.getItem('rihlat_custom_sticker_pack_v1');
      if(raw){
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if(Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
      }
    }catch(_){}
    return [];
  }
  function saveCustomPackToStorage(list){
    try{
      localStorage.setItem('rihlat_custom_sticker_pack_v1', JSON.stringify(list));
    }catch(_){}
  }

  let customStickerPack = loadCustomPackFromStorage();

  function syncPopupGeometry(){
    if(popup.hidden){
      hideStickerHoverPreview();
      return;
    }
    if(!penToolbar.classList.contains('show')){
      popup.hidden = true;
      hideStickerHoverPreview();
      toggleBtn.classList.remove('active');
      toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
      return;
    }
    const r = penToolbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(r.width > 40){
      popup.style.left = Math.round(r.left) + 'px';
      const bottomPx = Math.max(52, Math.round(window.innerHeight - r.top + 8));
      popup.style.bottom = bottomPx + 'px';
      const uiZ = Number(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--ui-zoom')) || 0.70;
      const exactToolbarW = penToolbar.offsetWidth || Math.round(r.width / uiZ);
      if(exactToolbarW > 60){
        popup.style.setProperty('--sticker-popup-width', exactToolbarW + 'px');
        popup.style.setProperty('width', exactToolbarW + 'px', 'important');
        popup.style.setProperty('max-width', exactToolbarW + 'px', 'important');
      }
    }
  }

  function spawnStickerOnBoard(src, customPos){
    if(typeof window.setPenTool === 'function' && penToolbar.classList.contains('show')){
      window.setPenTool('stop');
    }
    const addFn = window.addSticker;
    if(typeof addFn !== 'function') return null;
    const size = 160;
    const step = (spawnCascadeCount % 7) * 20;
    spawnCascadeCount++;
    const defaultLeft = Math.max(24, Math.min(window.innerWidth - size - 24, Math.round((window.innerWidth - size) * 0.5 + (step - 50))));
    const defaultTop = Math.max(85, Math.min(window.innerHeight - size - 165, Math.round((window.innerHeight - size) * 0.36 + (step - 40))));
    const left = customPos && Number.isFinite(customPos.left) ? Math.max(12, Math.min(window.innerWidth - size - 12, Math.round(customPos.left))) : defaultLeft;
    const top = customPos && Number.isFinite(customPos.top) ? Math.max(24, Math.min(window.innerHeight - size - 12, Math.round(customPos.top))) : defaultTop;
    const item = addFn(src, spawnCascadeCount, {kind:'image', left, top, width:size, height:size}, true);
    if(typeof window.selectSticker === 'function') window.selectSticker(null);
    item?.classList.remove('selected-sticker','multi-selected');
    return item;
  }

  function bindStickerThumbHover(btn, src, title){
    btn.addEventListener('pointerenter', ()=>showStickerHoverPreview(btn, src, title));
    btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', ()=>showStickerHoverPreview(btn, src, title));
    btn.addEventListener('focus', ()=>showStickerHoverPreview(btn, src, title));
    btn.addEventListener('pointerleave', ()=>hideStickerHoverPreview());
    btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>hideStickerHoverPreview());
    btn.addEventListener('blur', ()=>hideStickerHoverPreview());
  }

  function renderStickerPackItems(){
    listHost.innerHTML = '';
    customStickerPack.forEach((item, idx)=>{
      if(!item || !item.src) return;
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.type = 'button';
      btn.className = 'sticker-pack-thumb custom-pack-item';
      const titleText = item.title || ('ملصق مضاف ' + (idx + 1));
      btn.title = titleText;
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', titleText);
      const img = document.createElement('img');
      img.src = item.src;
      img.alt = titleText;
      btn.appendChild(img);
      const del = document.createElement('span');
      del.className = 'sticker-pack-thumb-del';
      del.textContent = '×';
      del.title = 'حذف من شريط الملصقات';
      del.addEventListener('click', e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        hideStickerHoverPreview();
        customStickerPack.splice(idx, 1);
        saveCustomPackToStorage(customStickerPack);
        renderStickerPackItems();
      });
      btn.appendChild(del);
      bindStickerThumbHover(btn, item.src, titleText);
      btn.addEventListener('click', e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        hideStickerHoverPreview();
        spawnStickerOnBoard(item.src);
      });
      listHost.appendChild(btn);
    });
    BUILTIN_GLOSSY_STICKERS.forEach(st=>{
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.type = 'button';
      btn.className = 'sticker-pack-thumb';
      btn.title = st.title;
      btn.setAttribute('aria-label', st.title);
      const img = document.createElement('img');
      img.src = st.src;
      img.alt = st.title;
      btn.appendChild(img);
      bindStickerThumbHover(btn, st.src, st.title);
      btn.addEventListener('click', e=>{
        e.stopPropagation();
        hideStickerHoverPreview();
        spawnStickerOnBoard(st.src);
      });
      listHost.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }

  listHost.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    if(Math.abs(e.deltaY) > Math.abs(e.deltaX)){
      e.preventDefault();
      listHost.scrollLeft -= e.deltaY;
    }
  }, {passive:false});
  popup.addEventListener('mouseleave', hideStickerHoverPreview);

  toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const willOpen = popup.hidden;
    popup.hidden = !willOpen;
    if(!willOpen) hideStickerHoverPreview();
    toggleBtn.classList.toggle('active', willOpen);
    toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(willOpen));
    if(willOpen){
      renderStickerPackItems();
      syncPopupGeometry();
      requestAnimationFrame(syncPopupGeometry);
    }
  });

  addCustomBtn?.addEventListener('click', e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    if(customFileInput){
      customFileInput.value = '';
      customFileInput.click();
    }
  });

  customFileInput?.addEventListener('change', async e=>{
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []).filter(f=>f.type.startsWith('image/')).slice(0, 8);
    if(!files.length) return;
    for(const file of files){
      try{
        const dataUrl = await new Promise((resolve, reject)=>{
          const reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = ()=>resolve(String(reader.result || ''));
          reader.onerror = ()=>reject(reader.error);
          reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        });
        if(dataUrl){
          customStickerPack.unshift({title: file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || 'ملصق مضاف', src: dataUrl});
          spawnStickerOnBoard(dataUrl);
        }
      }catch(_){}
    }
    saveCustomPackToStorage(customStickerPack);
    renderStickerPackItems();
    syncPopupGeometry();
    if(typeof window.showToast === 'function') window.showToast('تمت إضافة الملصق إلى شريط الملصقات وعلى الشاشة.');
    e.target.value = '';
  });

  window.addEventListener('resize', syncPopupGeometry, {passive:true});
  if('ResizeObserver' in window){
    new ResizeObserver(syncPopupGeometry).observe(penToolbar);
  }
  new MutationObserver(()=>{
    if(!penToolbar.classList.contains('show') && !popup.hidden){
      popup.hidden = true;
      hideStickerHoverPreview();
      toggleBtn.classList.remove('active');
      toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    } else if(!popup.hidden){
      syncPopupGeometry();
    }
  }).observe(penToolbar, {attributes:true, attributeFilter:['class','style']});

  renderStickerPackItems();

  // === ثالثًا: التكبير والتصغير للمصحف وإطاره يكون فقط عبر Ctrl + (+) و Ctrl + (-) أو Ctrl + عجلة الماوس ===
  if(typeof window.applyWorkspaceZoom === 'function'){
    const zs = typeof window.getZoomState === 'function' ? window.getZoomState() : {uiZoom: 0.70, mushafZoom: 1.0};
    window.applyWorkspaceZoom(zs.uiZoom, zs.mushafZoom, false);
  }

  document.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{
    // التكبير والتصغير بعجلة الماوس يعمل فقط عند الضغط على زر Ctrl
    if(!(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) return;
    const target = e.target;
    if(!target) return;
    if(target.closest('.settings-panel,.modal,#stickerPackPopup,#verseNumberPopup,#boardTextFontPanel,#pdfSidePanel,#screenItemTray,.quran-search-results,.surah-search-results')) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    const zs = typeof window.getZoomState === 'function' ? window.getZoomState() : {uiZoom: 0.70, mushafZoom: 1.0};
    const dir = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const inMushaf = Boolean(target.closest('#allVersesCard, .verse-card, .verse-stage'));
    if(inMushaf){
      const nextMushafZoom = Math.max(0.50, Math.min(1.85, Number((zs.mushafZoom + dir * 0.05).toFixed(3))));
      if(typeof window.applyWorkspaceZoom === 'function'){
        window.applyWorkspaceZoom(zs.uiZoom, nextMushafZoom, true);
      }
    } else {
      const nextUiZoom = Math.max(0.45, Math.min(1.30, Number((zs.uiZoom + dir * 0.04).toFixed(3))));
      if(typeof window.applyWorkspaceZoom === 'function'){
        window.applyWorkspaceZoom(nextUiZoom, zs.mushafZoom, true);
      }
    }
  }, {passive:false});

  document.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{
    if(!(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) || e.altKey) return;
    const isPlus = (e.key === '+' || e.key === '=' || e.code === 'Equal' || e.code === 'NumpadAdd');
    const isMinus = (e.key === '-' || e.key === '_' || e.code === 'Minus' || e.code === 'NumpadSubtract');
    const isZero = (e.key === '0' || e.code === 'Digit0' || e.code === 'Numpad0');
    if(!isPlus && !isMinus && !isZero) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const zs = typeof window.getZoomState === 'function' ? window.getZoomState() : {uiZoom: 0.70, mushafZoom: 1.0};
    if(isZero){
      if(typeof window.applyWorkspaceZoom === 'function'){
        window.applyWorkspaceZoom(zs.uiZoom, 1.0, true);
      }
      return;
    }
    const dir = isPlus ? 1 : -1;
    const nextMushafZoom = Math.max(0.50, Math.min(1.85, Number((zs.mushafZoom + dir * 0.06).toFixed(3))));
    if(typeof window.applyWorkspaceZoom === 'function'){
      window.applyWorkspaceZoom(zs.uiZoom, nextMushafZoom, true);
    }
  }, true);

  // === ثانيًا + خامسًا + سادسًا: أداة السهم وتحديد العناصر المرسومة والنصوص والملصقات (مفردة أو مجمعة) والتحكم فيها معًا (تحريك، تكبير/تصغير، حذف) ===
  const marqueeEl = document.createElement('div');
  marqueeEl.id = 'boardSelectionMarquee';
  marqueeEl.hidden = true;
  document.body.appendChild(marqueeEl);

  const canvasSelBox = document.createElement('div');
  canvasSelBox.id = 'canvasSelectionBox';
  canvasSelBox.hidden = true;
  canvasSelBox.innerHTML = `
    <button type="button" class="group-corner-handle corner-nw" data-corner="nw" tabindex="-1" aria-label="تكبير وتصغير"></button>
    <button type="button" class="group-corner-handle corner-ne" data-corner="ne" tabindex="-1" aria-label="تكبير وتصغير"></button>
    <button type="button" class="group-corner-handle corner-sw" data-corner="sw" tabindex="-1" aria-label="تكبير وتصغير"></button>
    <button type="button" class="group-corner-handle corner-se" data-corner="se" tabindex="-1" aria-label="تكبير وتصغير"></button>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(canvasSelBox);

  let selectedDrawings = new Set();

  function distToSegment(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y2){
    const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
    const lenSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if(lenSq === 0) return Math.hypot(px - x1, py - y1);
    let t = ((px - x1) * dx + (py - y1) * dy) / lenSq;
    t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
    return Math.hypot(px - (x1 + t * dx), py - (y1 + t * dy));
  }

  function getStrokeBoundingBox(d){
    if(!d) return null;
    const pad = Math.max(6, ((d.tool === 'marker' ? Math.max(18, (Number(d.width)||5)*3.5) : (Number(d.width)||5)) / 2) + 4);
    if(d.tool === 'free' || d.tool === 'marker'){
      const pts = d.points || [];
      if(!pts.length) return null;
      let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
      for(const p of pts){
        if(p.x < minX) minX = p.x;
        if(p.x > maxX) maxX = p.x;
        if(p.y < minY) minY = p.y;
        if(p.y > maxY) maxY = p.y;
      }
      return {left: minX - pad, top: minY - pad, right: maxX + pad, bottom: maxY + pad};
    }
    if(d.from && d.to){
      const minX = Math.min(d.from.x, d.to.x) - pad;
      const maxX = Math.max(d.from.x, d.to.x) + pad;
      const minY = Math.min(d.from.y, d.to.y) - pad;
      const maxY = Math.max(d.from.y, d.to.y) + pad;
      return {left: minX, top: minY, right: maxX, bottom: maxY};
    }
    return null;
  }

  function getStrokeSamplePoints(d){
    if(!d) return [];
    if(d.tool === 'free' || d.tool === 'marker') return d.points || [];
    if(!d.from || !d.to) return [];
    const pts = [];
    const steps = 16;
    if(d.tool === 'line'){
      for(let i = 0; i <= steps; i++){
        const t = i / steps;
        pts.push({x: d.from.x + (d.to.x - d.from.x)*t, y: d.from.y + (d.to.y - d.from.y)*t});
      }
    } else if(d.tool === 'rect'){
      const x1 = d.from.x, y1 = d.from.y, x2 = d.to.x, y2 = d.to.y;
      for(let i = 0; i <= 8; i++){
        const t = i / 8;
        pts.push({x: x1 + (x2 - x1)*t, y: y1}, {x: x2, y: y1 + (y2 - y1)*t}, {x: x2 - (x2 - x1)*t, y: y2}, {x: x1, y: y2 - (y2 - y1)*t});
      }
    } else if(d.tool === 'circle'){
      const cx = (d.from.x + d.to.x)/2, cy = (d.from.y + d.to.y)/2;
      const rx = Math.max(1, Math.abs(d.to.x - d.from.x)/2), ry = Math.max(1, Math.abs(d.to.y - d.from.y)/2);
      for(let i = 0; i < 20; i++){
        const ang = (i / 20) * Math.PI * 2;
        pts.push({x: cx + Math.cos(ang)*rx, y: cy + Math.sin(ang)*ry});
      }
    }
    return pts;
  }

  function hitTestStrokeAtPoint(d, px, py, extraTol=10){
    const bb = getStrokeBoundingBox(d);
    if(!bb) return false;
    const tol = Math.max(10, ((d.tool === 'marker' ? Math.max(18, (Number(d.width)||5)*3.5) : (Number(d.width)||5)) / 2) + extraTol);
    if(px < bb.left - tol || px > bb.right + tol || py < bb.top - tol || py > bb.bottom + tol) return false;
    if(d.tool === 'free' || d.tool === 'marker'){
      const pts = d.points || [];
      if(pts.length === 1) return Math.hypot(px - pts[0].x, py - pts[0].y) <= tol;
      for(let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++){
        if(distToSegment(px, py, pts[i-1].x, pts[i-1].y, pts[i].x, pts[i].y) <= tol) return true;
      }
      return false;
    }
    if(d.tool === 'line' && d.from && d.to){
      return distToSegment(px, py, d.from.x, d.from.y, d.to.x, d.to.y) <= tol;
    }
    if(d.tool === 'rect' && d.from && d.to){
      const x1 = Math.min(d.from.x, d.to.x), x2 = Math.max(d.from.x, d.to.x);
      const y1 = Math.min(d.from.y, d.to.y), y2 = Math.max(d.from.y, d.to.y);
      const nearEdge = distToSegment(px, py, x1, y1, x2, y1) <= tol ||
                       distToSegment(px, py, x2, y1, x2, y2) <= tol ||
                       distToSegment(px, py, x2, y2, x1, y2) <= tol ||
                       distToSegment(px, py, x1, y2, x1, y1) <= tol;
      const inside = px >= x1 && px <= x2 && py >= y1 && py <= y2;
      return nearEdge || inside;
    }
    if(d.tool === 'circle' && d.from && d.to){
      const cx = (d.from.x + d.to.x)/2, cy = (d.from.y + d.to.y)/2;
      const rx = Math.max(4, Math.abs(d.to.x - d.from.x)/2 + tol);
      const ry = Math.max(4, Math.abs(d.to.y - d.from.y)/2 + tol);
      const norm = ((px - cx)*(px - cx))/(rx*rx) + ((py - cy)*(py - cy))/(ry*ry);
      return norm <= 1.05;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function rectsIntersect(a, b){
    return !(a.right < b.left || a.left > b.right || a.bottom < b.top || a.top > b.bottom);
  }

  function strokeIntersectsBox(d, box){
    const bb = getStrokeBoundingBox(d);
    if(!bb || !rectsIntersect(bb, box)) return false;
    const pts = getStrokeSamplePoints(d);
    for(const p of pts){
      if(p.x >= box.left && p.x <= box.right && p.y >= box.top && p.y <= box.bottom) return true;
    }
    for(let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++){
      const mx = (pts[i-1].x + pts[i].x)/2, my = (pts[i-1].y + pts[i].y)/2;
      if(mx >= box.left && mx <= box.right && my >= box.top && my <= box.bottom) return true;
    }
    return rectsIntersect(bb, box);
  }

  function strokesAreConnected(a, b){
    if(a === b) return true;
    const ba = getStrokeBoundingBox(a), bb = getStrokeBoundingBox(b);
    if(!ba || !bb) return false;
    const gap = 16;
    if(ba.right + gap < bb.left || ba.left - gap > bb.right || ba.bottom + gap < bb.top || ba.top - gap > bb.bottom) return false;
    const ptsA = getStrokeSamplePoints(a), ptsB = getStrokeSamplePoints(b);
    const stepA = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ptsA.length / 25));
    const stepB = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ptsB.length / 25));
    const thresh = Math.max(Number(a.width)||5, Number(b.width)||5) + 14;
    for(let i = 0; i < ptsA.length; i += stepA){
      for(let j = 0; j < ptsB.length; j += stepB){
        if(Math.hypot(ptsA[i].x - ptsB[j].x, ptsA[i].y - ptsB[j].y) <= thresh) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  function expandToConnectedDrawings(seedStrokes){
    const all = window.boardDrawings || [];
    const result = new Set(seedStrokes);
    const queue = [...seedStrokes];
    while(queue.length){
      const cur = queue.shift();
      for(const other of all){
        if(!result.has(other) && strokesAreConnected(cur, other)){
          result.add(other);
          queue.push(other);
        }
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  window.eraseVectorStrokesByPath = function(eraserPts, eraserWidth){
    const all = window.boardDrawings || [];
    if(!all.length || !Array.isArray(eraserPts) || !eraserPts.length) return;
    const tol = Math.max(10, (Number(eraserWidth)||15)/2);
    for(let i = all.length - 1; i >= 0; i--){
      const d = all[i];
      const hit = eraserPts.some(ep => hitTestStrokeAtPoint(d, ep.x, ep.y, tol));
      if(hit){
        selectedDrawings.delete(d);
        all.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    if(typeof window.redrawVectorCanvas === 'function') window.redrawVectorCanvas(true);
    updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
  };

  function getSelectedStickers(){
    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker, #stickerLayer .sticker-item.multi-selected')).filter(el=>el.dataset.trayHidden !== '1');
  }
  function getSelectedTexts(){
    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item.selected, #boardTextLayer .board-text-item.multi-selected')).filter(el=>el.dataset.trayHidden !== '1');
  }
  function getSelectedDrawingsList(){
    const all = window.boardDrawings || [];
    return all.filter(d => selectedDrawings.has(d));
  }

  function computeCombinedSelectionBounds(){
    const stickers = getSelectedStickers();
    const texts = getSelectedTexts();
    const drawings = getSelectedDrawingsList();
    const totalCount = stickers.length + texts.length + drawings.length;
    if(totalCount === 0) return null;
    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    for(const el of stickers){
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(r.width > 0 && r.height > 0){
        minX = Math.min(minX, r.left); minY = Math.min(minY, r.top);
        maxX = Math.max(maxX, r.right); maxY = Math.max(maxY, r.bottom);
      }
    }
    for(const el of texts){
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(r.width > 0 && r.height > 0){
        minX = Math.min(minX, r.left); minY = Math.min(minY, r.top);
        maxX = Math.max(maxX, r.right); maxY = Math.max(maxY, r.bottom);
      }
    }
    for(const d of drawings){
      const bb = getStrokeBoundingBox(d);
      if(bb){
        minX = Math.min(minX, bb.left); minY = Math.min(minY, bb.top);
        maxX = Math.max(maxX, bb.right); maxY = Math.max(maxY, bb.bottom);
      }
    }
    if(!Number.isFinite(minX)) return null;
    return {left: minX - 4, top: minY - 4, right: maxX + 4, bottom: maxY + 4, totalCount, hasDrawings: drawings.length > 0};
  }

  function updateUnifiedSelectionBox(){
    const b = computeCombinedSelectionBounds();
    if(!b || (!b.hasDrawings && b.totalCount <= 1)){
      canvasSelBox.hidden = true;
      return;
    }
    canvasSelBox.hidden = false;
    canvasSelBox.style.left = Math.round(b.left) + 'px';
    canvasSelBox.style.top = Math.round(b.top) + 'px';
    canvasSelBox.style.width = Math.max(20, Math.round(b.right - b.left)) + 'px';
    canvasSelBox.style.height = Math.max(20, Math.round(b.bottom - b.top)) + 'px';
  }

  function clearAllSelections(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item.multi-selected, #stickerLayer .sticker-item.selected-sticker').forEach(el=>{
      el.classList.remove('multi-selected','selected-sticker');
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item.multi-selected, #boardTextLayer .board-text-item.selected').forEach(el=>{
      el.classList.remove('multi-selected','selected');
    });
    selectedDrawings.clear();
    canvasSelBox.hidden = true;
  }
  window.clearAllSelections = clearAllSelections;

  function captureGroupSnapshot(){
    const bounds = computeCombinedSelectionBounds() || {left:0, top:0, right:100, bottom:100};
    const stickers = getSelectedStickers().map(el=>({
      el,
      left: parseFloat(el.style.left) || el.getBoundingClientRect().left,
      top: parseFloat(el.style.top) || el.getBoundingClientRect().top,
      width: el.offsetWidth || 150,
      height: el.offsetHeight || 150
    }));
    const texts = getSelectedTexts().map(el=>({
      el,
      left: parseFloat(el.style.left) || el.getBoundingClientRect().left,
      top: parseFloat(el.style.top) || el.getBoundingClientRect().top,
      fontSize: Number(el.dataset.fontSize) || 36
    }));
    const drawings = getSelectedDrawingsList().map(d=>({
      d,
      width: Number(d.width) || 5,
      points: (d.points || []).map(p=>({x: p.x, y: p.y})),
      from: d.from ? {x: d.from.x, y: d.from.y} : null,
      to: d.to ? {x: d.to.x, y: d.to.y} : null
    }));
    return {bounds, stickers, texts, drawings};
  }

  function applyGroupTranslate(snap, dx, dy){
    for(const s of snap.stickers){
      s.el.style.left = Math.round(s.left + dx) + 'px';
      s.el.style.top = Math.round(s.top + dy) + 'px';
    }
    for(const t of snap.texts){
      t.el.style.left = Math.round(t.left + dx) + 'px';
      t.el.style.top = Math.round(t.top + dy) + 'px';
    }
    for(const dr of snap.drawings){
      if(dr.points.length && dr.d.points){
        for(let i = 0; i < dr.points.length; i++){
          dr.d.points[i].x = dr.points[i].x + dx;
          dr.d.points[i].y = dr.points[i].y + dy;
        }
      }
      if(dr.from && dr.d.from){
        dr.d.from.x = dr.from.x + dx;
        dr.d.from.y = dr.from.y + dy;
      }
      if(dr.to && dr.d.to){
        dr.d.to.x = dr.to.x + dx;
        dr.d.to.y = dr.to.y + dy;
      }
    }
    if(snap.drawings.length && typeof window.redrawVectorCanvas === 'function'){
      window.redrawVectorCanvas(false);
    }
    updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
  }

  function applyGroupScale(snap, scale, anchorX, anchorY){
    const s = Math.max(0.18, Math.min(8.0, scale));
    for(const st of snap.stickers){
      const nl = anchorX + (st.left - anchorX) * s;
      const nt = anchorY + (st.top - anchorY) * s;
      const nw = Math.max(24, Math.min(10000, st.width * s));
      const nh = Math.max(24, Math.min(10000, st.height * s));
      st.el.style.left = Math.round(nl) + 'px';
      st.el.style.top = Math.round(nt) + 'px';
      st.el.style.width = Math.round(nw) + 'px';
      st.el.style.height = Math.round(nh) + 'px';
    }
    for(const tx of snap.texts){
      const nl = anchorX + (tx.left - anchorX) * s;
      const nt = anchorY + (tx.top - anchorY) * s;
      const nfs = Math.round(Math.max(12, Math.min(240, tx.fontSize * s)));
      tx.el.style.left = Math.round(nl) + 'px';
      tx.el.style.top = Math.round(nt) + 'px';
      tx.el.dataset.fontSize = String(nfs);
      const content = tx.el.querySelector('.board-text-content');
      if(content) content.style.fontSize = nfs + 'px';
    }
    for(const dr of snap.drawings){
      dr.d.width = Math.max(1, Math.min(60, dr.width * s));
      if(dr.points.length && dr.d.points){
        for(let i = 0; i < dr.points.length; i++){
          dr.d.points[i].x = anchorX + (dr.points[i].x - anchorX) * s;
          dr.d.points[i].y = anchorY + (dr.points[i].y - anchorY) * s;
        }
      }
      if(dr.from && dr.d.from){
        dr.d.from.x = anchorX + (dr.from.x - anchorX) * s;
        dr.d.from.y = anchorY + (dr.from.y - anchorY) * s;
      }
      if(dr.to && dr.d.to){
        dr.d.to.x = anchorX + (dr.to.x - anchorX) * s;
        dr.d.to.y = anchorY + (dr.to.y - anchorY) * s;
      }
    }
    if(snap.drawings.length && typeof window.redrawVectorCanvas === 'function'){
      window.redrawVectorCanvas(false);
    }
    updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
  }

  function persistGroupChanges(snap){
    if(snap.stickers.length && typeof window.syncStickerState === 'function') window.syncStickerState();
    if(snap.texts.length && typeof window.syncBoardTextState === 'function') window.syncBoardTextState();
    if(snap.drawings.length && typeof window.redrawVectorCanvas === 'function') window.redrawVectorCanvas(true);
    updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
  }

  // تحريك المجموعة أو الرسم المحدد من داخل إطار التحديد الموحد (#canvasSelectionBox)
  let groupMoveState = null;
  canvasSelBox.addEventListener('pointerdown', e=>{
    if(e.target.closest('.group-corner-handle')) return;
    if(e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
    const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
    groupMoveState = {
      pointerId: e.pointerId,
      startX: e.clientX,
      startY: e.clientY,
      snap
    };
    try{ canvasSelBox.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(_){}
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
  });
  canvasSelBox.addEventListener('pointermove', e=>{
    if(!groupMoveState || e.pointerId !== groupMoveState.pointerId) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    applyGroupTranslate(groupMoveState.snap, e.clientX - groupMoveState.startX, e.clientY - groupMoveState.startY);
  });
  const endGroupMove = e=>{
    if(!groupMoveState || (e && e.pointerId !== groupMoveState.pointerId)) return;
    const snap = groupMoveState.snap;
    groupMoveState = null;
    persistGroupChanges(snap);
  };
  canvasSelBox.addEventListener('pointerup', endGroupMove);
  canvasSelBox.addEventListener('pointercancel', endGroupMove);

  // تكبير وتصغير المجموعة أو الرسم المحدد من المقابض الأربعة على #canvasSelectionBox
  canvasSelBox.querySelectorAll('.group-corner-handle').forEach(handle=>{
    let gRes = null;
    const corner = handle.dataset.corner;
    handle.addEventListener('pointerdown', e=>{
      if(e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
      const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
      const b = snap.bounds;
      const oppX = (corner === 'nw' || corner === 'sw') ? b.right : b.left;
      const oppY = (corner === 'nw' || corner === 'ne') ? b.bottom : b.top;
      const startDist = Math.max(16, Math.hypot(e.clientX - oppX, e.clientY - oppY));
      gRes = {pointerId: e.pointerId, snap, oppX, oppY, startDist};
      try{ handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); }catch(_){}
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    handle.addEventListener('pointermove', e=>{
      if(!gRes || e.pointerId !== gRes.pointerId) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const scale = Math.hypot(e.clientX - gRes.oppX, e.clientY - gRes.oppY) / gRes.startDist;
      applyGroupScale(gRes.snap, scale, gRes.oppX, gRes.oppY);
    });
    const endGRes = e=>{
      if(!gRes || (e && e.pointerId !== gRes.pointerId)) return;
      const snap = gRes.snap;
      gRes = null;
      persistGroupChanges(snap);
    };
    handle.addEventListener('pointerup', endGRes);
    handle.addEventListener('pointercancel', endGRes);
  });

  // تكبير وتصغير المجموعة أو الرسم المحدد بعجلة الماوس فوق #canvasSelectionBox
  canvasSelBox.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
    const cx = (snap.bounds.left + snap.bounds.right) / 2;
    const cy = (snap.bounds.top + snap.bounds.bottom) / 2;
    const factor = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.08 : (1 / 1.08);
    applyGroupScale(snap, factor, cx, cy);
    persistGroupChanges(snap);
  }, {passive:false});

  function isActivePenDrawingNow(){
    if(!penToolbar.classList.contains('show')) return false;
    const activeToolBtn = penToolbar.querySelector('.toolset .tool-btn.active[data-tool]');
    const tool = activeToolBtn ? activeToolBtn.getAttribute('data-tool') : 'stop';
    return tool !== 'stop';
  }

  // دعم سحب وتحريك النص مباشرة بالسهم من أي مكان داخله، وكذلك تحريك المجموعة المحددة عند سحب أي عنصر فيها (فقط في وضع السهم)
  let directItemDrag = null;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e=>{
    if(isActivePenDrawingNow()) return;
    if(e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
    const target = e.target;
    if(!target) return;
    if(target.closest('.sticker-corner-handle,.sticker-resize-handle,.text-corner-handle,.board-text-resize,.board-text-drag,.group-corner-handle')) return;
    const textItem = target.closest('.board-text-item');
    const stickerItem = target.closest('.sticker-item');
    if(!textItem && !stickerItem) return;

    const totalSel = getSelectedStickers().length + getSelectedTexts().length + selectedDrawings.size;
    const isAlreadyInMulti = (textItem && (textItem.classList.contains('multi-selected') || (textItem.classList.contains('selected') && totalSel > 1))) ||
                             (stickerItem && (stickerItem.classList.contains('multi-selected') || (stickerItem.classList.contains('selected-sticker') && totalSel > 1)));

    // إذا كان العنصر ضمن مجموعة محددة، فإن سحبه يحرك المجموعة كلها معًا
    if(isAlreadyInMulti && totalSel > 1){
      const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
      directItemDrag = {
        mode: 'group',
        pointerId: e.pointerId,
        startX: e.clientX,
        startY: e.clientY,
        moved: false,
        snap
      };
      e.stopPropagation();
      if(!textItem) e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }

    // إذا ضغط المستخدم على نص مكتوب على الشاشة في وضع السهم (أو سحبه)، حدد النص كاملًا وحركه بحرية
    if(textItem && !isActivePenDrawingNow()){
      if(typeof window.selectBoardText === 'function') window.selectBoardText(textItem, false);
      const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
      directItemDrag = {
        mode: 'text',
        item: textItem,
        pointerId: e.pointerId,
        startX: e.clientX,
        startY: e.clientY,
        moved: false,
        snap
      };
    }
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('pointermove', e=>{
    if(!directItemDrag || e.pointerId !== directItemDrag.pointerId) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - directItemDrag.startX;
    const dy = e.clientY - directItemDrag.startY;
    if(!directItemDrag.moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 5) return;
    directItemDrag.moved = true;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    try{ window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges(); }catch(_){}
    applyGroupTranslate(directItemDrag.snap, dx, dy);
  }, true);

  function finishDirectItemDrag(e){
    if(!directItemDrag || (e && e.pointerId !== undefined && e.pointerId !== directItemDrag.pointerId)) return;
    const wasMoved = directItemDrag.moved;
    const snap = directItemDrag.snap;
    directItemDrag = null;
    if(wasMoved){
      persistGroupChanges(snap);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup', finishDirectItemDrag, true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishDirectItemDrag, true);

  // التحديد بالسحب (Marquee Drag) أو النقر بالسهم على أي جزء من خط مرسوم أو رسمة لتحديدها كاملة (مسموح فقط في وضع السهم، وممنوع تمامًا في وضع القلم)
  let marqueeDrag = null;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e=>{
    if(isActivePenDrawingNow()) return;
    if(e.button !== undefined && e.button !== 0) return;
    const target = e.target;
    if(!target) return;
    if(target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a,.pen-toolbar,#stickerPackPopup,#screenItemTray,.settings-panel,.modal,.settings-backdrop,.modal-backdrop,#boardNav,#penToggle,#settingsToggle,#pdfSidePanel,.sticker-item,.board-text-item,.board-text-font-panel,#canvasSelectionBox')) return;

    // فحص ما إذا كان النقر مباشرة على خط مرسوم أو رسمة لتحديدها وتحريكها فورًا
    const allDrawings = window.boardDrawings || [];
    let hitStroke = null;
    for(let i = allDrawings.length - 1; i >= 0; i--){
      if(hitTestStrokeAtPoint(allDrawings[i], e.clientX, e.clientY, 10)){
        hitStroke = allDrawings[i];
        break;
      }
    }
    if(hitStroke){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if(!e.shiftKey) clearAllSelections();
      const connected = expandToConnectedDrawings([hitStroke]);
      connected.forEach(d => selectedDrawings.add(d));
      updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
      const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
      directItemDrag = {
        mode: 'group',
        pointerId: e.pointerId,
        startX: e.clientX,
        startY: e.clientY,
        moved: false,
        snap
      };
      return;
    }

    if(target.closest('.word,.ayah-end')) return;

    marqueeDrag = {
      pointerId: e.pointerId,
      startX: e.clientX,
      startY: e.clientY,
      curX: e.clientX,
      curY: e.clientY,
      moved: false
    };
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('pointermove', e=>{
    if(!marqueeDrag || e.pointerId !== marqueeDrag.pointerId) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - marqueeDrag.startX;
    const dy = e.clientY - marqueeDrag.startY;
    if(!marqueeDrag.moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 6) return;
    marqueeDrag.moved = true;
    marqueeDrag.curX = e.clientX;
    marqueeDrag.curY = e.clientY;
    try{ window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges(); }catch(_){}

    const box = {
      left: Math.min(marqueeDrag.startX, marqueeDrag.curX),
      top: Math.min(marqueeDrag.startY, marqueeDrag.curY),
      right: Math.max(marqueeDrag.startX, marqueeDrag.curX),
      bottom: Math.max(marqueeDrag.startY, marqueeDrag.curY)
    };
    marqueeEl.hidden = false;
    marqueeEl.style.left = Math.round(box.left) + 'px';
    marqueeEl.style.top = Math.round(box.top) + 'px';
    marqueeEl.style.width = Math.round(box.right - box.left) + 'px';
    marqueeEl.style.height = Math.round(box.bottom - box.top) + 'px';

    document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item').forEach(item=>{
      if(item.dataset.trayHidden === '1') return;
      const r = item.getBoundingClientRect();
      const hit = rectsIntersect(box, r);
      item.classList.toggle('multi-selected', hit);
      item.classList.toggle('selected-sticker', hit);
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item').forEach(item=>{
      if(item.dataset.trayHidden === '1') return;
      const r = item.getBoundingClientRect();
      const hit = rectsIntersect(box, r);
      item.classList.toggle('multi-selected', hit);
      item.classList.toggle('selected', hit);
    });

    const allDrawings = window.boardDrawings || [];
    const hitSeed = allDrawings.filter(d => strokeIntersectsBox(d, box));
    selectedDrawings = expandToConnectedDrawings(hitSeed);
    updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
  }, true);

  function finishMarqueeDrag(e){
    if(!marqueeDrag || (e && e.pointerId !== undefined && e.pointerId !== marqueeDrag.pointerId)) return;
    const wasMoved = marqueeDrag.moved;
    marqueeDrag = null;
    marqueeEl.hidden = true;
    if(!wasMoved){
      clearAllSelections();
    } else {
      updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup', finishMarqueeDrag, true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishMarqueeDrag, true);

  // اختصارات لوحة المفاتيح: Ctrl+A للتحديد الكلي، + / - للتكبير والتصغير الجماعي، و Delete لحذف جميع العناصر المحددة
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{
    const target = e.target;
    const tag = target?.tagName || '';
    const inModalOrInput = /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(tag) || Boolean(target?.closest?.('.settings-panel,.modal,.settings-backdrop,.modal-backdrop'));
    if(inModalOrInput) return;

    if((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'a' && !target?.isContentEditable){
      const stickers = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#stickerLayer .sticker-item')).filter(el=>el.dataset.trayHidden !== '1');
      const texts = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#boardTextLayer .board-text-item')).filter(el=>el.dataset.trayHidden !== '1');
      const allDrawings = window.boardDrawings || [];
      if(stickers.length || texts.length || allDrawings.length){
        e.preventDefault();
        stickers.forEach(el=>el.classList.add('multi-selected','selected-sticker'));
        texts.forEach(el=>el.classList.add('multi-selected','selected'));
        selectedDrawings = new Set(allDrawings);
        updateUnifiedSelectionBox();
        if(typeof window.showToast === 'function') window.showToast('تم تحديد جميع العناصر على الشاشة؛ يمكنك تحريكها أو تكبيرها أو حذفها بزر Delete.');
      }
      return;
    }

    // تكبير وتصغير العناصر المحددة معًا بأزرار + و -
    const bigger = e.key === '+' || e.key === '=' || e.code === 'NumpadAdd';
    const smaller = e.key === '-' || e.key === '_' || e.code === 'NumpadSubtract';
    if((bigger || smaller) && !target?.isContentEditable){
      const totalSel = getSelectedStickers().length + getSelectedTexts().length + selectedDrawings.size;
      if(totalSel > 0){
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopImmediatePropagation();
        const snap = captureGroupSnapshot();
        const cx = (snap.bounds.left + snap.bounds.right) / 2;
        const cy = (snap.bounds.top + snap.bounds.bottom) / 2;
        applyGroupScale(snap, bigger ? 1.12 : (1 / 1.12), cx, cy);
        persistGroupChanges(snap);
        return;
      }
    }

    // حذف جميع العناصر المحددة (ملصقات، صور، نصوص مكتوبة، وخطوط/رسومات القلم) عند الضغط على Delete
    if(e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace'){
      const selStickers = getSelectedStickers();
      const selTexts = getSelectedTexts();
      const selDraws = getSelectedDrawingsList();
      const totalSel = selStickers.length + selTexts.length + selDraws.length;
      if(totalSel === 0) return;

      // عند الكتابة داخل النص: Backspace يمسح الحروف، بينما زر Delete يحذف عنصر النص المحدد كما طُلب
      if(target?.isContentEditable && e.key === 'Backspace' && totalSel === 1 && selTexts.length === 1) return;

      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();

      if(selStickers.length){
        selStickers.forEach(item=>{
          if(typeof window.removeSticker === 'function') window.removeSticker(item, false);
          else item.remove();
        });
        if(typeof window.syncStickerState === 'function') window.syncStickerState();
      }

      if(selTexts.length){
        selTexts.forEach(item=>{
          if(typeof window.removeBoardText === 'function') window.removeBoardText(item, false);
          else item.remove();
        });
        if(typeof window.syncBoardTextState === 'function') window.syncBoardTextState();
      }

      if(selDraws.length){
        const all = window.boardDrawings || [];
        for(let i = all.length - 1; i >= 0; i--){
          if(selectedDrawings.has(all[i])) all.splice(i, 1);
        }
        selectedDrawings.clear();
        if(typeof window.redrawVectorCanvas === 'function') window.redrawVectorCanvas(true);
      }

      canvasSelBox.hidden = true;
      if(typeof window.renderScreenTray === 'function') window.renderScreenTray();
    }
  }, true);

})();
</script>

</body>
</html>
